### [1] [Chiral Phonons Protected by Symmetry in a Strongly Anharmonic Helical Material](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25089)
*Supriyo Ghosh,Dipti Jasrasaria*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作采用从头算多体方法研究强非谐螺旋晶体碲（Te），发现旋转对称性对声子-声子相互作用施加赝角动量选择定则，使手性声子的圆偏振在有限温度下受保护、动态手性可延续至600 K，但寿命显著下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手性声子（chiral phonons）如何在有限温度下的非谐声子-声子相互作用（anharmonic phonon-phonon interactions）中存活，此前理解不足；这一问题关系到有限温度晶格动力学的基础认识。

Method: 使用从头算多体方法（ab initio many-body approach），以强非谐螺旋晶体碲（crystalline tellurium）为研究对象，分析其旋转对称性对声子-声子相互作用的约束。

Result: Te中的旋转对称性在布里渊区（Brillouin zone）的旋转不变对称点上施加赝角动量（pseudoangular momentum）选择定则，限制模式混合与散射；这些对称性保护振动模式的圆偏振（circular polarization）但不保护其寿命，手性振动模式的动态手性（dynamic chirality）在寿命急剧下降时仍延续至600 K。热梯度（thermal gradient）产生的角动量的温度依赖性由寿命而非手性主导，在10至600 K间下降两个数量级。

Conclusion: 手性声子的动态手性可因对称性保护而非谐散射中存活，但其热梯度诱导角动量响应主要由寿命决定；因此手性保持与角动量输运的温度行为需分别由对称性与寿命两个因素描述。

Abstract: How chiral phonons survive anharmonic phonon-phonon interactions, which underpin lattice dynamics at finite temperature, remains poorly understood. Using an ab initio many-body approach, we study crystalline tellurium (Te), a strongly anharmonic helical crystal. We show that rotational symmetries in Te impose pseudoangular momentum selection rules on phonon-phonon interactions, restricting mode mixing and scattering at rotationally invariant symmetry points in the Brillouin zone. These symmetries protect the circular polarization of vibrational modes at finite temperature but not their lifetimes such that the dynamic chirality of handed vibrational modes persists to 600 K, even as lifetimes drop sharply. We find that the temperature-dependence of angular momentum generated by a thermal gradient is governed by lifetimes rather than chirality, decreasing by two orders of magnitude between 10 and 600 K.

</details>


### [2] [A plane-wave auxiliary-field quantum Monte Carlo study of silicon point defects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25121)
*Moritz Humer,Faruk Salihbegovic,Martin Schlipf,Zoran Sukurma,Georg Kresse*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文利用平面波基下的辅助场量子蒙特卡罗（auxiliary-field quantum Monte Carlo, AFQMC）结合投影缀加波（projector-augmented wave, PAW）框架，计算硅自间隙原子与空位的形成能，并提出基于随机相位近似（random-phase approximation, RPA）的修正以消除有限尺寸误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 点缺陷能量预测在计算材料科学中仍是难题；以四面体硅自间隙原子为例，此前周期性耦合簇（CCSD(T)）与扩散蒙特卡罗（diffusion Monte Carlo, DMC）结果相差约1.2 eV，亟需更高精度且受控的方法加以澄清。

Method: 采用平面波基、在PAW框架下构建的AFQMC计算硅自间隙原子与空位形成能，并与RPA和CCSD(T)对比；使用16原子与64原子超胞，验证基于RPA的修正可消除twist平均后残留的两体有限尺寸误差。

Result: 经修正后，16原子超胞中哑铃型与六角型自间隙原子形成能分别为4.4 eV和4.6 eV，与DMC及transcorrelated耦合簇结果吻合良好；四面体间隙原子与Jahn–Teller畸变空位需64原子超胞，形成能分别为5.7 eV和5.2 eV。相同16原子设置下CCSD(T)系统性偏低，最大差异250 meV出现在四面体间隙原子。

Conclusion: 结果表明，此前CCSD(T)对四面体间隙原子的高估源于耦合簇流程中的有限尺寸处理，而非缺陷的多组态（multi-configurational）特征。

Abstract: Accurately predicting the energetics of point defects remains a formidable challenge in computational materials science. For the tetrahedral silicon self-interstitial, for example, previous periodic coupled-cluster [CCSD(T)] and diffusion Monte Carlo (DMC) calculations disagree by about 1.2 eV. Here, we present formation energies of silicon self-interstitials and vacancies using auxiliary-field quantum Monte Carlo (AFQMC) in a plane-wave basis formulated within the projector-augmented wave framework, and compare them with the random-phase approximation (RPA) and CCSD(T). Using 16- and 64-atom cells, we demonstrate that an RPA-based correction effectively removes the two-body finite-size errors that remain after twist averaging in AFQMC for bulk silicon and for the dumbbell and hexagonal self-interstitials. With this correction, we obtain formation energies of 4.4 eV and 4.6 eV for these two self-interstitials in 16-atom cells, in good agreement with DMC and transcorrelated coupled-cluster results. For the tetrahedral interstitial and the Jahn--Teller-distorted vacancy, 16-atom cells are too small to obtain realistic formation energies; in 64-atom cells, we obtain 5.7 eV and 5.2 eV, respectively. With identical setups in 16-atom cells, CCSD(T) yields systematically lower formation energies than AFQMC, with the largest difference, 250 meV, for the tetrahedral interstitial. Our results indicate that the earlier overestimation of CCSD(T) for the tetrahedral interstitial stems from the finite-size treatment in the coupled-cluster workflow rather than from multi-configurational character of the defect.

</details>


### [3] [Machine Learning-Driven High-Throughput Screening of Equimolar and Non-Equimolar High-Entropy Carbides](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25127)
*Arina A. Svinukhova,Faisal Baber,Michel M. Lukanov,Viktor S. Baidyshev,Daria Terentieva,Alexander Ya. Pak,Alexander G. Kvashnin*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究利用大原子模型MACE与深度学习原子间势DeePMD，对IVB、VB族过渡金属（Ti、Zr、Hf、V、Nb、Ta）构成的高熵碳化物（high-entropy carbides, HEC）进行高通量筛选，预测出一批等摩尔与非等摩尔稳定组分以供后续合成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高熵陶瓷（high-entropy ceramics）的组分空间极其庞大，既难以通过实验逐一测试，也无法全部依赖从头算（ab initio）计算，因此需要高效方法在广阔组分空间中寻找最优组分。

Method: 采用基于从头算数据集训练的大原子模型MACE与深度学习原子间势（deep learning-based interatomic potential）DeePMD，对Ti、Zr、Hf、V、Nb、Ta六种过渡金属高熵碳化物的数千种潜在非等摩尔组分开展高通量筛选（high-throughput screening）。

Result: 从等摩尔与非等摩尔HEC组分中预测出一组稳定组分，可作为后续实验合成的候选对象。

Conclusion: 深度学习原子间势可在传统实验与从头算计算难以覆盖的庞大组分空间中高效筛选高熵碳化物，为稳定组分的预测与后续合成提供依据。

Abstract: Search for optimal compositions among high-entropy ceramics are challenging due to large composition space, which are unable to test either experimentally or by \textit{ab initio} calculations. Here we present a high-throughput screening for equimolar and non-equimolar high-entropy carbides (HEC) among group IVB and VB transition metals (Ti, Zr, Hf, V, Nb and Ta). Large Atomistic Model MACE, and deep learning based interatomic potential DeePMD trained on \textit{ab initio}-based dataset of structures are used to effectively screen over the thousands of potential non-equimolar compositions among high-entropy carbides of Ti, Zr, Hf, V, Nb and Ta transition metals. A set of both equimolar and non-equimolar HECs are predicted to be stable in order to further synthesize those compositions based on the predictions.

</details>


### [4] [A Symmetry-Stratified Framework for Phase Transitions: Thermodynamic States, Critical Dynamics, and Spectral Mechanical Signatures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25212)
*Pengfei Zhu,Leiming Du*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出一个按对称性分层（symmetry-stratified）的框架，通过有限维内态（finite-dimensional internal state）与母相不变自由能（parent-invariant free energy），把结构对称性、热力学相稳定性与弹性波响应统一联系起来，并给出声学二重简并（acoustic doublet）分裂阶数 $p$ 与可观测声学能隙 $\sim|t|^{p\beta}$ 的判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结构对称性、热力学相稳定性与弹性波响应虽是描述相变固体的相关却彼此不同的描述，缺乏统一联系；且从力学谱推断结构变化所依赖的本构与测量假设尚不明确。

Method: 构建对称性分层框架：以稳定子（stabilizer）分类结构态，以自由能比较与稳定性条件确定平衡分支和耗散演化；用等变性（equivariance）使冻结弹性切线（frozen elastic tangent）继承结构稳定子，并讨论可辨识性条件；借助小群表示（little-group representations）与简并微扰论（degenerate perturbation theory）确定声学二重简并是否分裂及首个非零分裂阶 $p$。

Result: 在序参量指数为 $\beta$ 的连续分支上，若临界自能（critical self-energies）与结构弛豫不主导测量，则正则可观测声学能隙按 $|t|^{p\beta}$ 标度；显式张量与立方-四方（cubic-to-tetragonal）实现给出线性与二次分裂通道及方向保护的简并。数值基准检验了自由能重构、共存、弹性相容性、有限温度谱与临界读数；NiTi 数据再分析展示了有效弹性简并、热历史依赖及相分数独立测量的预测价值。

Conclusion: 该框架明确了从力学谱推断结构变化所必需的本构与测量假设，从而为对称性、热力学与弹性波响应之间建立可检验的定量联系。

Abstract: Structural symmetry, thermodynamic phase stability, and elastic-wave response provide related but distinct descriptions of a transforming solid. We formulate a symmetry-stratified framework that connects these descriptions through a finite-dimensional internal state and a parent-invariant free energy. Stabilizers classify structural states, whereas free-energy comparisons and stability conditions determine equilibrium branches and dissipative evolution. Equivariance ensures that the frozen elastic tangent inherits the structural stabilizer. Equality requires additional identifiability conditions. Little-group representations and degenerate perturbation theory then determine whether an acoustic doublet splits and the first nonzero splitting order $p$. On a selected continuous branch with order-parameter exponent $β$, a regular, observable acoustic gap scales as $|t|^{pβ}$, provided critical self-energies and structural relaxation do not dominate the measurement. Explicit tensorial and cubic-to-tetragonal realizations yield linear and quadratic splitting channels, together with directionally protected degeneracies. Separate numerical benchmarks examine free-energy reconstruction, coexistence, elastic compatibility, finite-temperature spectra, and controlled critical readouts. Reanalysis of NiTi measurements illustrates effective elastic degeneracy, thermal-history dependence, and the predictive value of independently measured phase fractions. Most importantly, the framework identifies the constitutive and measurement assumptions required to infer structural changes from mechanical spectra.

</details>


### [5] [Single-Crystal Growth, Structure, and Physical Properties of Yb2MoO6](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25225)
*Ali Sarikhani,Santhoshkumar Sundaramoorthy,Sadracd Gamez,Mathew Pollard,Deepak Singh,Jeremy Watts,Steven Kelley,Amitava Choudhury,Yew San Hor*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作合成毫米级Yb₂MoO₆单晶，确定其晶体结构并表征光学与磁学性质，报道光学带隙约3.28 eV及近4.2 μ_B/Yb的有效磁矩与约-65.7 K的Weiss温度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀土钼酸盐（rare-earth molybdates）因其结构与化学灵活性而呈现丰富的磁、电子与光学行为，具有功能氧化物研究价值；但Yb₂MoO₆单晶的生长与基础物性数据仍较缺乏。

Method: 采用单晶生长获得毫米级Yb₂MoO₆晶体，结合单晶X射线衍射（single-crystal X-ray diffraction）、扫描电镜与能谱（SEM/EDS）、漫反射光谱与Tauc分析（Tauc analysis），以及磁化率测量进行表征。

Result: 晶格参数为a=16.228(2) Å、b=10.901(3) Å、c=5.286(2) Å，与以往中子粉末衍射数据一致；Yb:Mo比接近2:1；直接允许跃迁假设下光学带隙约3.28 eV；磁化率呈Curie-Weiss型顺磁行为，μ_eff≈4.2 μ_B/Yb，θ≈-65.7 K，10 K以上未见明显磁有序转变。

Conclusion: 研究确立了Yb₂MoO₆单晶的生长方法，并给出其结构、光学与磁学性质的初步认识；负Weiss温度提示可能存在反铁磁相互作用或关联。

Abstract: Rare-earth molybdates are functional oxides of considerable interest because their structural and chemical flexibility enables diverse magnetic, electronic, and optical behavior. Here, millimeter-sized single crystals of $Yb_2MoO_6$ are synthesized, their crystal structure determined and physical properties investigated. Single-crystal X-ray diffraction confirms the formation of $Yb_2MoO_6$ and yielded lattice parameters of $a = 16.228(2)$ $\mathring{A}$, $b = 10.901(3)$ $\mathring{A}$, and $c = 5.286(2)$ $\mathring{A}$, in good agreement with previously reported neutron powder diffraction data. Scanning electron microscopy and energy-dispersive spectroscopy show a Yb:Mo ratio close to the expected 2:1 stoichiometry. Optical measurements based on diffuse-reflectance spectra and Tauc analysis yield an estimated optical gap of $\sim 3.28$ eV under the direct-allowed transition assumption. Magnetic susceptibility measurements reveal Curie-Weiss-like paramagnetic behavior, with an effective magnetic moment of $μ_{\mathrm{eff}} \approx 4.2μ_B$ per Yb and an apparent Weiss temperature of $θ\approx -65.7$ K, consistent with possible antiferromagnetic interactions or correlations, with no clear magnetic-ordering transition observed down to 10 K. These results establish the growth of $Yb_2MoO_6$ single crystals and provide initial insight into their structural, optical, and magnetic properties.

</details>


### [6] [Molecular engineering for crack-free transfer of single-crystalline oxide membranes onto silicon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25246)
*Jeongkeun Song,Jihye Choi,Benjamin Doughty,Young-Hoon Kim,Keun-Yeol Park,Uvinduni I. Premadasa,Miaofang Chi,Seung Sae Hong,Celesta S. Chang,Zheng Gai,Ambrose Seo,Tomonori Saito,Ho Nyung Lee*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究提出一种界面能调控释放策略，可在低分子量聚碳酸丙烯酯支撑下制备厘米级无裂纹单晶自支撑氧化物膜，并成功转移至硅上保持功能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将单晶复杂氧化物（complex oxides）与硅异质集成可赋予下一代微电子新功能，但自支撑氧化物膜（freestanding oxide membranes）在释放与转移过程中易产生裂纹、褶皱与碎裂，限制了膜尺寸与质量。

Method: 采用界面能控制释放（interfacial energy-controlled release）策略；以SrTiO3为模型体系，通过时间分辨释放研究定位水致界面起泡（blistering）为裂纹来源；并通过聚合物解构降低聚碳酸丙烯酯（poly(propylene carbonate)）支撑层分子量，提高其极性与表面能。

Result: 低分子量支撑层抑制界面起泡并减少去支撑后的有机残留；所得SrTiO3膜光学性质与块体单晶相当，横向尺寸达厘米级（centimeter-scale）且无裂纹；该策略扩展至金属铁磁体La0.7Sr0.3MnO3，在Si上获得保持铁磁性与金属性的无裂纹膜。

Conclusion: 界面能调控的释放策略可制备大面积高质量单晶自支撑氧化物膜，并实现与硅的异质集成，为复杂氧化物功能器件的集成提供可行途径。

Abstract: Heterogeneous integration of single-crystalline complex oxides with silicon offers a route to incorporating their functionalities into next-generation microelectronics. Released from their growth substrates, freestanding oxide membranes enable this integration. However, cracks, wrinkles, and fragmentation during release and transfer limit membrane size and quality. Here, we present an interfacial energy-controlled release strategy that enables crack-free, single-crystalline freestanding oxide membranes with centimeter-scale lateral dimensions. Using SrTiO3 as a model system, time-resolved release studies identify water-induced blistering at the membrane-support interface during prolonged water exposure as the origin of membrane cracking. Lowering the molecular weight of the poly(propylene carbonate) support through polymer deconstruction increases its polarity and surface energy, suppresses interfacial blistering, and reduces organic residue after support removal. The resulting SrTiO3 membranes exhibit optical properties comparable to those of bulk single crystals. We extend this approach to the metallic ferromagnet La0.7Sr0.3MnO3, obtaining crack-free membranes on Si that preserve ferromagnetism and metallicity.

</details>


### [7] [Altermagnetic magnon splitting in hematite: from bulk g-wave to planar i-wave](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25284)
*Tobias Dannegger,Julian A. Beisch,Levente Rózsa,László Szunyogh,Ulrich Nowak*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作通过全相对论第一性原理计算研究赤铁矿（hematite）的磁振子（magnon）性质，定量评估各向异性与交错磁（altermagnetic）能带劈裂及磁振子寿命，揭示偶极相互作用导致的非交错磁劈裂、手性混合以及三角交错磁体兼具g波与i波分量的特殊性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 赤铁矿兼具共线反铁磁与弱铁磁相，具有长程自旋输运等自旋电子学（spintronics）潜力，并被列为g波交错磁（g-wave altermagnetism）候选材料，但其磁振子层面的交错磁效应尚缺乏定量研究。

Method: 采用全相对论从头算（fully relativistic ab initio）方法计算赤铁矿的磁振子性质，定量评估各向异性与交错磁的磁振子能带劈裂及寿命，并与六角g波交错磁体碲化锰（manganese telluride）进行对比，同时推导第四个节点面的隐式解析表达式。

Result: 偶极相互作用产生可观的非交错磁劈裂，并使相反手性磁振子发生杂化，导致Γ点附近及节点面附近磁振子自旋期望值显著降低（即使在共线面外相中亦然）；频率与寿命在k_z=0平面均表现出显著的、赤铁矿特有的交错磁劈裂，而这对g波交错磁体并非普遍预期。与具有四个对称保护节点面的碲化锰相比，赤铁矿的第四个节点面并不完全由对称性固定。

Conclusion: 赤铁矿代表三角交错磁体（trigonal altermagnets）这一特殊情形，其劈裂函数同时包含体态（g波）与面内（i波，向布里渊区边界过渡为i波对称性）分量；仅凭点对称性不足以刻画劈裂函数的全部对称性决定行为。

Abstract: Hematite is an insulating iron oxide with both a collinear antiferromagnetic and a weakly ferromagnetic phase with intriguing properties, such as long-range spin transport distances, that make it promising for spintronics applications. It has also been identified as a candidate for g-wave altermagnetism. Here, we present the results of fully relativistic ab initio calculations of the magnon properties of hematite, with a focus on altermagnetic effects. We quantitatively evaluate both the anisotropic and altermagnetic magnon band splitting as well as the magnon lifetimes. We show that dipolar interactions lead to a sizeable non-altermagnetic splitting and a hybridisation between opposite-chirality magnons resulting in a strong reduction of the spin expectation value of magnons close to the Γ point and around nodal planes, even in the collinear out-of-plane phase. Both the frequencies and the lifetimes have a large altermagnetic splitting in the $k_z = 0$ plane, which is characteristic of hematite and not generally expected for g-wave altermagnets. We compare our results to the prototypical, hexagonal g-wave altermagnet manganese telluride, which has four symmetry-protected nodal planes, whereas the fourth nodal surface in hematite is not completely fixed by symmetry. <details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 破缺时间反演对称性的反铁磁体（含交错磁体）可表现出类铁磁响应，其序参量可由铁性时间反演奇多极（如反映局域轨道各向异性的各向异性磁偶极 AMD）描述。已有 AMD 响应主要见于元胞磁周期性的反铁磁体，长波长磁织构中空间调制的 AMD 尚未被探索。

Result: 观测到显著的散射圆二色性，而既有的螺旋与 Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya 相互作用诱导的正弦自旋结构无法解释该信号。分析表明，尽管自旋反平行排列，FeO₆ 八面体的交错旋转仍产生铁性 AMD 分量；随螺旋相位变化，局域磁对称性改变，使这些 AMD 发生空间调制，并通过与正弦自旋分量的干涉产生二色性。

Conclusion: 长波长反铁磁织构可承载空间调制的各向异性磁偶极，为在磁性与多铁材料中探索隐藏的多极织构（hidden multipole textures）提供了新途径。

Such time-reversal symmetry breaking is characterized by ferroic time-reversal-odd multipoles, distinct from the conventional spin and orbital moments. Among them, the anisotropic magnetic dipole (AMD), which reflects the local orbital anisotropy, shares the symmetry of magnetization and can have a ferroic component even when the spin moments cancel. AMD responses have thus far been identified primarily in antiferromagnets with unit-cell magnetic periodicity, whereas spatially modulated AMDs in long-wavelength magnetic textures remain unexplored. We observe pronounced circular dichroism in the resonant scattering intensity, i.e., scattering circular dichroism, which is not expected from the established cycloidal and Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya-interaction-induced sinusoidal spin structures. As the local magnetic symmetry varies with the cycloidal phase, these AMDs are spatially modulated and produce the dichroism through interference with the sinusoidal spin component. Our findings demonstrate that long-wavelength antiferromagnetic textures can host spatially modulated AMDs, opening new avenues for exploring hidden multipole textures in magnetic and multiferroic materials.

</details>


### [11] [AdsNet: A Graph Neural Network for Direct Adsorption Energy Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25646)
*Changzhi Ai,Frank Abild-Pedersen,Johannes Voss*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: AdsNet 是一种等变图神经网络（equivariant graph neural network），仅基于吸附物及其截断半径内近邻表面原子构建局部图，单次前向传播即可预测吸附能，RMSE 为 0.032 eV，兼具高效率与对局部成键环境的几何敏感性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高通量计算催化剂筛选（high-throughput computational catalyst screening）需要快速且准确的吸附能（adsorption energy）预测，但现有方法或牺牲评估速度（全图机器学习势，full-graph machine learning potentials），或牺牲几何敏感性（如基于配位数的 alpha-scheme 模型）。

Method: AdsNet 在仅包含吸附物原子与截断半径内最近表面原子的局部图（local graph）上运行，编码原子间距离与方向向量等连续几何特征，采用含标量与向量节点特征的多层消息传递架构（message-passing architecture）。模型在 AdsTraj 数据集上训练，该数据集包含 33,920 个密度泛函理论（DFT）计算结构，覆盖 26 种过渡金属与 6 种吸附物。

Result: 模型达到吸附能均方根误差（RMSE）0.032 eV、力 RMSE 0.047 eV/Å；通过自动微分（automatic differentiation）获得的解析力配合投影梯度下降（projected gradient descent）与自适应线搜索可完成结构弛豫，无需完整的几何优化。此外，模型可将预测吸附能分解为局部吸附物图中各原子的贡献。

Conclusion: AdsNet 构建了准确、高效且可解释的框架，适用于多种材料表面的大规模吸附能预测与局部界面弛豫，其局部图设计使计算复杂度与体系总尺寸解耦，并提供了传统机器学习势无法实现的可解释性。

Abstract: Fast and accurate prediction of adsorption energies is critical for high-throughput computational catalyst screening, existing methods compromise either on evaluation speed (full-graph machine learning potentials) or geometric sensitivity (coordination-based models like the alpha-scheme). Here, we present AdsNet, an equivariant graph neural network that directly predicts adsorption energies in a single forward pass, which operates on a local graph restricted only to adsorbate atoms and its immediate surface neighbors within a cutoff radius. This local graph formulation decouples computational complexity from total system size while encoding continuous geometric features (interatomic distances and directional vectors) enabling sensitivity to subtle variations in local bonding environments. AdsNet adopts a message-passing architecture with scalar and vector node features over multiple interaction layers. Trained on AdsTraj, a dataset of 33,920 density functional theory (DFT) calculated structures spanning 26 transition metals and 6 adsorbates (H*, C*, O*, CO*, OH*, HOCO*), the model achieves an adsorption energy root-mean-square error (RMSE) of 0.032 eV and a force RMSE of 0.047 eV/A. Analytic forces obtained via automatic differentiation enable structure relaxation through a projected gradient descent algorithm with adaptive line search, eliminating entire geometry optimizations. Additionally, AdsNet uniquely decomposes predicted adsorption energies into contributions from individual atoms within the local adsorbate graph - an interpretability feature unattainable with conventional machine-learning potentials. AdsNet thus establishes an accurate, highly efficient, and interpretable framework for large-scale adsorption energy prediction and local interface relaxation across various material surfaces.

</details>


### [12] [Thermally driven multiple sliding states and avalanche dynamics in rhombohedral Pb(TaSe2)2 nanoflakes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25739)

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 范德华材料中的层间滑移（interlayer sliding）为调控功能性质提供了有前景的途径，但对其转变过程中复杂集体畴动力学（collective domain dynamics）的全面理解仍然困难。

Method: 结合输运测量与原子级分辨扫描透射电子显微镜（scanning transmission electron microscopy, STEM），研究 Pb(TaSe₂)₂ 纳米片中热驱动的滑移动力学，并对电阻跃变进行统计分析。

Result: 减薄样品厚度会诱发多级阶跃式电阻跳变并增强迟滞（hysteresis），对应伴随波纹位错（ripplocations）的堆叠畴（stacking domains）间转变；跳变的统计分析显示畴动力学具有普适幂律标度（universal power-law scaling）的集体雪崩行为。

Conclusion: 基于上述特征，通过热循环实现了可控、非易失的多级阻变开关（multi-level resistive switching），包括写入/擦除操作；研究为多种二维材料的滑移动力学提供见解，并对滑移电子学（slidetronics）器件发展具有意义。

Abstract: Interlayer sliding in van der Waals materials offers a promising route to control functional properties, yet a comprehensive understanding of the complex collective domain dynamics governing these transitions remains challenging. Here, we investigate thermally driven sliding dynamics in Pb(TaSe2)2 nanoflakes, incorporating transport and atomic-resolution scanning transmission electron microscopy (STEM) measurements. Reducing the flake thickness induces multiple stepwise resistance jumps with enhanced hysteresis, corresponding to transitions between stacking domains accompanied by ripplocations. Statistical analysis of the jumps reveals collective avalanche behavior of domain dynamics with universal power-law scaling, quantifying the domain motion. Harnessing these features, we demonstrate controllable, non-volatile multi-level resistive switching, including write/erase operations, via thermal cycling. These findings provide valuable insights into the sliding dynamics for various 2D materials, with significant implications for the development of slidetronics devices.

</details>


### [13] [Intelligent Imaging of Thermal Transport in Nanostructures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25779)
*Xin Qian,Te-Huan Liu,Xiaobo Li,Ronggui Yang*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该综述（Review）总结了人工智能（artificial intelligence, AI）辅助高空间分辨热表征（thermal characterization）技术的最新进展，涵盖高通量数据处理、界面热输运可视化与非侵入式亚表面分析，并展望了载热子光谱成像（spectral mapping）、三维物性映射与AI智能体实验设计等方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着新型材料与高性能电子器件的关键尺寸缩小、结构异质化加剧，热输运（thermal transport）不再仅由体材料性质决定，而受界面、缺陷等微结构局部变化及工况条件影响，因此迫切需要高空间分辨的热物性测量与热输运可视化手段。

Method: 以非接触式泵浦-探测热反射（non-contact pump-probe thermoreflectance）技术为基础，通过宽场热反射成像（wide-field thermoreflectance imaging）或扫描方式进行比热、热导率与界面热导（interface conductance）的映射；并综述AI在数据处理与成像分析中的辅助作用。

Result: 综述指出该领域仍存在成像通量与测量精度之间的权衡、多参数高置信度提取、非侵入式亚表面表征等关键挑战；同时梳理了AI在高通量数据处理、界面热输运可视化及非侵入亚表面分析方面的近期进展。

Conclusion: AI与实验技术的结合有望实现纳米尺度材料与器件热表征的前所未有能力，推动材料发现与电子技术变革；未来方向包括载热子光谱映射、三维物性映射与AI智能体（AI-agentic）实验设计。

Abstract: The ability to measure thermal properties and visualize heat transport processes with high spatial resolution is becoming increasingly important for materials discovery, next-generation electronics, and advanced manufacturing. As critical dimensions and architectures of novel materials and high performance electronics shrink and become more heterogeneous, thermal transport is no longer governed by bulk material properties alone, but by local variations in microstructure, such as interfaces and defects, while the operating conditions can also play a role. With the development of non-contact pump-probe thermoreflectance techniques, specific heat, thermal conductivity, and interface conductance can be mapped either through wide-field thermos-reflectance images or by scanning technique. However, key challenges remain such as the balance between imaging throughput and measurement accuracy, the extraction of multiple parameters with high confidence, and non-intrusive subsurface characterization, etc. This Review highlights recent advancements in artificial intelligence (AI)-assistance in high-throughput data processing, visualizing interfacial thermal transport, and nonintrusive subsurface analysis, and offers outlooks for future research directions such as spectral mapping of heat carriers, three-dimensional property mapping, and AI-agentic experimental designs. We anticipate this Review will motivate future efforts in combining AI with experimental techniques to achieve unprecedented capabilities for thermal characterization of nanoscale materials and device characterization, facilitating materials discovery and electronics revolution.

</details>


### [14] [Molecular Dynamics Simulation of the Solidification of AlCoCuFeNi High-Entropy Alloy Nanowire](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25836)

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究纳米尺度高熵合金体系在凝固过程中的相形成规律，明确冷却条件对最终结构的影响。

Conclusion: 冷却速率是调控AlCoCuFeNi高熵合金纳米线凝固最终结构的关键因素，纳米尺度高熵合金的相形成对冷却条件高度敏感。

Abstract: Molecular dynamics simulation of the solidification behavior of an AlCoCuFeNi high-entropy alloy nanowire was performed using an embedded atom model potential at cooling rates of 10^11, 10^12, and 10^13 K/s. Periodic boundary conditions were applied along the nanowire axis to model an effectively infinite nanowire. The crystallization process was characterized through the temperature dependence of the potential energy. Structural evolution was analyzed using adaptive common-neighbor analysis (CNA) and radial distribution functions (RDF). The results show that the final structure changes from amorphous to crystalline with decreasing cooling rate, demonstrating the strong influence of cooling conditions on phase formation in nanoscale high-entropy alloy systems.

</details>


### [15] [Precise experimental investigation of the reflection properties caused by s-polarized evanescent waves and the Casimir puzzle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25856)
*M. Dhital,G. L. Klimchitskaya,V. M. Mostepanenko,U. Mohideen*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该实验测量振荡磁偶极子被金属板反射的磁场横向分量的幅值与相位（由低频s偏振倏逝波主导），发现与标准Drude响应函数的理论预言在定量与定性上均不符，从而排除Drude响应函数在该频段-s偏振倏逝波区域的适用性，为卡西米尔疑难（Casimir puzzle）的成因提供实验线索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 采用标准电磁波响应函数的Lifshitz理论预言与大量高精度卡西米尔力（Casimir force）测量数据相矛盾，即所谓卡西米尔疑难；该研究旨在通过独立实验揭示这一矛盾的物理根源。

Method: 测量振荡磁偶极子产生并经金属板反射的磁场横向分量的幅值和相位；这些量完全由横电（s）偏振的倏逝波（evanescent waves）决定，随后将数据与基于金属标准Drude响应函数的理论预言进行对比。

Result: 测量数据与Drude响应函数的预言在定量和定性两个层面均出现不一致，表明在低频s偏振倏逝波区域，Drude响应函数被实验数据所排除。

Conclusion: 由于s偏振倏逝波正是卡西米尔疑难的成因所在，结果凸显了该疑难出现的根源；并强调除卡西米尔效应外，寻找金属对倏逝波的恰当非局域响应函数（nonlocal response functions）对近场光学显微、纳米光子学、表面等离激元极化激元（surface plasmon polaritons）、辐射传热等领域均具有重要意义。

Abstract: We report a high-precision experiment on measuring the low-frequency magnetic field reflected from a metallic plate. This experiment is designed to give insight into why theoretical predictions of the fundamental Lifshitz theory using the standard response function to the electromagnetic waves are in contradiction with the data of numerous precision measurements of the Casimir force (the Casimir puzzle). To attain these ends, we measured the magnitude and phase of the lateral component of the magnetic field produced by an oscillating magnetic dipole and reflected from a metallic plate. These quantities are completely determined by the evanescent waves with the transverse electric (s) polarization. The obtained measurement data are compared with theoretical predictions using the standard Drude response function of the metal to the low-frequency electromagnetic waves. It is shown that there are both quantitative and qualitative disagreements with these predictions, so that in the region of low-frequency s-polarized evanescent waves the Drude response function is excluded by the data. Keeping in mind that just the s-polarized evanescent waves are responsible for the Casimir puzzle, our results highlight the causes for its emergence. It is emphasized that, in addition to the Casimir effect, searching for the adequate nonlocal response functions of metals to the evanescent waves is of utmost significance for many areas of physics as near-field optical microscopy, nanophotonics, surface plasmon polaritons, radiative heat transfer, and others.

</details>

Conclusion: momipy 为时间分辨动量显微产生的高维数据提供了集成化、易用的分析方案，有望促进该技术在实验室与大型设施中的推广应用。

Abstract: Photoemission momentum microscopy is an emerging approach to photoelectron spectroscopy that is transforming electronic band structure mapping at both laboratory-based and large-scale facilities. Its extension to time-resolved measurements has recently enabled major advances, ranging from the femtosecond imaging of exciton wavefunctions to dark-field photoelectron microscopy of light-induced dynamics and strong light-matter interactions. However, the rapid acquisition of high-dimensional data sets (including electron in-plane momenta $k_x$ and $k_y$, kinetic energy $E_\mathrm{kin}$, time $Δt$, and more) poses substantial challenges for data handling and analysis. A typical workflow encompasses data acquisition, exploratory visualization, calibration and targeted feature extraction, with data management ideally guided by FAIR principles. Here, we present momipy, a Python package that streamlines the momentum microscopy data analysis workflow efficiently and at low computational cost. Among other features, momipy provides interactive 4D data visualization, energy and momentum calibration, space-charge correction, and a broad selection of multidimensional data analysis routines, including binning, momentum-map extraction and time-, energy- and momentum-resolved model fitting. momipy is accompanied by a selection of interactive example scripts, supporting adoption of momipy by both experienced researchers and users new to momentum microscopy.

Conclusion: 磁交换相互作用敏感依赖于稀土离子半径与所施加的外延应变（epitaxial strain），凸显结构畸变和衬底诱导应变在调控无限层镍酸盐磁相互作用中的关键作用。

Abstract: In this paper we present a systematic first-principles investigation of the structure and nearest-neighbor magnetic exchange interactions in the infinite-layer RNiO2 thin-film series (R = La-Lu), under the constrain of different substrates. Density functional theory calculations are first employed to optimize the crystal structures, while CAS+DDCI correlated wave-function calculations were used for the determination of the magnetic exchanges. This methodology allows to obtain magnetic interactions in good agreement with RIXS experimental estimations. In the pristine case, our results confirm that the structure of RNiO2 (except for R = La and Ce) adopts the I4/mcm space group, characterized by an out-of-plane rotation of the NiO4 plaquettes. This result remains valid under substrate constrains, even if compressive strain reduces the amplitude of the rotational distortion and leads to an enhancement of the magnetic exchange interaction relative to the pristine structures. The latter are found to depend sensitively on both the rare-earth ionic radius and the applied epitaxial strain. These results highlight the role of structural distortions and substrate-induced strain in tuning magnetic interactions in infinite-layer nickelates.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Conclusion: 微丝中高达14.0%的宏观应变与近乎完全的马氏体相变相关，且其应力–温度斜率与[001]取向Ni–Fe–Ga单晶（single crystals）相当。

EBSD mapping at room temperature reveals cubic austenite in a nonstrained part of the microwire and tetragonal martensite in the strained part. The axial transformation strain calculated from the measured lattice parameters is approximately $15.4\%$. Thermal cycling under constant stresses of $20\text{--}100~\mathrm{MPa}$ reveals experimental transformation strains of $13.2\text{--}14.0\%$. Increasing tensile stress raises the transformation temperatures, with a stress--temperature slope of approximately $1.52~\mathrm{MPa}/^\circ\mathrm{C}$, comparable to that reported for $[001]$-oriented $\mathrm{Ni\text{--}Fe\text{--}Ga}$ single crystals. Overall, the observations confirm that the large macroscopic strain of up to $14.0\%$ relates to the nearly complete martensitic transformation in the microwire.

TL;DR: 本研究比较了三类声子输运模型，用于描述现代 HEMT 设计中薄膜层的热输运；第一性原理模型表现最佳，并据此建立了以 Umklapp 散射为主的内在输运性质标定流程，进而评估附加散射项与模型固有因素对预测精度的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代 HEMT 设计中薄膜层的热输运对器件性能至关重要，但不同复杂度的声子输运模型在准确性上存在差异；同时，三元半导体中的合金散射与非理想多晶薄膜中的晶界/膜边界散射会使建模复杂化，需要评估各模型的可靠性。

Method: 采用三个复杂度递增的声子输运（phonon transport）模型，从较简单的 Debye 近似（Debye approximation）模型到引入密度泛函理论（Density Functional Theory, DFT）导出简谐与非简谐声子性质的第一性原理（first-principles）框架；模型中计入合金散射（alloy scattering）以及晶界与膜边界散射（grain and film boundary scattering），并将后者拟合到时间域热反射（Time-Domain Thermoreflectance, TDTR）测得的导热系数数据。

Result: 总体上第一性原理模型表现最佳，给出物理上最为一致的结果；研究还建立了确定以 Umklapp 散射（Umklapp scattering）为主导的内在输运性质的可靠流程，并可通过系统添加额外散射项，评估散射率处理方式、声子色散（phonon dispersion）描述等模型固有因素对精度与整体预测能力的影响。

Conclusion: 第一性原理声子输运框架在 HEMT 薄膜热输运建模中优于简化模型；以 Umklapp 散射为基准的内在性质标定方法为后续引入附加散射机制提供了系统化基础，并可用于识别影响模型预测能力的关键建模选择。

Abstract: In this work, we investigate thermal transport in a set of thin-film layers used in modern HEMT designs. Our theoretical analysis employs three phonon-transport models of increasing sophistication, ranging from a relatively simple Debye-approximation model to a first-principles framework that incorporates Density Functional Theory (DFT)-derived harmonic and anharmonic phonon properties. To describe ternary semiconductors, alloy scattering is also considered, and to account for the properties of non-ideal, polycrystalline thin films, scattering at grain and film boundaries is taken into account. For modeling the specific samples at hand, the latter are fitted to thermal conductivity data measured with Time-Domain Thermoreflectance (TDTR). Overall, we find that the first-principles model performs best and produces the most physically consistent results. Special emphasis is placed on establishing a reliable procedure for determining intrinsic transport properties dominated by Umklapp scattering. Building on these, it is then possible to systematically add additional scattering terms to the models and assess how model-inherent factors, such as the treatment of scattering rates or the description of the phonon dispersion, affect model accuracy and overall predictive capabilities.

### [20] [Alloying effects on the interface cohesion of grain boundaries in the Ni--Fe--Cr system](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25944)
*Aljoscha Felix Baumann,Reyhaneh Ghassemizadeh,Daniel F. Urban*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作结合密度泛函理论（DFT）与机器学习原子间势（MLIP）基础模型，系统量化 Fe、Cr 合金化对镍晶界内聚强度的影响，并建立可推广的多组分合金晶界内聚定量分析流程。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多晶镍基合金中晶界显著影响力学强度与断裂行为，但 Fe、Cr 等合金元素对晶界内聚的影响难以系统量化，且 DFT 受体系尺寸限制难以覆盖精细成分采样。

Conclusion: 研究提供了经 DFT 验证的、量化多组分合金晶界内聚效应的计算工作流，所得数值可作为连续尺度断裂模型的输入，支撑原子尺度信息驱动的多尺度建模。

Abstract: Grain boundaries (GBs) in polycrystalline Ni-based alloys strongly influence mechanical strength and fracture behavior, yet the effect of alloying elements such as Fe and Cr on GB cohesion remains difficult to quantify systematically. Using density functional theory (DFT), we compute rigid-body separation (RBS) curves for six symmetric-tilt Ni GBs, extracting the work of separation $W_{\mathrm{sep}}$ and maximum traction $σ_{\max}$ via the universal binding energy relation (UBER). We find that substitution of Cr or Fe into the GB plane increases both quantities relative to pure Ni GBs. To overcome the size limitations of DFT, we compare several machine-learning interatomic potential (MLIP) foundation models against the DFT dataset and identify a model achieving quantitative agreement with DFT for $W_{\mathrm{sep}}$ and $σ_{\max}$. This MLIP enables systematic sampling of alloying configurations at finer concentration steps. We show that alloying-induced changes in GB cohesion depend on both, element type and local structures, revealing trends that are inaccessible with DFT alone. Extending the analysis to a ternary $\mathrm{Ni}_{56}\mathrm{Fe}_{20}\mathrm{Cr}_{24}$ composition, inspired by the principal constituents of the widely used commercial Alloy 718, shows that the binary trends provide a useful basis for interpreting the combined effect of both substitutes, while explicit ternary calculations are required to capture their interplay quantitatively. Together, these results provide a validated workflow for quantifying the effect of alloying elements on GB cohesion in multicomponent alloys. The resulting values may serve as input for continuum-scale fracture models, paving the way toward atomistically informed multiscale modeling.

</details>


### [21] [Multicharged Kramers-Weyl Fermions, Quantum Geometric Hall Responses and de Haas-van Alphen Oscillations in WAl$_2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25987)
*Saurav Patel,Meet Shah,Prafulla K. Jha*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 通过第一性原理计算与对称性分析研究实验合成的WAl\(_2\)（含两种结构对映体），揭示Kramers-Weyl交叉、费米弧、自旋劈裂费米面、Berry曲率偶极非线性霍尔响应与本征自旋霍尔电导在同一材料中的共存与协同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 布洛赫波函数的量子几何（quantum geometry）与晶体对称性决定电子拓扑与物理响应；Kramers-Weyl费米子、量子振荡探测的自旋劈裂费米面、量子几何非线性霍尔与本征自旋霍尔响应虽各自受到关注，但它们与拓扑表面态、费米弧及手性依赖输运的共存仍鲜有报道。

Conclusion: 两种对映体的相反结构手性会反转手性、BCD、费米弧方向与非线性霍尔电流；高阶Chern数（三重荷KW点）可能在增强SHC中起作用，表明WAl\(_2\)是实现手性依赖量子几何输运与多种拓扑响应协同的平台。

Abstract: Quantum geometry of Bloch wave functions and crystalline symmetries play a central role in condensed matter physics, shaping the electronic topology and physical responses of solids. In particular, Kramers-Weyl fermions, spin-split Fermi surfaces probed through quantum oscillations and quantum geometric nonlinear Hall and intrinsic spin Hall responses have garnered significant attention. Despite progress in understanding these phenomena individually, their coexistence with topological surface states, Fermi arcs and chirality dependent quantum geometric transport remains scarce, as pronounced responses involve suitable symmetries, SOC and Fermi surface geometry. Here, we explore this synergy in experimentally synthesized WAl$_2$, considering both structural enantiomers using first-principles calculations and symmetry analysis. We observed twelve Kramers-Weyl (KW) crossings with quantized chiral charges $|C|=1$ and $|C|=3$, accompanied by nontrivial surface states and Fermi arcs. The pronounced metallic character gives rise to six Fermi surface sheets exhibiting antisymmetric SOC, with distinct extremal orbits and candidate spin-split pairs resolved through calculated de Haas-van Alphen (dHvA) quantum oscillations. The electronic structure also exhibits large intrinsic Berry curvature dipole (BCD) arising from the asymmetric distribution of Berry curvature, giving rise to a nonlinear Hall response. The opposite structural handedness of the two enantiomers reverses the chiralities and BCD, reversing the direction of Fermi arcs and nonlinear Hall current. BCD attains $|D_{zz}|\approx0.338$, yielding nonlinear Hall current densities $|j_x|\approx5.04\times10^{10}~\mathrm{A\,m^{-2}}$ and $|j_y|\approx2.52\times10^{10}~\mathrm{A\,m^{-2}}$. ### [22] [Lattice dynamics study of (Bi1-xSbx)2Te3 compounds: Comparative compositional study of force constants and Grüneisen parameters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26010)

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在建立BST化合物中组分、晶格动力学与热输运行为之间的关联，以阐明其热电性能优化（thermoelectric optimization）的微观起源。

Result: 常压下所有拉曼活性模式频率随Sb浓度增加而蓝移（blue-shifting），但速率随浓度而异；低频模式（E1g、A11g）力常数几乎与组分无关，高频模式则分别出现明显软化（E2g）或硬化（A21g）。平均Grüneisen参数在BST-07处呈现清晰极大值，并依据Slack方程与该组分的最优ZT值直接关联。

Abstract: We performed a comprehensive Raman spectroscopy study of (Bi1-xSbx)2Te3 (BST-x) compounds, with varying Sb concentration, under ambient and high-pressure conditions. At ambient conditions, the frequency of all observed Raman-active modes (E1g, A11g, E2g, A21g) is blue-shifting with increasing Sb concentration, albeit with varying concentration-dependent rate. Based on these results, we calculated the relative force constants as a function of Sb concentration using a harmonic approximation, which shows a mode-dependent variation. The force constants for the lower frequency Raman modes (E1 g and A11g) remain almost composition-independent, while for the higher-frequency ones a profound softening (E2g) or stiffening (A21g) was determined. From the Raman spectroscopy measurements under high-pressure conditions, the corresponding Grüneisen parameters for the three observed Raman-active modes (A11g, E2g, A21g) as a function of composition were determined by combining them with the previously reported Equation of States and the corresponding bulk modulus B0 of the ambient-conditions R-3m phase. The average Grüneisen parameter exhibits a clear maximum for BST-07. Based on Slack's equation, these results are directly related to the optimal ZT values for this composition. Our study provides a solid framework for connecting composition, lattice dynamics, and thermal-transport behaviour, towards establishing the microscopic origin of thermoelectric optimization in BST compounds.

Abstract: Reward misalignment is usually regarded as a failure of artificial intelligence. We learn the experts' preferences from pairwise comparisons and use them to guide a crystal diffusion model. The learned preferences and numerical score agree on the flattest bands but diverge elsewhere. Our findings suggest that reward misalignment can expose opportunities for materials discovery beyond what fixed rewards are designed to find.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: This work investigates how defect engineering and nanostructuring via severe plastic deformation influence electrical and thermal transport in the high-entropy alloy CrMnFeCoNi. High-pressure torsion (HPT) for up to N = 4 turns produces a hierarchical defect structure, including deformation twins at low strains, dislocations and Lomer-Cottrell locks at intermediate strains, and a high fraction of high-angle grain boundaries at large strains. While hardness naturally increases and saturates at ~520 Hv, both electrical and thermal conductivity exhibit an unusual increase upon HPT processing (from 0.953 MS m-1 and 10.3 W m-1 K-1 after homogenization to 1.003 +- 0.005 MS m-1 and 14.4 W m-1 K-1 after N = 4). Analysis based on the Wiedemann-Franz law indicates a modest increase in electron transport (~5%) and a significant increase in phonon transport (~100%) after HPT processing. The phonon mean free path remains small on the order of 0.6-1.1 nm, and comparison with the Cahill-Pohl model shows that heat transport occurs near the minimum thermal conductivity limit, where vibrational modes are strongly scattered. This suggests that the enhancement in thermal transport is not governed by conventional defect scattering, but it is hypothesized that modifications in atomic-scale disorder, such as deformation-induced short-range ordering, are responsible for this unusual behavior. Specific heat capacity increases after HPT, consistent with stored defect energy. The linear thermal expansion exhibits a non-monotonic behavior, decreasing initially due to internal stresses and relaxation effects, and increasing at higher strains due to grain boundary-induced anharmonicity. These findings highlight the complex interplay between defects, atomic-scale disorder, and nanostructure in governing the electrical and thermal properties of severely deformed high-entropy alloys via electron and phonon scattering.

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 第一性原理结构弛豫（structural relaxation）与能带计算（band calculation）计算成本高且依赖专家经验，需要降低门槛并加速计算流程。

Method: Perfumer 包含 Perfumer-relax 预弛豫模型与 Perfumer-band 能带预测模型；后者结合周期图神经网络（periodic graph neural network）与隐厄米谱头（latent Hermitian spectral head），在任意波矢（wavevector）直接输出 12 个能带能量，无需构造哈密顿量；智能体将自然语言请求转换为弛豫到能带的工作流。

Conclusion: Perfumer 在保持周期性与时间反演对称性（time-reversal symmetry）的同时显著提升计算效率，并支持从单一上传结构到自动构建候选材料并返回预测性质的端到端智能体工作流。

Abstract: First-principles structural relaxation and band calculations require substantial computation and expertise. Here we present Perfumer, a large language model-based agentic framework that couples Quantum ESPRESSO with two predictive models. Perfumer-band model combines a periodic graph neural network with a latent Hermitian spectral head returns 12 band energies at any requested wavevector, preserving reciprocal lattice periodicity and time-reversal symmetry without constructing Hamiltonian. Trained on 125,153 self-curated band structures, Perfumer-band model reaches a median per-material mean absolute error of 0.157 eV on 2,607 test materials, including lanthanide, actinide and magnetic compounds, from held-out composition families spanning 85 elements, taking about 97 ms per material on two consumer CPU cores. The agent turns natural-language requests into relaxation-to-band workflows, from a single uploaded structure to automatically constructed new candidates and returns predicted properties.

</details>


### [26] [Two distinct notions of speed in antiferromagnets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26482)
*Paul Herrgen,Stephan Wust,Christin Schmitt,Yaryna Lytvynenko,Lorenzo Baldrati,Rafael Ramos,Takashi Kikkawa,Eiji Saitoh,Martin Jourdan,Mathias Kläui,Hans Christian Schneider,Benjamin Stadtmüller,Martin Aeschlimann*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 通过对比NiO、Mn2Au与Ni的超快退磁（ultrafast demagnetization），指出反铁磁序的快速淬灭取决于自旋翻转概率与电子散射相空间（electronic scattering phase space）的协同，而非仅由太赫兹共振频率决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反铁磁体的太赫兹共振频率虽表征其进动动力学，但未必意味着激光诱导磁序淬灭更快，因此需厘清磁补偿（magnetic compensation）与电子散射相空间在超快响应中的各自角色。

Method: 比较三种材料的超快退磁：反铁磁电荷转移绝缘体（charge-transfer insulator）NiO、金属反铁磁体Mn2Au，以及铁磁参照样品Ni，并提取其响应时间尺度。

Conclusion: 绝缘性NiO中受限的电子散射通道削弱了超快响应；研究区分了超快退磁与交换增强进动（exchange-enhanced precession），并确定自旋翻转概率（spin-flip probability）与电子散射速率的相互作用是支配补偿磁体（compensated magnets）非平衡响应的关键因素。

Abstract: The terahertz resonance frequencies of antiferromagnets characterize their precessional dynamics but do not necessarily imply faster laser-induced quenching of magnetic order. Here, we investigate the distinct roles of magnetic compensation and electronic scattering phase space by comparing ultrafast demagnetization in the antiferromagnetic charge-transfer insulator NiO, the metallic antiferromagnet Mn2Au, and a ferromagnetic Ni reference. The extracted response in Mn2Au is approximately four times faster than in NiO and substantially faster than in Ni. These observations are consistent with exchange-mediated scattering between antiparallel sublattices enabling rapid loss of antiferromagnetic order without requiring net angular-momentum transfer to an external reservoir, provided sufficient electronic scattering phase space is available. In insulating NiO, restricted electronic scattering channels reduces the ultrafast response. Our results distinguish ultrafast demagnetization from exchange-enhanced precession and identify the interplay of spin-flip probability and electronic scattering rate as a key factor governing the nonequilibrium response of compensated magnets.

</details>


### [27] [Constitutive Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks in Finite Element Model Updating: Closed-Form Hyperelastic Strain Energies from Heterogeneous Experiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26528)
*Kian P. Abdolazizi,Kevin Linka,Christian J. Cyron*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究将本构Kolmogorov-Arnold网络（constitutive Kolmogorov-Arnold network, CKAN）嵌入有限元模型更新（finite element model updating, FEMU），以小样本全场测量训练，并通过剪枝与符号回归（symbolic regression）将其约简为满足本构要求的紧凑闭式应变能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统超弹性本构模型标定（calibration of hyperelastic constitutive model）面临两难：需专家选择函数形式，且需多项均质试验确定参数。神经本构模型虽免除形式选择，却存在黑箱性或依赖预设候选项库；全场测量（如数字图像相关，digital image correlation, DIC）可替代多项均质试验，但仍缺乏训练时的灵活性与事后的可解释性，以及仅凭单一非均质试验训练模型的途径。

Method: 构建CKAN并嵌入FEMU框架，通过最小化预测与实测运动学场及反力的失配进行训练；随后采用剪枝（pruning）与符号回归将训练后网络约简为闭式应变能（strain energy），并使其满足各项本构要求。验证涵盖穿孔天然橡胶膜、穿孔硅胶板及受压膨胀的主动脉试样。

Result: 穿孔天然橡胶膜上，CKAN对DIC位移场的中位误差为1%–2%，并可迁移至训练中未出现的孔洞图案；穿孔硅胶板上，仅用单一载荷下的一条拉伸曲线训练的CKAN，对该曲线与实测反力的复现误差在百分之几以内；主动脉试样上，各向异性CKAN可预测受压膨胀的三维形状。所发现的闭式能量在训练数据上与对应CKAN表现一致，并可与既有模型直接比较。

Conclusion: 该方法同时弥补了可解释性与单一非均质试验训练两方面的缺口，实现了从全场测量到紧凑、物理可解释且可直接与经典模型对照的闭式本构模型的端到端标定。

Abstract: Calibrating a hyperelastic constitutive model for a soft material traditionally poses two problems: an expert must choose its functional form, and several homogeneous tests must determine its parameters. Neural constitutive models solve the first, but either remain black boxes or draw on a library of candidate terms fixed in advance. Full-field measurements, such as those from digital image correlation (DIC), promise to solve the second, replacing those tests by a single heterogeneous one. Two gaps remain: flexibility during training with interpretability afterwards, and a way to train such a model on one heterogeneous test. Here we close both. We embed a constitutive Kolmogorov-Arnold network (CKAN) in finite element model updating (FEMU) and train it by minimizing the mismatch between the predicted and the measured kinematic fields and reaction forces. Pruning and symbolic regression then reduce the trained network to a compact closed-form strain energy that satisfies every constitutive requirement. On perforated natural-rubber membranes, the CKANs reproduce the DIC displacement fields with median errors between one and two percent, and transfer to a hole pattern withheld from training. On a perforated silicone plate, a CKAN trained on one stretch profile at a single load reproduces that profile and the measured reaction force to within a few percent. On an aortic specimen inflated under pressure, an anisotropic CKAN predicts the inflated three-dimensional shape. The discovered closed-form energies match the CKANs they replace on the training data and can be compared directly with established models.

</details>


### [28] [Emergent Fatigue Dynamics from Foundational Interatomic Potentials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26533)
*Jingye Tan,Benjamin A. Jasperson,Eric Rothchild,Anders Johansson,Rèmi Dingreville,Krishna Garikipati,Mitchell A. Wood*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该文比较基础机器学习原子间势（foundational machine-learned interatomic potentials）与物理动机势（EAM）在静态与涌现性质上的表现，发现两者对近平衡性质一致，但NequIP预测的层错能异常偏低，导致疲劳载荷下塑性变形显著更多。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础机器学习势基于大规模DFT数据训练，但其验证主要依赖平衡态，无法保证大规模分子动力学中涌现行为（emergent behavior）的正确性；在缺乏直接实验验证时，与物理动机势比较可提供洞察。

Method: 区分热可及的静态性质（static properties）与需外部疲劳应力驱动稀有事件的涌现性质，在两类性质上比较EAM势与NequIP基础势；采用类经典Griffith裂纹问题的纳米尺度缺陷进行疲劳循环模拟，并分析加载后的每原子能量分布。

Result: 两种势在多数近平衡性质上与参考数据吻合良好，但NequIP给出的本征层错能（intrinsic stacking fault energy）异常低（约为EAM的1/7），因而预测疲劳循环下明显更多的塑性变形；加载后每原子能量分布显示两者差异主要出现在远高于热可及范围的能量区间。

Conclusion: 基础机器学习势的平衡态验证不足以推断涌现行为，需关注高能非热可及区域；此外记录了内存相关崩溃与静默外推（silent extrapolation）作为实践者的现实风险。

Abstract: Foundational machine-learned interatomic potentials are trained on large, diverse DFT datasets, but their validation rests on equilibrium states, which does not guarantee correct emergent behavior in large-scale molecular dynamics. Where no direct experimental validation is available, comparisons to physically motivated potentials are valuable for drawing insight. We distinguish static properties, which are thermally accessible, from emergent properties, which require work from an external fatigue stress to drive rare events, and compare the potentials on both. An EAM potential and the NequIP foundational potential both agree closely with reference data for many near-equilibrium properties, but NequIP predicts an anomalously low intrinsic stacking fault energy ($\sim7$ times lower than EAM) and thus predicts far more plastic deformation under fatigue cycling of a nanoscale flaw modeled after the classical Griffith crack problem. Per-atom energy distributions after loading show that the two potentials differ most at energies far above what is thermally accessible. We also document memory-related crashes and silent extrapolation as practical concerns for practitioners of these foundational model forms.

</details>

### [29] [Propagation of chaos with singular potentials: A weighted duality approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25687)
*Phan Thành Nam,Toan T. Nguyen*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文在平均场（mean-field）区域、奇异性满足 $|x|^{-\alpha-1}$（$0\le\alpha<(d-1)/2$，$d\ge2$）的多体 $N$ 粒子系统中证明混沌传播（propagation of chaos）并导出 Vlasov 方程，涵盖三维亚库仑（sub-Coulomb）全范围与二维库仑情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前基于对偶方法（duality method）的证明受限于局部平方可积（locally square-integrable）的相互作用力核，无法覆盖更奇异的作用力；本文旨在去除该限制并处理更奇异的区域。

Method: 采用基于能量（energy-based）的框架推导累积量（cumulants）的加权一致界，该框架建立在 Bresch–Duerinckx–Jabin 近期提出的对偶方法之上，并结合一个新的 Morawetz 型估计（Morawetz-type estimate）。

Result: 成功将结果推广至由 $|x|^{-\alpha-1}$ 界定、$0\le\alpha<(d-1)/2$ 的奇异相互作用力，包括三维完整亚库仑范围及二维库仑相互作用。

Conclusion: 结合对偶方法与 Morawetz 型估计的能量框架可移除对局部平方可积力核的限制，从而在更奇异情形下建立混沌传播与 Vlasov 方程的导出。

Abstract: We establish the propagation of chaos and derive the Vlasov equation from a many-body $N$-particle system in the mean-field regime with singular interaction forces bounded by $|x|^{-α-1}$ for $0\le α<(d-1)/2$ in dimensions $d\ge 2$, which in particular include the full range of sub-Coulomb interactions in three dimensions as well as the Coulomb interaction in two dimensions. Our proof is based on an energy-based framework for deriving weighted uniform bounds on cumulants that builds upon the recent duality method developed by Bresch--Duerinckx--Jabin in combination with a new Morawetz-type estimate, allowing us to remove the restriction to locally square-integrable interaction force kernels and handle more singular regimes.

</details>

*Andrea Belloni*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 该文为圆周上立方复克莱因-戈尔登方程的小振幅准周期解族建立了全局时间上的非相对论极限，并证明存在正测度频率集使克莱因-戈尔登解沿子序列全局收敛到耦合NLS系统的固定准周期解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究目标是建立小振幅准周期解在非相对论极限（nonrelativistic limit）下的全局时间有效性，并厘清KAM构造与光速参数的依赖关系。

Method: 采用KAM分块格式（KAM block scheme）构造两频不变环面，并控制KAM格式对光速的依赖，配合规范变换（gauge transformation）使频率与极限耦合NLS系统的频率精确匹配。

Result: 线性稳定性取决于两个激发傅里叶指标是否重合：重合给出线性稳定环面，相异给出部分双曲环面；所得近似在非相对论区域内对所有时间一致，并存在正测度频率集使克莱因-戈尔登解沿子序列全局收敛到固定的NLS准周期解。

Conclusion: 结果表明非相对论极限可在全局时间上实现，且极限NLS准周期解可由克莱因-戈尔登解族沿子序列逼近。

Abstract: This paper establishes a global-in-time nonrelativistic limit for families of small-amplitude quasi-periodic solutions of the cubic complex Klein-Gordon equation on the circle. Firstly, we construct by a KAM block scheme, small-amplitude two-frequency invariant tori and show that their linear stability depends on whether the two excited Fourier indices coincide or are distinct: coincident indices yield linearly stable tori, whereas distinct indices yield partially hyperbolic tori. Moreover, controlling the dependence of the KAM scheme on the speed of light, we construct quasi-periodic solutions whose frequencies, after a gauge transformation, coincide exactly with those of quasi-periodic solutions of the limiting coupled NLS system, obtaining an approximation uniform for all times in the nonrelativistic regime. By this result, we further show that there exists a positive-measure set of frequencies such that, for each frequency, we can fix a quasi-periodic solution of the coupled NLS system and a suitable subsequence of Klein-Gordon solutions converging globally in time to the fixed NLS solution.

Adding more axions does not help: the same branes become actionless on every Calabi-Yau threefold, because what counts is the degree of homogeneity of the saxion sector, not the number of axions. </details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: We consider what the entanglement between two regions of a critical system can reveal about its symmetries, including non-invertible ones. For two intervals in a two-dimensional conformal field theory, the second Rényi entropy is a partition function on a torus, and a topological defect line can be wrapped around one of the intervals. For an on-site symmetry this computes the overlap of the reduced density matrix with its image under the symmetry acting on one interval, and in general it defines an observable that depends only on the cross-ratio. We show that existing bounds on the tensor-scalar-scalar (TSS) bispectrum strongly constrain the possibility of generating a detectable parity-odd trispectrum. For tensor-to-scalar ratios near current observational limits, detectability is incompatible with, or pushes against, the regime of validity of these UV completions. A detectable signal can remain possible for a sufficiently small tensor-to-scalar ratio, but only at the cost of an extremely large enhancement of the TSS vertex. Main category: hep-th

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: Two regular black hole horizons need not have an entropy product fixed by the charges and couplings. In Einstein--Maxwell--scalar theory, we construct asymptotically flat branches with products above or below the squared extremal entropy. Regularity selects their horizon scalar values, and different branches can share the charges, couplings, and asymptotic scalar. Each product stays constant as mass and asymptotic scalar vary. The horizon areas stay apart and no extremal limit is reached: the temperature vanishes only where the mass diverges. We prove local existence.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: The Laser Interferometer Space Antenna (LISA) will observe the millihertz gravitational-wave sky, including millions of long-lived Galactic binaries (GBs), tens of thousands of which are expected to be individually resolvable throughout the mission's lifetime. Extracting these sources requires jointly determining their number and physical parameters from strongly overlapping signals, posing a computationally demanding trans-dimensional inference problem. We introduce SlotFlow-GB, a neural framework that constructs a learned approximation to the trans-dimensional posterior, producing a probability distribution over source multiplicity and parameter densities for individual binaries in a single evaluation, without requiring their number to be specified in advance. We demonstrate this framework using year-long LISA simulations containing up to ten binaries within a 2 $μ$Hz frequency interval, focusing on a high-SNR regime in which source overlap, rather than individual-source detectability, is the primary challenge. The inferred source count differs from the true count by at most one in 94% of cases, while the model identifies individual binaries even when their signals overlap strongly in frequency. A complete evaluation, including parameter sampling, takes just 41 ms per band on one NVIDIA GH200 GPU, or 530 ms on eight laptop CPU threads. We further demonstrate that these distributions can be used to initialize subsequent likelihood-based inference, connecting fast amortized predictions with joint parameter estimation.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 宇宙学耦合黑洞（cosmologically coupled black holes, CCBH）的引力质量随宇宙尺度因子（scale factor）演化，可写为 m_BH ∝ a^κ，其检验通常依赖长时标观测，易受系统偏差影响；脉冲星计时（pulsar timing）基线仅为数十年，偏差源更可控，因此提供了新的检验途径。

Method: 以球状星团黑洞+脉冲星候选体 J0514−4002E（自转周期 P∼5.6 ms，轨道周期 P_b∼7 天）为检验案例，假设伴星为 CCBH；将观测到的轨道周期导数与自转周期导数相减以消除全部运动学效应（kinematic effects），仅余脉冲星内禀自转减慢（intrinsic spin-down）Ṗ_int 为未知量。取 Ṗ_int=0（物理下限）得到稳健上限；并借助自转参数匹配的毫秒脉冲星（millisecond pulsar）群体估计 Ṗ_int。

Abstract: Cosmologically coupled black holes (CCBHs) respond to the expansion of the Universe, independently of conventional astrophysical processes. This can be tested through the orbital period timing of black hole (BH) pulsar (PSR) binaries. In contrast to previous methods used to constrain $κ$, pulsar timing measurement baselines span decades instead of gigayears, allowing sources of bias to be well-controlled. As a test case, we analyze the globular cluster BH+PSR candidate J0514--4002E (spin $P\sim 5.6~{\rm ms}$, orbital period $P_{\rm b}\sim 7~\rm days$) under the assumption that the companion is a CCBH. Setting $\dot{P}_{\rm int} = 0$, its physical minimum, gives a robust upper bound $κ\leqslant 3.95$ at $95\%$ confidence. We estimate $\dot{P}_{\rm int}$ through the population of millisecond pulsars matched in spin and infer $κ= 0.82 \pm 1.67$ and $0.75 \pm 1.54$, using $H_0 = 67.6$ and $73.5\,{\rm km\,s^{-1}\,Mpc^{-1}}$, respectively. Adopting typical baseline scaling of timing precision, the value $κ= 3$, argued elsewhere to link energized vacuum BH interiors to accelerated cosmological expansion, can be excluded or detected for this $2.35^{+0.20}_{-0.18}\,M_\odot$ companion at $3σ$ and $5σ$ confidence by 2031 and 2036, respectively.

Result: 推导出非线性因果性与强双曲性（strong hyperbolicity）的充要条件；证明常用无力平衡表述因Donnelly-Glaberson不稳定性而短波不适定；数值模拟了全球广义相对论双流体中子星平衡态并验证振荡模式，首次给出含涡旋释放与重钉扎的简化超流内部自转减速计算。

Abstract: Neutron stars provide a natural laboratory for superfluid matter under extreme conditions. Its nonlinear dynamics is central to pulsar glitches, their possible links to fast radio bursts, and tidal interactions in inspiralling binaries. We develop a general-relativistic superfluid hydrodynamics framework incorporating entrainment, vortex forces, and line energy, and derive, for the first time, necessary and sufficient conditions for nonlinear causality and strong hyperbolicity. We also show that commonly used dissipative, force-balanced formulations without line tension are generically ill-posed at short wavelengths because of the Donnelly-Glaberson instability.

This motivates a new finite-volume scheme combining constrained transport of superfluid vorticity with implicit electric-field evolution, enabling stable numerical evolution of the full system. We validate the scheme using an exact solution to the relativistic superfluid Riemann problem and Kelvin-wave dispersion relations, among other tests.

The resulting stationary state retains both a net twist in the vortex array and residual differential rotation of the superfluid. Our framework thus enables nonlinear numerical studies of general-relativistic superfluid dynamics in neutron-star interiors.

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光传播仿真（propagation simulation）对光学系统的设计与尺寸标定具有实用价值，而现有教学或实践中缺乏对数值仿真中物理空间尺度正确性的系统说明与验证。

Conclusion: 基于傅里叶光学的 Python 传播仿真在尺度正确性与解析基准上得到确认，可作为光学系统设计、衍射元件生成及实验相位恢复的实用工具。

Abstract: Simulation of the propagation of light is useful for the design and dimensioning of optical systems. In this paper, we present the basics of propagation simulation using Fourier optics and practical implementation in Python, with a particular emphasis on dimensions. We first verify that our numerical wave-optics simulations maintain correct physical spatial scaling. Next, we benchmark numerical propagation against closed-form analytical formulas for Gaussian beams, lens focusing effects, and grating diffraction. We then show how those simulations can be used to generate diffractive optical elements such as Fresnel zone plates, and we bridge the gap between numerical simulations and experiments by collecting data and recovering phase information using a phase retrieval algorithm.

It is the goal in this article to fill in this gap by deriving finite-energy spatiotemporally localized scalar-valued wave solutions characterized specifically by Hopf and trefoil nodal knot topologies. This will extend to spatiotemporally localized pulsed fields the diverse important applications of mono-chromatic light beams, having nontrivial topological structures, in trapping and guiding particles, image processing and optical communications; also, in applications to biology and medicine.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光学薄膜性能与可靠性要求提高，需要更灵敏的表征手段来研究薄膜界面与局部机械应力；SHG可作为无损探针追踪薄膜表面、体相及埋入界面的局部对称性变化，但需要区分各区域的贡献。

Method: 采用角度分辨与偏振分辨测量，结合考虑多层反射与光学干涉的SHG理论模型；先用沉积于不同玻璃衬底上的Ta2O5薄膜进行实验验证，再通过合成数据集检验分解的唯一性与准确性，并分析膜厚、测量噪声、线性光学参数不确定度及偏振通道选择的影响。

Result: 拟合程序能准确重现实测SHG响应，并揭示埋入界面贡献存在显著的衬底依赖性，而表面与有效体相响应基本相似；合成数据检验明确了可靠分离所需的条件及参数敏感度。

Conclusion: 可靠分离SHG各区域贡献需要精确的线性光学参数、多偏振通道的全局拟合以及足够显著的干涉特征；该工作界定了基于SHG的区域特异性薄膜表征的能力与实用局限。

Abstract: Increasing demands on the performance and reliability of optical thin films stimulate the development of more sensitive characterisation methods capable of investigating thin-film interfaces or local mechanical stress. Second-harmonic generation (SHG) is a phenomenon that can serve as a non-destructive probe, tracing local symmetry changes in the film surface, bulk, and buried interfaces. Here, we investigate the decomposition of the SHG response from these three regions by combining angle- and polarisation-resolved measurements. We utilised a theoretical model of SHG accounting for multiple reflections and optical interference in a thin layer. The theory was first experimentally evaluated using Ta2O5 films deposited on different glass substrates. The fitting procedure accurately reproduced the measured SHG responses, revealing pronounced substrate-dependent variations in the buried-interface contribution, while the surface and effective-bulk responses remained similar. As the next step, we studied the reliability of the SHG signal decomposition into the surface, bulk and buried-interface components. The decomposition was evaluated by generating synthetic datasets and testing whether their retrieval was unique and accurate. The influence of film thickness, measurement noise, uncertainties in linear-optical parameters, and the polarisation channels used on the retrieval of nonlinear susceptibilities was also examined. Our results demonstrate that reliable separation of SHG contributions requires accurate linear optical parameters, global fitting of multiple polarisation channels, and sufficiently pronounced interference features, outlining the capabilities and practical limitations of SHG-based region-specific characterisation of optical thin films.

</details>
