### [74] [Exact Fractional Chern Insulators from Hubbard Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25010)
*Jonah Herzog-Arbeitman,Rui-Heng Liu,Jiangping Hu,Chen Fang*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该工作针对紧束缚模型构造分数陈绝缘体（fractional Chern insulator, FCI）的精确波函数-母哈密顿量配对，母体为在位哈伯德相互作用（onsite Hubbard interaction），并证明 FCI 基态可存在于非理想能带（beyond ideal bands）中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Laughlin 波函数与 Haldane 赝势（pseudopotential）构成分数量子霍尔效应（fractional quantum Hall effect）的精确配对，是解析理解的基石；本文旨在为分数陈绝缘体寻找对应层次的精确配对，以突破对理想能带（ideal bands）的依赖。

Method: 引入“朗道对偶陈能带（Landau-dual Chern band）”，即在 Bravais 格点上直接采样自旋-s 狄拉克朗道能级（spin-s Dirac Landau level）所得的多轨道陈能带；据此将多体波函数以矩阵坐标 X+iY+J 解析表达，其中 (X,Y) 为格点坐标，J 为自旋空间中的幂零矩阵（nilpotent matrix）。

Result: 构造出的精确配对覆盖 Laughlin、Read-Rezayi 与 Halperin 系列的分数陈态；所给多体波函数为解析形式，且其母哈密顿量为无阻挫（frustration-free）的在位哈伯德相互作用。

Conclusion: 结果表明 FCI 基态的存在性不依赖于理想能带条件，即存在超越理想能带的 FCI 基态；这为分数陈绝缘体提供了类似 Laughlin–赝势 的解析精确配对框架。

Abstract: Laughlin's wavefunction and its parent interaction, Haldane's pseudopotential, underpin our analytic understanding of fractional quantum Hall effect. They are an exact pair in the sense that the wavefunction is the unique, zero-energy ground state of its frustration-free parent Hamiltonian. In this work, we propose exact pairs of many-body wavefunctions for fractional Chern insulators in tight binding models with onsite Hubbard interactions as their parents. The key to our construction is introduction of a Landau-dual Chern band, a multi-orbital Chern band obtained from directly sampling the spin-$s$ Dirac Landau level on a Bravais lattice. The exact many-body wavefunctions are given, analytically, in terms of the matrix coordinate $X+iY+J$, where $(X,Y)$ are the lattice coordinates and $J$ is a nilpotent matrix in spin space. The constructed exact pairs cover the fractional Chern states of the Laughlin, the Read-Rezayi, and the Halperin series. Our results prove that FCI ground states exist beyond ideal bands.

</details>


### [75] [Anyon Coherent States in Fractional Chern Insulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25034)
*Pedro Fittipaldi de Castro,Haylen Gerhard,Wladimir A. Benalcazar*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该工作提出一种基于算符的系统性构造方法，用于得到分数量子霍尔（fractional quantum Hall, FQH）态与分数量子陈绝缘体（fractional Chern insulator）中任意电荷任意子的最大局域化波函数，并证明其为饱和投影密度不确定度界的局部湮灭算符零模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在FQH体系中，相干任意子（coherent anyon）激发是描述任意子动力学、相互作用与编织（braiding）的自然对象，但除Laughlin准空穴外尚无一般性的波函数表达式，且现有构造难以推广至非理想、不可涡旋化（non-vortexable）的能带。

Method: 引入一种系统性、基于算符（operator-based）的构造，生成FQH态及分数量子陈绝缘体中任意电荷任意子的最大局域化波函数（maximally-localized wavefunctions），并适用于非理想、不可涡旋化能带。

Result: 所构造波函数为局部湮灭算符的零模（zero modes），对应饱和投影密度不确定度界（projected-density uncertainty bounds）的相干准粒子；其存在依赖任意子能带的非平凡拓扑，Bloch系数的绕数（winding）编码该能带的Chern数，导引中心（guiding center）可位于晶格任意位置，波函数间交叠按由任意子能带有效磁长度（effective magnetic length）决定的高斯形式衰减。

Conclusion: 该构造为FQH态与分数量子陈绝缘体中的任意子提供了统一、普适的相干态波函数描述，将任意子能带的拓扑（Chern数绕数）与其空间局域化及交叠性质直接联系起来，突破了Laughlin准空穴与理想涡旋化能带的限制。

Abstract: In fractional quantum Hall (FQH) systems, coherent anyonic excitations are the natural entities for describing anyon dynamics, their interactions, and braiding, yet no general expression for their wavefunctions is known beyond Laughlin's quasiholes. We introduce a systematic, operator-based construction of maximally-localized wavefunctions of anyons of either charge in FQH states and fractional Chern insulators, including those in non-ideal, non-vortexable bands. We find that these wavefunctions describe coherent quasiparticles: zero modes of local annihilation operators that saturate projected-density uncertainty bounds. These coherent states owe their existence to a nontrivial topology of the anyon band, with the winding of their Bloch coefficients encoding an anyon band's Chern number. As such, their guiding centers exist anywhere within the lattice, and their mutual overlaps decay as Gaussians determined by the effective magnetic length of the anyon band.

</details>


### [76] [Quantum many-body scars and zero modes in the spin-1 Kitaev chain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25051)
*Ayush Sharma,Jay Pandey*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过分析自旋-1 Kitaev 链在笛卡尔基（Cartesian basis）下的希尔伯特空间连通性，证明手征对称性（chiral symmetry）保护了指数多个精确零能模，并揭示其缺陷动力学中嵌入的紧束缚结构可支持新型 PXP 类量子多体疤痕（quantum many-body scars, QMBS）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于探究自旋-1 Kitaev 链中零能模的数量与结构，以及其缺陷动力学是否蕴含此前未报道的多体疤痕机制，从而加深对对称性保护态与非遍历动力学之间联系的理解。

Method: 方法包括：在笛卡尔基下分析希尔伯特空间连通性以判定手征对称性保护的零能模；借助算符-态映射（operator-state mapping）构造解析零能模；并通过前向散射近似（forward-scattering approximation, FSA）哈密顿量刻画疤痕扇区中的缺陷动力学。

Result: 结果表明，偶数长度周期链的零能模数下界为 Jacobsthal 数 (2^{L+1}+1)/3；可解析构造出 2^{L/2} 个零能模，其对连续二分具有最大混合密度矩阵；并识别出支持 PXP 类 QMBS 的嵌入紧束缚结构，其 FSA 哈密顿量在所研究疤痕扇区中呈普适形式。

Conclusion: 结论是：缺陷在“墙”之间传播的简单物理图像可直观解释上述动力学，并为识别具有保真度复苏（fidelity revivals）的扇区提供结构指引；该图像的局限性则暗示存在其他疤痕机制。

Abstract: We study the spin-1 Kitaev chain and, by analyzing its Hilbert-space connectivity in the Cartesian basis, show that it hosts an exponentially large number of exact zero-energy modes protected by chiral symmetry. For even-length periodic chains, their count is lower-bounded by the Jacobsthal number $(2^{L+1}+1)/3$, where $L$ is the chain length. We then construct an exponentially large number ($2^{L/2}$) of zero modes with an analytic expression that have a maximally mixed density matrix for a contiguous bipartition using the operator-state mapping. We further identify an embedded tight-binding structure governing defect dynamics that supports PXP-like quantum many-body scars (QMBS) in multiple sectors previously not reported. The associated forward-scattering approximation (FSA) Hamiltonian takes a universal form across the scarred sectors studied. A simple physical picture of defects propagating between walls provides an intuitive description of these dynamics and a structural guide to identifying sectors with fidelity revivals, while its limitations point to additional scarring mechanisms.

</details>


### [77] [The phase diagram of fluid hydrogen near melting from machine-learned interatomic potentials trained on quantum Monte Carlo](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25178)
*Shubhang Goswami,Hongwei Niu,Ilnur Saitov,Mathieu Istas,Yubo Yang,Scott Jensen,Markus Holzmann,Carlo Pierleoni,David Ceperley*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该工作利用两个基于量子蒙特卡洛（quantum Monte Carlo）数据训练的机器学习原子间势（machine-learned interatomic potentials），结合经典与量子路径积分分子动力学（Path Integral molecular dynamics）模拟稠密氢（dense hydrogen），构建了熔化温度以上温度—压力相图，确认了终结于临界点的液—液一阶相变（first-order phase transition）并定位了三相点（triple point）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稠密氢在固体熔化温度以上的相图尚不精确：实验与理论虽投入大量努力，但分子氢经升温或加压解离是渐进发生还是通过相变突变仍不清楚，亟需更可靠的相图预测。

Method: 采用两种机器学习原子间势开展经典与量子路径积分分子动力学模拟，两类势均以量子蒙特卡洛能量和力为训练数据；随后以有限尺寸标度（finite-size scaling）等标准技术确定临界点并构建相图。

Result: 两种模型一致显示从分子液体到原子液体的一阶相变，该相变终结于临界点；由熔化曲线与液—液转变线的交点给出三相点，位于熔化曲线的最高温度处，并预期在斜坡压缩（ramp compression）实验中产生清晰特征。

Conclusion: 机器学习势驱动的多方法模拟能够给出自洽的稠密氢熔化以上相图，并提出可被斜坡压缩实验检验的三相点与临界点预言，为后续高压氢实验提供了明确参照。

Abstract: Dissociation of molecular hydrogen increasing temperature or pressure may either occur gradually or abruptly, via a phase transition. Despite considerable experimental and theoretical efforts, the phase diagram of dense hydrogen at temperatures above solid melting is not precisely known. Here, we perform classical and quantum Path Integral molecular dynamics simulations of dense hydrogen using two machine-learned interatomic potentials both trained on quantum Monte Carlo energies and forces. The resulting phase diagram is constructed using state-of-the-art techniques. Both models show a first-order phase transition from the molecular to the atomic liquid terminating in critical points whose locations were determined using finite-size scaling techniques. Triple points, located at the highest temperature of the melting curve, are found at the intersection of the liquid-liquid lines with the melting curve and should lead to clear signatures in ramp compression experiments.

</details>


### [78] [Canted antiferromagnetism and spin-flop transition in honeycomb $α$-Na$_2$PrO$_3$ revealed by angle-dependent torque magnetometry up to 35 T](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25291)
*Kylie MacFarquharson,Ryutaro Okuma,Matthew Pearce,Amalia I. Coldea,Alix McCollam,Roos Leenen,Uli Zeitler,Yurii Skourski,Radu Coldea*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 对蜂窝磁体α-Na₂PrO₃单晶的角度依赖压电悬臂力矩测量表明，其零场基态为弱倾斜反铁磁结构，磁矩近乎垂直于蜂窝层、沿单斜a轴产生自发磁化，并存在时间反演相关的两个磁畴；结合易轴交换各向异性与Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya（DM）相互作用的唯象自旋哈密顿量可复现主要实验特征，并预言DM相互作用会稳定一条连接自旋翻转转变与饱和的相边界线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: α-Na₂PrO₃被认为由自旋-轨道纠缠的赝自旋1/2 Pr⁴⁺磁矩间的各向异性交换作用稳定出非常规磁性，此前粉末磁化率仅提示低温反铁磁有序伴随弱铁磁分量，缺乏单晶层面的磁结构确定与微观相互作用认识。研究旨在通过单晶力矩测量厘清其零场磁结构并揭示各向异性交换与DM耦合的作用。

Method: 采用角度依赖压电悬臂力矩测量（piezocantilever torque measurements），在三个正交晶面内测量单晶力矩角度依赖，最高磁场达35 T且近乎垂直于蜂窝层。提出包含易轴交换各向异性与对称性允许的DM耦合的最小自旋哈密顿量（minimal spin Hamiltonian），以平均场计算与实验力矩曲线和磁相图进行对比。

Result: 推断出零场倾斜反铁磁结构：磁矩几乎垂直于蜂窝层、弱倾斜导致沿面内单斜a轴的自发磁化，并存在由时间反演联系的两个磁畴；当沿a方向场投影变号时力矩出现尖锐跳变，解释为两个铁磁矩反向的简并磁畴间的场致切换。近乎垂直加场至35 T观察到强异常，归因于接近上述哈密顿量预言的场致自旋翻转转变（spin-flop transition）。

Conclusion: DM相互作用可把部分磁转变转化为平滑渡越（crossover），并稳定一条连接垂直场自旋翻转转变与面内（垂直于a）场饱和磁化的相边界线，该相边界在无DM相互作用时不存在。这凸显了DM耦合在该蜂窝磁体相图中的关键作用，并为不同晶面磁场方向下的相图差异提供统一解释。

Abstract: We report angle-dependent piezocantilever torque measurements on single crystals of the honeycomb magnet $α$-Na$_2$PrO$_3$, proposed to display unconventional magnetism stabilized by anisotropic exchange interactions between spin-orbit entangled, pseudospin 1/2 Pr$^{4+}$ magnetic moments. Previous powder susceptibility observed signatures of a transition at low temperatures to antiferromagnetic order with a weak ferromagnetic moment. From the torque angular dependence in three orthogonal crystallographic planes, we infer a zero-field canted antiferromagnetic structure, with moments almost normal to the honeycomb layers, but weakly canted such as to give rise to a spontaneous magnetization along the in-plane monoclinic $a$-axis, with two magnetic domains related by time reversal. Sharp jumps in the torque angular dependence when the field projection along $a$ changes sign are interpreted as field-induced switching between the two degenerate domains with oppositely aligned ferromagnetic moments. We propose a minimal spin Hamiltonian including easy-axis exchange anisotropy and symmetry-allowed Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya (DM) couplings, for which mean-field calculations reproduce the main features of the observed angle-dependent torque. Torque measurements up to 35 T applied almost normal to the layers observe a strong anomaly attributed to proximity to the field-induced spin-flop transition predicted for the above Hamiltonian. We contrast the predicted magnetic phase diagrams for magnetic fields in different orthogonal crystallographic planes normal to the honeycomb layers and discuss how DM interactions convert some transitions into smooth crossovers and stabilize a phase boundary line connecting the spin-flop transition in field normal to the layers to magnetization saturation for in-plane field normal to $a$; such a phase boundary line does not exist in the absence of DM interactions.

</details>


### [79] [Learning Spin Hamiltonians from Interacting Magnon Spectra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25321)
*Ang-Kun Wu,David A. Dahlbom,Kipton Barros,Cristian D. Batista,Hao Zhang*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该工作证明逆映射神经网络会继承训练数据所用正向求解器的近似误差，并以截断希尔伯特空间阶梯（THL）方法替代线性自旋波理论（LSWT）生成训练数据，从而实现更强的各向异性三角晶格海森堡哈密顿量参数推断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由合成中子散射谱反推自旋哈密顿量时，训练数据来自正向求解器，神经网络会继承该求解器的近似，例如线性自旋波理论（LSWT）无法刻画磁振子相互作用导致的谱移与展宽；因此需要更精确且足够快速的多体求解器。

Method: 研究聚焦于各向异性三角晶格自旋哈密顿量族（处于饱和磁场略上方），采用截断希尔伯特空间阶梯（THL）方法，在单磁振子与双磁振子空间内非微扰地计算动力学自旋结构因子（DSSF）；以THL生成大规模DSSF训练集，训练多种神经网络架构预测哈密顿量参数，并对比LSWT与THL训练的网络。

Result: 经验基准测试表明一维卷积网络（1D convolutional network）表现最佳；学习得到的前向解码器可由预测参数重构DSSF，提供快速自洽检验与响应可视化。相互作用较强时，分别以LSWT与THL谱训练的网络推断出的参数显著不同；Fisher信息矩阵（Fisher information matrix）分析表明，测量协议（包括分辨哪些自旋关联分量以及动量截断）决定了哪些耦合可被识别。

Conclusion: 将机器学习与快速多体理论结合，是从散射数据中进行定量哈密顿量推断的必要途径；仅依赖近似正向求解器（如LSWT）会限制逆问题推断的可靠性。

Abstract: A neural network that learns an inverse map inherits the approximations of the forward solver used to generate its training data. We demonstrate this in the inference of spin Hamiltonians from synthetic neutron-scattering spectra and show how a fast many-body solver can overcome the limitation of linear spin-wave theory (LSWT). We consider a family of anisotropic triangular-lattice spin Hamiltonians at fields just above saturation, where magnon interactions shift and broaden the spectrum in ways that LSWT cannot capture. Using the truncated Hilbert-space ladder (THL) method, we compute the dynamical spin structure factor (DSSF) nonperturbatively within the one- and two-magnon space. THL is fast enough to generate large training sets of DSSF spectra, enabling us to train neural networks that accurately predict Hamiltonian parameters. By empirically benchmarking several neural-network architectures, we find that a one-dimensional convolutional network performs best. A learned forward decoder then reconstructs the DSSF from the predicted parameters, providing a rapid self-consistency check and a visualization of the predicted response. When interactions are strong, networks trained on LSWT and THL spectra infer markedly different parameters. Analysis of the Fisher information matrix shows that the measurement protocol, including which spin-correlation components are resolved and the momentum cut, determines which couplings can be identified. Combining machine learning with fast many- body theory is therefore essential for quantitative Hamiltonian inference from scattering data.

</details>


### [80] [Symmetry-resolved spectroscopy of truncation-induced perturbations in loop tensor network renormalization of the six-vertex model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25322)
*Akira Otsuki,Yuta Yamamoto,Kokin Cho,Rikuto Fushio,Masahito Yamazaki*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文从对称性分辨的转移矩阵谱与共形微扰论出发，逐算符识别了LoopTNR在有限键维χ下于六顶点模型c=1临界线上的截断误差，并给出与理论定量吻合的验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有限键维χ的张量网络重整化（tensor network renormalization）会引入截断误差，且该误差被重整化群（renormalization-group, RG）流放大，但其具体算符来源与增长规律此前缺乏逐项辨识。

Method: 针对六顶点模型（six-vertex model）c=1临界线（Luttinger参数K）上的圈张量网络重整化（loop tensor network renormalization, LoopTNR），结合对称性分辨的转移矩阵谱（symmetry-resolved transfer-matrix spectra）与共形微扰论（conformal perturbation theory）逐算符分析误差。

Result: 在施加O(2)对称性时主导误差为二聚化算符（dimerization operator），源于LoopTNR破坏单格点平移；对称性较少时为U(1)流或荷二顶点算符。每RG步二聚化的增长及无参数振幅比与共形微扰论吻合至百分之几；K因弱边缘误差及二聚化引起的跑动而缓慢漂移；增大χ在0.75≤K≤1.5内降低误差初始振幅，但不改变误差算符及其增长率。

Conclusion: LoopTNR的有限χ截断误差可由共形微扰论定量刻画，主导算符由残余对称性决定，而增大键维主要抑制初始振幅而非改变RG流下的误差结构与增长速率。

Abstract: Tensor network renormalization at finite bond dimension $χ$ introduces truncation errors that are amplified by the renormalization-group (RG) flow. With $O(2)$ symmetry imposed, the dominant error is the dimerization operator, which is allowed because LoopTNR breaks one-site translation. With less symmetry it is the $U(1)$ current or a charge-two vertex operator. The growth of the dimerization per RG step and a parameter-free ratio of its amplitudes agree with conformal perturbation theory to within a few percent. The parameter $K$ drifts slowly, owing to a weak marginal error and to the running induced by the dimerization. A larger $χ$ lowers the initial amplitude of the error for $0.75\le K\le 1.5$ but changes neither the operator nor its growth rate.

</details>


### [81] [Momentum-space quantum Monte Carlo for twisted bilayer graphene: Interaction effects in the presence of remote bands](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25522)
*Shibo Shan,Cheng Huang,Zi Yang Meng*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该工作通过将动量空间量子蒙特卡洛（quantum Monte Carlo, QMC）扩展至显式包含远程能带，检验扭曲双层石墨烯（twisted bilayer graphene）中平带投影（flat-band projection）的适用范围，发现投影在 u0/u1≲0.9 的广泛参数区间内仍然可靠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平带与远程色散带（remote dispersive bands）分离为投影到中心平带提供了依据，可大幅降低多体计算复杂度；但投影模型中的相互作用诱导莫特能隙（Mott gap）可能与平带—远程带间距相当，从而使投影有效性受到质疑，本文即针对此问题展开检验。

Method: 在电荷中性条件下，将动量空间量子蒙特卡洛（momentum-space QMC）扩展至在较宽能量窗口内显式纳入远程 BM（Bistritzer–MacDonald）能带，并与投影模型的结果进行对照计算。

Result: 当 u0/u1≲0.9 时，投影与远程带计算的低能电子谱和激子—三子（trion）谱相似，Kramers 谷间相干（intervalley-coherent）基态保持稳定；狄拉克三子（Dirac trion）在有限温正常态下依然稳健。而在接近带接触极限 u0=u1 时，远程带电子的低能谱权重显著增加，三子权重被抑制，Kramers 谷间相干态消失。

Conclusion: 研究确立了平带投影的广泛有效性，并通过识别必须以显式远程带自由度处理的近带接触（near-band-touching）区间，调和了投影视角与 THF 视角之间的分歧。

Abstract: Twisted bilayer graphene hosts strong correlation effects within topological flat bands separated from remote dispersive bands. However, the interaction-induced Mott gap obtained in the projected model can be comparable to the noninteracting flat--remote band separation, raising the question of whether the projection remains valid. Here, we extend momentum-space QMC to explicitly include remote BM bands within a broad energy window at charge neutrality. We find that the projected description remains reliable over a broad parameter regime, up to $u_0/u_1\lesssim0.9$: the projected and remote-band calculations yield similar low-energy electron and trion spectra, and the Kramers intervalley-coherent ground state remains stable. In particular, the Dirac trion remains robust in the finite-temperature normal state when remote bands are included. Near the band-touching limit $u_0=u_1$, remote-band electrons acquire substantial low-energy spectral weight, accompanied by suppression of the trion weight and loss of the Kramers intervalley-coherent state. </details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多重近能带（nearly flat bands）格点模型为研究分数量子Chern绝缘体（fractional Chern insulators）及不同拓扑有序（topological order）之间的竞争提供了额外自由度；现有研究多依赖传统多层体系，需寻找新平台。

Method: 构建含两条低能近能带的六格点Kekulé晶格模型（Chern数分别为1和0），在每原胞一个玻色子（位点填充1/6）下采用实空间与能带投影的精确对角化（exact diagonalization）并结合无限密度矩阵重整化群（infinite DMRG）计算；通过低能谱、粒子纠缠谱（particle entanglement spectra）与多体Chern数（many-body Chern numbers）识别物态。

Result: 弱的二体排斥（two-body repulsion）倾向于形成主要占据最低能带的Moore-Read态；较强排斥则倾向于形成两条低能带均有显著占据的Laughlin-like态。

Conclusion: 结果表明该多近能带格点模型可作为研究不同拓扑有序之间量子相变（quantum phase transition）的替代平台，超越传统多层系统。

Abstract: Lattice models with multiple nearly flat bands provide additional degrees of freedom for realizing fractional Chern insulators and studying competition between distinct topological orders. Here we report numerical evidence for the interaction-driven phase transition between the non-Abelian and Abelian topologically ordered states at fixed filling factor in a multiple flat-band lattice model. We construct a six-site Kekulé lattice model with two low-energy nearly flat bands whose Chern numbers are $1$ and $0$, respectively. At one boson per unit cell (a site filling of $\frac{1}{6}$), the weak two-body repulsion favors a Moore-Read state predominantly occupying the lowest band, whereas stronger repulsion favors a Laughlin-like state with substantial occupations of both lower bands. We identify the two states by the low-energy spectra, particle entanglement spectra, and many-body Chern numbers, using the real-space and band-projected exact diagonalization, supplemented by infinite density-matrix renormalization group calculations. Our results provide alternative platform to study the quantum phase transition of distinct topological orders beyond the conventional multilayer systems.

</details>

*Ling Zhang,Chunyu,Guo,David Graf,Carsten Putzke,Mitchell M. Bordelon,Eric D. Bauer,Sean M. Thomas,Filip Ronning,Priscila F. S. Rosa,Philip J. W. Moll*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: UTe₂中高场超导（superconductivity）持续至约70 T，其母态FP态性质不明，尤其是不清楚它与低场PM态是否共享底层物理，二者被一阶变磁（metamagnetic）转变突然分隔。

Method: 利用聚焦离子束制备微结构，在bc面宽场角范围内施加高达30–60 kA/cm²的电流密度，测量PM与FP区域的电阻响应及临界电流密度随温度的变化，以辨别热效应与非热效应。

Result: 超过约10 kA/cm²的临界电流密度后，电阻率陡升至接近变磁转变临界端点（critical end point）附近的数值；临界电流密度随温度升高而增大，与焦耳热趋势相反，否定热起源，并在变磁转变两侧观察到相同的不稳定性（instability）。

Conclusion: FP态与PM态之间存在意外的共性；重费米子（heavy-fermion）金属中电流诱导转变的观测表明关联电子（correlated electron）体系存在多重邻近电子态，且电流可如磁场与压力一样在态之间切换。

Abstract: In UTe$_2$, superconductivity persists up to 70 T for fields tilted by roughly 20-40° from the b axis toward c. Its normal state is a field-polarized (FP) state, which emerges above a first-order metamagnetic transition when the field component along b exceeds $\sim$35 T. Despite being the parent of this high-field superconductivity, the FP state remains poorly understood, including whether it shares underlying physics with the low-field paramagnetic (PM) state from which it is abruptly separated. Using microstructures fabricated by focused ion beam, we reach current densities of 30-60 kA/cm2 and observe a current-induced high-resistivity state in both the PM and FP regimes over a wide range of field angles in the bc plane. The critical current density increases with temperature, opposite to the trend expected from Joule heating, ruling out a thermal origin. The emergence of the same instability on both sides of the metamagnetic transition reveals an unexpected commonality between the FP and PM states. More broadly, the observation of a current-induced transition in a heavy-fermion metal exposes the multitude of nearby electronic states in correlated electron systems and shows that electric current, like magnetic field and pressure, can switch between them.

</details>


### [84] [Fast extraction of universal data from temporal spectrum: Applications to Haagerup CFT and monopole scattering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26257)
*Tokiro Numasawa,Boris De Vos,Masataka Watanabe,Atsushi Ueda*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 提出一种从无限矩阵乘积态（infinite matrix product state）转移矩阵谱（transfer matrix spectrum）中快速读取共形场论（CFT）谱的算法，并在 Haagerup CFT 上取得中心荷 c = 2.137(27)，同时揭示 Maldacena–Ludwig 单极子边界与 Fidkowski–Kitaev 模型基态的对应关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上在格点上提取共形场论（conformal field theory, CFT）的精确普适数据需要对临界系统基态与激发态进行大规模数值模拟，计算代价高。因此需要一种更高效且保持精度的替代方案。

Method: 该方法仅需短时间虚时演化（imaginary-time evolution），随后从无限矩阵乘积态（infinite matrix product state, iMPS）的转移矩阵谱（transfer matrix spectrum）中直接读出 CFT 谱；并将此方法用于提取 Haagerup CFT 的电荷分辨谱（charge-resolved spectrum）。此外，通过时空旋转（space-time rotation）论证 Maldacena–Ludwig 单极子边界可作为 Fidkowski–Kitaev 哈密顿量的基态实现。

Result: 在 Haagerup CFT 上提取到更高能级的电荷分辨谱，并得到中心荷（central charge）c = 2.137(27)；同时给出 Maldacena–Ludwig 单极子边界（monopole boundary）与 Fidkowski–Kitaev 哈密顿量基态之间的对应。

Conclusion: 该算法为在格点上高精度、快速地获取 CFT 普适数据提供了可行途径，并建立了边界 CFT 与具体格点模型基态之间的新联系。

Abstract: Extracting precise universal data on conformal field theory (CFT) on the lattice traditionally requires large-scale simulations of the ground states and excitations of critical systems. Here, we propose an alternative accurate and fast algorithm, where we read off the CFT spectrum from the transfer matrix spectrum of an infinite matrix product state after a short imaginary-time evolution. We apply this method to extract the charge-resolved spectrum of a Haagerup CFT to higher levels and obtain a central charge $c = 2.137(27)$. Moreover, we show that the Maldacena--Ludwig monopole boundary can be realized as the ground state of the Fidkowski--Kitaev Hamiltonian after space-time rotation.

</details>


### [85] [Phase diagram of the attractive lattice Fermi polaron and the instability of Nagaoka ferromagnetism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26304)
*Gerard Pascual,Kris Van Houcke*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 方格子上，吸引极化子（polaron）在低填充下依次与二聚子（dimeron）及精确映射到Nagaoka铁磁体的Yang η二聚子（η dimer）竞争，存在三相点与直接转变；填充超过25%后基态恒为极化子。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为厘清吸引极化子、二聚子与Yang η二聚子（对应Nagaoka铁磁体）三类态在方格子的竞争关系，并获得比已有判据更锐利的Nagaoka铁磁性失稳条件。

Method: 采用两粒子—空穴试探波函数（two-particle–hole Ansätze），并以图解蒙特卡洛（diagrammatic Monte Carlo）作为基准进行校验。

Conclusion: Yang η二聚子的精确性使相边界成为严格的Nagaoka铁磁性失稳判据，并给出低填充对数行为与25%填充上限，为方格子的磁性与极化子相图提供定量约束。

Abstract: On the square lattice, the attractive polaron competes not only with the dimeron but also with Yang's $η$ dimer, an exact eigenstate that maps onto the Nagaoka ferromagnet. With two-particle--hole Ansätze benchmarked by diagrammatic Monte Carlo, we find that the ground state evolves from polaron to dimeron to $η$ dimer at low filling, and, beyond a triple point, directly from polaron to $η$ dimer. Above 25\% filling it stays a polaron. As the $η$ dimer is exact, its boundary is a rigorous instability criterion for Nagaoka ferromagnetism, sharper than the previous best and logarithmic at low filling.

</details>


### [86] [Quantum Metric Control of Topological Superconductivity and Incommensurate Charge Density Wave in an Interacting Lieb-Kitaev Chain Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26327)
*Jiale Xuan,Grant Z. X. Yang,K. T. Law,Xiao Yan Xu*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文研究近平坦带（nearly flat-band）区域内相互作用的无自旋 Lieb-Kitaev 链模型，揭示量子几何（quantum metric）与相互作用共同调控拓扑超导与电荷密度波相，并驱动非公度—公度（IC-C）不连续相变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机源于近期发现：非相互作用 Lieb-Kitaev 体系中的马约拉纳零模（Majorana zero modes）具有两种衰减模式，除常规 Bogoliubov-de Gennes（BdG）长度外，还存在由量子几何（quantum metric）控制的更长几何长度，因此有必要考察近平坦带下相互作用与量子几何的联合效应。

Method: 通过调节相互作用强度与量子几何，对无自旋 Lieb-Kitaev 链进行相图分析；采用低能有效理论（low energy effective theory）与密度矩阵重整化群（density-matrix renormalization group, DMRG）计算对相与结论进行验证。

Result: 识别出三种相：拓扑超导（topological superconducting, TSC）相、非公度电荷密度波（incommensurate charge-density-wave, ICDW）相与公度电荷密度波（commensurate charge-density-wave, CDW）相。TSC 相内量子度量长度（QML）随相互作用增强而增大；ICDW 相中相互作用经量子几何调制产生有效化学势（effective chemical potential），导致非公度—公度（IC-C）不连续相变。

Conclusion: 量子几何与相互作用共同决定近平坦带 Lieb-Kitaev 链的相结构与相变性质，相关相与结论经低能有效理论与 DMRG 计算得到一致验证。

Abstract: We study an interacting spinless Lieb-Kitaev chain model in the nearly flat-band regime, motivated by the recent observation that Majorana zero modes in the noninteracting Lieb-Kitaev system inherit two decay modes: an ordinary Bogoliubov-de Gennes (BdG) length and a longer geometric length controlled by the quantum metric. By tuning both the interaction strength and quantum metric, we identify a topological superconducting (TSC) phase, an incommensurate charge-density-wave (ICDW)phase, and a commensurate charge-density-wave (CDW) phase. Inside the TSC phase, the QML is enhanced when increasing the interactions. In the ICDW phase, the interactions are modulated by quantum metric to generate an effective chemical potential, leading to a discontinuous incommensurate-to-commensurate (IC-C) transition. We verify these phases and conclusions using low energy effective theory and density-matrix renormalization group (DMRG) calculations.

</details>


### [87] [Nonstandard quantum statistics for strongly correlated systems: Superexclusion in momentum space and comparison with exact results](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26489)
*Piotr Kuterba,Mateusz Wójcik,Józef Spałek*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文详细分析了强关联费米子（strongly correlated fermions）的非标准统计（nonstandard statistics），包括统计自旋液体（statistical spin liquid, SSL）与模型非费米液体（model non-Fermi liquid, NFL）两类情形，二者均建立在局域双占据被完全排除的"超排除"（superexclusion）原理之上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于刻画强关联费米子体系中偏离标准费米统计（Fermi statistics）的非标准统计行为，并厘清双占据排除在实空间与倒空间两种表示下的作用及其对关联相（correlated phase）出现的影响。

Method: SSL情形基于实空间对局域双占据的完全排除，NFL情形基于倒空间（reciprocal space, k空间）的完全排除；纯物理层面的结果辅以对倒空间中类Hubbard微观模型（Hubbard-like model）的精确分析，并讨论这些统计向局域磁矩（localized magnetic moments）的嬗变（transmutation）。

Result: 分析给出了非标准统计向局域磁矩统计的嬗变、费米液体（Fermi liquid）向强关联费米液体或Mott绝缘态（Mott insulating state）的转变（在半满带情形发生），并强调实空间或倒空间中双占据排除在关联相出现中所起的关键作用。

Conclusion: 结论讨论了模型的普适特征，即与实空间或k空间表示无关；其中一项为玻色子-费米子统计嬗变（boson to fermion statistics transmutation），在强排斥玻色子相互作用极限下，该嬗变导致粒子局域化（particle localization）而非玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚（Bose-Einstein condensation）。

Abstract: We analyze in detail nonstandard statistics for strongly correlated fermions, which comprise the statistical spin liquid (SSL) and model non-Fermi liquid (NFL) cases. Both of those cases are based on the total exclusion of local doubly occupancies ($the~superexclusion$) either in real space (SSL case) or in reciprocal ($\mathbf{k}$) space (NFL). The results obtained on a purely physical ground are supplemented with the exact analysis of those coming from microscopic Hubbard-like model in $\mathbf{k}$ space. Specifically, we discuss the transmutation of those statistics to that of localized magnetic moments, as well as the transformation of the Fermi liquid into a strongly correlated fermionic liquid or Mott insulating state; the latter taking place for the half-filled-band situation. The role of exclusion of double occupancies in either real or reciprocal space in the appearance of the correlated phase is stressed. Conclusions concerning the universal features of the models, independence of either the real- or $\mathbf{k}$-space representation, are also discussed. One of those is the boson to fermion statistics transmutation which in the limit of strong repulsive interboson interaction leads to the particle localization instead to the Bose-Einstein condensation.

</details>


### [88] [Magnon high-harmonic generation by nonlinearities of spin torque driven by phonon-mediated crossing of many-body energy levels of bright and dark excitons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26589)
*Jalil Varela-Manjarres,Federico Garcia-Gaitan,Branislav K. Nikolic*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文提出单一微观机制可同时解释CrSBr中磁振子高次谐波产生与磁振子振荡持续约1纳秒这两个实验现象，该机制源于磁振子、明/暗激子与声子四类准粒子的复杂耦合，并导出扩展的Landau-Lifshitz-Gilbert方程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期对二维反铁磁半导体CrSBr的超快光学驱动实验揭示了诸多意外现象，但缺乏统一的微观解释；本文旨在用一个机制统一说明磁振子高次谐波产生与长寿命磁振子振荡。

Method: 将磁振子视为系统、明激子与暗激子及声子视为量子多体热浴（quantum many-body bath），推导出扩展的Landau-Lifshitz-Gilbert方程（extended Landau-Lifshitz-Gilbert equation, eLLG）；热浴产生的新项具有空间非局域性与时间依赖性，并在声子介导的明暗激子多体能级交叉（crossing of many-body energy levels）时呈强非线性。

Result: 该新项形式上类似于自旋电子学中的场致自旋转移矩（field-like spin-transfer torque, STT），但STT由电流脉冲驱动，因而为实验推测的激子型自旋转移矩（excitonic STT）提供了微观起源；暗激子在飞秒激光脉冲（fsLP）关闭后长时间向磁振子供能。

Conclusion: 暗激子作为能量储库为光自旋电子学（optospintronics）、光磁振子学（optomagnonics）、神经形态计算（neuromorphic computing）与量子转换（quantum transduction）等方向的工程化与应用开辟了途径。

Abstract: Recent intense experimental focus on CrSBr, a two-dimensional (2D) antiferromagnetic semiconductor, has revealed numerous surprises in its response to ultrafast optical drive. We demonstrate that a single microscopic mechanism accounts for two of them---high-harmonic generation of magnons and persistence of magnon oscillations over \mbox{$\sim 1$ ns} time scale that is much longer than the femtosecond laser pulse (fsLP) pump duration. This mechanism arises from an unusually complex interplay of four different fundamental quasiparticles of condensed matter physics: magnons, optically bright and dark excitons, and phonons. By viewing magnons as the system and the other three quasiparticles as a quantum many-body bath, we derive an extended Landau-Lifshitz-Gilbert (eLLG) equation. Its new term generated by the bath is spatially nonlocal and time-dependent, as well as strongly {\em nonlinear} when the {\em crossing of many-body energy levels} of bright and dark excitons occurs via phonon mediation. It also resembles the so-called field-like spin-transfer torque (STT) in spintronics, but STT is driven there with current pulses, so it explains the microscopic origin of the recently experimentally conjectured {\em excitonic STT}. Since dark excitons supply energy to magnons long after the fsLP is switched off, such a reservoir opens avenues for its engineering and applications in optospintronics and optomagnonics, neuromorphic computing, and quantum transduction.

</details>


### [89] [Scalable Simulation of Strongly Correlated Fermions with Neural Gaussian Tensor Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26604)
*Runze Chi,Ao Chen,Garnet Kin-Lic Chan*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 论文提出神经网络高斯投影纠缠对态（NN-GPEPS），利用高斯态的局域纠缠子空间构建与现代神经网络更兼容的架构，在二维晶格上实现 O(N^{3/2}) 的精确收缩成本，并在大尺度晶格上获得强关联费米子问题的领先变分能量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强关联电子（strongly correlated electrons）的精确模拟仍是计算物理（computational physics）的核心难题，需要兼顾表达能力和计算效率的新方法。

Method: 提出神经网络高斯投影纠缠对态（neural-network Gaussian projected entangled-pair states, NN-GPEPS），利用高斯态（Gaussian states）的局域纠缠子空间，使架构更适配现代神经网络，并将二维晶格上的精确收缩成本降至 O(N^{3/2})。

Result: 在 Hubbard 模型（U/t=8、1/8 空穴掺杂）上，于最多 1024 个格点的大晶格中取得当前最优（state-of-the-art）的变分能量。

Conclusion: 结果表明显式连接神经网络（neural networks）与高斯张量网络（Gaussian tensor networks），可为强关联费米子提供一种精确的求解途径。

Abstract: Accurate simulations of strongly correlated electrons remain a central challenge in computational physics. We introduce neural-network Gaussian projected entangled-pair states (NN-GPEPS), which exploit the locally-entangled subspace of Gaussian states to give an architecture more compatible with modern neural networks and an exact contraction cost of $O(N^{3/2})$ on two-dimensional lattices. For the Hubbard model at $U/t=8$ and $1/8$ hole doping, we obtain state-of-the-art variational energies in large lattices of up to 1024 sites. These results demonstrate an accurate approach to strongly correlated fermions by connecting neural networks with Gaussian tensor networks.

</details>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [90] [MatVerse: A Foundation Model for Materials Science](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25651)
*Xuanyu Zhu,Qian Tan,Bo Chen,Jiaqing Xie,Yang Han,Mei Wu,Wanhao Liu,Xuanze Lin,Yuchen Zhou,Shijie Wang,Biqing Qi,Shixiang Tang,Wenwei Zhang,Tianfan Fu,Kai Yu,Lu Chen,Yuqiang Li,Xin Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MatVerse 是在 Intern-S2-Preview 基础上经大规模材料领域后训练得到的材料科学基础模型，通过统一数据构建流程聚合多模态语料，在 OmniMatBench、MatSciBench 与 MaCBench 三个基准上均取得稳定提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 材料科学信息高度异质，涵盖文本知识、分子与晶体结构、实验表征数据、显微图像及构效关系（structure-property relationships），构建可理解并推理这些多样表征的通用基础模型（foundation model）仍具挑战。

Method: 以 Intern-S2-Preview 为基座，进行大规模材料领域后训练（post-training）；构建覆盖材料知识、科学推理、定量问题求解、结构理解与多模态科学解读的大规模多模态训练语料，并设计结合教师引导推理蒸馏（teacher-guided reasoning distillation）、科学问题合成、一致性过滤（consistency-based filtering）与检索式多模态数据构建的统一数据流水线。

Result: 在自建人工校准的 OmniMatBench、MatSciBench 与 MaCBench 三个代表性基准上，材料领域专用后训练后各基准均较基座模型有一致提升，且在计算密集型问题上推理效率（inference efficiency）亦得到改善。

Conclusion: 针对性的领域专用后训练可有效增强基础模型的科学推理、材料知识理解与多模态科学能力；MatVerse 为材料科学提供统一的语言-视觉接口（language-and-vision interface），并可作为未来 AI 辅助材料研究工作流的基础。

Abstract: Materials science involves highly heterogeneous scientific information, including textual knowledge, molecular and crystal structures, experimental characterization data, microscopy images, and structure-property relationships. Developing a general-purpose foundation model capable of understanding and reasoning over these diverse materials representations remains challenging. In this technical report, we introduce MatVerse, a materials foundation model built upon Intern-S2-Preview and further specialized through large-scale materials-domain post-training. We construct a large-scale multimodal training corpus covering materials knowledge, scientific reasoning, quantitative problem solving, structural understanding, and multimodal scientific interpretation. To improve domain adaptation, we develop a unified data construction pipeline combining teacher-guided reasoning distillation, scientific problem synthesis, consistency-based filtering, and retrieval-based multimodal data construction. We evaluate MatVerse on three representative materials-science benchmarks: our human-calibrated OmniMatBench, MatSciBench, and MaCBench. Results demonstrate consistent improvements after materials-specific post-training over the base model on all three benchmarks, alongside improved inference efficiency on calculation-intensive problems. These results show that targeted domain-specific post-training can effectively enhance scientific reasoning, materials knowledge understanding, and multimodal scientific capabilities of foundation models. MatVerse provides a unified language-and-vision interface for materials science and serves as a foundation for future AI-assisted materials research workflows.

</details>

### [91] [Periods, isomonodromy, and modularity of ALG/ALG$^*$ gravitational instantons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25623)
*Shiyu Cao*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 证明所有类型为 $A_0,A_1,A_2,D_0,D_1,D_2,D_3$ 的光滑 ALG 或 ALG$^*$ 引力瞬子（gravitational instanton）在同伦伸缩与超凯勒旋转下等距于 $\PP^1$ 上带 $L^2$ 度量的秩二 wild Hitchin 模空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在建立 ALG/ALG$^*$ 型引力瞬子与 $\PP^1$ 上秩二 wild Hitchin 模空间（wild Hitchin moduli space）之间的完整对应，从而借助模空间的已知结构对这类引力瞬子进行分类与刻画。

Method: 计算这些模空间的紧致超凯勒周期（compact hyperkähler periods）与渐近参数并给出显式逆公式；对三种 ALG 类型用整体等单值传输（global isomonodromic transport）确定紧致周期之外的实参数；结合哈密顿变分公式（Hamiltonian variation formula）与半平坦度量（semiflat metric）的一致比较来应用 Torelli 定理与唯一性定理。

Result: 得到所涉模空间紧致超凯勒周期与渐近参数的显式计算，并对每组容许数据给出显式逆公式；整体等单值传输实现了三种 ALG 类型下由紧致周期未定的两个实参数。

Conclusion: 在上述等距对应下，ALG/ALG$^*$ 引力瞬子与 $\PP^1$ 上秩二 wild Hitchin 模空间互为等同描述，且分类可由周期数据与渐近参数完整刻画。

Abstract: We prove that every smooth ALG or ALG$^*$ gravitational instanton of type $A_0,A_1,A_2,D_0,D_1,D_2,D_3$ is, up to homothety and hyperkähler rotation, isometric to a rank-two wild Hitchin moduli space on $\PP^1$ with its $L^2$ metric. We compute the compact hyperkähler periods and asymptotic parameters of these moduli spaces and give explicit inverse formulas for every admissible choice of these data. For the three ALG types, global isomonodromic transport realizes the two real parameters left undetermined by the compact periods. We use a Hamiltonian variation formula and uniform comparison with the semiflat metric to apply the Torelli and uniqueness theorems.

</details>


### [92] [Asymptotics of Hitchin metrics and modularity of exceptional ALG gravitational instantons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25655)
*Shiyu Cao*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 该论文证明三孔球面上抛物 SL(n,C)-Higgs 丛模空间（对应 Dynkin 型 E6、E7、E8）的 Hitchin 度量（Hitchin metric）沿端渐近收敛于半平坦度量（semiflat metric），并据此将锥参数为 1/3、1/4、1/6 的 ALG 引力瞬子（ALG gravitational instanton）完全分类为这些模空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究四维模空间上 Hitchin 度量的渐近行为，并借助 ALG Torelli 定理刻画特定锥参数的 ALG 引力瞬子，从而建立模空间度量与引力瞬子分类之间的联系。

Method: 对固定权重（位于 alcove 内部）与固定标量分级留数（scalar graded residues）证明 Hitchin 度量的渐近估计；将 Dimakis–Fredrickson–Yae 的周期计算（period calculation）推广到边界，并允许紧支撑的标架变更（compactly supported changes of marking），以覆盖归一化周期域（normalized period domain）的每个点。

Result: Hitchin 度量沿端在 C^∞ 意义下收敛到半平坦度量，一般情形为多项式衰减，当所有分级留数为零时为指数衰减；对权重位于闭 alcove 的每个可容许参数，模空间配 Hitchin 度量为二阶完备非平坦 ALG 引力瞬子；周期计算可达归一化周期域的所有点。

Conclusion: 由 ALG Torelli 定理可得：锥参数为 1/3、1/4 或 1/6 的每个 ALG 引力瞬子，在同伦伸缩（homothety）与超凯勒旋转（hyperkähler rotation）意义下，等距于上述某一模空间配 Hitchin 度量。

Abstract: We prove asymptotic estimates for the Hitchin metrics on four-dimensional moduli spaces of parabolic $\mathrm{SL}(n,\C)$-Higgs bundles on the three-punctured sphere with flag types corresponding to the Dynkin types $E_6$, $E_7$ and $E_8$. For fixed weights in the alcove interiors and fixed scalar graded residues, the Hitchin metric converges in $C^\infty$ to the semiflat metric along the end. The decay is polynomial in general and exponential when all graded residues vanish. For every admissible parameter with weights in the closed alcoves, the moduli space with its Hitchin metric is a complete nonflat ALG gravitational instanton of order two. Extending the period calculation of Dimakis--Fredrickson--Yae to the boundary and allowing compactly supported changes of marking, we obtain every point of the normalized period domain. It follows from the ALG Torelli theorem that every ALG gravitational instanton with cone parameter $1/3$, $1/4$ or $1/6$ is isometric, up to homothety and hyperkähler rotation, to one of these moduli spaces with its Hitchin metric.

</details>


### [93] [Supersymmetric Hilbert complexes and Witten deformation on stratified pseudomanifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25737)
*Gayana Jayasinghe,Manousos Maridakis,Hadrian Quan,Alex R. Taylor,Xinran Yu*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 该文提出一个研究指标理论（index theory）与N=1,2超对称（SUSY）Dirac算子关系的框架，并将其推广到带楔形度量（wedge metrics，即迭代锥度量）的分层伪流形（stratified pseudomanifolds）上。由此构造Witten形变子复形，对Morse多项式与等变指标公式（equivariant index formulae）进行范畴化（categorify）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机在于将超对称Dirac算子的指标理论从光滑流形拓展至含奇点的分层伪流形，并为等变指标定理与Morse型不等式在奇异空间上提供统一处理框架。

Method: 方法是利用SUSY Hilbert复形的Witten形变子复形，在带迭代锥度量的分层伪流形上处理spin-c Dirac算子的Hamiltonian作用，并在Kähler情形分析Dolbeault复形在光滑与非孤立奇点不点集处的局部上同调群（local cohomology groups）。

Result: 证明了带Hamiltonian作用的spin-c Dirac算子的等变指标定理，以及Kähler情形下的全纯Morse不等式（Holomorphic Morse inequalities），特别是奇异空间上新型等变Riemann-Roch（ERR）定理；同时改进了de Rham情形下的分层Morse-Bott不等式，并给出Kervaire半特征标（Kervaire semi-characteristic）与等变符号差（equivariant signature）等应用；局部上同调群对ERR数进行了范畴化。

Conclusion: 该框架统一并推广了奇点空间上的等变指标理论；附录（与Alex R. Taylor合作）综述SUSY与带边界及奇点流形上指标理论的关联，重点介绍作者近期在N=2 SUSY方面获得的新见解与结果。

Abstract: We present a framework to study topics related to index theory and Dirac operators with N=1,2 supersymmetry (SUSY), extending it to stratified pseudomanifolds with wedge metrics (iterated conic metrics). We use this to construct Witten deformed subcomplexes of SUSY Hilbert complexes that categorify Morse polynomials and equivariant index formulae for a large class of operators.
  This allows us to prove equivariant index theorems for spin-c Dirac operators with Hamiltonian actions, and Holomorphic Morse inequalities in the Kähler case, in particular proving a new type of equivariant Riemann-Roch (ERR) theorem on singular spaces. We also improve on stratified Morse-Bott inequalities in the de Rham case, and review other applications including for the Kervaire semi-characteristic and the equivariant signature. We compute local cohomology groups for the Dolbeault complexes at both smooth and singular non-isolated fixed point sets which categorify the ERR numbers.
  In an Appendix joint with Alex R. Taylor, we give a broad overview of the relevance of SUSY to index theory on manifolds with boundaries and singularities, mainly focusing on the new insights and results related to N=2 SUSY that the authors have uncovered recently.

</details>

### [94] [The Universality of Halo Growth and Merger Rates in Modified Gravity Theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25472)
*Haicong Xu,Yechun Yong,Feihong He,Zhongxu Zhai,Jiaxin Han,Baojiu Li*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 基于ELEPHANT大N体模拟，检验暗物质晕并合率与质量吸积历史在修改引力（modified gravity, MG）模型下是否保持标准ΛCDM的普适性，发现并合率近普适性保留，而质量吸积历史出现系统性偏离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准ΛCDM框架下暗物质晕并合率呈近普适形式，仅弱依赖晕质量与红移；需检验该普适性在宇宙结构增长偏离广义相对论（general relativity, GR）的修改引力模型中是否依然成立。

Method: 利用ELEPHANT大N体模拟套件，在两类代表性超出GR情形（Hu–Sawicki变色龙f(R)模型与Dvali–Gabadadze–Porrati模型正规分支）中测量晕并合率与质量吸积历史，并与GR比较。

Result: 质量吸积历史出现系统性偏离GR，总体表现为晕增长更快；MG效应弱时偏差较小，偏离GR强时更显著，极端情形可超过15%。并合率的近普适行为在所有引力模型中均得到良好保留，仅弱依赖红移与晕质量；但若晕质量定义与修改引力模型不一致，会出现表观偏离。

Conclusion: 晕增长的无偏吸积性质使并合率普适性在MG下依然稳健，而质量吸积历史对引力模型更敏感；分析MG中的晕统计时须采用与模型一致的晕质量定义，以避免虚假的普适性破坏。

Abstract: In the standard $Λ$CDM framework, the merger rate of dark matter haloes has been found to follow a nearly universal form, with only weak dependence on halo mass and redshift. We examine whether this universality is preserved in modified gravity (MG) models, where the growth of cosmic structure can deviate from that of general relativity (GR). We use the ELEPHANT suite of large $N$-body simulations to study halo merger rates and mass accretion histories in different modified gravity models, including two representative scenarios beyond-GR: the Hu--Sawicki chameleon $f(R)$ model and the normal branch of the Dvali--Gabadadze--Porrati model. For the mass accretion histories, the modified gravitational effect can produce systematic departures from GR, generally corresponding to more rapid halo growth. The deviations are relatively small when the MG effect is weak, while they become more pronounced in models with strong deviation from GR and can exceed $15\%$ in the extreme case.
  For the halo merger rate, we find that the nearly universal behavior is well preserved across all the gravity models, with only weak dependence on redshift and halo mass, reflecting the unbiased accretion nature of halo growth. However, apparent deviation from this universality can arise if the halo mass is not defined consistently with the modified gravity model.

</details>


### [95] [Galaxy Bias and Soft Limits from Inflation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26070)
*Charalampos Nikolis,Henrique Rubira*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 该论文建立了在任意阶原初n点函数存在时的偏置算符多圈重整化框架，可统一处理高阶原初非高斯性（PNG）对星系偏置展开的贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原初非高斯性通过尺度依赖的星系偏置（galaxy bias）影响大尺度结构；将高阶PNG一致地纳入偏置展开，需要超越双谱（bispectrum）挤压极限（squeezed limit）的重整化框架。

Method: 在非零任意阶原初n点关联函数存在下，发展偏置算符的多圈重整化（multi-loop renormalization），并纳入随机贡献（stochastic contributions）；论证星系偏置重整化由原初关联函数与偏置核（bias kernels）的多软极限（multi-soft limits）决定。

Result: 给出了确定性算符（deterministic operators）在偏置展开至五阶时的一圈与两圈重整化的显式计算，并推导了偏置参数在一圈与两圈的重整化群方程（renormalization group equations）。

Conclusion: 该工作为高阶原初非高斯性下的偏置重整化提供了系统框架，其多软极限刻画与重整化群方程可用于一致地预言尺度依赖偏置及随机项演化。

Abstract: Primordial non-Gaussianity (PNG) leaves an imprint on large-scale structure through a scale-dependent galaxy bias, and properly incorporating the effects of higher order PNG into the bias expansion requires a consistent renormalization framework that extends beyond the squeezed limit of the bispectrum. We develop the multi-loop renormalization of bias operators in the presence of non-vanishing primordial n-point functions of arbitrary order, including stochastic contributions. We show that galaxy bias renormalization is determined by the multi-soft limits of primordial correlators and bias kernels. We provide explicit computations for one- and two-loop renormalization of deterministic operators up to fifth order in the bias expansion. Finally, we derive the renormalization group equations for the bias parameters at one and two loops.

</details>


### [96] [Sourcing vector perturbations in warm inflation through shear viscous effects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26225)
*Arjun Berera,Suddhasattwa Brahma,Zizang Qiu,Rudnei O. Ramos*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 在温暴胀（warm inflation）中，辐射浴的非平衡动力学导致剪切黏性（shear viscosity），通过随机流体力学（fluctuating hydrodynamics）引入随机各向异性应力（stochastic anisotropic stress），使矢量模（vector mode）满足受约束的朗之万方程（Langevin equation）。其功率谱正比于剪切黏性并依赖哈勃慢滚参数（Hubble slow-roll parameter）ε，衰减约为 a^{-(1-ε)}，远慢于无源无黏情形下的 a^{-4}，但仍随超视界尺度衰减，故晚期矢量-标量比可忽略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准宇宙学微扰理论（cosmological perturbation theory）通常认为矢量模因快速衰减而无显著作用，但温暴胀时期辐射浴的非平衡动力学产生剪切黏性效应，超出理想流体（perfect-fluid）近似，因而需要重新审视矢量模的生成与演化。

Method: 采用随机流体力学（fluctuating hydrodynamics）方法描述与剪切黏性相关的随机各向异性应力，并在暴胀背景（inflationary background）下推导矢量模所满足的受约束朗之万方程（Langevin equation），进而在弱黏性（weak-viscosity）区域计算其功率谱（power spectrum）。

Result: 所得功率谱正比于剪切黏性，并依赖哈勃慢滚参数 ε，近似按 a^{-(1-ε)} 衰减，显著慢于无源、无黏情形下按 a^{-4} 衰减的功率谱；但持续受源的矢量模在超视界尺度（superhorizon scales）上仍会衰减，数值示例中的基准参数给出可忽略的晚期矢量-标量比（vector-to-scalar ratio）。

Conclusion: 尽管不存在直接的大尺度可观测矢量信号，该工作确立了温暴胀中矢量微扰的一个来源，并为研究矢量通道（vector channel）中的次级效应与大尺度白噪声（large-scale white noise）提供了框架。

Abstract: Vector modes are generally believed not to play a significant role in standard cosmological perturbation theory because they decay rapidly. Non-equilibrium dynamics in the radiation bath present during warm inflation can lead to shear viscous effects, requiring a description beyond the perfect-fluid approximation. We adopt the fluctuating hydrodynamic approach to account for stochastic anisotropic stress related to shear viscosity. We show that the vector mode obeys a constrained Langevin equation in an inflationary background. The resulting power spectrum, considered in the weak-viscosity regime, is proportional to the shear viscosity and it also depends on the Hubble slow-roll parameter $ε$, decaying approximately as $a^{-(1-ε)}$. This behaviour is significantly slower than the unsourced, non-viscous power spectrum, which decays as $a^{-4}$. The continuously sourced vector modes, nevertheless, decay on superhorizon scales, and the illustrative benchmark parameter choices adopted in our numerical examples give a negligible late-time vector-to-scalar ratio. Despite the absence of direct large-scale observable vector signatures, the main results establish a source of vector perturbations during warm inflation and provide a framework for studying possible secondary effects along with large-scale white noises in the vector channel.

</details>


### [97] [Two powers, one scalar field: unified early and late dark energy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26230)
*Bikash R. Dinda,Roy Maartens*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 该论文检验单一正则标量场能否同时解释早期与晚期暗能量（dark energy），采用双幂势（double-power potential）模型，并用CMB、DESI BAO与Pantheon+超新星数据（含SH0ES标定）进行拟合，发现相较平坦ΛCDM拟合改善（Δχ²_data=-6.90），H0提升至69.53，但未显著解决哈勃张力（Hubble tension）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于考察是否需要引入多个组分或修改引力，抑或单一正则标量场（canonical scalar field）即可统一描述早期与晚期暗能量。

Method: 采用含正幂陡峭项与反幂项的双幂势，使陡峭项早期主导、后期次主导；结合CMB、DESI重子声学振荡（baryon acoustic oscillations）、Pantheon+超新星（supernova）数据及SH0ES宿主距离标定进行参数拟合，并与平坦ΛCDM比较χ²。

Result: 相对平坦ΛCDM，纯数据拟合改善Δχ²_data=-6.90，含高斯参数先验（Gaussian parameter-prior）惩罚后为Δχ²_objective=-3.76；标量场模型给出H0=69.53±0.26 km/s/Mpc，ΛCDM为68.45±0.24 km/s/Mpc，推断膨胀率更接近SH0ES值但差异仍不显著。

Conclusion: 单一正则标量场可通过双幂势在定性上兼顾早期能量密度峰与晚期加速，并在数据拟合上略有改善、使H0上移，但不足以显著缓解哈勃张力。

Abstract: We investigate whether a single canonical scalar field can account for both early and late dark energy. We consider a double-power potential in which a steep positive-power term can generate an early peak in the scalar energy-density fraction, while an inverse-power term supports late-time acceleration. This evolution requires the steep term to dominate initially and become subdominant later. We test the model against cosmic microwave background (CMB) measurements, Dark Energy Spectroscopic Instrument (DESI) baryon acoustic oscillations, and Pantheon+ supernova data, with the SH0ES host-distance calibration. Relative to flat $Λ$CDM, the data fit improves by $Δχ^2_{\rm data}=-6.90$, or $Δχ^2_{\rm objective}=-3.76$ when Gaussian parameter-prior penalties are included. The scalar-field model gives $H_0=69.53\pm0.26\,{\rm km\,s^{-1}\,Mpc^{-1}}$, compared with $68.45\pm0.24\,{\rm km\,s^{-1}\,Mpc^{-1}}$ in flat $Λ$CDM. This shift brings the inferred expansion rate closer to the SH0ES measurement, but does not resolve the discrepancy significantly.

</details>


### [98] [Modeling the gravitational wave spectrum from slowly-evolving sources in the early Universe: constant-in-time and coherent-decay models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26576)
*Antonino S. Midiri,Alberto Roper Pol,Madeline Salomé,Chiara Caprini*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文在恒定不变（Constant-in-Time, CiT）模型基础上，引入源的非等时关联器（unequal-time correlator, UETC）的相干衰减描述，构建了可统一处理引力波（gravitational wave, GW）产生的通用框架，并揭示CiT与相干衰减模型成立的条件依赖源的长退关联时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 早期宇宙中缓慢演化源产生的引力波背景缺乏统一、可扩展的理论描述；CiT模型原已针对衰减湍流经模拟验证，但尚未涵盖源衰减过程，需推广以适用于不同类型的源。

Method: 将CiT模型推广至包含源衰减的情形，采用对源UETC的相干描述，建立可适用于不同源类型的通用框架；并以衰减磁流体湍流（magnetohydrodynamic turbulence）、声波（sound waves）与一阶相变中的气泡碰撞（bubble collisions）为例讨论其适用性。

Result: 模型的有效性取决于源的长退关联时间；当UETC退关联时间尺度变短时，在CiT预测的谱上会叠加一个共振项，这与早期湍流和声波产生GW的解析研究一致。模型与已有数值结果相容，并解释了辐射主导流体中声波比亚相对论MHD湍流更高效产生GW、而相对论湍流下此差异消失的原因。CiT预言通用谱形：红外（IR）区按k^3标度，中间频率呈线性k，超过源逆关联长度后按k^{1-b_Π}衰减，其中b_Π由源类型及其谱的紫外（UV）斜率唯一确定。

Conclusion: 由于k^3与k标度在缓慢演化源中普遍出现且常见于GW产生研究，本文据此提出可将CiT模型推广检验至其他引力波源的潜在适用性。

Abstract: We study the production of gravitational wave (GW) backgrounds from slowly-evolving sources in the early Universe, following the Constant-in-Time (CiT) model, presented and validated against simulations of decaying turbulence in arXiv:2201.05630. We extend the CiT model to account for the source decay using a coherent description for the unequal-time correlator (UETC) of the source. We present a generic framework that allows us to study different types of sources. As examples, we discuss the applicability of this framework to the production of GWs from decaying MHD turbulence, sound waves, and bubble collisions in first-order phase transitions. We show that the validity of the CiT and the coherent-decay models presented in this work relies on long decorrelation times of the source. However, as the decorrelation timescale of the UETC becomes short, a resonant term grows on top of the GW spectrum predicted by the CiT model, as found, for example, in earlier analytical studies of GW production from turbulence and sound waves. This model is compatible with previous numerical results and explains why sound waves in a radiation-dominated fluid are more efficient than subrelativistic MHD turbulence in producing GWs, which is no longer the case for relativistic turbulence. The CiT model predicts a generic spectrum that scales as $k^3$ in the IR regime, develops a linear $k$ at intermediate frequencies, and decays as $k^{1-b_Π}$ at frequencies above the inverse correlation length scale of the source, where $b_Π$ is uniquely determined by the type of source and the UV slope of its spectrum. Since the $k^3$ and $k$ scalings always appear for slowly-evolving sources and they are commonly found in studies of GW production, we motivate checking the potential applicability of the CiT to other sources of GWs.

</details>


### [99] [Little Red Dots from Primordial Vacuum Bubbles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26620)
*Miguel Barroso Varela,Katherine Freese,Matthew Kleban,Cameron Norton,Evangelos I. Sfakianakis*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 量子隧穿（quantum tunneling）在暴胀子（inflaton）轨迹的横向方向上产生真空泡（vacuum bubbles），这些气泡在暴胀短暂区间内成核并随后坍缩为原初黑洞（primordial black holes），其质量可达 $10^6$–$10^8 M_\odot$，丰度可对应 JWST 小红点（little red dots），且无需依赖已被 CMB 谱畸变（spectral distortions）限制排除的增强曲率扰动（curvature perturbations）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接通过增强曲率扰动形成大质量原初黑洞的机制受到 CMB 谱畸变等观测限制的排除，因此需要一个不依赖曲率扰动增强的替代产生通道，以解释 JWST 观测到的小红点（little red dots）所暗示的 $10^6$–$10^8 M_\odot$ 黑洞族群。

Method: 利用暴胀子轨迹（inflaton trajectory）横向的量子隧穿（quantum tunneling）：在暴胀的短暂区间内稀有地成核真空泡（vacuum bubbles），这些气泡随后形成黑洞；该机制的质量与丰度可调，且质量谱（mass spectrum）较窄，宽度约为百分之一量级。

Result: 该机制可产生 $10^6$–$10^8 M_\odot$ 的原初黑洞（primordial black holes），其丰度与 JWST 小红点相符，同时避免超出 CMB 谱畸变（spectral distortions）限制的曲率扰动（curvature perturbations）。主要限制来自吸积（accretion），即使所有辐射均以硬 X 射线（hard X-rays）逃逸，匹配明亮小红点的族群仍满足该限制。

Conclusion: 横向量子隧穿（quantum tunneling）成核真空泡为产生大质量原初黑洞提供了可行途径，可在不违反 CMB 谱畸变限制的前提下解释 JWST 小红点，并在吸积限制下依然成立。

Abstract: Quantum tunneling transverse to the inflaton trajectory can produce $10^6$--$10^8M_\odot$ primordial black holes at the abundance of JWST's little red dots, without the enhanced curvature perturbations excluded by limits on CMB spectral distortions. Rare vacuum bubbles nucleate during a brief interval of inflation and later form black holes, with adjustable mass and abundance and a narrow mass spectrum of percent-level width. The leading constraint, from accretion, is satisfied for a population matching the bright little red dots even if all the emitted radiation escapes as hard X-rays.

</details>
