### [100] [Geometric formulation of the orbital magnetic susceptibility of periodic insulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26356)
*J. W. F. Venderbos*

Main category: cond-mat.other

TL;DR: 本文从密度矩阵微扰展开导出周期绝缘体轨道磁化率的规范不变投影算子表达式，将响应分解为 Van Vleck 顺磁贡献与几何贡献，并在两带/四带模型中化为量子度规（quantum metric）的简化公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在建立规范不变（gauge-invariant）的轨道磁化率表述，并厘清量子几何（quantum geometry）在轨道磁响应中的作用。

Method: 在均匀磁场中对密度矩阵作微扰展开（density-matrix perturbation expansion），采用基于投影算子（projector-based）的构造，并借助投影算子导数构成的曲率（curvature）、度规（metric）算子及磁矩算符等基本几何量表达结果。

Result: 磁化率可自然分为 Van Vleck 顺磁贡献与几何贡献；在两带与四带模型中，磁化率表达式约化为以量子度规表示的简化公式。

Conclusion: 量子几何对轨道磁响应具有实质意义，并在描述经由 Dirac 半金属相（Dirac semimetallic phase）发生两种绝缘体转变的二维晶格模型中得到考察。

Abstract: A gauge-invariant projector-based expression for the orbital magnetic susceptibility of periodic insulators is obtained from a density-matrix perturbation expansion in a uniform magnetic field. It is shown that, within such an approach, it is natural to distinguish two contributions: the Van Vleck paramagnetic contribution and a geometric contribution. The latter can be expressed in terms of fundamental geometric quantities built from projector derivatives such as the curvature and metric operators, as well as the closely related magnetic moment operator. When applied to two-band and four-band models, the expression for the susceptibility reduces to a simplified formula expressed in terms of the quantum metric. The significance of quantum geometry for the orbital magnetic response is then examined within a lattice model in two dimensions describing the transition between two distinct insulators via a Dirac semimetallic phase.

</details>


<div id='cs.ET'></div>

# cs.ET [[Back]](#toc)

### [101] [Compiling Constraints into Neuromorphic Ising Machines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25352)
*Faiek Ahsan,Shantanu Chakrabartty*

Main category: cs.ET

TL;DR: No AI summary available.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: No AI summary available.

Method: No AI summary available.

Result: No AI summary available.

Conclusion: No AI summary available.

Abstract: Most practical combinatorial optimization problems involve constraints, and an Ising machine that flips one spin at a time can respect them only through energy penalties that raise barriers between feasible solutions. Here we show that a higher-order neuromorphic Ising machine can enforce constraints through programmable transitions rather than penalties. In an autoencoder-like architecture, each inner-layer neuron represents a move, a family of multi-spin transitions that maps every feasible state to another feasible state, receives the energy change of its move through connections that outer-layer neurons multiplicatively switch on or off, and updates the state through the same connections. A compiler turns constraints into this wiring with a number of auxiliary clauses that grows only polynomially for parity, cardinality, one-hot, partition and permutation constraints. Restricting the search to feasible states reduces constraint handling to move design, and we show that the fastest-annealing moves are not those that mix fastest but those that lower the deepest energy barriers, and that moves chosen from the objective lower them further than random ones. On third-order graph matching, the machine needs orders of magnitude fewer iterations than penalty-based neuromorphic machines. Configured as a sampler on a field-programmable gate array, it decodes the surface code with maximum-likelihood accuracy up to code distance 15 within 52 $μ$s per shot and, under noisy syndrome measurements, decodes in real time for millisecond measurement rounds up to distance 9 with fewer logical errors than belief-propagation decoders.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [102] [FamilySpace: From Emergent Chemical Semantics to Controllable Crystal Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25452)
*Haowei Hua,Irwin King,Wanyu Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该工作发现普通晶体变分自编码器（VAE）的原子级隐空间会自发按元素族（elemental family）分簇，称为“涌现化学语义（emergent chemical semantics）”，据此提出 FamilySpace 框架，通过族索引多源分布与流量模型（flow model）实现生成晶体的元素族组成可控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有潜变量晶体生成模型多关注生成质量，对原子级隐空间（atom-wise latent space）的结构与生成可用性缺乏探究，元素组成难以直接控制。

Method: 先揭示普通晶体 VAE 的隐空间按元素族分离；FamilySpace 为每个元素族分配独立源分量，构成族索引的多源分布（family-indexed multi-source distribution），并用族匹配的源—目标配对训练流量模型；推断时由用户给定族模板（family template）为每个原子表示选择源分量。

Result: 在 MP-20 与 MPTS-52 上，FamilySpace 在保持有竞争力的晶体生成性能的同时匹配指定的元素族组成；部分族模板还能提高预测为稳定或亚稳晶体的比例。

Conclusion: 隐空间组成控制可在无需强化学习（reinforcement learning）等额外优化手段的情况下支持对潜在化学区域的定向探索，代码已开源。

Abstract: Latent crystal generative models typically combine a variational autoencoder (VAE) for learning atom-wise representations with a flow model that transports a simple source distribution to the learned latent distribution. However, previous studies have mainly focused on the quality of generated crystals, leaving the structure and generative utility of the atom-wise latent space largely unexplored. In this work, we show that even a vanilla crystal VAE implicitly organizes atom-wise latent representations according to elemental families, with different families occupying largely separated regions of the latent space. We term this phenomenon emergent chemical semantics. This structure suggests that sampling from a family-specific latent region can bias the decoder toward atoms from the corresponding elemental family. Building on this insight, we propose FamilySpace, a framework for controlling the elemental-family composition of generated crystals. FamilySpace assigns each elemental family a distinct source component, forming a family-indexed multi-source distribution, and trains a flow model using source-target pairs matched by family. In inference, a user-specified family template selects the source component for each atom-wise representation, enabling direct control over the family composition of generated crystals. Experiments on MP-20 and MPTS-52 show that FamilySpace achieves competitive crystal-generation performance while matching prescribed elemental-family compositions. Moreover, selected family templates increase the fraction of generated crystals predicted to be stable or metastable, demonstrating that latent-space composition control can support targeted exploration of promising chemical regions without additional optimization techniques such as reinforcement learning. Code is available at https://github.com/hhw11/FamilySpace

</details>


### [103] [Attention Head Specialization: Phase Transitions in Sample Complexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26228)
*Léo Catteau,O. Duranthon,Lenka Zdeborová*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究多头注意力（multi-head attention）中注意力头功能专门化（head specialization）如何随训练数据量变化，并在可解高维模型中证明其对应于样本复杂度（sample complexity）意义上的相变（phase transition）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往对注意力头专门化的研究主要从训练时间维度考察，而该工作关注其与可用训练数据量之间的关系，并试图区分专门化的统计起源与动力学起源。

Method: 结合实证与理论：在 BERT 中观察句法关系（syntactic relations）专门化头随数据集规模增长而出现的现象；并构建可解的高维多头注意力模型进行解析分析。

Result: 实证上，面向句法关系的专门化头随数据量增加而涌现；理论上，专门化是样本复杂度中的真实相变，对应测试误差等可观测量（observables）的非解析性（non-analyticity）。依数据结构与正则化（regularization）不同，专门化可表现为无信息头分化，或经由一系列相变逐步恢复较弱潜在特征（latent features），构成统计形式的简单性偏置（simplicity bias）。

Conclusion: 研究将统计相变与在线学习（online learning）中专门化头的阶段性涌现相联系，从而区分注意力头专门化的统计起源与动力学起源。

Abstract: Attention heads specialize into distinct functions during training, often emerging in stages together with sharp changes in model behavior. While this phenomenon has mostly been studied as a function of training time, we ask how head specialization depends on the amount of available training data. Empirically, we find that heads specialized to syntactic relations in BERT emerge as the dataset size increases. We then study this phenomenon in a solvable high-dimensional model of multi-head attention and establish that, in this model, head specialization is a genuine phase transition in sample complexity, corresponding to a non-analyticity in the test error and other observables. Depending on data structure and regularization, specialization can follow uninformative head differentiation or occur through a cascade of phase transitions that progressively recover weaker latent features, providing a statistical form of simplicity bias. Finally, we relate these statistical transitions to the stagewise emergence of specialized heads during online learning, disentangling statistical from dynamical origins of head specialization.

</details>


<div id='physics.soc-ph'></div>

# physics.soc-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [104] [Particle physics science communication with grass-roots Instagram: a success story](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25806)
*Freya Blekman,Lucia Coll Saravia*

Main category: physics.soc-ph

TL;DR: 该摘要介绍 CMSatDESY——一个由 DESY 的 CMS 组成员众包运营的 Instagram 科普账号，每位成员以自身口吻讲述科研故事；2026 年上半年触达 14 万以上独立账号、获得 25 万以上浏览量、互动率 7%，并可作为大型粒子物理团队的科普培训试验场。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科研合作中的科学传播（science communication）应被视为本职工作的一部分，而非事后附加。机构与合作级别的社交媒体账号通常由专业传播人员而非在职科学家运营，而单科学家“网红”式账号难以长期维持，因此需要替代性的运营模式。

Method: 采用众包（crowd-sourced）方式运营 Instagram 账号 CMSatDESY，由 DESY 的 CMS 组各成员以各自的声音讲述自身故事，并总结可供其他团队借鉴的经验与最佳实践（best practices）。

Result: 2026 年上半年，该账号的科普活动触达 14 万以上独立账号（unique accounts），获得 25 万以上浏览量（views），互动率（engagement rate）为 7%。

Conclusion: 该众包账号模式具有可推广性，可供其他研究团队参考其经验与最佳实践；同时该账号兼具科普培训平台功能，为大型粒子物理（particle physics）团队的外联（outreach）培训提供实践场所。

Abstract: Communicating science to the public is an integral part of the work of a research collaboration, not an afterthought. Institutional and collaboration-level social media accounts are often run by professional communicators rather than by practising scientists, and single-scientist ``influencer'' accounts are demanding to sustain. We describe {\em CMSatDESY}, a crowd-sourced Instagram account on which the CMS group at DESY tells its own stories, each member in their own voice. In the first half of 2026, the science communication activities through this reached 140k+ unique accounts and received 250k+ views, with an engagement rate of 7\%. We discuss experiences and best practices that can be used by other groups, and describe how the account doubles as a science communication playground for outreach training for a large particle physics group.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.quant-gas'></div>

# cond-mat.quant-gas [[Back]](#toc)

### [105] [Genuinely Out-of-Equilibrium Bose-Einstein Condensation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26123)
*Tatsuhiko N. Ikeda,Masahito Ueda*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 本文建立驱动-耗散（driven-dissipative）理论框架，研究基态为真空的激子（exciton）体系中的玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚（Bose-Einstein condensation, BE condensation），证明在一维与二维中强激发下可形成凝聚体，并在三维中给出与实验同量级的凝聚分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 常规BE凝聚发生于基态，而激子的基态为真空，无法通过平衡态冷却实现凝聚；其快速衰减迫使研究转向非平衡（out-of-equilibrium）机制，作者据此针对驱动-耗散条件下激子凝聚问题展开分析。

Method: 采用驱动-耗散（driven-dissipative）理论框架描述激子体系，在一维和二维中考察强激发条件下的凝聚行为；在三维情形中进一步引入有效损耗率（effective loss rates）与给定的注入能量（injection energy）进行定量计算。

Result: 在一维和二维中，激发足够强时可出现BE凝聚体；在三维中，计入有效损耗率与指定注入能量后得到的凝聚分数（condensate fraction）达百分之一量级（percent level），与Morita等（Nat. Commun. 13, 5388 (2022)）的激子实验在数量级上一致。

Conclusion: 驱动-耗散框架可用于处理基态为真空的激子体系中的BE凝聚问题，并能在一维至三维维度上给出可检验的凝聚判据与定量结果，其中三维预测与已有实验观测在数量级上相符。

Abstract: Bose-Einstein (BE) condensation typically occurs in the ground state. Prominent exceptions include excitons, whose ground state is the vacuum; consequently, they undergo BE condensation only in out-of-equilibrium regimes to counteract rapid decay. Here, we develop a driven-dissipative framework to address this challenge, demonstrating that BE condensates emerge under sufficiently strong excitation in one and two dimensions. In three dimensions, by incorporating effective loss rates and a specified injection energy, we find a percent-level condensate fraction, consistent in order of magnitude with the exciton experiment of Morita et al. (Nat. Commun. 13, 5388 (2022)).

</details>


### [106] [Determining quantum phases of eight-fold quasicrystal optical lattices with angular structure factor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26320)
*Kohenjit Pebam,Deepak Gaur,Laxmi Angom,Bhumika Thoudam,Momin Hrangbung,Bikramjit Shoibam,D. Angom*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 本文研究超冷原子在Ammann-Beenker准晶光学晶格中的量子相及其旋转对称性，采用准晶扩展Bose-Hubbard模型（qeBHM）与平均场方法确定软核（soft-core）区域的基态，并发展结构因子计算方法以区分各密度波（DW）与超固态（SS）相。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准晶（quasicrystal）具有独特几何性质，被视为探索奇异物质态的潜在平台；本文旨在揭示准晶光学晶格中超冷原子量子相的性质与旋转对称性。

Method: 以准晶扩展Bose-Hubbard模型（quasicrystalline extended Bose-Hubbard model, qeBHM）对体系建模，在软核（soft-core）区间用平均场方法（mean field methods）确定基态；并发展一种计算态结构因子（structure factor）的理论方法，用于考察对角长程有序（diagonal long-range order）。

Result: 所提出的结构因子计算方法能够清晰区分准晶中的多种密度波（density wave, DW）相与超固态（supersolid, SS）相。

Conclusion: 准晶扩展Bose-Hubbard模型结合结构因子分析，可有效刻画Ammann-Beenker准晶光学晶格中量子相的密度波与超固态特征，为研究准晶体系的奇异物质态提供理论工具。

Abstract: Quasicrystals have unique geometrical properties and are potential candidates to explore exotic states of matter. In this work, we explore the properties and the rotational symmetries of the quantum phases of ultracold atoms in the Ammann-Beenker quasicrystal optical lattice. We model the system with the quasicrystalline extended Bose-Hubbard model (qeBHM) and use mean field methods to identify the groundstate of qeBHM in the soft-core regime. To examine the diagonal long-range order of the groundstate quantum phases we develop a theoretical method to calculate the structure factor of the state. Our method clearly distinguishes various density wave (DW) and supersolid (SS) phases of the quasicrystal.

</details>


### [107] [Are superfluidity and superconductivity related to the spontaneous breaking of $U(1)$ symmetry?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26360)
*Maksim Tomchenko*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 该文依据自发对称破缺（spontaneous symmetry breaking, SSB）的严格判据指出，真实有限系统中全局U(1)对称性不会自发破缺，故超流与超导只能在无限系统中与SSB相联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学界普遍认为超流（superfluidity）与超导（superconductivity）源于全局U(1)规范对称性的自发破缺，作者试图在量子力学框架下检验这一流行看法是否成立。

Method: 采用SSB的精确判据——哈密顿量与边界条件在对称群变换下不变而基态不变；通过简单分析检验有限系统中U(1)对称性能否自发破缺，并讨论SSB理论与现实的关系。

Result: 分析表明，真实世界系统总是有限的，有限系统中不会发生U(1)对称性的自发破缺；因此超流与超导只能在无限系统中与SSB相关。

Conclusion: 由于宇宙中不存在无限系统，将超流和超导归因于U(1)自发破缺仅在理想化的无限系统极限下成立，需重新审视SSB理论与物理现实之间的关系。

Abstract: It is widely believed that superfluidity and superconductivity are caused by the spontaneous breaking of global $U(1)$ symmetry. We investigate this question for quantum-mechanical systems using the following precise criterion for spontaneous symmetry breaking (SSB): the system's Hamiltonian and boundary conditions are invariant under transformations of a certain symmetry group, while the ground state is not. Through a simple analysis, we show that in real-world systems, which are always finite, spontaneous breaking of $U(1)$ symmetry does not occur. Therefore, superfluidity and superconductivity can be related to SSB only in infinite systems, which do not exist in our Universe. We also discuss the relationship between the theory of SSB and reality.

</details>


### [108] [Local velocimetry for atomic superfluids: from Riemann rarefaction waves to quantized vortices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26608)
*Yansheng Zhang,Yi Jiang,Feiyang Wang,Louis Depaepe,J. Q. Matthew Chia,Christoph Eigen,Gehrig Carlse,Zoran Hadzibabic*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 该工作实现了二维原子超流体的局域测速（local velocimetry），利用拉曼-布拉格分束器（Raman-Bragg beam splitters）的物质波干涉（matter-wave interferometry）将波函数相位梯度映射为干涉条纹位移，从而重建二维速度场，并在黎曼稀疏流与量子化涡旋两个典型问题上验证了该技术。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维超流体的局域速度场测量缺少直接手段，而速度场是刻画超流动力学的核心物理量。

Method: 采用基于拉曼-布拉格分束器的物质波干涉术，把超流体波函数的局域相位梯度（local phase gradient）映射为干涉条纹的位移，进而重建二维速度场。

Result: 在一维黎曼稀疏流（Riemann rarefaction flows）中直接观测到速度场的自相似演化（self-similar evolution）；并完整测绘了量子化涡旋（quantized vortices）周围的二维速度场，明确展示了速度环量的量子化（quantization of velocity circulation）。

Conclusion: 该技术为二维原子超流体提供了局域、全场式的测速能力，适用于检验超流动力学中的经典与拓扑（涡旋）问题。

Abstract: We demonstrate local velocimetry for atomic superfluids in two dimensions (2D). Using matter-wave interferometry based on Raman--Bragg beam splitters, we map the local gradient of the phase of the superfluid's wavefunction onto displacement of the interference fringes and reconstruct the 2D velocity field. We illustrate the capabilities of this technique by applying it to two canonical problems. First, we directly observe the self-similar evolution of the velocity field in 1D Riemann rarefaction flows. Second, we map out the full 2D velocity field around quantized vortices, explicitly demonstrating the quantization of velocity circulation.

</details>


<div id='quant-ph'></div>

# quant-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [109] [Entanglement Geometry Beyond Polygons: Graph-Theoretic Entanglement Inequalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25019)
*Masoud Gharahi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: No AI summary available.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: No AI summary available.

Method: No AI summary available.

Result: No AI summary available.

Conclusion: No AI summary available.

Abstract: Entanglement entropies of pure multipartite states can be constrained by organizing subsystem cuts as edges (hyperedges) of graphs (hypergraphs). Starting from the one-party entanglement polygon inequality, we derive these constraints from strong subadditivity and pure-state complementarity, with optimal coefficients and explicit equality conditions. Our central result determines, for a distinguished two-party cut and an arbitrary weighted family of cuts, the optimal polygon coefficient: the smallest total weight of the cuts separating a member of the distinguished pair from a remaining party. A refinement involving three mutual information terms gives the exact minimum of the weighted sum at fixed entropies of the two parties and of their union, attained by at most three independent bipartite pure states. This criterion recovers the cycle and complete-graph inequalities and shows that uniform incidence parity, which we prove sufficient for polygon inequalities on hypergraphs, is not necessary. Averaging strong subadditivity over both sides of a bipartition gives a sharp $k$-marginal inequality with coefficient $2\binom{n-2}{k-1}$, and we determine uniformly optimal coefficients for collective hyperstar inequalities. For paths and overlapping hyperchains, the deficits are exact sums of conditional mutual information terms; genuinely multipartite entangled cluster states saturate the hyperchain inequality while the chain inequality remains strict. We characterize pure chain equality states, bound the trace distance to cycle equality states independently of the number of parties and local dimensions, and obtain bond-dimension bounds for matrix product states and sequential-recovery error estimates.

</details>


### [110] [Certified Discovery of Quantum Phase Diagrams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25064)
*Yuanxi Liu,John Preskill,Hsin-Yuan Huang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文证明在物理上合理的假设下，量子算法可被证明地发现新物相：对任意几何局域哈密顿量（geometrically local Hamiltonians）的光滑族，算法仅凭经典描述与未经核验的参考基态制备，即可输出指定精度的相图，并为报告的每个相变提供可验证证书（certificate）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于回答“机器能否可证明地发现新量子物相”这一问题，并摆脱对序参量（order parameter）、对称破缺模式或训练数据的依赖，使相图发现具备可验证的严格保证。

Method: 方法上，算法对每个参考基态进行准绝热或绝热输运（quasi-adiabatic/adiabatic transport）以探索参数空间，并用频率滤波局域探针（frequency-filtered local probe）激发输运态，以激发信号见证能隙闭合（gap closing）；并证明连续相变的能隙闭合可由哈密顿量的频率滤波梯度（frequency-filtered gradient）检测。该测量是温和的（gentle），故唯一基态下单个可复用副本即可支撑完整探索。

Result: 结果显示：量子算法在逆多项式能隙分辨率下以多项式时间运行，经典算法在常数分辨率下以准多项式时间运行；每个参数区间只需至少一个正确制备即可成功，简并基态情形下需多项式多个副本。数值实验表明算法能准确判定若干模型量子系统的相结构。

Conclusion: 结论是：在物理上合理的假设下，量子相与相变的可证明、可验证发现是可行的，且不需要预先给定序参量或训练数据。

Abstract: Can a machine provably discover new quantum phases of matter? We show that the answer is yes under physically motivated assumptions. For any smooth family of geometrically local Hamiltonians, we construct an efficient quantum algorithm that outputs the phase diagram to prescribed accuracy and provides a verifiable certificate for every transition it reports. The algorithm takes as input only a classical description of the family together with unverified preparations of reference ground states; no order parameter, symmetry-breaking pattern, or training data are supplied. It succeeds provided that at least one preparation is correct in each gapped region of parameter space. The algorithm explores parameter space by quasi-adiabatically or adiabatically transporting each reference state, detecting the approach of a phase boundary by tapping the transported state with a frequency-filtered local probe: excitations created by the probe witness the closing of the energy gap. We prove that the gap closing for continuous phase transitions can always be detected using the frequency-filtered gradient of the Hamiltonian. The gap-detecting measurement is gentle, so when the ground state is unique a single reusable copy of each reference ground state supports the full exploration (for degenerate ground states, polynomially many copies suffice). The quantum algorithm runs in polynomial time at inverse-polynomial gap resolution. We also give a classical algorithm that runs in quasipolynomial time at constant gap resolution. Numerical experiments demonstrate how our algorithm accurately determines the phase structure of several model quantum systems.

</details>


### [111] [Tunable Ground-State Entanglement in Fully Connected Quantum Systems: From Logarithmic to Volume-Law Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26430)
*Donghoon Kim,Tomotaka Kuwahara*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过构造具有傅里叶配对振幅的全连接二分哈密顿量，证明仅连续放松Kac归一化即可使基态纠缠熵从对数标度过渡到体积律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全连接哈密顿量在自然Kac归一化下，非简并有能隙基态已知服从对数纠缠上界，但缺乏对无几何局域性时纠缠如何依赖相互作用归一化的系统理解，本文即探究放松Kac归一化对基态纠缠的影响。

Method: 构造n量子比特的全对全二分哈密顿量（all-to-all bipartite Hamiltonian），配对振幅取傅里叶形式，相互作用强度正比于n^{-κ}；在小固定耦合下对纯基态的纠缠熵（entanglement entropy）做严格证明，并用弱耦合分析与数值计算考察能隙。

Result: 证明每个纯基态纠缠熵在1/2<κ≤1时为Θ(n^{2-2κ} log n)，κ=1/2时为Θ(n)；对3/4≤κ≤1，基态唯一且与其余谱有与系统尺寸无关的能隙，弱耦合分析与数值结果表明1/2≤κ<3/4时能隙仍保持。

Conclusion: 仅改变相互作用归一化即可得到从Kac归一化（κ=1）的对数标度到次广延纠缠增强直至体积律（volume law）的可控层级，表明即使任意弱的归一化放松也会破坏普适的对数纠缠界。

Abstract: Entanglement scaling is a central probe of the complexity of quantum many-body ground states, yet once geometric locality is absent, it can depend sensitively on the structure and normalization of interactions. For fully connected Hamiltonians with the natural Kac normalization, nondegenerate gapped ground states are known to obey a logarithmic entanglement bound. Here we ask how the ground-state entanglement changes when this interaction normalization is continuously relaxed away from the Kac scale. We answer this question by constructing an explicit all-to-all bipartite Hamiltonian of $n$ qubits with Fourier pairing amplitudes and pairwise interaction strength proportional to $n^{-κ}$. For sufficiently small fixed coupling, we prove that every pure ground state has entanglement entropy $Θ(n^{2-2κ} \log n)$ for $1/2<κ\leq1$ and $Θ(n)$ at $κ=1/2$. We further show that the ground state is unique and separated from the rest of the spectrum by a system-size-independent gap for $3/4 \leq κ\leq 1$, while weak-coupling analysis and numerical calculations suggest that the gap remains open also for $1/2 \leq κ< 3/4$. Thus, varying only the interaction normalization yields a controlled hierarchy from logarithmic scaling at the natural Kac normalization $κ=1$ to increasingly strong subextensive entanglement and ultimately to a volume law, showing that even an arbitrarily weak relaxation of this normalization precludes a universal logarithmic entanglement bound.

</details>


### [112] [The Clifford Hierarchy from Clifford Stabilizers in 2D](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25025)
*Alison Warman,Sakura Schafer-Nameki*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该摘要指出二维拓扑泡利稳定子码所受的 Bravyi-König 定理限制可被完全绕过：基于 D₄ 与 ℤ₂ 量子双重（quantum double）副本构造的二维拓扑码，其 Clifford 稳定子可在任意层级实现横向逻辑门。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Bravyi-König 定理将二维拓扑泡利稳定子码（2D topological Pauli stabilizer codes）的恒定深度逻辑门限制在 Clifford 群（Clifford group）内；已知带 Clifford 稳定子的量子比特码仅超出该界限一个 Clifford 层级（Clifford hierarchy）层级，且此前被猜想这一上限普遍成立。本文旨在检验并突破该猜想性上界。

Method: 给出两种显式码构造，分别基于 D₄ 与 ℤ₂ 的量子双重（quantum double）的副本，具有 k 个逻辑量子比特并实现位于 Clifford 层级第 k 层的逻辑 C^{k-1}Z 门。实现这些门的物理电路仅由物理 Z 门或 CZ 门组成；核心新要素来自逻辑编码（logical encoding），其关键依赖非阿贝尔群乘法（non-Abelian group multiplication）。

Result: 证明了该界限可被完全绕过：二维中带 Clifford 稳定子的拓扑码能够实现 Clifford 层级任意层级的横向逻辑门（transversal logical gates），突破了“仅超出一层”的既有结论。

Conclusion: 二维拓扑泡利稳定子码的横向门能力不受 Bravyi-König 型界限的实质约束，非阿贝尔群乘法的逻辑编码是实现高层级横向门的关键机制。

Abstract: The Bravyi-König theorem limits the constant-depth logical gates of 2D topological Pauli stabilizer codes to be in the Clifford group. Known qubit codes with Clifford stabilizers exceed it by only one level of the Clifford hierarchy, and this was conjectured to hold generally. We show that this bound can be completely bypassed, and that topological codes with Clifford stabilizers in 2D can realize transversal logical gates at any level of the Clifford hierarchy. We present two explicit constructions of codes based on copies of the quantum doubles of $D_4$ and $\mathbb{Z}_2$, with $k$ logical qubits and a logical $C^{k-1}Z$ gate, which is at level $k$ of the Clifford hierarchy. The physical circuits realizing the gates consist only of physical $Z$ or $CZ$ gates. The main new ingredient comes from the logical encoding, which depends crucially on non-Abelian group multiplication.

</details>


### [113] [Analytic and Numerical Investigation of a Proposed Non-linear Extension of Quantum Mechanics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25188)
*Alan Chodos,Anastasiia Purtova*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究此前提出的量子力学（quantum mechanics）非线性推广模型的附加性质，通过解析与数值方法揭示其线性极限与微扰行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作提出了一种量子力学的非线性推广模型，并研究了某个精确解；本文旨在进一步刻画该模型的附加性质，特别是其与普通线性量子力学的关系。

Method: 解析上找到模型退化为普通线性量子力学（ordinary linear quantum mechanics）的极限，并对先前的精确解进行微扰分析（perturbation analysis）；该系统受周期性驱动（periodic driver），借助 Floquet 理论确定其性质；数值上将精确解与解析近似进行比较。

Result: 识别出模型约化为线性量子力学的极限，获得围绕精确解的解析近似，并给出多个示例展示非线性效应（non-linear effects）如何影响粒子轨迹（particle trajectories），重点考察接近线性极限的轨迹。

Conclusion: 非线性效应在接近线性极限的轨迹上表现尤为值得关注，解析近似与精确解的数值对比支持了对该模型行为的刻画。

Abstract: In this paper, additional properties of a previously introduced non-linear extension of quantum mechanics are investigated. Analytically, we uncover a limit in which the model reduces to ordinary linear quantum mechanics. We also perform a perturbation analysis about an exact solution studied in earlier work. The equations represent a system that is subject to a periodic driver, and we use Floquet theory to help determine its properties. Numerically, we compare the exact solution to the analytic approximation, and we exhibit several examples of how the non-linear effects influence particle trajectories, with particular emphasis on trajectories near the linear limit.

</details>


### [114] [Accelerating Quantum Error Correction Simulations under Coherent Noise with Compiled Clifford-Augmented Matrix Product States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24044)
*Jiale Huang,Yaojian Chen,Ke Liu,Dingshun Lv,Mingpu Qin*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出编译式Clifford增强矩阵乘积态（CAMPS）框架，通过在多次采样间复用Clifford变换、回收测量释放的量子比特并压缩残余态，实现相干错误下逻辑性能的高效准确评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可靠评估量子纠错（quantum error correction）需理解相干错误（coherent errors）如何影响逻辑性能；张量网络（tensor network）虽能自然刻画相干干涉，但纠缠增长与大比特数使重复采样代价高昂。

Method: 构建编译式Clifford增强矩阵乘积态（Clifford-Augmented Matrix Product State, CAMPS）框架：跨采样复用Clifford变换、回收测量释放的量子比特、演化压缩的残余态，并通过旋转融合（rotation fusion）与面向MPS布局的编译加速采样。

Result: 固定符号相干噪声下的表面码（surface code）基准在小键维度即可给出准确逻辑错误估计；随机符号模拟至距离d=9及[[72,12,6]]双变量自行车码（bivariate bicycle code）结果与Pauli-twirling近似一致；相比未编译CAMPS，编译版在更小键维度收敛，并在同等键维度下实现数量级采样加速。

Conclusion: 编译式CAMPS为研究更大容错电路中的相干错误传播提供了可扩展路径。

Abstract: Reliable assessment of quantum error correction requires understanding how coherent errors affect logical performance. Although tensor networks naturally capture coherent interference, entanglement growth and large qubit counts make repeated sampling costly. Here we develop a compiled Clifford-Augmented Matrix Product State (CAMPS) framework that reuses Clifford transformations across shots, recycles qubits released by measurements, evolves a compressed residual state, and accelerates sampling through rotation fusion and compilation tailored to the MPS layout. Surface-code benchmarks under fixed-sign coherent noise yield accurate logical error estimates at small bond dimensions, while random-sign simulations up to distance $d=9$ and on the $[[72,12,6]]$ bivariate bicycle code match the Pauli-twirling approximation results. Compared with uncompiled CAMPS, compiled CAMPS converges at smaller bond dimensions and achieves order-of-magnitude sampling speedups at equal bond dimension, opening a scalable route to studying coherent error propagation in larger fault-tolerant circuits.

</details>


### [115] [Passive memories beyond the Ising model: from SFQ to GKP](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25030)
*Simon Lieu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该综述借助闪存（flash memory）与单通量量子（single-flux quantum, SFQ）逻辑中的经典被动存储机制，类比电荷量子比特（charge qubit）与通量量子比特（flux qubit），并提出二维"浮动 XY 模型"（floating XY model）与自对偶超导电路以保护量子比特。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自纠错量子存储器（self-correcting quantum memories）旨在将磁硬盘式的经典存储推广至同时抵御比特翻转（bit flip）与相位翻转（phase flip）。除铁磁伊辛模型（ferromagnetic Ising model）外，现代计算机中的其他被动经典存储尚未被充分借鉴，其保护机制不依赖热力学极限（thermodynamic limit）下的晶格尺度扩展，而源于可调的大能垒（energy barrier）。

Method: 回顾闪存（电荷陷阱于导电岛）与 SFQ 逻辑中通量存储环（flux storage loop）的存储与写入机制，并与电荷量子比特、通量量子比特作类比。进而构造二维"浮动 XY 模型"，融合浮动电荷岛的指数型相位翻转保护与铁磁 XY 模型的多项式型比特翻转保护；并研究一种自对偶超导电路（self-dual superconducting circuit），其对称破缺基态承载 GKP 码字（GKP codewords）。

Result: 分析指出上述经典存储的保护源自存储期大而可调的能垒、写入时该能垒可被抑制；浮动 XY 模型兼具指数型相位翻转保护与多项式型比特翻转保护；自对偶超导电路以 $\mathbb{Z}_2 \times \mathbb{Z}_2$ 对称性破缺基态实现 GKP 码字，其保护能力与局限性亦被讨论。

Conclusion: 被动经典存储的能垒调控思路可为量子比特保护提供设计借鉴；所提模型与电路为兼具相位与比特翻转保护的量子存储提供候选方案，但自对偶电路的保护仍存在局限。

Abstract: Self-correcting quantum memories are spin Hamiltonians that aim to generalize the classical memory of the magnetic hard disk (i.e. ferromagnetic Ising model) to protect against both bit flips and phase flips. Beyond the magnetic hard disk, there are other passive classical memories that are used in modern computers, notably: the flash memory in laptops (which traps charge on a conducting island), and the flux storage loop in single-flux quantum (SFQ) logic (which traps flux in a superconducting loop). In this paper we review how storage and writing work in these two classical memories, drawing analogies to the charge qubit and the flux qubit. Protection in these systems derives not from scaling a lattice to the thermodynamic limit, but from a large, tunable energy barrier that separates codewords during memory, yet can be suppressed during a write. Towards protecting a qubit: We introduce a 2D ``floating XY model'' that combines the exponential (phase-flip) protection of the floating charge island with the polynomial (bit-flip) protection of the ferromagnetic XY model. We also study a self-dual superconducting circuit that hosts GKP codewords as symmetry-broken ground states of a $\mathbb{Z}_2 \times \mathbb{Z}_2 $-symmetric circuit, and discuss its protection and limitations.

</details>


### [116] [Learning Integer Winding Evolution Times in Quantum Phase Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25124)
*Elijah Pelofske,Stephan Eidenbenz*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 通过引入参数学习循环，最小化量子相位估计（quantum phase estimation, QPE）输出相位分布的香农熵（Shannon entropy），可在不增加相位量子比特数的前提下突破相位数字化误差（digitization error）限制，并在量子磁体模型上验证了最低本征值计算精度的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准QPE的相位数字化误差受相位量子比特数限制，构成精度上的基本原理性瓶颈；作者希望规避该限制，并提升近期量子设备上QPE的实用性。

Method: 在QPE量子线路中引入优化循环，学习哈密顿量演化时间，使测得本征值分布的香农熵最小化；其依据是标准QPE中相位分布熵与最低本征值相位的模拟精度（analog accuracy）存在自然对应关系。

Result: 在量子磁体模型（quantum magnet models）上的数值实验表明，最低本征值计算精度获得显著提升，且所需的QPE线路深度更短。

Conclusion: 该工作首次将参数学习（parameter learning）引入QPE，使短深度QPE线路即可获得更高精度，从而提升QPE在基础科学研究中近期应用的可行性。

Abstract: Learning a Hamiltonian evolution time in Quantum Phase Estimation that produces phase distributions with low Shannon entropy circumvents the fundamental limit of digitization error of phase, independent of the number of phase qubits of accuracy. By introducing an optimization loop to minimize the Shannon entropy of measured eigenvalues produced by a QPE quantum circuit, minimum eigenvalue computation accuracy is greatly improved, which we show numerically on quantum magnet models. This technique works because standard QPE has a natural correspondence between entropy of the phase distribution and the analog accuracy of the minimum eigenvalue phase. This introduces for the first time a type of parameter learning to QPE, which makes near term practicality of QPE for fundamental sciences more feasible by using shorter depth QPE circuits.

</details>


### [117] [Tunable source of entangled microwaves by nonlinear scattering of magnons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25207)
*Marco Brühlmann,Carlos Gonzalez-Ballestero*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文提出利用铁磁自旋波（magnons，磁振子）的本征非线性动力学，在空间分离的传输线中制备传播微波的纠缠态（entangled states）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在建立仅依靠磁振子非线性动力学产生传播磁振子量子态的方法，并为将磁振子集成到混合量子平台（hybrid quantum platforms）提供路径。

Method: 先证明受驱磁振子模式在低温下可通过三磁振子过程（three-magnon processes）实现的自发参量下转换（spontaneous parametric down-conversion）产生纠缠磁振子对；薄膜中磁振子的各向异性导致严格相位匹配条件（phase-matching conditions），仅放大概率确定的一对磁振子模式，从而将各放大模式分别输出耦合至独立微波传输线。

Result: 在现有实验参数下可产生非简并的传播微波纠缠（non-degenerate propagating microwave entanglement）；通过改变偏置磁场方向，两束纠缠微波的频率差可在数 GHz 范围内动态调节；优化材料与几何结构可显著提升微波纠缠量等品质因子（figures of merit）。

Conclusion: 研究表明，传播磁振子的量子态可仅由其非线性动力学产生，并开辟了将磁振子集成进混合量子平台的途径。

Abstract: We propose a route to prepare entangled states of microwaves propagating in spatially separated transmission lines using the intrinsic nonlinear dynamics of ferromagnetic spin waves (magnons). We first show that a driven magnon mode can produce entangled magnon pairs at low temperatures by spontaneous parametric down-conversion enabled by three-magnon processes. The anisotropic magnon properties in thin films result in strict phase-matching conditions which allow only a well-defined pair of magnon modes to be amplified. This mode selectivity allows to out-couple each amplified mode to an independent microwave transmission line, producing non-degenerate propagating microwave entanglement with current experimental parameters. In addition, we prove that the frequency difference between the two entangled microwaves can be dynamically tuned across a few-GHz range by changing the orientation of a bias magnetic field. Our figures of merit - .g., the amount of microwave entanglement - can be largely improved by optimizing material and geometry. Our work establishes that quantum states of propagating magnons can be created by their nonlinear dynamics alone, and opens a pathway toward integrating magnons in hybrid quantum platforms.

</details>


### [118] [Resilience of emergent Hamiltonian quantum memories in the presence of noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25286)
*Anubhab Sur,Rubem Mondaini*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作表明，在由 emergent Hamiltonian 冻结纠缠态的方案中，构造所余自由度可在不改变存储态的前提下调控存储谱，从而适配噪声环境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相干演化制备的纠缠态可作为其后存储所用哈密顿量的本征态而被“冻结”，但存储谱与噪声环境之间的匹配问题尚未解决。

Method: 针对由工程化链中密度波制备的 Bell 对（Bell pairs）乘积态，利用选择定则（selection rule）阻断局域密度噪声与简并伙伴态的耦合，并通过缩放存储哈密顿量（$\\alpha$）与每粒子能量移动（$\\mu$）调节跃迁频率；以显式经典 $1/f$ 场演化、Ohmic 浴及可解费米子损失模型进行检验。

Result: 在 $1/f$ 噪声下存储时间随 $\\alpha$ 线性增长；Ohmic 浴下最优尺度依赖截断（cutoff）；费米子损失中存在缩放无法移动的零频跃迁，需由 $\\mu$ 移离零频，并给出精确轨道解得到的寿命。

Conclusion: 在保持制备过程中存储哈密顿量始终为最近邻（nearest neighbor）的数目守恒二次参考哈密顿量中，$\\alpha$ 与 $\\mu$ 是仅有的控制参数，因而该协议兼具局域冻结与存储谱调控能力。

Abstract: Emergent Hamiltonians can freeze an entangled state prepared through coherent evolution, since that state is an eigenstate of the Hamiltonian under which it is then stored. We show that the freedom left in this construction allows the storage spectrum to be adapted to a noisy environment without changing the stored state. For a product of Bell pairs prepared from a density wave in an engineered chain, a selection rule prevents local density noise from connecting the target to any of its degenerate partners, so that rescaling the storage Hamiltonian by a factor $α$ moves every transition this noise drives. Under $1/f$ noise, the storage time then grows in proportion to $α$, a result we confirm by evolving the memory under explicit classical $1/f$ fields, whereas for an Ohmic bath the most favorable scale depends on its cutoff. Fermionic loss, treated as a solvable model, includes a transition that rescaling leaves at zero frequency, and an energy shift $μ$ per particle moves it away from zero, with an exact orbital solution giving the resulting lifetimes. Finally, among number-conserving quadratic reference Hamiltonians, $α$ and $μ$ are the only controls that keep the storage Hamiltonian nearest neighbor at every time along the preparation, so that the protocol combines local freezing with control over the storage spectrum.

</details>


### [119] [Local-channel inequivalence within analytic symmetry-broken phases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26139)
*Guanxi Xun,Rintaro Masaoka,Weiguang Cao,Haruki Watanabe*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 论文证明：在连续对称性自发破缺的单一有序相内，即使自由能解析，不同温度下的吉布斯态（Gibbs states）在双向准局域通道（two-way quasi-local channels）下仍可不等价。判据是远距观测者最大互信息（mutual information）的对数增长常数项 \(\mathcal{C}(T)=\tfrac12\ln[\Upsilon(T)/T]+c_{\mathrm{geom}}\)，其随温度降低严格增大且在准局域转换下不变，故有序相内不同温度之间不可能通过冷却相互转化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统观点认为，若系统处于同一相且自由能解析，则不同温度的平衡态应可通过局域操作近似相互转化；论文旨在检验这一直觉在连续对称性自发破缺时是否成立，并寻找区分同一相内不同温度吉布斯态的不变量（invariant）。

Method: 以固定密度的正则理想玻色气体（canonical ideal Bose gas）的对称不变吉布斯态和 \(d\ge3\) 维超立方格点上的经典无涡 Villain 模型为对象，分析两个远距观测者在各自区域独立测量所能获得的最大互信息，并探讨其在双向准局域通道下的单调性与不变性；进而推广至量子与多分量 Villain 模型。

Result: 最大互信息随观测区域半径对数增长，首项在整个有序相内相同，常数项由相位刚度（phase stiffness）\(\Upsilon(T)\) 与几何因子 \(c_{\mathrm{geom}}\) 决定；\(\mathcal{C}(T)\) 在较低温度下严格更大，且准局域通道不能增大该常数，因而其在双向转换下不变。有限格点上，若要以趋于零的误差实现冷却，所需线路的因果半径（causal radius）至少随格点边长线性增长。

Conclusion: 同一连续对称破缺有序相内，不同温度的吉布斯态在双向准局域通道下并不等价，冷却在该相内不可实现；论文给出以相位刚度为载体的温度不变量，并对更一般的连续对称破缺模式提出刚度公式的猜想（conjecture）。

Abstract: Gibbs states at different temperatures can be inequivalent under two-way quasi-local channels even within a single phase with spontaneously broken continuous symmetry and analytic free energy. We prove this for the symmetry-invariant Gibbs states of a canonical ideal Bose gas at fixed density and a classical vortex-free Villain model on hypercubic lattices in $d\ge3$ dimensions. At lower temperatures, two distant observers can estimate the shared direction of order more accurately. The maximum mutual information they can obtain by separate measurements grows logarithmically with the radius of their regions. The leading term is the same throughout each ordered phase, while the constant term $\mathcal{C}(T)=\tfrac12\ln[Υ(T)/T]+c_{\mathrm{geom}}$ is strictly larger at lower temperatures. Here $Υ(T)$ is the phase stiffness and $c_{\mathrm{geom}}$ depends only on the region geometry. A quasi-local channel cannot increase this constant, so it is invariant under two-way conversion. Cooling would increase it and is therefore impossible between any two distinct temperatures in the ordered phase. On finite lattices, cooling with vanishing error requires a circuit whose causal radius grows at least linearly with the lattice side length. We also obtain the invariant for quantum and multicomponent Villain models and conjecture a stiffness formula for more general patterns of continuous symmetry breaking.

</details>


### [120] [To predict or to persist: how decoherence shapes learning in a spin-qubit quantum reservoir](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26215)
*Nikolaos Petropoulos,Elena Blokhina*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 在六量子比特自旋链储备池（reservoir computing）模拟中，退相干（dephasing）与可观测量选择对学习的影响强烈依赖具体任务：记忆容量、混沌预测与自主生成表现出不同甚至相反的相干性依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究目的在于厘清马尔可夫耗散（Markovian dissipation）下退相干与测量可观测量选择如何影响量子储备池的学习能力，并检验这种影响是否随任务类型而变化。

Method: 构建含马尔可夫耗散、采用精确期望值读取（exact expectation-value readout）的六比特自旋链模拟储备池；针对外部驱动的非线性自回归滑动平均信号（nonlinear autoregressive moving-average, NARMA）比较单比特与两比特可观测量组合和仅布居（population-only）读取；自主生成任务采用计划采样（scheduled sampling）与迭代数据集聚合训练读取层；另测试混沌预测与噪声驱动奇异非混沌吸引子（strange non-chaotic attractor）任务。

Result: 在可比多项式特征数下，单比特与两比特可观测量组合优于仅布居读取。多数配置优于关闭弛豫与退相干的参考基线，T₂=1 μs 时最强（此时总记忆容量下降），T₂=5–10 μs 时记忆仅在延迟间重新分布。自主生成中未发现极限环（limit-cycle）持续性与相干性的明确系统性依赖；混沌预测中 T₂=1 μs 时全部 10 个储备池实现预测视野均缩短；奇异非混沌吸引子任务中模型产生平滑近似闭合轨道，既未再现目标的散布，也未逐点跟随其轨迹。

Conclusion: 在该模拟储备池范围内，相干性的作用强烈依赖任务，无法用单一趋势概括；退相干既可提升某些任务的精度，也可能削弱混沌预测的预测视野。

Abstract: In this work we investigate how dephasing and the choice of measured observables affect learning in a simulated six-qubit spin chain with Markovian dissipation and exact expectation-value readout. For externally driven nonlinear autoregressive moving-average signals, combining single- and two-qubit observables gives better predictions than a population-only readout at comparable polynomial feature counts. Most of the configurations we tested become more accurate than a reference with relaxation and dephasing disabled, most strongly at $T_2=1\,μ$s, where total memory capacity drops, and still at $T_2=5$-$10\,μ$s, where memory is only redistributed across delays. For autonomous generation, we train the readout with scheduled sampling and iterative dataset aggregation to expose it to states reached under its own feedback. We do not find a clear systematic dependence of limit-cycle persistence on coherence across the reservoir ensemble. For chaotic forecasting, in contrast, prediction horizons at $T_2=1\,μ$s shorten in all 10 reservoir realizations relative to that reference. For the noise-driven strange non-chaotic attractor, the model produces a smooth, approximately closed orbit, and neither captures the target's scatter nor follows its trajectory pointwise. We conclude that, within this simulated reservoir, the impact of coherence depends strongly on the task.

</details>


### [121] [When the Noise Gets Noisy: Quantum Error Correction with Disordered Noise and Misinformed Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26506)
*Cory T. Aitchison,Lucas H. English,Benjamin Béri,Dominic J. Williamson,Campbell K. McLauchlan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文将无序噪声信道的物理错误率视为随机变量，研究在噪声知识不完整或不准确情况下的解码（decoding）性能，并指出解码性能由噪声分布的少数信息论泛函决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实验中的量子纠错（quantum error correction）噪声随空间和时间变化，刻画这种无序（disorder）可显著提升解码性能，但理论研究通常假设噪声固定、完全已知且均匀，二者存在明显差距。

Method: 构建将物理错误率作为随机变量的建模框架，以信息论泛函刻画噪声分布对解码的影响，并对表面码（surface code）的最大似然解码（maximum-likelihood decoding）与最小权误差解码（minimum-weight-error decoding）进行数值研究，覆盖码容量（code-capacity）、唯象（phenomenological）与电路级（circuit-level）噪声场景，并纳入开放获取超导器件中观测到的噪声分布。

Result: 理论表明阈值（threshold）由噪声分布的熵（entropy）决定，而 Bhattacharyya 系数给出深亚阈值区域逻辑失败衰减率的上界；数值结果与理论在多种噪声设置下吻合良好，并给出刻画噪声所带来收益的定量度量。

Conclusion: 在现实纠错场景中，报告噪声分布的平均熵与 Bhattacharyya 系数可能比报告器件各组件错误率的均值或中位数更具信息量。

Abstract: Experimental demonstrations of quantum error correction often exhibit noise that varies across space and time, and attempting to characterize this disorder has been shown to substantially improve decoding performance. However, theoretical studies of quantum error correction typically assume fixed, perfectly known, homogeneous noise. Here we model decoding with incomplete or inaccurate knowledge of disordered noise channels by treating physical error rates as random variables. We show that the decoding performance is set by a small number of information-theoretic functionals of the noise distribution: the entropy determines the threshold while Bhattacharyya coefficients bound the deep sub-threshold decay rate of logical failures. Furthermore, our results give a quantitative measure of the benefits of characterizing this noise. We numerically investigate both maximum-likelihood and minimum-weight-error decoding of surface codes, showing good agreement with theory across the code-capacity, phenomenological, and circuit-level noise settings, including with noise distributions seen in open-access superconducting devices. When it comes to error correction in realistic settings, reporting the average entropy and Bhattacharyya coefficient of noise distributions could therefore be more informative than the mean or median error rates of a device's components.

</details>


### [122] [High-Accuracy Neural-Network Quantum States via Randomized Real-Time Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26587)
*John M. Martyn,Rod Rofougaran,Samuel Stein,Di Luo*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 论文提出“动力平均（dynamical averaging）”算法，通过对实时演化的态（或可观测量）取平均来估计期望值，将神经网络量子态（neural-network quantum states, NQSs）中任意基态可观测量的误差抑制提升至近二次方量级，从而与能量的快速收敛相匹配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络量子态在能量最小化过程中存在收敛不平衡：能量随变分波函数误差呈二次方收敛，而其他可观测量仅线性收敛，限制了物理预测的精度。

Method: 提出称为动力平均（dynamical averaging）的新算法，利用实时演化态（或可观测量）的平均来估计期望值，并借助量子信息（quantum information）中的洞见证明任意基态可观测量的误差获得近二次方抑制（up-to-quadratic suppression）；该方法利用随机化（randomization）对量子态的作用，无需进一步能量最小化或提升模型复杂度。

Result: 在非可积自旋模型（non-integrable spin models）上验证，动力平均加速了收敛并显著降低了可观测量的误差。

Conclusion: 动力平均在不增加计算与模型成本的前提下，弥补了NQSs中能量与其他可观测量收敛速度的差距，提升了物理预测精度。

Abstract: Neural-network quantum states (NQSs) exhibit a disparity in converging to the ground state during energy minimization: while the energy converges quadratically with the error in the variational wave function, other observables converge only linearly, which limits the accuracy of physical predictions. Here we show how to close this gap by introducing a new algorithm that improves the accuracy of NQSs. Our method, termed dynamical averaging, estimates expectation values by averaging over a state (or observable) evolved in real-time. Using insights from quantum information, we prove an up-to-quadratic suppression of error in arbitrary ground state observables, thus matching the fast convergence of the energy. Importantly, this improved accuracy requires neither further energy minimization nor increased model complexity, but rather exploits the effect of randomization on quantum states. We demonstrate dynamical averaging on non-integrable spin models, where it accelerates convergence and substantially reduces errors in observables.

</details>


### [123] [High-temperature Gibbs states are uniformly Markovian and trivial](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26626)
*Andrew D. Kim,Tai Hsuan Yang,Jong Yeon Lee*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 论文证明在高于某一常数温度阈值时，局域非对易 qudit 哈密顿量的 Gibbs 态具有互信息与条件互信息（conditional mutual information, CMI）的指数衰减；借助基于投影 2-设计（projective 2-designs）的经典表示绕开非对易性导致的团簇展开不收敛问题，进而建立一致马尔可夫性质（uniform Markov property）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 证明此类界的主要障碍在于：由于非对易性（noncommutativity），对约化 Gibbs 态取对数后的传统团簇展开（cluster expansion）可能不收敛。论文旨在克服该障碍，为高温 Gibbs 态的关联衰减与马尔可夫性质提供严格基础。

Method: 核心思路是用过完备基（overcomplete basis）构造 Gibbs 态的经典表示；虽然此类表示通常含负的准概率权重（quasiprobability weights），但作者证明基于投影 2-设计（projective 2-designs）的分解在足够高温下具有严格为正的权重，并可进行收敛的团簇展开。

Result: 对任意空间三分划（spatial tripartition）A ⊔ B ⊔ C（不要求覆盖整个系统），证明了 CMI 的指数衰减，从而确立高温 Gibbs 态的一致马尔可夫性质；高温 Gibbs 态的可分性（separability）是该分解的自然副产物。

Conclusion: 在混合态物相（mixed-state phases of matter）语境下，结果表明高温 Gibbs 态与最大混合态（maximally mixed state）属于同一混合态相。

Abstract: We prove that, above a constant temperature threshold, Gibbs states of local noncommuting qudit Hamiltonians exhibit exponential decay of mutual information and conditional mutual information (CMI). Specifically, we prove exponential decay of the CMI for arbitrary spatial tripartitions $A \sqcup B \sqcup C$ which need not comprise the entire system, thereby establishing a \textit{uniform Markov property} for high-temperature Gibbs states. A central obstruction to proving such bounds is that conventional cluster expansions for the logarithm of reduced Gibbs states can fail to converge due to noncommutativity. Our main insight is to construct a classical representation of the Gibbs state using an overcomplete basis. Although such representations generally involve negative quasiprobability weights, we show that a decomposition based on projective 2-designs admits a convergent cluster expansion with strictly positive weights at sufficiently high temperatures, allowing us to circumvent the obstruction. The separability of high-temperature Gibbs states is a natural byproduct of this decomposition. Finally, in the context of mixed-state phases of matter, our results imply that high-temperature Gibbs states belong to the same mixed-state phase as the maximally mixed state.

</details>

# cond-mat.soft [[Back]](#toc)

### [124] [A Liquid Film Can Survive without Being Bulk-Like: Rupture and Mechanical Isotropy Set Different Thickness Limits in Lennard-Jones Films](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25008)
*Jose Luis Altamirano-Corona,Alfonso Lemus-Solorio,Francisco Villanueva-Mejia,Mariana Ramos-Estrada,Jose L. Rivera*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 通过分子模拟比较“不破裂”与“中心力学性质达到体相”两判据，确定Lennard-Jones自由液膜（free-standing film）的临界厚度：约6-7σ以下会破裂，约11-15σ以上中心才呈体相行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自由液膜（free-standing film）是计算表面性质与模拟肥皂泡、涂层、聚合物层的标准几何构型，但“液膜需多厚其中部才表现为普通体相液体（bulk liquid）”这一核心问题鲜有定量回答。

Method: 对Lennard-Jones液膜进行分子模拟（molecular simulation），覆盖三种相互作用截断（cutoff，rc = 3.5-7.5σ）、三个温度及20-100σ的横向尺寸，并用Irving-Kirkwood定义计算切向各向异性（tangential anisotropy），比较破裂判据与中心压力弛豫判据。

Result: 厚度小于6-7σ（氩约2 nm）的膜在10^5τ（约0.2微秒）内破裂，该动力学极限（kinetic limit）几乎不依赖截断但随横向尺寸增大，且接近该极限时破裂为活化事件（activated event）；力学体相化的临界厚度h*随截断从约11σ（3.8 nm）增至约15σ（5 nm），对温度和横向尺寸几乎不敏感。表面张力无厚度依赖、中心密度相对亏损比各向异性小约16倍，均未能揭示该扰动。

Conclusion: 两判据给出不同厚度并受不同参数控制；两极限之间存在寿命较长但整体受扰动的中间区，且应力（stress）比结构更早探测到限域（confinement）效应。

Abstract: How thick must a liquid film be before its middle behaves like ordinary bulk liquid? Free-standing films are the standard geometry for computing surface properties by molecular simulation and the simplest model of soap and foam films, coatings and polymer layers, yet the question is seldom answered quantitatively. We answer it for Lennard-Jones films by comparing two criteria: whether the film survives without rupturing, and whether the lateral pressure at its center has relaxed to the bulk value. Covering three interaction ranges (cutoffs rc = 3.5-7.5 times sigma), three temperatures and lateral system sizes from 20 to 100 times sigma, we find that the two criteria give different thicknesses and respond to different parameters. Films thinner than 6-7 times sigma (about 2 nm for argon) rupture within 105 tau (~0.2 microseconds); this kinetic limit hardly depends on the interaction range but grows with the lateral size of the system, and close to it rupture is an activated event. The center becomes mechanically bulk-like only at a larger thickness h*, where the tangential anisotropy computed with the Irving-Kirkwood definition falls below 0.003 epsilon x sigma^3; h* grows from ~11 times sigma (3.8 nm) to ~15 times sigma (5 nm) as the cutoff increases from 3.5 to 7.5 times sigma, but it is nearly independent of temperature and of lateral size. Between the two limits films are long-lived yet perturbed throughout. Neither the surface tension, which shows no thickness dependence, nor the central density, whose relative deficit is about 16 times smaller than the relative anisotropy, reveals this perturbation: the stress detects confinement before the structure does.

</details>


### [125] [Mixtures of a fluid with competing interactions and a simple solvent near a hard wall](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25097)
*Ivan Kravtsiv,Oksana Patsahan,Taras Patsahan*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 基于场论平均场方法研究近平面壁的软粒子二元混合物，推导密度剖面耦合方程与接触定理，并用YUKEV势连续性边界条件确定线性化剖面振幅。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软粒子混合物的竞争性短程吸引-长程排斥（short-range attraction and long-range repulsion, SALR）势在界面附近会在中间尺度组织，但双组分体系在壁面附近的密度结构及接触定理尚缺乏系统解析处理。

Method: 主组分采用三项Yukawa势之和实现SALR相互作用，溶剂及交叉相互作用各用两项Yukawa势描述；用场论平均场（field-theoretical mean-field）方法导出两组分密度剖面耦合方程，建立双组分接触定理（contact theorem），并在介观均匀区线性化求解本征值问题，剖面振幅由Yukawa势在壁面连续性给出的精确边界条件确定。

Result: 理论预测高温下远离壁面处两组分混合，降温或提高密度时发生交替层状微相分离（microphase separation）；平均场剖面在考虑其对微相分离起始温度的高估后与分子动力学（molecular dynamics）数据吻合良好，解析剖面除紧邻壁面区域外亦能紧密复现。

Conclusion: Yukawa势壁面连续性边界条虽然隐含于接触定理推导中，但此前未被用于确定线性化剖面振幅，其引入使线性化剖面与非线性解定量一致，可作为改进双组分界面平均场描述的有效工具。

Abstract: We study a binary mixture of soft particles near a planar surface. The main component interacts through a competing short-range attraction and long-range repulsion (SALR) potential, modelled by a sum of three Yukawa terms, while the solvent and the cross interactions are described by two Yukawa terms. Using a field-theoretical mean-field approach we derive the coupled equations for the density profiles of both species, formulate the contact theorem for the two-component system, and obtain closed-form profiles in the mesoscopically homogeneous region by linearizing the theory and solving the associated eigenvalue problem. Their amplitudes are fixed by exact boundary conditions following from the continuity of the Yukawa potentials at the wall, which supply as many relations as there are unknowns. Although these conditions are implicit in the derivation of the contact theorem, they have not previously been used to fix the amplitudes of the linearized profiles, and they bring those profiles into quantitative agreement with the nonlinear solution. The equations are solved numerically for a fluid confined by hard walls. The theory predicts mixing of the two species far from the surface at high temperatures, and their microphase separation into alternating layers upon cooling or upon increasing the density. The mean-field profiles agree well with molecular dynamics data once the mean-field overestimate of the onset temperature of microsegregation is taken into account, and the analytical ones reproduce them closely except in the immediate vicinity of the wall.

</details>


### [126] [Topological trapping of active tracers in branched ring polymer matrices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25528)
*Rahul Bag,Baburao Simma,Vrinda Garg,Surya K. Ghosh*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该文利用朗之万动力学模拟（Langevin dynamics simulations）比较支化环状聚合物（branched-ring polymeric, BRP）与未连接线形-环状聚合物混合体系（linear-ring mixture, LRM）中被动与主动示踪粒子的扩散行为，发现主动示踪粒子对聚合物拓扑结构极为敏感，刚性增强在LRM中促进主动扩散、在BRP中却导致显著的长时亚扩散（subdiffusion）与拓扑笼蔽。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 支化大分子结构在生物系统与合成体系中普遍存在，形成拓扑复杂的介质，其对示踪粒子动力学的影响超出单纯堆积分数（packing fraction）驱动的拥挤效应；研究受变形虫、星形胶质细胞、细胞骨架组件及环状瓶刷聚合物等体系启发，旨在厘清聚合物拓扑结构如何改变输运行为。

Method: 采用朗之万动力学模拟，在以相同单体密度为基准的条件下，将支化环状聚合物环境（BRP）与未连接线形-环状聚合物混合体系（LRM）进行对照，以分离分子内拓扑与单体密度各自的作用；通过改变链刚性、推进力强度、支链长度与数目，并借助多种物理观测量分析示踪粒子的扩散。

Result: 被动示踪粒子基本不受聚合物连接性与弯曲刚性的影响，而自驱动主动示踪粒子能明显区分两种结构：刚性增加促进LRM中的主动扩散，却强烈限制BRP中的迁移性，并随推进力增强出现显著长时亚扩散。刚性极限下BRP形成互联的拓扑笼（topological cages），导致瞬态弱遍历性破缺（weak ergodicity breaking）与尖峰位移统计（leptokurtic statistics）；自中间散射函数（self-intermediate scattering function）显示短程动力学不受连接性影响，而大空间尺度的弛豫由拓扑笼蔽主导；此外存在使主动捕获最大化的最优支链结构。

Conclusion: 在相同堆积分数条件下，大分子连接性（connectivity）与刚性（stiffness）仍主导主动输运行为，拓扑结构是决定主动示踪粒子动力学的关键因素。

Abstract: Branched macromolecular architectures are ubiquitous in living systems and synthetic polymeric systems, create topologically complex media that strongly influence tracer dynamics beyond simple packing fraction-driven crowding effects. Motivated by biological systems such as amoebae, astrocytes, cytoskeletal assemblies, and cyclic bottlebrushes, we investigate active and passive tracer transport in polymeric environments of topologically distinct architectures. Here, using Langevin dynamics simulations, we focus on how polymer topology alters diffusion behavior in a branched-ring polymeric (BRP) environment. To isolate the role of intramolecular topology from monomer density, we specifically benchmark the BRP dynamics against an unlinked linear-ring polymer mixture (LRM). In general, we find that while passive tracers are largely unaffected by the polymer connectivity and bending rigidity, the self-propelled active tracers strongly distinguish between the two architectures. Increasing chain rigidity facilitates active diffusion in the unlinked LRM but strongly restricts mobility in the BRP matrix, driving a transition to pronounced long-time subdiffusion that becomes more prominent with propulsion strength. Different physical observables reveal that interconnected topological cages are formed in the stiff limit, leading to transient weak ergodicity breaking and leptokurtic displacement statistics. Further, the self-intermediate scattering function confirms that the short-range dynamics remain unaltered by the matrix connectivity, while relaxation at large spatial length scales is governed by the topological caging. Finally, varying the branch length and number, we identified an optimal branch architecture that maximizes active trapping. Our results establish that macromolecular connectivity and stiffness dominate the active transport even in the system of identical packing fraction.

</details>


### [127] [Geometry-induced orientational trapping dictates escape of active rods in corrugated channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25563)
*Baburao Simma,Rahul Bag,Hari K. Reddy,Radha Ramana,Vrinda Garg,Surya K. Ghosh*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文用过阻尼朗之万动力学（overdamped Langevin dynamics）比较了自驱动棒状活性粒子（self-driven rods）在正弦波纹通道（SC）与圆形空腔通道（CC）中的逃逸机制，发现圆形通道中平均首次逃逸时间（MFET）随Peclet数（Pe）呈现严格非单调依赖，而正弦通道中为单调依赖，表明局部通道拓扑结构是受限活性输运的关键决定因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 各向异性活性物质（anisotropic active matter）在几何受限环境中的非平衡输运，本质上由自驱动（self-propulsion）与几何诱导的熵垒（entropy barriers）之间的相互作用决定，但不同局部通道拓扑如何影响逃逸机制尚缺乏对比性认识。

Method: 采用过阻尼朗之万动力学（overdamped Langevin dynamics）模拟自驱动棒在窄波纹通道中的运动，对比平滑渐变的 sinusoidal（SC）几何与具有局部均匀曲率的 circular（CC）空腔几何，并分析MFET随Pe的变化、轨迹间逃逸涨落、径向概率分布以及van Hove相关函数（van-Hove correlation function）的自部分。

Result: CC通道中MFET随Pe增加呈严格非单调依赖，SC通道中则为单调依赖；该非单调性源于低至中等推进强度下活性增强逃逸与高推进强度下沿圆形边界的持续性几何诱导取向捕获（orientational trapping）之间的竞争，后者抑制了朝向出口颈部的再取向。双峰径向概率分布与轨迹涨落印证了壁面碰撞与贴壁滑移两种输运模式，且van Hove自相关函数在SC通道中呈各向异性非高斯位移，在CC空腔中则对称并出现延展的活性捕获平台。

Conclusion: 局部通道拓扑（local channel topology）是受限活性输运（confined active transport）的关键决定因素，研究结果对微流控分选（microfluidic sorting）、生物导航（biological navigation）及波纹环境中的靶向药物递送（targeted drug delivery）具有直接意义。

Abstract: The non-equilibrium transport of anisotropic active matter through geometrically confined environments is fundamentally governed by the interplay between self-propulsion and geometry-induced entropic barriers. Using overdamped Langevin dynamics, we reveal contrasting escape mechanisms of self-driven rods in narrow corrugated channels by comparing smoothly varying sinusoidal (SC) geometries to circular (CC) cavities with localized, uniform curvature. We observe a strictly non-monotonic dependence of the mean first escape time (MFET) with increasing Peclet number (Pe) in the CC channel, contrary to the monotonic dependence in the SC channel. This non-monotonicity emerges from a competition between activity-enhanced escape at low-to-intermediate propulsion and persistent, geometry-induced orientational trapping along the circular boundary at high propulsion, which inhibits reorientation toward the exit neck. This regime crossover is further corroborated by trajectory-to-trajectory escape fluctuations and bimodal radial probability distributions reflecting persistent wall-collision and wall-aligned sliding transport. The self part of van-Hove correlation function exhibits distinct anisotropic non-Gaussian displacements in the SC channel, in contrast to symmetric profiles with extended active-trapping plateaus in the CC cavities. These results establish local channel topology as a critical determinant of confined active transport, with direct implications for microfluidic sorting, biological navigation, and targeted drug delivery in corrugated environments.

</details>


### [128] [Nematic equilibria on a Möbius strip: topology and extrinsic curvature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26552)
*Massimo Frittelli,Sébastien Fumeron,Gaetano Napoli*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 研究莫比乌斯带薄管状邻域内限制的向列相液晶（nematic liquid crystal）平衡构型，推导考虑中曲面内蕴与外蕴曲率的有效二维Frank能量，并揭示拓扑分类依赖半扭曲数奇偶性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索不可定向拓扑（non-orientable topology）与外蕴曲率（extrinsic curvature）如何共同塑造曲面衬底上的向列相平衡态。

Method: 在简并切向锚定（degenerate tangential anchoring）与厚度方向指向矢均匀假设下，导出有效二维Frank能量；利用不可定向性导致的恒等条件（局部标架反转与指向矢头尾对称性耦合）进行拓扑分类，并分析窄条极限、以有限元方法（finite-element methods）求解完整二维平衡问题。

Result: 拓扑分类取决于半扭曲数奇偶性：偶数时为绕数（winding number），奇数时仅宇称有意义且净指向矢旋转非量子化。竞争结果依赖条带纵横比（aspect ratio）与弹性各向异性（elastic anisotropy）：单半扭曲时约0.58处偏好扇区改变；双半扭曲时约0.60处同扇区两个极小值交换能量次序而无拓扑变化。

Conclusion: 不可定向拓扑与外蕴曲率联合决定曲面衬底上向列相的平衡构型，扇区选择与能量次序由几何纵横比与弹性各向异性共同调控。

Abstract: We investigate the equilibrium configurations of a nematic liquid crystal confined in a thin tubular neighbourhood of a Möbius strip. Assuming degenerate tangential anchoring and a director uniform across the thickness, we derive an effective two-dimensional Frank energy that accounts for both the intrinsic and extrinsic curvature of the mid-surface. The non-orientability of the substrate imposes identification conditions that couple the reversal of the local frame to the head-to-tail symmetry of the nematic director. We show that the resulting topological classification depends on the parity of the number of half-twists: the sector index is a winding number for even parity, whereas only its parity is physically meaningful for odd parity, in which case the net director rotation is not quantised. We analyse the narrow-strip limit and solve the full two-dimensional equilibrium problem by finite-element methods. The competition between topological sectors depends on the strip aspect ratio and elastic anisotropy. In particular, for a single half-twist, the preferred sector changes at an aspect ratio of approximately 0.58, while for two half-twists two distinct minima of the same sector exchange their energy ordering near 0.60, without any change of topology. These results reveal how non-orientable topology and extrinsic curvature jointly shape nematic equilibria on curved substrates.

</details>


### [129] [Soft biological and bio-inspired materials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25959)
*Livio Nicola Carenza,Giuseppe Negro,Adriano Tiribocchi*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文是一篇综述，聚焦于软生物材料与生物启发材料中活性物质（active matter）动力学建模，重点介绍活性液晶理论（active liquid crystal theory）、拓扑缺陷（topological defects）、活性湍流（active turbulence）以及几何与拓扑限域（geometrical and topological confinement）的作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软生物材料具有消耗内部能量以完成复杂任务、维持个体或群体生命的独特能力，生物启发材料也可模仿生物系统特征并应用于医疗、机器人、能源与环境等领域，因此需要系统梳理描述此类活性物质动力学的建模方法。

Method: 综述采取文献回顾方式，从取向对称性（orientational symmetries）的观察出发，梳理活性液晶理论的基本框架，并讨论其在生物与人工体系多种现象解释中的应用，进而考察拓扑缺陷与活性湍流等复杂动力学现象。

Result: 活性液晶理论被成功用于解释和研究生物与人工实现中出现的多种现象；拓扑缺陷在生物系统中具有重要作用，活性湍流作为一种混沌动力学 regime 在多种生物与合成体系中均可观察到。

Conclusion: 几何与拓扑限域在许多生物过程中发挥重要作用，涵盖上皮组织中的细胞凋亡（cellular apoptosis）至动物形态发生（animal morphogenesis），提示限域效应是理解活性物质动力学的重要维度。

Abstract: Soft biological materials are a fascinating kind of matter characterized by the unique ability of consuming internal energy to perform complex tasks to preserve the life of the single organism or a whole community. Moreover, biologically inspired materials able to mimic specific features of biological systems can be exploited to design novel materials with vast potential applications in areas ranging from healthcare and robotics to energy and environmental sustainability. In this review, we focus on the modeling approaches used to capture the dynamics of this peculiar kind of active matter, starting from the observation of orientational symmetries and reviewing the basis of the so-called active liquid crystal theory, a celebrated model that was successfully used to interpret and investigate a number of different phenomena occurring in both biological and artificial realizations. We review the role of topological defects in biological systems and discuss the complex phenomenon of active turbulence, a chaotic dynamical regime observed in a variety of different realizations of systems of both biological and synthetic origin. Finally, we discuss how geometrical and topological confinement can also play an important role in many biological processes, from cellular apoptosis in epithelial tissue, to animal morphogenesis.

</details>


### [130] [Sequence-encoded amphiphilicity controls the interfacial tension of biomolecular condensates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25966)
*Nora-Elen Giesinger,Jens-Uwe Sommer,Arash Nikoubashman*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该研究提出生物分子凝聚体（biomolecular condensates）的低表面张力部分源于内在无序蛋白（IDPs）的链段两亲性（segmental amphiphilicity）所产生的类表面活性剂（surfactant-like）行为，并通过最小粗粒化模型（coarse-grained model）验证了极性残基比例与序列排布模式对表面张力的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物分子凝聚体虽为致密的液态相，却表现出异常低的表面张力（surface tension），其物理来源尚不明确；本文旨在探究内在无序蛋白的链段两亲性是否能解释这一现象。

Method: 构建仅含疏水（hydrophobic）与极性（polar）残基的最小粗粒化IDP模型，系统改变极性残基比例并在部分情形下考察序列排布模式（sequence patterning）的影响；对每个序列确定相共存曲线（binodals），并在匹配的临界点（critical point）距离下进行比较以控制热力学相行为差异。

Result: 凝聚体表面出现不成比例高比例的极性残基，且与表面张力下降密切相关；总体组成决定相行为和表面张力总体趋势，极性残基比例增至三分之一可使表面张力较纯疏水链降低约两倍；序列排布模式进一步增强极性残基的界面富集，可在组成效应之外进一步降低表面张力。

Conclusion: IDP的链段两亲性及其类表面活性剂行为是低表面张力的部分来源；组成设定总体趋势，而序列排布模式提供不改变体相（bulk phase）行为的序列依赖性贡献。

Abstract: Biomolecular condensates exhibit surprisingly low surface tensions, despite forming dense, liquid-like phases. We propose that the low surface tension partially originates from the segmental amphiphilicity of intrinsically disordered proteins (IDPs) that results in a surfactant-like behavior. We investigate this mechanism using a minimal coarse-grained IDP model containing only hydrophobic and polar residues, allowing us to systematically vary the polar fraction and, for selected cases, to probe the influence of sequence patterning of polar residues. To control for differences in thermodynamic phase behavior, we determine phase coexistence (binodals) for each sequence and compare systems at matched distances from the critical point. We find a disproportionately high fraction of polar residues at the condensate surface, which is closely correlated with the reduction in surface tension. For the sequences studied here, overall composition alone sets the phase behavior and establishes the overall trend in surface tension: increasing the polar fraction lowers the surface tension, with a polar fraction of one third reducing the surface tension by roughly twofold relative to a purely hydrophobic chain. In addition, sequence patterning enhances interfacial localization of polar residues and can lower surface tension beyond what composition alone achieves, revealing a sequence-dependent contribution to the surface tension that leaves the bulk phase behavior essentially unchanged.

</details>


### [131] [Attraction kinetics controls permanent shear banding in particulate dispersions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26150)
*Liheng Yao,Romain Mari,Kirsten Martens*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文研究动态吸引相互作用（kinetic attractive interactions，即以有限速率开启/关闭的粒子吸引）与颗粒体系中永久剪切带（permanent shear bands）形成之间的联系，提出基于平均场的本构理论（mean field constitutive theory）并通过堵塞颗粒体系模拟加以验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究尚未阐明动态（非静态）吸引相互作用如何导致颗粒体系在剪切下形成永久剪切带，本文旨在建立二者之间的机理联系。

Method: 构建平均场本构理论（mean field constitutive theory），其核心假设是：在剪切下，仅当剪切速率与吸引相互作用的动力学速率相当（comparable）时，动态吸引才会产生过应力（overstress）；并用含动态吸引相互作用的堵塞粒子体系（jammed particle system）模拟进行验证，测量小尺寸体系的均匀流动曲线（homogeneous flow curves）。

Result: 小体系尺寸下测得的均匀流动曲线（homogeneous flow curves）与理论在定性上吻合良好；在更大体系尺寸下则观察到永久剪切带（permanent shear banding）。

Conclusion: 理论框架能够解释动态吸引诱导的过应力（overstress）及永久剪切带的出现，作者进一步讨论了该理论框架的普适性（generality）。

Abstract: We investigate the connection between kinetic attractive interactions (i.e. particle attraction that turn on and off at finite rates) and the formation of permanent shear bands in particulate systems. To that end, we develop a mean field constitutive theory based on the idea that, under shear, kinetic attraction generates an overstress only when the shear rate is comparable to the attraction kinetic rates. We verify our theory using simulations of a jammed particle system with kinetic attractive interactions. We measure the homogeneous flow curves of our system at small system sizes, and find them to be in good qualitative agreement with our theory. At larger system sizes, permanent shear banding is observed. We discuss the generality of our theoretical framework.

</details>


### [132] [Activity-driven motion and deformation of a circular membrane domain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26578)
*Gonzalo Ferrandez Quinto,Sho Kawakami,Petia M. Vlahovska*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文在二维 Stokes 流（two-dimensional Stokes flow）框架下，将嵌入的分子机器（molecular machine）建模为点奇异（point singularity），研究其对脂质双分子层膜畴（membrane domain）运动与形变的驱动作用；结果表明无力偶极子亦可产生主动流（active flow）并驱动膜畴。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有的关于膜畴（如脂筏，lipid rafts）的研究主要集中于被动扩散（passive diffusion）与平衡形状涨落（equilibrium shape fluctuations），而分子机器（如蛋白泵、酶）活性如何影响膜畴的迁移性与形变仍知之甚少。

Method: 采用二维 Stokes 流（two-dimensional Stokes flow）框架描述膜畴动力学，将嵌入的分子机器抽象为二维 Stokes 方程的点奇异（point singularity），包含对应力多极（force multipoles）与源多极（source multipoles）的旋转型与无旋型奇异。

Result: 即使整体无力（force-free），力偶极（force dipole）与源偶极（source dipole）均能产生主动流（active flow）并推动膜畴运动；对经历旋转扩散（rotational diffusion）的力偶极，膜畴运动在长时间尺度上呈扩散（diffusive）行为，而在中间时间尺度存在瞬态超扩散（transient superdiffusive）区制。

Conclusion: 分子机器的活性可在无力条件下通过 Stokes 奇异产生的主动流驱动膜畴迁移，其长时间扩散行为与瞬态超扩散区制共同刻画了活性驱动的膜畴动力学特征。

Abstract: Lipid bilayer membranes are nanometer-thin fluid interfaces characterized by both in-plane fluidity and lateral compositional heterogeneity. Cholesterol-rich liquid domains ("lipid rafts") are thought to regulate the spatial organization of membrane proteins and numerous cellular processes. While the passive diffusion and equilibrium shape fluctuations of membrane domains are well understood, much less is known about how the activity of molecular machines, such as protein pumps and enzymes, influences their mobility and deformation. Here, we analyze the dynamics of a membrane domain driven by an embedded molecular machine within the framework of two-dimensional Stokes flow. The molecular machine is modeled as a point singularity of the two-dimensional Stokes equations, including both rotational and irrotational singularities corresponding to force and source multipoles. We show that, despite being force-free, both force and source dipoles generate active flows that propel the membrane domain. For a force dipole undergoing rotational diffusion, the domain motion is diffusive at long times but exhibits a transient superdiffusive regime at intermediate times.

</details>
