### [133] [Where electrons conduct and where to change it: gauge-invariant sensitivities and the networks of sensitive bonds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25104)
*D A Drabold,C Ugwumadu*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 该文区分了"电流在无序固体中由何处承载"与"局域改动在何处改变电导"两个问题，指出前者可用局域输运图刻画，而后者应由Kubo-Greenwood电导对哈密顿量的导数（规范不变）精确描述，二者在非晶硅中表现显著不同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有局域电导分配方法（如空间投影电导、KuboMap、局域态密度）回答的是"电流在哪里"，但实际问题常是"何处改动最影响电导"，二者被默认等同却缺乏验证，文中旨在厘清并精确求解后者。

Method: 解析证明Kubo-Greenwood电导对哈密顿量的导数具有规范不变性（gauge invariant），可刻画对任意局域改动的响应，且适用于任意哈密顿量；随后在非晶硅（amorphous silicon）上以紧束缚（tight-binding，至多10000原子）与密度泛函（density-functional，至多512原子）胞进行检验，并与空间投影电导（SPC）、KuboMap及局域态密度（LDOS）三种分配方式对比。

Result: KuboMap与LDOS高度一致（Spearman相关中位数0.97），而灵敏度排序不同：对格点在位移动响应排序准确，对移除原子排序较差，跨哈密顿量迁移性弱于其他方法；最敏感键构成连通团簇，在三个大网络中表现为价带尾的24–38原子类树枝细丝与带隙更深处含环网络，相对LDOS加权随机选择超额达48个标准差，仅在展宽约覆盖五个能级间距时出现。

Conclusion: 细丝与承载其权重最大的带尾本征态及一条主导导电通道显著重叠，但无单一态或通道可解释；团簇在300 K热扰动快照中基本消失。结论是电导承载位置与响应位置属不同问题，局域输运图对前者有用、对后者作用有限。

Abstract: Where is the electrical current carried in a disordered solid, and where would a local change alter it? The two questions differ. We answer the second exactly: the derivative of the Kubo-Greenwood conductivity with respect to the Hamiltonian is gauge invariant and gives the response to any local change. The construction applies to any Hamiltonian. We try it on amorphous silicon, with tight-binding cells of up to 10000 atoms and density-functional cells of up to 512 atoms, and compare it with three ways of allocating the conductivity: the space-projected conductivity (SPC), KuboMap, and the local density of states (LDOS). KuboMap follows the LDOS closely (median Spearman correlation 0.97). The sensitivity does not. It ranks the response to an on-site shift accurately and the response to removing an atom poorly, and it transfers between Hamiltonians less well than the other maps. The most sensitive bonds form connected clusters. In three large networks they are tree-like filaments of 24 to 38 atoms in the valence tail and looped networks deeper in the gap; the excess over an LDOS-weighted random selection reaches 48 standard deviations and appears only when the broadening spans about five level spacings. The filament overlaps the tail eigenstate with the most weight on it, and one of the leading conduction channels, far more than a random cluster does, but no single state or channel explains it. The networks are largely lost in thermal snapshots at 300 K. Where the conductivity is carried and where it responds are different problems: local transport maps are useful for the first and limited for the second.

</details>


### [134] [Three-mode chimera-like states in a minimal network of active camphor ribbons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26039)
*Ratan Sarkar,Sapna Yadav,Punit Parmananda,Charo I. del Genio,Stefano Boccaletti*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 该研究在四个相同樟脑驱动椭圆转子（camphor-infused elliptical rotors）的水面实验体系中，观测到同步、反同步与非相干三种动力学行为共存的三模态类奇美拉态（three-mode chimera-like state），验证了反应-扩散模型中三域奇美拉的理论预言。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 奇美拉态（chimera states）是相同单元自发分裂为相干与非相干群体的对称破缺现象，此前相关研究多为理论工作，三域奇美拉在反应-扩散模型中的预言尚缺乏实验验证。

Method: 构建四个相同的樟脑浸润椭圆转子（camphor-infused elliptical rotors），各置于方形底座的一角；在水面上由 Marangoni 驱动流（Marangoni-driven flows）自推进并旋转，并通过共享水界面介导的化学耦合（chemical coupling）实现单元间相互作用。

Result: 实验观察到三种不同的集体动力学状态：同步态（synchronized）、反同步态（antisynchronized）与非相干态（incoherent），三者共存构成三模态类奇美拉态。

Conclusion: 实验证实了近期关于反应-扩散模型（reaction-diffusion models）中三域奇美拉（three-domain chimeras）的理论工作，为非线性动力学（nonlinear dynamics）提供新见解，并提示该类奇美拉可能在广泛的物理系统中被观测到。

Abstract: Chimera states are a remarkable manifestation of symmetry breaking, wherein identical units spontaneously split into coherent and incoherent groups. The term "chimera" itself is inspired by the mythological creature having parts of different animals, reflecting the coexistence of distinct dynamical behaviors within a single system. Here, we experimentally demonstrate the existence of a three-mode chimera-like state in a system of four identical camphor-infused elliptical rotors, each pivoted at a corner of a square base. When placed on a water surface, the rotors self-propel and rotate due to Marangoni-driven flows. Chemical coupling mediated through the shared water interface gives rise to three distinct collective dynamical states, namely a synchronized one, an antisynchronized one and an incoherent one. Our results validate recent theoretical work about three-domain chimeras in reaction-diffusion models, providing new insights into nonlinear dynamics and suggesting that this new type of chimera may be observed in a broad class of physical systems.

</details>


### [135] [Onset of subdiffusion in dephased quasiperiodic lattices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26082)
*Ashlin V. Thomas,Sanchayan Banerjee,Tapan Mishra*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 该论文研究退相干（dephasing）在具有奇异在位势（onsite potentials）或消失跃迁（vanishing hoppings）的准周期晶格（quasiperiodic lattices）中的输运行为，发现当晶格"平均电阻"（mean resistance）发散时可出现亚扩散（subdiffusive）输运，并在扩散到亚扩散的转变起点处形成"亚扩散沟槽"（subdiffusive trench）。输运指数具有普适性，仅由准周期调制的奇异性阶数决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通常认为退相干会因相位相干性（phase coherence）丧失而在准周期晶格中诱导扩散。然而，对于含奇异在位势或消失跃迁、本应平凡局域化的晶格，这一预期可能严重失效，作者由此探究此类体系中退相干下的真实输运性质。

Method: 采用解析分析与数值计算相结合的方法，考察准周期调制的强度与奇异性阶数对输运的影响，并以精确的 Lindblad 模拟（Lindblad simulations）补充验证，同时提出可能的实验实现途径。

Result: 在"平均电阻"（mean resistance）发散的条件下，此类晶格在适当条件下呈现亚扩散输运，并随准周期调制强度出现从扩散到亚扩散的转变（transition from diffusive to subdiffusive transport）。输运最慢的位置并非亚扩散区深处，而是恰在转变起点处，形成尖锐的"亚扩散沟槽"（subdiffusive trench）；输运指数（transport exponents）具有普适性，仅由对角（在位）与非对角（跃迁）准周期调制的奇异性（零点）阶数决定。

Conclusion: 退相干在含奇异在位势或消失跃迁的准周期晶格中可产生非平凡的亚扩散输运，其普适指数由调制奇异性的阶数唯一确定；精确 Lindblad 模拟支持该结论，并给出了实验实现该类输运的可能方案。

Abstract: Dephasing is widely expected to induce diffusion in quasiperiodic lattices due to the loss of phase coherence. However, this expectation can fail dramatically in lattices possessing singular onsite potentials or vanishing hoppings which are expected to trivially localize the particle. Remarkably, we show that the transport in such lattices can be subdiffusive under proper conditions for which the "mean resistance" of the lattice diverges. As a result, we obtain a transition from diffusive to subdiffusive transport depending on the strength of quasiperiodic modulation. Even more strikingly, the transport is slowest not deep in the subdiffusive regime but precisely at its onset, forming a sharp "subdiffusive trench". Our analytical and numerical analysis reveal that the transport exponents are universal, fixed solely by the order of singularities (zeros) of the diagonal (off-diagonal) quasiperiodic modulation. In the end, we complement our findings through exact Lindblad simulations and provide possible experimental routes to realize such non-trivial transport.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.stat-mech'></div>

# cond-mat.stat-mech [[Back]](#toc)

### [136] [Dynamical Variational Theory of Stochastic Reaction Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25491)
*Moshir Harsh,Peter Sollich*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于Plefka展开的动力学变分近似方法，为离散随机种群动力学（含化学反应网络、基因调控、传染病与生态等）提供可解析处理的非马尔可夫有效动力学与自洽记忆，并预测均值、关联、响应函数及稳态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散随机种群动力学中有限种群产生强烈内禀涨落，而化学主方程（chemical master equation）无通用解析解、数值求解随体系规模扩展性差、标准近似在低拷贝数下失效，故需新的通用近似框架。

Method: 借助反应动力学的场论表示（field-theoretic representation），基于自旋玻璃物理中的Plefka展开（Plefka expansion）构建随机反应网络的动力学变分近似；对一般化学计量与时变速率得到带自洽记忆的可处理非马尔可夫有效动力学，并引入广义自洽气泡重求和（bubble resummation）给出均值、关联与响应函数的封闭预测。

Result: 在随机基因调控、酶动力学与感染动力学等基准系统中，该方法准确刻画了时间依赖行为与稳态，并捕捉到传统近似遗漏的涨落驱动效应（fluctuation-driven effects）。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了适用于强涨落低拷贝数系统、覆盖瞬态与稳态区间的随机反应动力学通用解析框架，并提供支持用户自定义系统、含自适应时间步长（adaptive time stepping）的数值实现。

Abstract: Discrete stochastic population dynamics describe systems ranging from biochemical reaction networks and gene regulation to epidemic spreading, ecology, and population genetics, where finite populations generate strong intrinsic fluctuations. Predicting their dynamics, including mean populations, fluctuations, time-lagged correlations, response functions, and steady states, remains challenging: the chemical master equation admits no general analytical solution, numerical solutions scale poorly with system size, and standard approximations fail at low copy numbers. Using a field-theoretic representation of the reaction dynamics, we develop a dynamical variational approximation for stochastic reaction networks based on the Plefka expansion from spin-glass physics. For reaction systems with general stoichiometry and time-dependent rates, the expansion yields tractable non-Markovian effective dynamics with self-consistent memory. We further introduce a generalized self-consistent bubble resummation that provides closed predictions for means, correlations, and response functions. The formulation applies broadly to discrete stochastic population dynamics on networks, and we provide a numerical implementation with adaptive time stepping for user-defined systems. We benchmark the method on stochastic gene regulation, enzyme kinetics, and infection dynamics, where it accurately captures both time-dependent behaviour and steady states, including fluctuation-driven effects missed by conventional approximations. Together, these results establish a general analytical framework for stochastic reaction dynamics across transient and stationary regimes in strongly fluctuating low-copy-number systems.

</details>


### [137] [Critical droplets and metastability in long-range chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25764)
*Laura Batini,Valerio Pagni,Nicolò Defenu*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究一维长程（long-range）伊辛链中一级相变（first-order phase transition）的亚稳衰变（metastable decay）与临界液滴（critical droplet）成核，发现液滴表面能随尺寸变化，临界半径按 R_c∝h^{-1/σ} 标度，且 σ=1 区分三类激活势垒行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在一维最近邻伊辛链中，液滴两侧壁面的能量代价与其尺寸无关；而实际长程相互作用可能改变这一图像，因此需要考察表面代价与体自由能增益之间的竞争如何重塑成核尺度与激活势垒。

Method: 针对按 1/r^{1+σ} 衰减的相互作用分析液滴表面代价，推导临界半径与势垒随偏置场 h 的标度；并借助非局域（nonlocal）φ^4 场论与随机长程动力学（stochastic long-range dynamics）进行验证。

Result: 小 h 下临界半径满足 R_c∝h^{-1/σ}；势垒行为随 σ 分三类：σ<1 时在 h→0^+ 发散，σ=1 时呈对数增长，σ>1 时保持有限（与最近邻情形一致）。非局域 φ^4 场论给出带代数尾（algebraic tails）的临界液滴并证实半径与势垒标度，随机长程动力学独立复现临界半径标度。

Conclusion: 长程相互作用使液滴表面代价依赖于尺寸，并以 σ=1 为分界改变激活势垒的发散性质；场论与随机动力学两种独立途径一致支持所预测的标度关系。

Abstract: Metastable decay in first-order phase transitions proceeds through the nucleation of critical droplets. In the one-dimensional nearest-neighbor Ising chain, the cost of the two walls bounding a droplet is independent of its size. For interactions decaying as $1/r^{1+σ}$, this surface cost instead depends on the droplet size and competes with the bulk free-energy gain. The critical radius scales as $R_c\propto h^{-1/σ}$ at small $h$, where $h>0$ is the bias field. The value $σ=1$ separates two regimes: for $σ<1$, the activation barrier diverges as $h\to0^+$; at $σ=1$, it grows logarithmically; and for $σ>1$, it remains finite, as in the nearest-neighbor chain. A nonlocal $φ^4$ field theory yields critical droplets with algebraic tails and confirms the predicted radius and barrier scalings, while stochastic long-range dynamics independently reproduces the critical-radius scaling.

</details>


### [138] [Field theory and the biquadratic spin-1 chain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26610)
*Luisa Eck,Paul Fendley*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 反铁磁双二次自旋-1链具有能隙（gap）、SU(3)对称性与可积性（integrability），其低能物理通常被认为由θ=π的ℂP²非线性sigma模型描述。作者用Bethe ansatz表明低能激发可解释为去禁闭畴壁（deconfined domain walls），并指出"格点可积而场论不可积"的矛盾，提出一个保持可积性但非洛伦兹不变（non-Lorentz invariant）的低能有效场论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准论证认为该自旋链低能物理由ℂP²非线性sigma模型（θ=π）描述，但格点模型可积（integrable）而该场论一般认为不可积，这一矛盾构成研究动机。

Method: 采用Bethe ansatz分析低能激发；通过在链上加入更高守恒荷（higher conserved charge）并将链调至临界（criticality），构造保持可积性的标度极限（scaling limit）。

Result: 低能激发可解释为去禁闭畴壁（deconfined domain walls），与sigma模型一致；能隙以二次方式闭合（gap closes quadratically），故极限应由某种非相对论场论（nonrelativistic field theory）而非相对论性sigma模型描述；论文给出了该场论的两粒子散射矩阵（two-particle scattering matrix）。

Conclusion: 作者提出一个具有相同低能极限但非洛伦兹不变的可积场论，作为对该可积性矛盾的解答，并以此替代相对论性ℂP² sigma模型作为低能有效描述。

Abstract: The antiferromagnetic biquadratic spin-1 chain is gapped, $SU(3)$ symmetric, and integrable. Standard arguments suggest that its low-energy physics is described by the $\mathbb{CP}^2$ nonlinear sigma model at $θ=π$. Using the Bethe ansatz, we show that its low-energy excitations can be interpreted as deconfined domain walls, just as in the sigma model. However, the lattice model is integrable, whereas the field theory is generally believed not to be. We propose a solution to this mystery by finding an integrable field theory with the same low-energy limit but which is not Lorentz invariant. The integrability-preserving scaling limit is obtained by adding a higher conserved charge and tuning the chain to criticality. The gap closes quadratically, so that the limit must be described by some nonrelativistic field theory rather than the relativistic sigma model. We describe the two-particle scattering matrix of this field theory.

</details>


### [139] [Chasing the KPZ exponents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26166)
*Luciano C. Lapas,Márcio S. Gomes-Filho,Fernando A. Oliveira*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该展望（Perspective）文章指出，非平衡标度不变性的范式——Kardar–Parisi–Zhang（KPZ）方程虽在一维被精确求解，但在高于一维时连一个精确指数都未知、上临界维是否存在仍有争议；作者主张无需等待精确解，可将非线性项视为作用于分形子集上的有效噪声，从而给出粗糙度指数（roughness exponent）的界 $1/(d+1)\le\alpha\le 1/d$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: KPZ 方程提出四十年后，一维情形已被精确求解并由精密实验验证，其标度行为也在二维极化激元凝聚体（polariton condensates）中观测到；但在一维以上，尚无任何指数被精确确定，甚至上临界维（upper critical dimension）是否存在仍有争议。作者因此希望说明，稳健（firm）的结论不必依赖精确解。

Method: 采用展望（Perspective）式的论证：将方程的非线性（nonlinearity）视为一种作用于基底分形子集（fractal subset of the substrate）上的有效噪声（effective noise），据此推导粗糙度指数的上下界；并系统检视该推导所依赖的假设。

Result: 在 $d$ 维下得到粗糙度指数的双边界 $1/(d+1)\le\alpha\le 1/d$；作者将该结果与四十年来理论、数值模拟（simulations）及新实验进行对照检验。

Conclusion: 作者提出应以三维及更高维为首要检验对象，明确能够证实或否证这些界的判据性测试（tests）；即界的成立与否可通过具体实验与数值检验来裁定，而非等待精确解。

Abstract: Forty years after it was written down, the Kardar--Parisi--Zhang (KPZ) equation is the paradigm of scale invariance far from equilibrium. In one dimension it has been solved exactly and confirmed by precision experiments, and its scaling has now been observed in two-dimensional polariton condensates. Above one dimension, however, not a single exponent is known exactly, and even the existence of an upper critical dimension is disputed. In this Perspective we argue that firm statements need not wait for exact solutions. Treating the nonlinearity as an effective noise that acts on a fractal subset of the substrate leads to bounds on the roughness exponent, $1/(d+1)\leα\le 1/d$ in $d$ dimensions. We examine the assumptions behind these bounds, confront them with four decades of theory, simulations and the new experiments, and identify the tests---foremost in three and more dimensions---that would establish or refute them.

</details>


### [140] [Wigner-Dyson correlations in selected sectors of an integrable spin chain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26263)
*Balázs Pozsgay*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文给出一个可积自旋链（integrable spin chain）特例：开边界下具有均匀的指数简并，周期边界下该简并被劈裂，且少数由全局对称性选出的扇区呈现通常标志量子混沌的Wigner-Dyson谱统计（Wigner-Dyson spectral statistics）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子多体物理（quantum many-body physics）中，谱统计常被用作区分可积模型与混沌模型的探针；本文旨在探讨这一判据在何种情形下会失效，即是否存在谱统计"像混沌"但动力学并不混沌的可积模型。

Method: 研究一个干净（clean）、平移不变（translationally invariant）且可嵌入标准Yang-Baxter框架的可积自旋链，分别在开边界与周期边界条件下分析其谱简并结构，并通过识别希尔伯特空间碎片化（Hilbert space fragmentation）来解释所观察到的谱性质。

Result: 开边界条件下模型具有均匀的指数简并（exponential degeneracies）；周期边界条件下简并被劈裂，且少数由全局对称性（global symmetry）选定的扇区显示Wigner-Dyson谱统计。碎片化是非局域的（non-local），其程度在开边界与周期边界下不同。

Conclusion: 希尔伯特空间碎片化是该谱性质的关键机制；尽管出现异常的谱关联（spectral correlations），该模型的动力学并不混沌，说明Wigner-Dyson谱统计并非量子混沌的充分判据。

Abstract: In quantum many-body physics spectral statistics is often used as a probe to distinguish integrable and chaotic models. We find a special case of an integrable spin chain with unusual spectral properties. With open boundary conditions the model has homogeneous exponential degeneracies; these are split for periodic boundary conditions. In the latter case, we find that a small number of sectors (selected by a global symmetry) showcase Wigner-Dyson spectral statistics, which usually signals quantum chaos. Our model is clean, translationally invariant, and it fits into the standard Yang-Baxter framework. We identify Hilbert space fragmentation as the key mechanism behind the spectral properties. The fragmentation is non-local, and its extent is different in the case of open and periodic boundary conditions. We argue that the dynamics of the model is not chaotic despite the unusual spectral correlations.

</details>


### [141] [Exact finite-time fluctuation-response relations for Markov processes: A martingale framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26267)
*Tan Van Vu,Van Tuan Vo,Ruicheng Bao,Keiji Saito*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于鞅（martingale）的框架，用于求取一般马尔可夫过程（Markov processes）可加可观测量的精确有限时间涨落-响应恒等式，并将涨落与耗散的关系推广到欠阻尼动力学等情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间积分可观测量的涨落（fluctuations）与响应（responses）均可实验测量，其关系可揭示维持非平衡态（nonequilibrium state）的耗散，但此前缺乏对一般马尔可夫过程成立的精确有限时间关系。

Method: 构建鞅框架：用 Doob 鞅（Doob martingale）精确表示可观测量的方差，并将均值对容许扰动的响应表示为 Doob 鞅与表示扰动的第二个鞅之间的协方差（covariance）。

Result: 得到精确涨落-响应恒等式及由其导出的不等式、变分原理（variational principles）与多变量、频域界；对扩散与跳跃过程，单一鞅幅度同时决定涨落与响应。在欠阻尼情形导出可在任意有限时间饱和的摩擦-响应热力学不确定性关系（thermodynamic uncertainty relation, TUR），并证明速度分辨流（velocity-resolved currents）可指数级强烈违反传统 TUR；同时对极限环（limit cycles）给出弱噪声耗散-相干性界。

Conclusion: 该框架统一并推广了过阻尼动力学（overdamped dynamics）与跳跃过程（jump processes）中已有的涨落-响应恒等式，并明确了相应不确定性关系饱和的条件，为熵产生（entropy production）提供了变分刻画。

Abstract: Fluctuations and responses of time-integrated observables are both experimentally measurable, and relations between them can reveal the dissipation that sustains a nonequilibrium state. Here we develop a martingale framework that gives exact finite-time fluctuation-response identities for additive observables of general Markov processes. We obtain an exact identity for the variance of an observable in terms of its Doob martingale, which splits the total variance into a part inherited from the initial ensemble and a part generated by noise during the observation. We show that, at fixed preparation, the response of the mean of the observable to any admissible perturbation is the covariance between the Doob martingale and a second martingale that represents the perturbation. For diffusions and jump processes, a single martingale amplitude determines both the fluctuations and the response of the observable. These identities yield inequalities, variational principles, and multivariate and frequency-domain bounds, whose costs are set by the corresponding physical perturbations. Applying the framework to underdamped dynamics, we obtain a friction-response thermodynamic uncertainty relation (TUR) that can be saturated at any finite time and thus gives a variational principle for the entropy production. We also demonstrate exponentially strong violations of the conventional TUR by velocity-resolved currents, even in linear Gaussian dynamics, and derive a weak-noise dissipation-coherence bound for limit cycles, which shows that inertial oscillators can beat the overdamped bound only if their frequency is weakly sensitive to friction. For overdamped dynamics and jump processes, the framework recovers and extends recent fluctuation-response identities and identifies when the corresponding uncertainty relations are saturated.

</details>


### [142] [First-passage and correlated reset-count statistics under threshold and Poissonian resetting in a two-dimensional wedge geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26345)
*Fazil Najeeb,Arnab Pal,V. V Prasad*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该论文研究二维开放楔形区域（apex angle α）中布朗靶搜索问题里阈值重置（threshold resetting, TR）与泊松重置（Poissonian resetting）的相互作用，通过更新框架（renewal framework）导出首达时间（FPT）精确矩与联合计数统计，并揭示两种重置机制的竞争规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多关注单一重置机制，而对阈值重置与泊松重置并存时的竞争效应缺乏系统刻画，尤其缺乏对二者如何共同影响搜索效率及相关性的定量理解。

Method: 在二维开放楔形（apex angle α）中，零角边界为吸收靶，α 边界触发阈值重置；对单独 TR 采用 Biswas 等的更新框架（renewal framework）求 FPT 精确一、二阶矩，并发展基于随机变量的通用更新形式（random-variable-based renewal formalism）推导联合平均首达时间（MFPT），进而计算两种重置的联合计数统计与皮尔逊相关系数（Pearson's correlation coefficient）。

Result: 单独 TR 下 FPT 密度呈现代数长时尾 t^{-1-π/(2α)}；对 0<α<π/4，TR 动力学的变异系数（coefficient of variation）决定泊松重置加速还是阻碍搜索，而 α≥π/4 时泊松重置总降低 MFPT；联合计数统计显示随泊松重置率变化出现由弱相关到强相关的交叉（crossover），且该交叉行为对初始搜索位置有非平凡依赖。

Conclusion: 研究给出了竞争性重置机制在一般几何中的统一分析基础，表明变异系数与楔形夹角共同决定搜索效率，且两种重置机制的相关性存在依赖初始位置的非平凡交叉。

Abstract: We study the interplay between threshold resetting (TR) and Poissonian resetting in a Brownian target search problem confined to a two-dimensional open wedge of apex angle $α$. The boundary at zero angle acts as an absorbing target, while encounters with the boundary at $α$ trigger TR, instantaneously restarting the search from its initial position. For TR alone, using the renewal framework developed by Biswas et al. in Phys. Rev. Lett. 135, 227101 (2025), we obtain exact first and second moments of the first-passage time (FPT), whose density exhibits an algebraic long-time tail $t^{-1-\fracπ{2α}}$. To combine the two resetting mechanisms, we thereafter develop a generic random-variable-based renewal formalism to derive the combined mean FPT. We show that the coefficient of variation of the underlying TR dynamics determines whether Poissonian resetting accelerates or hinders the search for $0<α<π/4$, whereas for $α\geqπ/4$, Poissonian resetting always reduces the MFPT. Furthermore, we derive the joint count statistics of the two resetting mechanisms and characterize their mutual dependence through Pearson's correlation coefficient. Interestingly, we uncover a crossover from weak to strong correlations as the Poissonian resetting rate is varied, with the crossover behavior exhibiting a nontrivial dependence on the initial search position. Our framework provides a basis for studying competing resetting mechanisms in more general geometries.

</details>


### [143] [Imaginary-Time Density Correlations Encode Quantum Entropy and Free Energy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26381)
*Sagi Meir*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该文指出经典的最大熵推断（maximum-entropy inference）由静态结构因子（static structure factor）经单一动量求和估计过剩熵（excess entropy）在量子情形下失效，并证明虚时密度关联（imaginary-time density correlations）可承载所需信息，使经典关系式延续至量子系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子体系中相互作用同时改变动能，且离域化（delocalization）与交换效应（exchange）提供了等时关联（equal-time correlations）所不含的信息，因此经典的最大熵估计在量子情形下不成立，需要寻找新的信息来源与推断框架。

Method: 对虚时路径（imaginary-time paths）做相对熵推断（relative-entropy inference），以理想玻色气体或费米气体（ideal Bose or Fermi gas）为参考态，在与经典关系相同的高斯近似（Gaussian approximation）下导出过剩熵与自由能的闭式表达式；所需输入仅为单一状态点上的虚时密度关联及其理想气体参考，数据可由路径积分模拟（path-integral simulations）与X射线汤姆孙散射（X-ray Thomson scattering）获得。

Result: 所得闭式表达式可退化为经典结果，并能估计经典关系所遗漏的熵贡献；在弱相互作用玻色子、环上相互作用费米子以及温稠密电子气（warm dense electron gas）三类体系上进行了检验，且无需使用相互作用势（interaction potential）。

Conclusion: 虚时密度关联足以将经典熵估计关系推广到量子系统，该方法有望把其他经典熵估计器（classical entropy estimators）拓展至量子体系。

Abstract: In the classical limit, maximum-entropy inference yields an estimate of the excess entropy from the static structure factor through a single momentum sum. Quantum mechanically this estimate fails, because interactions also change the kinetic energy, while delocalization and exchange add information that equal-time correlations do not contain. We show that imaginary-time density correlations contain this information, and that with them the classical relation carries over to quantum systems. Such correlations are available from path-integral simulations and X-ray Thomson scattering. Relative-entropy inference over imaginary-time paths, with an ideal Bose or Fermi gas as reference, gives closed forms for the excess entropy and free energies, within the same Gaussian approximation as the classical relation. They recover the classical result and estimate entropy that the classical relation misses. We test them on weakly interacting bosons, interacting fermions on a ring, and the warm dense electron gas. The closed forms need only imaginary-time density correlations and their ideal-gas reference at a single state point, without using the interaction potential. Our approach can extend other classical entropy estimators to quantum systems.

</details>


### [144] [Nonequilibrium Statistical Mechanics: Physical Concepts & Mathematical Tools](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26394)
*Sagar Chakraborty*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 这是一份面向复杂系统（complex systems）的经典非平衡统计力学（nonequilibrium statistical mechanics）入门讲义的摘要，概览若干基础概念与技巧，并强调其在物理学之外（生物、生态、经济、社会学）的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非平衡范畴缺乏公认的完整主题清单，但非平衡统计力学已超越传统物理学，成为研究复杂系统的通用语言（language for complex systems），因此有必要对其入门概念与技巧作梳理。

Method: 采用讲义（lecture notes）形式，对经典非平衡统计力学中的若干入门性概念与技术进行概述式阐述；材料的组织与呈现以作者本人综合为主，并假定读者具备平衡统计力学（equilibrium statistical mechanics）的基础。

Result: 摘要仅说明这些概念与技巧在基础物理以及生物学、生态学、经济学、社会学等领域获得成功应用，未给出具体结论性成果或定量结果。

Conclusion: 作者声明除材料的特定综合与呈现外不主张原创性（claims no originality），并将本讲义定位为出于对复杂系统好奇心的初步性（cursory）导引。

Abstract: As anything not in equilibrium is, by definition, nonequilibrium, there is no universally agreed-upon set of topics that can constitute an exhaustive presentation of nonequilibrium statistical mechanics. At the same time, nonequilibrium statistical mechanics has transcended traditional physics to become a powerful language for complex systems. Prompted by an enthusiastic curiosity about complex systems, these cursory lecture notes attempt to elucidate some introductory concepts and techniques used in classical nonequilibrium statistical mechanics, which find successful applications not only in foundational physics, but also across other fields such as biology, ecology, economics, and sociology. Except for the specific synthesis and presentation of the material, which assumes basic familiarity with equilibrium statistical mechanics, the author claims no originality.

</details>


### [145] [Exact fermionic decay length and Majorana localization in the sign-alternating kagomé Ising model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26529)
*Shahane Khachatryan,Hrachya Babujian,Ara SEdrakyan*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该文针对固定横向动量 $p=\pi/2$ 下的符号交替 kagomé Ising 模型（sign-alternating kagomé Ising model），推导出精确的费米子衰减长度（fermionic decay length），并揭示其与横场 Ising 链（transverse-field Ising chain）能隙闭合及 Majorana 端模局域长度（localization length）的对应关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于将经典统计模型的衰减长度（decay length）与量子临界、拓扑性质联系起来，并厘清符号交替 kagomé Ising 模型在固定动量和标度极限下的临界分支结构及 Yang–Baxter 可解族的延拓性。

Method: 通过定义三个无量纲耦合 $x=\tanh K_1$、$y=\tanh K_2$、$z=\tanh K_3$，给出每晶胞逆衰减长度的精确闭式 $\xi_{\rm f}^{-1}=|\ln(U/|V|)|$，并结合行列转移矩阵（row transfer matrix）与 Jordan–Wigner 费米子化分析其临界与拓扑极限。

Result: 衰减长度在 $U=|V|$ 处发散，且 $V$ 的符号决定两条经典临界分支（classical critical branches）；在强弱键同时标度下，这两支分别对应所得横场 Ising 链在动量 $0$ 与 $\pi$ 处的能隙闭合条件（gap-closing conditions）。经典衰减长度在拓扑层面趋于 Jordan–Wigner 费米子链 Majorana 端模的局域长度。

Conclusion: 此外证明，在仅有一个键方向消失的固定基矢拟设（fixed-basis ansatz）下，正则强键 Yang–Baxter 族（regular strong-bond Yang–Baxter family）无法延拓至有限耦合内部区域。

Abstract: We derive an exact fermionic decay length for the sign-alternating kagomé Ising model at fixed transverse momentum $p=π/2$. Writing $x=\tanh K_1$, $y=\tanh K_2$, and $z=\tanh K_3$ for the three dimensionless couplings, the inverse length per lattice cell is $ξ_{\rm f}^{-1}=|\ln(U/|V|)|$, where $U=(1-x^2)(1+y^2z^2)$ and $V=(1+x^2)(y^2-z^2)$. The length diverges at $U=|V|$, with the sign of $V$ selecting one of two classical critical branches. In a simultaneous strong- and weak-bond scaling, these branches become the gap-closing conditions at momenta $0$ and $π$ of the transverse-field Ising chain obtained from the row transfer matrix. On the topological side, the classical decay length tends to the localization length of a Majorana end mode of the Jordan--Wigner fermion chain. We also prove that the regular strong-bond Yang--Baxter family cannot be continued to the finite-coupling interior within the fixed-basis ansatz with one vanishing bond direction.

</details>


<div id='math.DS'></div>

# math.DS [[Back]](#toc)

### [146] [Moduli of continuity preserved by the Hilbert Transform](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25275)
*Pedro Duarte,Tomé Graxinha*

Main category: math.DS

TL;DR: 本文推广了Avila–Last–Shamis–Zhou关于紧支撑t-对数Hölder连续函数的Hilbert变换具有至少(t-1)-对数Hölder连续性的结果，将其扩展至满足两个温和可积性条件的一类连续函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Goldstein–Schlag给出的模连续量在Hilbert变换下保持的双条件判据不适用于弱Hölder与对数Hölder模连续量，而近期对t-对数Hölder情形已有结果，本文旨在将这些结果推向更广的函数类。

Method: 在一个刻画连续函数的框架下（仅采用摘要所述“两个温和可积性条件（mild integrability conditions）”），将t-对数Hölder与弱Hölder情形的既有论证推广到该函数类。

Result: 摘要指出本文把Avila–Last–Shamis–Zhou的紧支撑t-对数Hölder连续函数（t&gt;1）结论及其类似的弱Hölder结论，扩展到满足两个温和可积性条件的一类连续函数。

Conclusion: 摘要显示，该注记在一类更广的连续函数上建立了Hilbert变换的模连续量（modulus of continuity）型结论，覆盖t-对数Hölder与弱Hölder情形。

Abstract: It has long been known that the Hilbert transform preserves certain moduli of continuity such as Hölder continuity. In~\cite{GS01}, M. Goldstein and W. Schlag provided a criterion, based on two simple conditions, for a modulus of continuity to be preserved under the Hilbert transform. These conditions are not satisfied by the weak-Hölder and the log-Hölder moduli of continuity. More recently, A. Avila, Y. Last, M. Shamis and Q. Zhou proved in~\cite{ALSZ21} that for compactly supported $t$-log-Hölder continuous functions ($t>1$) their Hilbert transforms are at least $(t-1)$-log-Hölder continuous. A similar result holds for weak-Hölder continuous functions. In this note we extend these results to a class of continuous functions satisfying a couple of mild integrability conditions.

</details>

### [147] [Elliptic analogues of KP and BKP hierarchies and their tau functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26354)
*Changyan Shi,Bao-Feng Feng*

Main category: nlin.SI

TL;DR: 本文为与Kadomtsev–Petviashvili（KP）族及其B型对应（BKP）相关的椭圆背景孤立子构造了一个tau函数（tau-function）框架，涵盖离散与连续两个层面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在为椭圆背景上的孤立子提供统一处理途径，将椭圆tau函数、离散/连续双线性恒等式及约化求解纳入同一框架。

Method: 从椭圆型离散KP（dKP）与离散BKP（dBKP）方程出发，借助Darboux变换与binary Darboux变换构造Casorati与Gram行列式tau函数，用Moutard变换构造椭圆Pfaffian tau函数；再经Pfaffian恒等式推广至高阶离散族，并通过Miwa变换得到连续椭圆KP、modified KP与BKP族。

Result: 得到椭圆背景上KdV、聚焦与非聚焦mKdV及Sawada–Kotera（SK）方程的解，并构造KP线孤立子解与KP-I方程的lump解；对若干行列式族给出实值与全局非奇异的充分条件。

Conclusion: 所建立框架为椭圆tau函数、其离散与连续双线性恒等式以及经由约化的解构造提供共同方法。

Abstract: We develop a tau-function framework for soliton solutions on elliptic backgrounds associated with the Kadomtsev--Petviashvili (KP) hierarchy and its B-type counterpart (BKP). Starting from discrete KP (dKP) and discrete BKP (dBKP) equations of elliptic type, we construct Casorati and Gram determinant tau functions for the elliptic dKP through Darboux and binary Darboux transformations, and elliptic Pfaffian tau functions for dBKP through the Moutard transformation. Moreover, the higher-order discrete KP and BKP hierarchies together with their tau functions are constructed via Pfaffian identities. Then, through Miwa transformations, the continuous elliptic KP, modified KP, and BKP hierarchies and their tau functions are provided. Suitable constraints on spectral parameters or wave numbers yield reductions to the Korteweg--de Vries (KdV), focusing and defocusing modified Korteweg--de Vries (mKdV), and Sawada--Kotera (SK) equations, together with their solutions on elliptic backgrounds. We also construct KP line-soliton solutions and obtain lump solutions for the KP-I equation. For selected determinant families, we establish sufficient conditions for real-valuedness and global nonsingularity. The resulting framework provides a common approach to elliptic tau functions, their discrete and continuous bilinear identities, and the construction of solutions through reductions.

</details>

Wei,T. Wester,A. J. White,A. Wilkinson,P. Wilson,T. Wongjirad,E. Worcester,M. Worcester,S. Yadav,E. Yandel,L. Yates,S. Yebes,B. Yu,H. Yu,J. Yu,B. Zamorano,J. Zennamo,C. Zhang*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: SBND 探测器在 Booster 中微子束流上给出了迄今统计量最高的氩靶 $\\nu_e+\\bar\\nu_e$ 带电流截面测量，总截面为 $[3.90\\pm0.18(\\text{stat})\\pm0.41(\\text{syst})]\\times10^{-39}$ cm$^2$/核子，并显示数据偏好整体截面较低的生成器模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电子中微子（electron neutrino）带电流（charged-current）截面的直接测量对长基线与短基线中微子振荡中的 $\\nu_e$ 出现（appearance）研究至关重要，是检验中微子唯象学开放问题及在强度前沿寻找超出标准模型（beyond the Standard Model）物理的基础输入。

Method: 利用费米实验室短基线近探测器（Short-Baseline Near Detector, SBND）在 Booster 中微子束流中采集的 $8.81\\times10^{19}$ 质子打靶数（protons on target）数据，以电子能量下限 500 MeV 筛选出 729 个候选事例，提取总截面及按电子能量与相对束轴角度划分的单微分截面。

Result: 测得通量平均总截面为 $\\sigma= [3.90 \\pm 0.18\\,(\\text{stat}) \\pm 0.41\\,(\\text{syst})] \\times 10^{-39}$ cm$^2$/核子，对应所选事例的预测平均中微子能量 $\\langle E_\\nu\\rangle=1.8$ GeV；该测量为目前氩靶上统计量最高的包容性（inclusive）$\\nu_e+\\bar\\nu_e$ 带电流截面结果，也是首次基于 Booster 中微子束流的同类测量。

Conclusion: 总截面与微分截面同常用中微子事例生成器（neutrino event generator）的预言进行比较，数据显示出对整体截面预言偏低的生成器模型的偏好。

Abstract: This work presents measurements of the total and single-differential electron neutrino charged-current cross sections on argon using the Short-Baseline Near Detector (SBND) at Fermilab. We present the highest statistics inclusive $ν_e + \bar ν_e$ (95.2% $ν_e$ and 4.8% $\barν_e$) charged-current cross section measurement on argon to date and the first inclusive $ν_e + \bar ν_e$ cross section measurement using the Booster Neutrino Beam. Direct measurements of this channel are crucial for future studies of both long- and short-baseline neutrino oscillations through electron neutrino appearance, which in turn are vital for answering open questions in neutrino phenomenology and searching for beyond the Standard Model physics at the intensity frontier. The data used for this work were collected with an exposure of $8.81 \times10^{19}$ protons on target, and 729 candidate events are selected with a minimum electron energy of 500 MeV. The single-differential cross sections are extracted in electron energy and angle with respect to the beam axis. The flux-averaged total cross section is measured to be $σ= [3.90 \pm 0.18\,\text{(stat)} \pm 0.41\,\text{(syst)}] \times 10^{-39}$ cm$^2$/nucleon for a predicted average neutrino energy of $\langle E_ν\rangle=1.8$ GeV for selected events. The total and differential cross sections are compared to predictions from common neutrino event generators. The data show a preference for generators that predict a lower overall cross section.

</details>


### [149] [Charm and beauty production in pp collisions with ALICE](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26017)
*Jianhui Zhu*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本贡献综述ALICE在LHC Run 3中对质子-质子（proton--proton, pp）碰撞中粲（charm）与底（beauty）夸克产生的最新测量，涵盖D介子与B介子横动量微分截面、快度依赖、重味强子化（hadronisation）产额比及来自底强子衰变的J/ψ与ψ(2S)份额，并与理论计算比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: pp碰撞中重味夸克产生可为微扰量子色动力学（perturbative QCD, pQCD）提供关键检验，并独特地敏感于重夸克强子化机制，因而需要精确的实验测量以约束理论与模型。

Method: 基于ALICE探测器在LHC Run 3的测量，报告√s=5.36与13.6 TeV下瞬发D介子（prompt D meson）及√s=13.6 TeV下全重建B介子的横动量微分产生截面，并分析粲、底产生的快度（rapidity）依赖及D*+/D0、Λc+/D0产额比。

Result: 给出不同质心能量下的重味产生截面及其与理论计算的对比，并通过快度依赖与重味强子产额比提供对重夸克产生与强子化的互补约束；同时测量来自底强子衰变的J/ψ与ψ(2S)份额以及包容性ψ(2S)/J/ψ产生比。

Conclusion: 这些测量共同约束微扰重夸克产生与非微扰束缚态形成（bound-state formation）之间的相互作用，深化对重味产生与强子化机制的理解。

Abstract: Measurements of charm and beauty production in proton--proton (pp) collisions provide crucial tests of perturbative quantum chromodynamics (pQCD) and unique access to heavy-quark hadronisation. In this contribution, a selection of recent ALICE results, with emphasis on LHC Run 3 measurements, is presented. The transverse-momentum-differential production cross sections of prompt D mesons at $\sqrt{s}=5.36$ and 13.6 TeV and of fully reconstructed B mesons at $\sqrt{s}=13.6$ TeV are discussed together with comparisons to theoretical calculations. The rapidity dependence of charm and beauty production and the $\rm D^{*+}/D^0$ and $\rm Λ_c^+/D^0$ yield ratios provide complementary constraints on heavy-quark production and hadronisation. Measurements of the fractions of ${\rm J}/ψ$ and $ψ({\rm 2S})$ originating from beauty-hadron decays, and of the inclusive $ψ({\rm 2S})$-to-${\rm J}/ψ$ production ratio, further constrain the interplay between perturbative heavy-quark production and non-perturbative bound-state formation.

</details>

Zhu,Lin Zhu,S. H. Zhu,W. D. Zhu,W. J. Zhu,W. Z. Zhu,Y. C. Zhu,Z. A. Zhu,X. Y. Zhuang,M. Zhuge,J. H. Zou,J. Zu*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: BESIII 实验利用约 90 亿 $J/\psi$ 事例首次观测到电磁 Dalitz 衰变 $J/\psi\to \mu^+\mu^-\eta$，显著性超过 10$\sigma$，并测得分支比及其与辐射衰变的比值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验矢量介子主导模型（vector meson dominance model）对电磁 Dalitz 衰变（electromagnetic Dalitz decay）的理论预言，并补充 $J/\psi$ 衰变中轻子对末态的实验信息。

Method: 基于 BEPCII 上 BESIII 探测器收集的 $(8.998 \pm 0.039)\times10^9$ 个 $J/\psi$ 事例，通过分波或事例选择重建 $\mu^+\mu^-\eta$ 末态，测量分支比（branching fraction）并计算其与 $J/\psi\to\gamma\eta$、$J/\psi\to e^+e^-\eta$ 的比值。

Result: 观测显著性大于 10$\sigma$；分支比为 $(3.05 \pm 0.14_{\rm stat.} \pm 0.13_{\rm syst.})\times 10^{-6}$；与辐射衰变之比为 $(2.80 \pm 0.18)\times 10^{-3}$，与电子道之比为 $0.219 \pm 0.020$。

Conclusion: 测量结果与基于矢量介子主导模型的理论预言一致，支持该模型对电磁 Dalitz 衰变的描述。

Abstract: Using a data sample of $(8.998 \pm 0.039)\times10^9$ $J/ψ$ events collected by the BESIII detector at BEPCII, we report the observation of the electromagnetic Dalitz decay $J/ψ\to μ^+ μ^- η$ for the first time, with a statistical significance greater than 10$σ$. The branching fraction is measured to be $\mathcal{B}(J/ψ\to μ^+ μ^- η) = (3.05 \pm 0.14_{\rm stat.} \pm 0.13_{\rm syst.})\times 10^{-6}$. The ratio of this branching fraction to that of the corresponding radiative decay $J/ψ\to γη$ is determined to be $\mathcal{B}(J/ψ\to μ^+μ^-η)/\mathcal{B}(J/ψ\to γη) = (2.80 \pm 0.18) \times 10^{-3}$. Combining this result with the previous BESIII measurement of the $J/ψ\to e^+e^-η$ branching fraction yields $\mathcal{B}(J/ψ\to μ^+μ^-η)/\mathcal{B}(J/ψ\to e^+e^-η) = 0.219 \pm 0.020$. These results are consistent with the theoretical predictions based on the vector meson dominance model.

</details>


### [151] [A Surrogate Detector Model for the MINERvA Open Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26271)
*Sparshita Dey,Alexander Himmel,David Last,Liang Liu,Pedro Machado,Vishvas Pandey,Linyan Wan*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 该工作构建了 MINERvA 探测器响应的概率代理模型（probabilistic surrogate），将产生器级别的末态粒子与相互作用几何映射至重建观测量，从而实现开放中微子数据与理论模型之间的可复用接口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放中微子数据虽日益可得，但将理论预测映射为重建观测量（reconstructed observables）此前依赖计算昂贵且仅限原实验合作组使用的方法，构成关键障碍。

Method: 基于开放模拟数据集训练 MINERvA 探测器响应的概率代理模型，不依赖产生器特有的相互作用标签；通过条件响应分布、重建观测量及基于分类器的闭合检验（closure tests）评估保真度，并在训练中完全剔除双粒子-双空穴（two-particle-two-hole）相互作用以检验泛化能力。

Result: 代理模型在估计不确定度范围内复现了被完全排除于训练之外的双粒子-双空穴相互作用的重建响应；在 MINERvA 带电电流包容性缪子中微子数据的端到端应用中，复现了选择效率与纯度，并对 GENIE、NuWro 与 GiBUU 的预测进行前向折叠（forward-folding）以供直接比较。

Conclusion: 通过将理论预测移入重建空间而非对数据做展开（unfolding），该工作建立了中微子相互作用模型与实验观测之间的可复用接口，扩展了开放数据集可触及的物理范围。

Abstract: The growing availability of open neutrino data promises broader scrutiny of interaction models, but a critical barrier remains: mapping theoretical predictions into reconstructed observables, which previously required computationally intensive methods inaccessible outside the original experimental collaborations. In this work, we present a probabilistic surrogate of the MINERvA detector response, trained on an open simulation dataset to map generator-level final-state particles and interaction geometry to reconstructed observables, without relying on generator-specific interaction labels. We assess its fidelity through conditional response distributions, reconstructed observables, and classifier-based closure tests. We further demonstrate generalization beyond the limitations of conventional phase-space-dependent reweighting by withholding two-particle-two-hole interactions entirely from training and showing that the surrogate reproduces their reconstructed response within the estimated uncertainties. In an end-to-end application to MINERvA charged-current inclusive muon-neutrino data, we reproduce selection efficiencies and purities and forward-fold predictions from GENIE, NuWro, and GiBUU for direct comparison with measurements. By moving theoretical predictions into reconstructed space rather than unfolding the data, this work establishes a reusable interface between neutrino interaction models and experimental observations, expanding the scope of physics accessible through open datasets.

</details>

Zies,D. Zuliani,X. Zuo*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: LHCb 实验对 CKM 角 γ 与粲介子混合及 CP 破坏参数开展联合测量与全局组合，给出迄今最精确的 γ 及混合参数结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: γ 角测量与粲混合（charm mixing）、CP 破坏（CP violation）测量同依赖 D^0 介子衰变的强子参数（hadronic parameters），二者互为补充且相互关联，对标准模型（Standard Model）弱相互作用部分（weak sector）进行严格检验需要统一处理。

Method: 综述 LHCb 在相关领域的进展，并对所有相关测量进行新的同时组合（simultaneous combination），数据涵盖 2011-2018 年采集样本，并首次纳入 2024 年数据对 γ 的测量。

Result: 得到 γ = (63.2^{+2.3}_{-2.2})°（模 180°）、x_{12} = (4.2^{+0.4}_{-0.5})×10^{-3}、y_{12} = (6.18^{+0.17}_{-0.18})×10^{-3}、φ^M_2 = (4^{+11}_{-10}) mrad、φ^Γ_2 = (19^{+23}_{-24}) mrad，均为目前最精确结果。

Conclusion: 在不同理论假设下还确定了 D^0→K^+K^- 与 D^0→π^+π^- 衰变的 CP 不对称性（CP asymmetries）；该联合组合体现了两类测量的互补性并提升了精度。

Abstract: Measurements of the CKM angle $γ$ and of the charm mixing and $C\!P$-violation parameters enable stringent and complementary tests of the weak sector of the Standard Model. The two categories of measurements are intimately related, since they both depend on hadronic parameters of $D^0$ meson decays, and knowledge of charm mixing is required to determine $γ$. The progress made in these sectors at the LHCb experiment is reviewed, and a new simultaneous combination of all relevant LHCb measurements is presented. The combination includes measurements based on data collected during 2011-2018, as well as the first measurement of $γ$ with data collected in 2024. The results, $γ= (63.2^{+2.3}_{-2.2})^{\circ}$ modulo $180^{\circ}$, $x_{12} = (4.2^{+0.4}_{-0.5})\times10^{-3}$, $y_{12} = (6.18^{+0.17}_{-0.18})\times10^{-3}$, $φ^M_2 = (4^{+11}_{-10})\,\mathrm{mrad}$ and $φ^Γ_2 = (19^{+23}_{-24})\,\mathrm{mrad}$, are the most precise to date. The $CP$ asymmetries in $D^0\to K^+K^-$ and $D^0\toπ^+π^-$ decays are also determined under different theoretical assumptions.

</details>

# math.CO [[Back]](#toc)

### [153] [\textsc{Max-Cut} on Token Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25153)
*José Angel Alejandro-Soto,Paulo T. Araujo,Ruy Fabila-Monroy,Sergio Gerardo Gómez-Galicia,Mohamed Hibat-Allah,Md Moshiur Rahman Raj,Mahmud Ashraf Shamim,Ana Laura Trujillo-Negrete*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文将最大割（Max-Cut）问题推广到 token 图（token graph）$F_k(G)$ 上，建立 $m(F_k(G))$ 与 $m(G)$ 之间的界，证明 $m(F_k(G))\geq\binom{n-2}{k-1}m(G)$ 且该界在 $1+O(k/n)$ 因子内紧，并给出算法复杂性结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理学中 Heisenberg 反铁磁体（Heisenberg antiferromagnet）的相位重构（phase-reconstruction）问题可重述为 token 图上的 Max-Cut 问题，作者由此受到启发，研究 token 图上的 Max-Cut。

Method: 研究对象为边带正权图的 token 图 $F_k(G)$，以顶点 $k$-子集为顶点、对称差为原图边时相邻；主要在全边等权的设定下，用 $m(G)$ 界定 $m(F_k(G))$，并结合构造性反例与复杂性归约分析。

Result: 证明 $m(F_k(G))\geq\binom{n-2}{k-1}m(G)$，且该界在乘性因子 $1+O(k/n)$ 内紧；当 $k=c\cdot n$（$0<c<1$ 为常数）时给出该界相差一个乘性常数的例子；另对多类图 $G$ 给出 $m(F_k(G))$ 的界。

Conclusion: 求得 $m(F_k(G))$ 在常数 $k$ 时是 NP-完全的（NP-complete）；当权重取有限多个值时，给出证据表明对任意常数 $\delta>0$、$k\geq n^\delta$ 情形并非 NP-难（NP-hard）。

Abstract: Let $G$ be a graph on $n$ vertices, with a positive weight assigned to each edge. A cut of $G$ is a partition of its vertices into two sets $X$ and $Y$; the weight of $(X,Y)$ is the sum of the weight of the edges of $G$ with one endpoint in $X$ and the other endpoint in $Y$. The Max-Cut problem asks for the maximum weight, $m(G)$, of a cut of $G$. The $k$-token graph of $G$, is the graph, $F_k(G)$, whose vertices are the $k$-subsets of vertices of $G$, two of them being adjacent when their symmetric difference is an edge of $G$. The phase-reconstruction problem for Heisenberg antiferromagnets in physics can be rephrased as a Max-Cut problem on token graphs. Motivated by this, we study the Max-Cut problem on token graphs, mainly when all edges are assigned the same weight. Our main focus is to bound $m(F_k(G))$ in terms of $m(G)$. We prove that $m(F_k(G))\geq\binom{n-2}{k-1}m(G)$, and that this bound is tight up to a multiplicative factor of $1+O(k/n)$; in the case when $k=c \cdot n$ for some constant $0 < c < 1$, we provide an example where this bound is off by a multiplicative constant factor. We also show bounds for $m(F_k(G))$ for various classes of graphs $G$. Finally, we consider the algorithmic complexity of finding $m(F_k(G))$, showing it to be NP-complete for a constant $k$, and giving evidence that, when the weights take finitely many values, it is not NP-hard for $k\geq n^δ$ for any constant $δ>0$.

</details>

### [154] [Compact GridPix Tracking: Test Beam Performance and Detector Response Modelling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26308)
*Dhruv Chouhan,Jochen Kaminski,Konstantinos Spyrou,Matthias Schott*

Main category: physics.ins-det

TL;DR: 本文表征了一个漂移长度 21 mm 的 Timepix 基 GridPix 探测器，用 2.6 GeV 电子验证其作为紧凑 μTPC 单层径迹元件的角度与空间分辨能力，并提出经数据标定的经验响应模型以支撑未来紧凑径迹探测器可行性研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 紧凑型径迹探测器（compact tracking detector）需要单层即可同时提供位置与方向信息的探测单元，因此需定量表征 GridPix/Timepix 类 μTPC 元件的角分辨与空间分辨，并建立可复用的探测器响应模型。

Method: 在 ELSA 用 2.6 GeV 电子照射漂移长度 21 mm 的 Timepix 基 GridPix 探测器，将不同入射角穿过窗口的径迹重建为紧凑 μTPC 径迹元（tracklet）；并以实测数据标定经验响应模型，将其叠加到 Geant4 电离信息上，加入气体输运（gas transport）与读出（readout）效应。

Result: 测得角宽度约 5–10 mrad；标定后的响应模型在 12 个角宽度中复现 10 个（偏差约 20% 内），全部空间残差宽度在约 12% 内复现；未经重新调参直接应用于独立的 5 mm 漂移测量时，角度偏差明显增大。

Conclusion: 该经验响应模型可为未来紧凑径迹探测器的可行性研究（feasibility study）提供输入，但其向不同漂移长度条件的迁移性有限，需重新标定或扩展模型。

Abstract: We characterise a Timepix-based GridPix detector with a 21 mm drift length using 2.6 GeV electrons at ELSA. Tracks entering through the window at several angles are reconstructed as compact $μ$TPC tracklets, providing position and local direction from one layer. Measured angular widths are approximately 5--10 mrad. An empirical response model calibrated to these data augments Geant4 ionisation information with gas-transport and readout effects, reproducing ten of twelve angular widths within approximately 20% and all spatial-residual widths within approximately 12%. Application without re-tuning to an independent 5 mm-drift measurement shows larger angular discrepancies. Our response model therefore provides input to feasibility studies of future compact tracking detectors.

</details>

### [155] [Equation of state of hot and dense QCD at the physical point via the complex Langevin equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25943)
*Michael Mandl,Dénes Sexty,Daniel Unterhuber*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 该工作首次利用复朗之万方程（complex Langevin equation）在物理点（physical point）上给出 2+1+1 味 QCD 的状态方程（equation of state），并在跨越温度（crossover temperature）以上、前所未有的大的重子化学势（baryon chemical potential）下计算了连续极限外推的热力学量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有限重子化学势下的 QCD 状态方程因符号问题（sign problem）难以用传统格点方法直接计算，本研究旨在借助复朗之万方程突破该限制并扩展至物理点与四味（2+1+1-flavor）情形。

Method: 采用复朗之万方程进行格点模拟，设置保持同位旋对称（isospin-symmetric）且奇异数与粲数中性（strangeness- and charm-neutral）；计算重子数密度（baryon density）、压强（pressure）、声速（speed of sound）与绝热压缩率（adiabatic compressibility），并做连续极限外推（continuum extrapolation）。

Result: 在跨越温度以上、极大重子化学势条件下获得了连续极限外推的热力学量结果，为该参数区域提供了此前缺失的定量数据。

Conclusion: 结果表明复朗之万方程可用于物理点 2+1+1 味 QCD 状态方程的计算，并可在超越常规方法可达的重子化学势范围内给出热力学信息。

Abstract: This study presents the first results for the equation of state of 2+1+1-flavor QCD at the physical point, obtained using the complex Langevin equation. We calculate continuum-extrapolated thermodynamic quantities, such as the baryon density, pressure, the speed of sound and the adiabatic compressibility. The simulations are performed in the isospin-symmetric, strangeness- and charm-neutral setting above the crossover temperature and at unprecedentedly large baryon chemical potentials.

</details>


### [156] [Half-strings across the deconfinement transition of a $U(1)$ quantum link model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26415)
*Indrajit Sau,Arnab Sen,Debasish Banerjee*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文在自旋-½ U(1) 量子链模型（quantum link model, QLM）中，系统提取有限温度相变的临界温度（critical temperature）与临界指数（critical exponents），并比较有无退火（annealed）Q=±2 电荷情形下的有效弦张力（effective string tension），发现前者 $T_c/\sqrt{\sigma}$ 与常规 $\mathbb{Z}_2$ 规范理论相近，后者给出新的普适值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 禁闭（confinement）是规范理论（gauge theories）的核心特征，量子模拟（quantum simulator）方案实现禁闭弦的前景推动了对简化格点规范理论（lattice gauge theories, LGTs）的研究；量子链模型（QLMs）推广 Wilson 型 LGT 并提供更丰富的禁闭情形，而已有工作（PRL 130, 071901 (2023)）表明加入退火电荷会定性改变禁闭—解禁闭转变，其有限温度性质尚待细致刻画。

Method: 针对含与不含退火 Q=±2 电荷的自旋-½ U(1) QLM，详细提取有限温度相变的临界温度 $T_c$ 与临界指数，并比较零温与有限温度下的（有效）弦张力 $\sigma$，进而计算无量纲比值 $T_c/\sqrt{\sigma}$。

Result: 含退火电荷的 QLM 的 $T_c/\sqrt{\sigma}$ 与通常的 $\mathbb{Z}_2$ 规范理论相似；不含该电荷的理论则给出一个新的普适值（universal value）。零温与有限温度下的有效弦张力在两种理论间亦作了对比。

Conclusion: 退火 Q=±2 电荷的存在不仅定性改变禁闭—解禁闭转变，也体现在 $T_c/\sqrt{\sigma}$ 的普适性归类上：有电荷情形归入 $\mathbb{Z}_2$ 规范理论类，无电荷情形对应新的普适类，为 QLM 禁闭相图提供定量基准。

Abstract: The phenomenon of confinement is arguably the key feature of gauge theories regularly studied in high-energy and condensed matter physics. This causes hadrons to exist as stable particles in quantum chromodynamics (QCD) as bound states of quarks (and gluons), and strings in pure gauge theories. The promise of realizing such strings in quantum simulator setups have fueled extensive studies of a plethora of simpler lattice gauge theories (LGTs). Quantum link models (QLMs) generalize Wilsonian LGTs and yield richer scenarios of confinement. For example, the string joining two static charges in the spin-$\frac{1}{2}$ $U(1)$ QLM fractionalize, giving rise to confining phases that break the point group and internal symmetries spontaneously. It was further shown in PRL 130, 071901 (2023) that adding annealed $Q = \pm 2$ charges to this model qualitatively changes the confinement-deconfinement transition. In this paper, we examine the finite temperature phase transition both in the presence and the absence of annealed charges through detailed extraction of critical temperature, $T_c$ , and exponents. The (effective) string tension $σ$ at zero and finite- temperatures are compared between the theory with and without annealed charges. The quantity $T_c/\sqrtσ$ for the QLM with annealed charges is similar to the usual $\mathbb{Z}_2$ gauge theory, while the theory without the charges provides a new universal value.

</details>

### [157] [Spinning particle dynamics around a non-singular Schwarzschild black hole in conformal gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24944)
*Shavkat Karshiboev,Bobur Turimov,Sulton Usanov,Ahmadjon Abdujabbarov,Chengxun Yuan,Hasanova Sumanbar,Gulhayo Hayitova*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本工作通过Mathisson-Papapetrou-Dixon方程研究共形引力非奇异Schwarzschild黑洞赤道面上自旋测试粒子的运动，揭示共形标度\(L\)对有效势、ISCO与最大允许自旋的调控作用，并指出\(L\)与Kerr自旋参数存在退化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自旋测试粒子（spinning test particles）是强场引力的精密探针，但其在共形引力（conformal gravity）无奇点黑洞周围的动力学仍缺乏约束，故需系统刻画其轨道行为。

Method: 采用Tulczyjew自旋补充条件（spin supplementary condition）下的Mathisson-Papapetrou-Dixon方程，在共形重标度球对称时空中推导守恒能量与角动量，并构造自旋粒子的径向有效势（effective potential），进而计算物理类时自旋上限、ISCO、\(L\)与Kerr转动参数的退简并及代表性束缚轨道。

Result: 共形标度\(L\)增强有效势垒，降低ISCO半径、角动量与能量，并降低轨道变为类空前所允许的最大自旋；数值轨道积分显示\(L\)主导束缚轨道的致密性（compactness），而粒子自旋主导累积的相对论进动（relativistic precession）。

Conclusion: 自旋粒子动力学可作为共形正则黑洞时空的敏感诊断工具，并为区分共形Schwarzschild几何与标准Schwarzschild背景提供可能途径。

Abstract: Spinning test particles provide a precision probe of strong-field gravity, yet their dynamics around singularity-free black holes in conformal gravity remain poorly constrained. We investigate equatorial motion around the non-singular Schwarzschild black hole of conformal gravity, in which the conformal scale \(L\) removes the curvature divergence at the origin while preserving the event horizon at \(r=2M\) and recovering the Schwarzschild geometry as \(L\to 0\). Using the Mathisson-Papapetrou-Dixon equations with the Tulczyjew spin supplementary condition, we derive the conserved energy and angular momentum in the conformally rescaled spherical spacetime and construct the radial effective potential for spinning particles. This allows us to determine the physical timelike spin bound, the innermost stable circular orbit (ISCO), the degeneracy between \(L\) and the Kerr rotation parameter, and representative bound trajectories. We show that \(L\) strengthens the effective-potential barrier, decreases the ISCO radius, angular momentum and energy, and lowers the maximum admissible spin before the trajectory becomes spacelike. The orbital integrations reveal a clear division of roles: \(L\) governs the compactness of the bound orbit, whereas the particle spin governs the accumulated relativistic precession. These results establish spinning-particle dynamics as a sensitive diagnostic of conformally regular black-hole spacetimes and provide a possible route to distinguish the conformal Schwarzschild geometry from standard Schwarzschild background.

</details>


### [158] [2-dimensional Metric-Affine Geometries and Dynamical Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24945)
*Damianos Iosifidis*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究二维非黎曼度规-仿射几何，证明当联络不满足度规兼容性时，底流形不再是爱因斯坦流形，且曲率由非对称的里奇张量完全确定，并发现标量曲率在矢量型联络平移下具有扩大对称性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在黎曼情形下二维流形具有爱因斯坦流形性质等特殊结构，而引入非度规兼容性（non-metricity）后这些经典结论是否成立尚需澄清，本文旨在系统刻画二维非黎曼度规-仿射几何的基本性质。

Method: 采用解析推导方式研究二维度规-仿射几何（metric-affine geometry），考察非零非度规性条件下联络、曲率与里奇张量（Ricci tensor）的关系，并将结果应用于二维度规-仿射引力理论（Metric-Affine Gravity）。

Result: 证明若联络非度规兼容，则二维底流形不是爱因斯坦流形（Einstein manifold）；一般情形下曲率由里奇张量完全决定，但该张量不再对称；同时发现标量曲率（scalar curvature）在矢量型联络平移下具有扩大对称性。

Conclusion: 非度规兼容性显著改变二维几何的基本性质，所得结论被用于讨论二维度规-仿射引力理论及其若干特殊情况。

Abstract: We study non-Riemannian metric-affine geometries in 2-dimensions. We explicitly show that if the connection of the space is not metric compatible (i.e. the non-metricity is non-vanishing) then the underlying 2-dimensional manifold is not an Einstein manifold, in contrast to the Riemannian case. Furthermore we prove that in general the curvature in such spaces is specified completely by the Ricci tensor which, however, is not symmetric now. An enlarged symmetry of the scalar curvature under vectorial connection shifts is also found. We then apply our results to the study of 2-dimensional Metric-Affine Gravity Theories and discuss some special cases.

</details>


### [159] [Extracting Energy from a Traversable Wormhole via Magnetic Reconnection: Circular Orbits and the Plunging Region](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24947)
*Ke Wang,Wei Xu,Xiao-Xiong Zeng*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 基于 Comisso-Asenjo 磁重联机制，比较 Teo 可穿越转动虫洞与 Kerr 黑洞的能量提取效率与功率，发现 Teo 虫洞在高自旋下具有更高的提取功率与效率，且坠落区的行为与 Kerr 黑洞相反。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究无事件视界、且在任何参数极限下均无法退化为 Kerr 黑洞的 Teo 可穿越转动虫洞（traversable rotating wormhole）在磁重联（magnetic reconnection）机制下的能量提取特性，并与可退化为 Kerr 黑洞的 Simpson-Visser 虫洞及 Kerr 黑洞进行对比。

Method: 采用 Comisso-Asenjo 磁重联机制，计算赤道面上的圆轨道（circular orbits）、顺行光子球（prograde photon sphere）与最内稳定圆轨道（ISCO），并在相同等离子体参数下与 Kerr 黑洞比较，进而将该机制推广至坠落区（plunging region）。

Result: 低于某一自旋值时，能层（ergosphere）内不存在圆轨道；效率与功率均随角动量单调增加。相同等离子体参数下，Teo 虫洞的能量提取功率与效率高于 Kerr 黑洞，与自旋增大时效率功率下降的 Simpson-Visser 虫洞截然不同。自旋不太高时坠落区的功率与效率高于圆轨道区，极高自旋时则相反，而 Kerr 黑洞中坠落区始终更高。

Conclusion: Teo 虫洞的磁重联能量提取行为在自旋依赖性与区域分布上均显著区别于 Kerr 黑洞及 Simpson-Visser 虫洞，表明视界的存在与否及度规可退化性对能量提取过程具有关键影响。

Abstract: Using the Comisso-Asenjo magnetic reconnection mechanism, we study energy extraction from the Teo traversable rotating wormhole at different orbital locations. This wormhole has no event horizon and cannot reduce to a Kerr black hole in any parameter limit. We compute circular orbits on the equatorial plane, the prograde photon sphere, and the innermost stable circular orbit (ISCO). We find that below a certain spin, no circular orbit exists inside the ergosphere. Both the efficiency and power calculated from magnetic reconnection increase monotonically with angular momentum. Compared with a Kerr black hole under the same plasma parameters, the Teo wormhole has higher energy extraction power and efficiency. This is completely different from the Simpson-Visser rotating wormhole, which can reduce to a Kerr black hole, where the large spin makes efficiency and power decrease. We also extend magnetic reconnection to the plunging region. We find that when the spin is not particularly high, the energy extraction power and efficiency in the plunging region are higher than those in the circular orbit region, while for very high spin the situation is reversed. This conclusion also differs from that obtained from a Kerr black hole, where the power and efficiency in the plunging region are always higher than those in the circular orbit region.

</details>


### [160] [Probing the Graviton Mass through the Stochastic Gravitational-Wave Background Last-Scattering Surface](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24950)
*Hongguang Zhang,Zhengyang Qiu,Sirui Wang,Jiayi Wei,Jing Zhang*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文将引力子质量（graviton mass）导致的传播距离色散转化为随机引力波背景（SGWB）在FLRW背景中的径向可视窗口厚度，并计算其对角功率谱的压低效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非零引力子质量使不同动量（momentum）的引力波在同一宇宙时距内传播不同距离，因此有限动量大带宽的遗迹SGWB对应有限厚度的源壳层，而非无质量传播下的薄壳，需要刻画这一效应。

Method: 基于几何光学（geometric-optics）传播关系，将动量与发射时间分布映射为径向可视窗口（radial visibility window），同时包含色散与有限发射持续时间，不作小质量或窄带近似；随后计算径向平均对早期产生背景角功率的影响。

Result: 对尺度不变源谱，薄壳平移到不改变角功率，故功率下降可孤立地反映有限厚度效应；对数动量宽度0.5时，q=0.3、1、3在ℓ=10处给出约0.974、0.771、0.508的角功率比，对应3 nHz频率下质量约3.6×10⁻²⁴–1.2×10⁻²³ eV。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了将引力子质量通过传播距离与径向窗口映射到SGWB角功率谱的框架，但仅为角响应计算，用于质量推断仍需源与探测器模型，不构成观测灵敏度估计。

Abstract: A nonzero graviton mass makes gravitational waves of different momenta travel different distances during the same cosmic interval. A relic stochastic gravitational-wave background (SGWB) with a finite momentum bandwidth therefore samples a shell of finite thickness, rather than the thin source shell of massless propagation from a single epoch. We describe this effective last-scattering surface by mapping the momentum and emission-time distribution into a radial visibility window in an FLRW background. The calculation uses the geometric-optics propagation relation and includes both dispersion and finite emission duration, without a small-mass or narrow-band approximation. We then calculate how radial averaging changes the angular power for an early-produced background whose momentum channels trace a common source-fluctuation field. For a scale-invariant source spectrum, moving a thin shell leaves its angular power unchanged, so the reduction in power isolates the effect of its finite thickness. For a logarithmic momentum width of $0.5$, mass-to-momentum ratios $q=0.3,1,3$ give angular-power ratios of approximately $0.974$, $0.771$, and $0.508$ at $\ell=10$. At a representative frequency of $3\,\mathrm{nHz}$, these ratios correspond to masses of approximately $3.6\times10^{-24}$--$1.2\times10^{-23}\,\mathrm{eV}$. We establish a framework that maps the graviton mass to the SGWB angular power spectrum through its effect on propagation distances and the radial window, for a specified source distribution and frequency selection. Using this framework for graviton-mass inference requires a source and detector model; the calculation here establishes the angular response rather than an observational sensitivity.

</details>


### [161] [Degenerate wormhole as a topological oscillator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24952)
*Juri Dimaschko*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 将无物质的球对称退化虫洞（degenerate wormhole）视为以喉部半径（throat radius）为唯一集体自由度的引力系统，正则量子化后得到具有 SU(1,1) 动力学对称性的谱问题，其精确解揭示出亚普朗克质量下的玻尔型（Bohr type）量子态与超普朗克质量下的半经典爱因斯坦-罗森虫洞（Einstein Rosen wormhole）两种定性不同的区制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于将虫洞喉部半径作为单一集体自由度处理，从而考察引力束缚态的量子行为与半经典几何（如引力视界）之间是否存在连续联系。

Method: 对喉部运动应用等效原理（equivalence principle）确定经典动力学，再通过正则量子化（canonical quantization）将动力学化为具有 SU(1,1) 动力学对称群的谱问题，并求其精确解。

Result: 谱问题精确解显示两个定性不同区制：亚普朗克质量下出现以引力玻尔半径（gravitational Bohr radius）ħ²/(GM³) 为特征的玻尔型态；超普朗克质量下呈半经典区制，对应具有史瓦西引力半径（Schwarzschild gravitational radius）2GM/c² 的爱因斯坦-罗森虫洞。

Conclusion: 该模型将熟悉的量子束缚态行为与引力视界的半经典几何联系起来，表明两类区制在同一框架内得到统一描述。

Abstract: A spherically symmetric, matter free degenerate wormhole is considered as a gravitational system with a single collective degree of freedom, the radius of its throat. The classical dynamics is determined by applying the equivalence principle to the motion of the throat, while canonical quantization reduces the dynamics to a spectral problem with the dynamical symmetry group \(SU(1,1)\). The exact solution of this spectral problem reveals two qualitatively different regimes. For subPlanckian masses, the quantum system exhibits Bohr type states characterized by a gravitational Bohr radius \(\hbar^2/(GM^3)\). For superPlanckian masses, the quantum system exhibits a semiclassical regime represented by the Einstein Rosen wormhole with Schwarzschild gravitational radius \(2GM/c^2\). Thus, the model connects the familiar quantum bound state behavior with the semiclassical geometry of a gravitational horizon.

</details>


### [162] [Accretion disks and photon rings in Palatini Kalb-Ramond gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24953)
*A. A. Araújo Filho,N. Heidari,P. H. M. Barros,Carlos F. S. Pereira*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 在 Palatini Kalb–Ramond 引力中的静态黑洞周围，薄吸积盘与光子环被发现具有与史瓦西（Schwarzschild）相同的 ISCO、临界碰撞参数和约 5.72% 的辐射效率；正曲率耦合 q 会增强局部热辐射并加快连续像的累积。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在把 Palatini Kalb–Ramond 引力中黑洞附近的热辐射与径向几何的光学效应联系起来，量化有效曲率耦合对薄吸积盘（thin accretion disk）与光子环（photon ring）观测特征的影响。

Method: 在固定轨道质量与吸积率条件下，解析推导局部辐射流量与有效温度对参数 σ=√(1−q/2) 的标度关系，并利用光子轨迹的精确角度重标度（angular rescaling）确定强偏折系数与连续盘像的指数累积；同时成像不透明热盘与透明发射薄片，计入引力频移、轨道运动与径向发射率。

Result: 最内稳定圆轨道（ISCO）与临界碰撞参数（critical impact parameter）取史瓦西值，辐射效率约 5.72%；局部流量按 1/σ 标度、有效温度按 1/σ^{1/4} 标度。正 q 增大局部热发射并使连续像在共同临界曲线附近更快累积。

Conclusion: 结合太阳系（Solar System）限制的普适耦合假设，流量与温度的分数修正分别约为 10^{-11} 与 10^{-12} 量级，表明该引力框架下的可观测偏离极小。

Abstract: We investigate thin accretion disks and photon rings around a static black hole in Palatini Kalb Ramond gravity, connecting thermal emission with the optical effects of the radial geometry. At fixed orbital mass and accretion rate, the local radiative flux scales as $1/σ$ and the effective temperature as $1/σ^{1/4}$, where $σ=\sqrt{1-q/2}$ and $q$ is the effective curvature coupling. The innermost stable circular orbit and the critical impact parameter share their Schwarzschild values, together with an emitted efficiency of approximately $5.72\%$. Moreover, photon trajectories admit an exact angular rescaling that determines the strong deflection coefficient and the exponential accumulation of successive disk images. We construct images of an opaque thermal disk and a transparent emitting sheet, taking into account gravitational frequency shifts, orbital motion, and radial emissivity. Positive $q$ increases the local thermal emission and accelerates the accumulation of successive images around the common critical curve. Under the universal coupling assumptions associated with the quoted Solar System bounds, the fractional corrections are of order $10^{-11}$ in flux and $10^{-12}$ in temperature.

</details>


### [163] [Damour--Solodukhin Wormholes: Matter Sources, Observational Constraints, and Accretion Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24954)
*G. Alencar,V. H. U. Borralho,T. M. Crispim,Marcos V. de S. Silva*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究Damour–Solodukhin虫洞的物理实现与可观测性质，构建了由幽灵标量场耦合非线性电动力学构成的物质源，并利用Sgr A*观测约束形变参数λ，指出在小形变情形下其阴影半径与Schwarzschild黑洞精确简并。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在为Damour–Solodukhin虫洞几何寻找显式物质源，并系统评估其与Schwarzschild黑洞在观测上的可区分性。

Method: 构建幽灵标量场（phantom scalar field）与非线性电动力学（nonlinear electrodynamics）耦合的物质源（含电场与磁场位形）；分析光子传播并用事件视界望远镜（Event Horizon Telescope）对Sgr A*的观测约束λ；采用Johnson SU发射率分布的薄吸积盘（thin accretion disk）成像；分析稳态相对论性吸积。

Result: 当λ² ≤ 1/2时阴影半径与Schwarzschild值完全一致，但径向几何仍影响引力透镜（gravitational lensing）与光子环结构；相同边界条件下外区能量密度与粒子数密度不依赖λ，径向速度仍显式依赖形变参数；外部声速点（sonic point）存在条件为0 &lt; w &lt; (1+4λ²)⁻¹。

Conclusion: 小形变情形下Damour–Solodukhin虫洞与Schwarzschild黑洞存在观测简并（observational degeneracy），但可通过透镜、光子环及吸积动力学等几何与动力学效应加以检验。

Abstract: We investigate the physical realization and observational properties of Damour--Solodukhin wormholes within general relativity. We first construct an explicit matter source supporting this geometry, consisting of a phantom scalar field coupled to nonlinear electrodynamics, and examine both electric and magnetic configurations. We then study the propagation of photons and derive constraints on the wormhole deformation parameter $λ$ using Event Horizon Telescope observations of Sgr A*. We show that, for $λ^2 \leq 1/2$, the shadow radius coincides exactly with the Schwarzschild value, although the radial geometry continues to affect gravitational lensing and the structure of photon rings. Using a thin accretion disk with a Johnson SU emissivity profile, we investigate the resulting optical images and examine the effects of the wormhole throat on their structure. Finally, we analyze stationary relativistic accretion and demonstrate that, for identical boundary conditions, the energy density and particle number density are independent of $λ$ throughout the common exterior region, while the radial velocity retains an explicit dependence on the deformation parameter. We also derive the condition $0 < w < (1+4λ^2)^{-1}$ for the existence of an exterior sonic point. These results highlight the observational degeneracy between Damour--Solodukhin wormholes and Schwarzschild black holes in the small-deformation regime, while identifying the geometric and dynamical effects through which this degeneracy may be investigated.

</details>


### [164] [Analysis of a Core Envelope Model for PSR J1614-2230 in $f(R)= R+αR^{2} +γR^{3}$ gravity and Tidal Deformability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24955)
*Ayesha Khalid,Muhammad Bilal Riaz,S A Mardan,Mustafa Inc*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 在立方 f(R) 引力框架下构建 PSR J1614-2230 的各向异性核-壳模型，通过求解标量场扰动方程计算潮汐 Love 数，所得潮汐可变形性与 GW170817 约束一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于考察修正引力（modified gravity）背景下致密星（compact star）的潮汐可变形性（tidal deformability），并检验各向异性核-壳（anisotropic core-envelope）结构在 f(R) 引力中的物理可行性。

Method: 恒星内部划分为核区与壳区：核区采用线性状态方程（equation of state, EoS），壳区采用二次 EoS，两区径向度规势均取 Tolman VII 形式；在核-壳界面及外部 Schwarzschild 时空处施加匹配条件（junction conditions），并通过求解标量场（scalar field）的耦合扰动方程（perturbation equations）确定潮汐 Love 数（tidal Love number）。

Result: 所得位形无奇点，并满足能量条件（energy conditions）、因果性要求（causality requirements）、绝热指数约束（adiabatic index constraint）、Herrera 开裂条件（Herrera cracking condition）及流体静力学平衡条件（hydrostatic equilibrium condition）；预测的潮汐可变形性与 GW170817 的约束相符。

Conclusion: 结果表明，所提出的核-壳位形在立方 f(R) 引力（cubic f(R) gravity）中具有物理可行性，并支持该模型对 PSR J1614-2230 的适用性。

Abstract: In this work, we investigate the tidal deformability of an anisotropic core-envelope model for the compact star PSR J1614-2230 within the framework of modified f(R) gravity. The stellar interior is divided into two regions, with the core described by a linear equation of state (EoS) and the envelope by a quadratic EoS. The Tolman VII form is adopted for the radial metric potential in both regions. The interior solutions are matched at the core-envelope interface and to the exterior Schwarzschild spacetime through the appropriate junction conditions. The tidal Love number is determined by solving the coupled perturbation equations for the scalar field. The resulting configuration is free from singularities and satisfies the energy conditions, causality requirements, adiabatic index constraint, Herrera cracking condition, and hydrostatic equilibrium condition. The predicted tidal deformability is consistent with the constraints from GW170817, supporting the physical viability of the proposed core-envelope configuration in cubic f(R) gravity.

</details>


### [165] [Photon sphere, shadow, and polarized images of thick accretion flows around a quantum Lee-Wick black hole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24956)
*Shokhzod Jumaniyozov,Ahmad Al-Badawi,Olimjon Toirov*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文首次给出六阶导数 Lee-Wick 引力中静态球对称黑洞的阴影、厚盘与偏振合成图像，并系统分类其光子球与视界结构及 EHT 约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高阶导数 Lee-Wick 引力（Lee-Wick gravity）作为可重整且可能规避奇点的引力修正方案，其黑洞的可观测特征尚缺乏系统性研究，尤其是偏振与厚盘辐射层面的合成图像尚未建立。

Method: 由零测地线（null geodesic）有效势导出光子球、临界碰撞参数与阴影半径；按视界和不稳定光子球数目对 (S1, S2) 参数平面分类；自研光线追踪（ray-tracing）代码积分精确 Lee-Wick 零测地线，计算 230 GHz 几何厚、光学薄 RIAF 类吸积流的热同步辐射，并用 Walker-Penrose 常数输运线偏振；另引入参数化激波锥（shock-cone）模型。

Result: 弱耦合（Block-1）区阴影半径迅速趋于 Schwarzschild 值 3√3 M；强耦合（Block-2）区呈非单调甚至强烈压低的阴影。无视界构型保留临界曲线但无中心亮度凹陷；双不稳定光子球构型阴影由内光子球界定并附外光子环。M87* 与 Sgr A* 的 1σ/2σ EHT 界限排除部分强耦合参数带。强耦合下束卷积环直径偏差达约 14%，光子环 Lyapunov 指数由 π 增至约 4–9，β₂ 振幅在径向-环向场下升高至多约 50%，而低倾角下 β₂ 相位对 (S1, S2) 几乎不敏感。

Conclusion: Lee-Wick 黑洞在强耦合区具有可观测上可区分的阴影尺寸、光子环稳定性与偏振特征；激波结构仅以视向方位依赖方式调制流量而不改变临界曲线尺度，为高阶导数引力的观测检验提供了合成图像基础。

Abstract: We study the optical appearance of a static, spherically symmetric black hole (BH) in sixth-derivative Lee-Wick gravity with an exponential coupling S1 and an oscillatory coupling S2. From the null-geodesic effective potential we derive the photon sphere, critical impact parameter, and shadow radius. In the weak-coupling (Block-1) regime the shadow radius rapidly approaches the Schwarzschild value 3 sqrt(3) M, while in the strong-coupling (Block-2) regime the interplay of exponential damping and oscillatory modulation yields non-monotonic and, in some regions, strongly suppressed shadows. We classify the (S1, S2) plane by the number of horizons and unstable photon spheres: horizonless configurations retain a critical curve but lack a central brightness depression, and configurations with two unstable photon spheres show a shadow bounded by the inner one plus an outer photon ring. The 1-sigma/2-sigma EHT bounds for M87* and Sgr A* exclude certain strong-coupling bands. We develop a ray-tracing code that integrates exact Lee-Wick null geodesics, computes 230 GHz thermal-synchrotron emission from a geometrically thick, optically thin RIAF-like flow, and transports linear polarization via the Walker-Penrose constants. Compared with Schwarzschild, in the strong-coupling regime the beam-convolved ring diameter deviates by up to ~14%, the photon-ring Lyapunov exponent grows from pi to about 4-9, and the beta_2 amplitude rises by up to ~50% for a radial-toroidal field, while the phase of beta_2 is nearly insensitive to (S1, S2) at low inclination, mainly tracing the field geometry. A parametrized shock-cone model, motivated by earlier Bondi-Hoyle-Lyttleton simulations, shows that shock structures modulate the flux in a viewing-azimuth-dependent way without changing the critical-curve size. These are the first shadow, thick-disk, and polarized synthetic images of the Lee-Wick BH.

</details>


### [166] [Traversable wormholes from entropy-corrected holographic dark energy with quasinormal mode stability in $f(R,T)$ gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24964)
*Nattar Gayathri S,Zinnat Hassan,Uday Nambeesan,Saddam Hossain*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在 $f(R,T)$ 引力框架下，以熵修正全息暗能量（holographic dark energy, HDE）能密度分布作为可穿越虫洞（traversable wormhole）的物质源，系统检验了其可穿越性、能量条件、复杂度、平衡与扰动稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在考察熵修正全息暗能量模型能否为 $f(R,T)$ 修正引力中的可穿越虫洞提供可行物质源，并评估不同熵 prescription 对虫洞几何与物理性质的影响。

Method: 基于 Bekenstein–Hawking、Rényi 与 Moradpour 三种熵修正 HDE 形式导出形状函数（shape function），分析 flare-out 条件与参数界限，检验零能量条件（null energy condition, NEC）、总引力能量与复杂度因子（complexity factor），用曲率不变量检验正则性，并借助广义 Tolman–Oppenheimer–Volkoff 方程及三阶 WKB 近似计算标量与电磁准正规模（quasinormal modes, QNMs）。

Result: 在满足可穿越性参数约束下，NEC 在虫洞喉部附近被违反，提供维持几何所需的奇异物质；曲率不变量显示无曲率奇异性且 Kretschmann 标量渐近衰减；所有准正规模频率的虚部为负，表明振荡受阻尼、构型在微扰意义下稳定；虫洞内部结构对熵 prescription 高度敏感。

Conclusion: 熵修正 HDE 模型（尤其 Rényi 与 Moradpour 情形）显著影响 $f(R,T)$ 引力中可穿越虫洞的曲率轮廓、结构复杂度、平衡行为与 ringdown 响应，说明熵修正是此类虫洞构建中的关键要素。

Abstract: In this paper, we investigate holographic dark energy (HDE) motivated energy density profiles as viable matter sources for the construction of traversable wormholes in the framework of $f(R,T)$ gravity. Wormhole solutions are developed using entropy-corrected HDE models based on the Bekenstein--Hawking, Rényi, and Moradpour formalisms. We derive the corresponding shape functions and analyze the influence of the model parameters on the flare-out conditions, obtaining the parameter bounds required for traversability. The null energy condition is examined under these constraints and is found to be violated near the wormhole throat, thereby providing the exotic matter content necessary to sustain the geometry. We further evaluate the total gravitational energy and the complexity factor, showing that the internal structure of the wormhole spacetime is highly sensitive to the underlying entropy prescription. The regularity of the solutions is tested through curvature invariants, confirming the absence of curvature singularities and the asymptotic decay of the Kretschmann scalar. In addition, the generalized Tolman--Oppenheimer--Volkoff equation is used to study hydrostatic equilibrium, where the inward hydrostatic force is balanced by the anisotropic force and the extra force induced by the matter--geometry coupling. Finally, scalar and electromagnetic quasinormal modes are computed using the third-order WKB approximation. The negative imaginary parts of all obtained frequencies indicate damped oscillations and hence perturbative stability of the wormhole configurations. Our results show that entropy-corrected HDE models, particularly the Rényi and Moradpour cases, significantly affect the curvature profile, structural complexity, equilibrium behavior, and ringdown response of traversable wormholes in modified $f(R,T)$ gravity.

</details>


### [167] [Exact ultrastatic multi-throat wormholes: Geometry, energy conditions, and optical cavities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24965)
*Francisco S. N. Lobo,Manuel E. Rodrigues,Gabriel I. Róis*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文构造了一类精确可解的静态球对称虫洞层级（wormhole hierarchy），喉咙数（throat multiplicity）可任意有限；并证明爱因斯坦方程给出三条普适的“超静态（ultrastatic）”结论：喉违反径向零能量条件、反喉满足全部经典能量条件、喉处圆零轨道不稳定而反喉处稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为获得可精确求解、喉咙数可控的虫洞解，并厘清喉（throat）与反喉（anti-throat）在能量条件（energy conditions）与径向稳定性（radial stability）上的普适性质，同时避免仅依赖“超静态性”解释 ADM 质量（Arnowitt-Deser-Misner mass）的消失。

Method: 采用面积半径导数（derivative of the areal radius）的因子化预设（factorized prescription），使每个静止球面处于给定固有距离，同时保持初等可积性（elementary integrability）与渐近平坦性（asymptotic flatness）；并结合爱因斯坦方程对超静态几何作局部分析，推导圆零轨道势的二阶导数关系。

Result: N 阶几何含 N+1 个喉与 N 个反喉，呈颈与有限内部腔交替的链状结构。所有非退化喉违反径向零能量条件（null energy condition），非退化反喉满足零、弱、主导与强能量条件且强能量和条件取饱和；V_null''=8πL²(ρ+p_ℓ)/R² 表明喉为不稳定光球（unstable light ring）、反喉为稳定光球。连续静止球面之间的每个有限胞、每个外部胞以及双端径向零测地线，其积分径向零能量严格为负。

Conclusion: 所得时空为精确的多腔光学链（multi-cavity optical chain），腔中心局部非奇异（nonexotic），但无任何胞可消除平均径向奇异性。首个非平凡成员可连续插值于 Ellis-Bronnikov 虫洞、四阶退化喉（quartically degenerate throat）与双喉几何之间，并给出源、零平均、分支 Morris-Thorne 形式、嵌入判据与可穿越性尺度（traversability scales）的闭式表达；零 ADM 质量源自该层级的特定径向衰减，而非仅由超静态性导致。

Abstract: We construct an exactly solvable hierarchy of static, spherically symmetric, wormholes with arbitrary finite throat multiplicity. A factorized prescription for the derivative of the areal radius places every stationary sphere at a prescribed proper distance while retaining elementary integrability and asymptotic flatness. The order-$N$ geometry contains exactly $N+1$ throats and $N$ anti-throats, arranged as an alternating chain of necks and finite interior cavities. Independently of this particular hierarchy, the Einstein equations imply three universal ultrastatic results. Every nondegenerate throat violates the radial null energy condition, whereas every nondegenerate anti-throat satisfies the null, weak, dominant, and strong energy conditions, with the strong-energy sum condition saturated. The same local combination $ρ+p_\ell$ fixes the radial stability of the associated circular null orbit through $V_{\mathrm {null}}''=8πL^2(ρ+p_\ell)/\mathcal R^2$; throats are therefore unstable light rings and anti-throats are stable ones. Finally, every finite cell between consecutive stationary spheres, each exterior cell, and the complete two-ended radial null geodesic carry strictly negative integrated radial null energy. The resulting spacetimes are exact multi-cavity optical chains whose cavity centers are locally nonexotic even though no cell can eliminate averaged radial exoticity. The first nontrivial member interpolates continuously from the Ellis-Bronnikov wormhole through a quartically degenerate throat to a double-throat geometry and admits closed expressions for its source, null average, branchwise Morris-Thorne form, embedding criterion, and traversability scales. Its vanishing Arnowitt-Deser-Misner mass follows from the specific radial falloff of the hierarchy, rather than from ultrastaticity alone.

</details>


### [168] [Orbital dynamics and gravitational wave of Schwarzschild-de Sitter black hole immersed in perfect fluid dark matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24967)
*Ruifang Wang,Fabao Gao,Ruihong Yue*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文研究浸没于完美流体暗物质（perfect fluid dark matter, PFDM）中的史瓦西–德西特（Schwarzschild-de Sitter）黑洞周围束缚粒子轨道的动力学性质，利用S2恒星近星点进动数据约束PFDM参数，并计算周期轨道的引力波波形特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机在于刻画完美流体暗物质对相对论性轨道动力学的局部印记，并给出可供未来高精度天体测量与引力波观测检验的预言。

Method: 方法包括：分析视界拓扑以确定事件视界与宇宙学视界（cosmological horizon）出现的参数区间；计算Ricci标量、Ricci张量平方与Kretschmann标量等曲率不变量（curvature invariants）以检验r=0处的曲率奇点；并借助Sgr A*中S2星的近星点进动（pericenter precession）数据约束PFDM参数。

Result: 结果表明：PFDM参数被约束为0<λ<7.726412×10^5 M_⊙；对沿周期轨道运动的源，引力波波形显示轨道周期随“zoom”数增大而增加、波幅略有增长；PFDM参数非线性地调制周期，导致振幅轻微压低，且相对真空情形仅引入可忽略的相位移动。

Conclusion: 结论是上述结果明确刻画了PFDM对相对论性轨道动力学的局部印记，并提供了可由未来高精度天体测量与引力波观测检验的预言。

Abstract: The dynamical properties of bound particle orbits around a Schwarzschild-de Sitter black hole immersed in perfect fluid dark matter (PFDM) are investigated. An analysis of the horizon topology shows that both event and cosmological horizons emerge within specific parametric regimes. Curvature invariants, including the Ricci scalar, Ricci-squared, and Kretschmann scalar, confirm the existence of a curvature singularity at $r = 0$. By utilizing the pericenter precession data of the S2 star in Sgr A*, the PFDM parameter is constrained to $0 < λ< 7.726412 \times 10^{5}$ M$_{\odot}$. For a source moving along a periodic orbit, the emitted gravitational waveforms exhibit two key features: (i) the orbital period increases with the ``zoom'' number, accompanied by a modest growth in wave amplitude, and (ii) the PFDM parameter nonlinearly modulates the period, leading to a slight amplitude suppression and introducing only a negligible phase shift relative to the vacuum case. These results explicitly characterize the local imprints of PFDM on relativistic orbital dynamics and offer predictions that are testable by future high-precision astrometric and gravitational-wave observations.

</details>


### [169] [Gyromagnetic ratios of slowly rotating charged Ellis-Bronnikov wormholes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24968)
*Safiqul Islam,Anirudh Pradhan,Francisco Tello-Ortiz,Safyan Mukhtar*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文在 Einstein–Maxwell 理论中由幻影标量场（phantom scalar field）支撑的带电 Ellis–Bronnikov 虫洞（wormhole）背景上，构造出闭式的慢转动解，并给出两端磁偶极矩与旋磁比（gyromagnetic ratio）所满足的精确亏损关系（deficit relation）。结果表明两端旋磁比均小于 2，其差值度量虫洞的不对称性，同时导出参考系拖曳（frame dragging）与电磁场角动量分配的定量性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 带电 Ellis–Bronnikov 虫洞连接两个渐近平坦区域，其转动版本在每个洞口携带磁偶极矩与旋磁比，但此前缺乏对慢转动情形的闭式刻画与对旋磁比的系统性约束，因而有必要在静止轴对称偶极扇区内建立唯一性并给出可检验的关系式。
