Method: 在三个背景分支上以闭式构造慢转动解族，并在无外加磁场的定常轴对称偶极扇区（stationary axial dipole sector）内论证一阶解的唯一性；随后由静态几何出发推导两端旋磁比的亏损关系，并分析极端极限（extremal edge）附近的行为。

Result: 两端旋磁比满足由静态几何单独决定的精确亏损关系：两端 $g_\pm<2$，亏损被标量电荷与电荷之比的平方的三分之二所界定；由约化磁矩（reduced magnetic moment）的正性可得每个比值取本洞口质量的符号；两比值仅在反射对称喉部（reflection-symmetric throat）处相等。转动使两端沿确定方向相互拖曳，拖曳剖面（dragging profile）跨喉部单调递减；电磁场在每个有限球面上携带严格正的角动量份额。在正洞口趋近极端边界时，亏损沿所研究的每条轨迹呈三次方闭合，因喉部红移因子（redshift factor）二次消失，而系数依赖于路径。

Conclusion: 电荷关闭时，参考系拖曳退化为已知的中性结果；但在大质量中性分支上，旋磁比并不趋近 Kerr–Newman 值。因此该类虫洞的转动特征与标准带电黑洞存在可区分的差异。

Abstract: Charged Ellis--Bronnikov wormholes, supported by a phantom scalar field in Einstein--Maxwell theory, connect two asymptotically flat regions, so a rotating member carries a magnetic dipole moment and a gyromagnetic ratio at each mouth. We construct the slowly rotating family in closed form on all three background branches and show that, within the stationary axial dipole sector and with no applied magnetic field, the first-order solution is unique. The two gyromagnetic ratios obey an exact deficit relation set by the static geometry alone. It gives $g_\pm<2$ at both mouths, bounds the deficit by two-thirds of the squared ratio of scalar to electric charge, and, through the positivity of the reduced magnetic moment, gives each ratio the sign of the mass of its own mouth; the two ratios coincide only for reflection-symmetric throats, so their difference measures the asymmetry of the wormhole. Rotation drags the two ends relative to each other in a definite sense, with a dragging profile that decreases monotonically across the throat, and the electromagnetic field carries a strictly positive share of the angular momentum on every finite sphere. Toward the extremal edge at the positive mouth the deficit closes cubically on every trajectory studied, because the redshift factor at the throat vanishes quadratically; the coefficient depends on the path. When the charge is switched off, the frame dragging reproduces known neutral results, whereas on the massive neutral branch the gyromagnetic ratios stay away from the Kerr--Newman value.

</details>


### [170] [Numerical Implementation and Tests of the Quadratic Effective Action for Lorentzian Spinfoam Amplitudes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25031)
*Dongxue Qu,Sean Tobin,Hongguang Liu,Muxin Han*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文在纯积分表示下数值实现了洛伦兹 EPRL 自旋泡沫（spinfoam）振幅的有效作用量框架，并用两个 Julia 程序包在双-Δ₃ 复形、1-5 Pachner 移动及其细化、24-单纯形超立方体三角剖分上验证了平坦背景下二次 Regge 作用与 ε² 曲率修正的解析预言。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 洛伦兹 EPRL 自旋泡沫振幅的纯积分表示中，对大自旋极限下非几何变量的积分会诱导出 Regge 几何的有效作用量；此前该框架仅有解析预言，缺乏数值检验，因此需要实现并验证其有效性。

Method: 采用两个 Julia 程序包实现工作流：LorentzianSimplexSolver 计算实与复临界点数据，Effective-Spinfoam 评估有效作用量；对象为双-Δ₃ 复形、1-5 Pachner 移动及其细化与 24-单纯形超立方体三角剖分，并进行两类数值检验。

Result: 第一类检验确认首项重现二次 Regge 作用，幂律拟合显示小 γ 区域 ε² 修正相对 Regge 作用在 γ 上为更高阶；第二类检验表明二次有效作用量可将非线性复临界点作用量精确复现至 δℓ 的三阶余项。

Conclusion: 数值结果与解析预言一致，支持将有效作用量框架作为洛伦兹自旋泡沫中提取 Regge 几何及其曲率修正的可行途径。

Abstract: In the pure integral representation of Lorentzian EPRL spinfoam amplitudes, the integration over non-geometric variables in the large-spin limit defines an effective action for Regge geometries. Expanding this effective action to quadratic order around a flat background recovers the classical Regge action $iS_{\mathrm{Regge}}$ at leading order, supplemented by a curvature correction quadratic in the bulk deficit angles $\varepsilon$. In this work, we numerically implement this effective-action framework and validate its analytic predictions using two Julia packages: LorentzianSimplexSolver, for the computation of real and complex critical-point data, and Effective-Spinfoam, for the evaluation of the effective action. We apply this workflow to the double-$Δ_3$ complex, the $1$-$5$ Pachner move, its refinement, and a $24$-simplex hypercube triangulation. Two complementary numerical tests are performed: first, starting from the real critical point of a flat background, we verify that the leading term reproduces the quadratic Regge action and evaluate the $\varepsilon^2$ curvature correction across a range of the Barbero-Immirzi parameter $γ$. Power-law fits confirm that in the small-$γ$ regime, the $\varepsilon^2$ correction is of higher order in $γ$ than the Regge action. Second, for a sample of curved geometries with finite length perturbations $δ\ell$, we numerically solve the full nonlinear critical equations at fixed perturbed edge lengths for complex critical points. By comparing the direct action evaluation at complex critical points with the quadratic effective action, we demonstrate that the latter accurately reproduces the nonlinear complex critical-point action up to remainders of cubic order in $δ\ell$.

</details>


### [171] [Pure integral formula of spinfoam amplitude and curvature corrections in effective theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25033)
*Muxin Han*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文提出一种针对固定三角剖分上洛伦兹 EPRL 自旋泡沫振幅的半经典分析新解析方法，通过将内部自旋的离散求和改写为连续变量的积分，得到适配大自旋极限下驻相近似（stationary phase approximation）的良好行为作用量，并由此导出 Regge 几何边长的有效作用量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始自旋泡沫振幅包含对内部自旋的离散求和，不利于在大自旋区域直接应用驻相近似，因此需要一个无离散求和、作用量行为良好的等价表述以便进行半经典分析。

Method: 推导纯积分形式的自旋泡沫振幅，将离散自旋求和替换为连续变量积分；积掉非几何变量得到 Regge 几何边长的有效作用量，并在平坦背景附近作二阶微扰展开，结合驻相近似进行分析。

Result: 领头阶给出经典 Regge 作用量，并附加一个关于亏损角（deficit angles）ε 的二次曲率修正；该修正源于新作用量带来的和乐（holonomy）修正与非 Regge 面积涨落两个来源，且在 Barbero-Immirzi 参数 γ 中相对 Regge 作用量出现在更高阶，从而形成大自旋与 γ 的双参数展开，其领头阶为二次 Regge 作用量。

Conclusion: 在长波（long wavelength）区域，领头阶的二次 Regge 作用量趋近于无质量自旋-2 引力子（massless spin-2 graviton）的作用量，支持自旋泡沫半经典极限与广义相对论及引力子图像的联系。

Abstract: We develop a new analytic method for the semiclassical analysis of the Lorentzian EPRL spinfoam amplitude on a fixed triangulation. We derive a pure integral formula for the spinfoam amplitude that trades the discrete sum over internal spins for integrals over continuous variables. This reformulation is free of discrete sums and provides a well-behaved action that is well-adapted to the stationary phase approximation in the large-spin regime. Integrating out the non-geometric variables yields an effective action for the edge lengths of the Regge geometry. Applying the stationary phase approximation and expanding this effective action to second order in perturbations around a flat background, we obtain the classical Regge action at leading order, supplemented by a quadratic correction in the deficit angles $\varepsilon$. This curvature correction originates from two distinct sources: a holonomy correction resulting from the new action and the fluctuations of non-Regge areas. We show that the quadratic $\varepsilon$ correction appears at a higher order in the Barbero-Immirzi parameter $γ$ relative to the Regge action. Together with the large-spin expansion, this yields a two-parameter expansion for the spinfoam effective action, whose leading order is the quadratic Regge action. In the long wavelength regime, the latter approaches the action of a massless spin-2 graviton.

</details>


### [172] [Subsolar neutron star-black hole binaries from collapsars: Gravitational waveform models and detectability in current searches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25040)
*Vishal Baibhav,Valerio De Luca,Loris Del Grosso,Emanuele Berti,Brian D. Metzger,Lam Hui*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 亚太阳质量天体并合若被探测到将具重大意义，但现有亚太阳质量搜寻模板可能因未考虑小质量比、中子星潮汐瓦解及周围气体效应而漏检相当比例的中子星—黑洞并合事件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准恒星演化（stellar evolution）无法产生亚太阳质量候选体，故确凿探测将指向新物理或非传统天体物理途径，如坍缩星（collapsar）中中微子冷却吸积盘外部分裂形成亚太阳质量中子星。当前专门搜寻未给出确凿探测，仅设定并合率上限，从而有必要评估搜寻方法的完备性。

Method: 通过分析现有亚太阳质量搜寻所用模板（template）与目标系统物理特征的匹配程度，论证模板未纳入小质量比（mass ratio）、中子星潮汐瓦解（tidal disruption）以及周围气体（surrounding gas）效应所带来的失配问题。

Result: 现有亚太阳质量搜寻可能漏掉相当比例（significant fraction）的中子星—黑洞并合事件，原因在于模板未考虑上述三类物理效应。

Conclusion: 该结果支持改进现有亚太阳质量搜寻所用的波形建模（waveform modeling），并构建针对此类系统的专用模板库（template bank）。

Abstract: Detecting subsolar objects in binary mergers would be an exceptional discovery in gravitational wave astronomy. So far, dedicated searches for subsolar coalescences reported no confident detections, setting upper bounds on their merger rate. Given that standard stellar evolution is not able to produce subsolar candidates, a confident detection would point either to new physics or to unconventional astrophysical pathways. These include collapsars, where the outer parts of neutrino-cooled accretion disks around newborn stellar-mass black holes are subject to fragmentation and give rise to subsolar neutron stars, which can later merge with the central black hole. We show that current subsolar searches may miss a significant fraction of these neutron star-black hole mergers, because their templates do not account for the small mass ratios, the tidal disruption of the neutron star, and the effects of the surrounding gas. This result motivates improving the waveform modeling used in current subsolar searches and building adequate template banks tailored to these systems.

</details>


### [173] [Strong Coupling of Cosmological Perturbations in mLQC-I: The Hybrid Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25180)
*Jamal Saeed,Rui Pan,Anzhong Wang*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该工作在混合方法（hybrid approach）框架下研究修正圈量子宇宙学I（mLQC-I）中标量扰动的非线性微扰一致性，发现前反弹量子几何de Sitter阶段非线性系数被强烈增强，并给出反弹态下等边三角诊断（equilateral cubic diagnostic）的临界波数约0.646–0.648倍普朗克质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有mLQC-I标量扰动研究多停留在线性层面，非线性微扰一致性（nonlinear perturbative consistency）尚未确立；需检验高阶哈密顿量在反弹前后是否可控，以及增强是否导致可定义的截断。

Method: 在施加背景约束前将二次与三次哈密顿量写为共同正则表示，采用保持扇区的最小三次正则化（minimal sector-preserving cubic regularization）；在四种暴胀势与假设的因子化四次延拓下计算三次核与四次Legendre贡献，并对反弹处初始化的瞬时二次态做等边诊断数值演化。

Result: 前反弹de Sitter相附近非线性系数强烈增强，α_I^3/κ项在选择频率分配下的固定非退化三角形中残留于领头紫外三次核，四种势均出现增强且形式压低尺度依赖归一化。等边诊断在K≃0.6483m_P首次达到1，演化至t/t_P≤10使交点降至K≃0.64626m_P；形状与频率分配抽样未进一步降低阈值，四阶绝热紫外尾可附加而不改变所测有限模。

Conclusion: 结果指认前反弹非线性增强及候选反弹态窗口，但完整振幅未定：被略去的四次扇区未证为次领头，形式尺度不构成物理截断；建立相互作用一致性仍需更广演化与形状检验、重整化反作用、一致归一化背景上的观测模映射及完整四阶理论。

Abstract: We study nonlinear perturbative consistency of scalar perturbations in modified loop quantum cosmology I (mLQC-I) within the hybrid approach. We express the quadratic and cubic Hamiltonians in a common canonical representation before imposing the background constraint and adopt a minimal sector-preserving cubic regularization. Near the pre-bounce quantum-geometrical de Sitter phase, nonlinear coefficients become strongly enhanced. The $α_I^3/κ$ term survives the leading ultraviolet cubic kernel for fixed nondegenerate triangles in the selected frequency assignment. This enhancement occurs in four inflationary potentials, with formal suppression scales sensitive to their normalization. In an assumed factorized quartic continuation, a selected lapse contact does not cancel enhanced cubic exchange in an analytic scattering family. However, the derived quartic Legendre contribution cancels that lapse contact at $V=K_φ$ in the Hamiltonian. The full amplitude remains unresolved: omitted quartic sectors are not proved subleading, and the formal scales do not determine a physical cutoff.
  For an instantaneous quadratic state initialized at the bounce, the equilateral cubic diagnostic first reaches unity at $K\simeq0.6483m_{\rm P}$. Evolution over $0\le t/t_{\rm P}\le10$ lowers this crossing to $K\simeq0.64626m_{\rm P}$. Sampled shape and independent frequency-assignment tests do not lower these thresholds. A fourth-order adiabatic ultraviolet tail can be attached without changing the tested finite modes. These results identify pre-bounce nonlinear enhancement and a candidate bounce-state window. Broader evolution and shape tests, renormalized backreaction, observational mode mapping on a consistently normalized background, and a complete fourth-order theory remain necessary to establish interacting consistency.

</details>


### [174] [Thermodynamic topology and orbital dynamics of charged RN--AdS black hole with logarithmic corrections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25518)
*Hafiza Bushra Mujahid,Muhammad Yasir,Shahid Qaisar,Faisal Javed*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究含对数修正（logarithmic corrections）的 Reissner–Nordström anti-de Sitter（RN–AdS）黑洞的拓扑热力学性质与轨道动力学，借助 Duan 的 Φ-映射理论（Duan's Φ-mapping theory）与哈密顿形式（Hamiltonian formalism），在 Sharma–Mittal 熵框架下分析热力学稳定性及中性试验粒子的轨道与振荡频率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文旨在刻画修正 RN–AdS 黑洞解的基本性质，尤其是其拓扑热力学结构与轨道动力学行为，并为未来高精度天体物理观测提供理论描述基础。

Method: 采用 Duan 的 Φ-映射理论在广义三参数 Sharma–Mittal 熵形式（generalized three-parameter Sharma–Mittal entropy formalism）下考察拓扑分类；在正则与混合正则系综（canonical and mixed canonical ensembles）中分析临界点、发散点与热容；并以哈密顿形式与有效势（effective potential）方法研究中性试验粒子的轨道稳定性、ISCO 及其轨迹与振荡频率。

Result: 研究识别了不同系综下的临界点、发散点与热容行为，据此判定黑洞的热力学稳定性；同时得到有效力、圆轨道稳定性与最内稳定圆轨道（innermost stable circular orbit, ISCO），并导出了径向、纬向与方位角振荡频率及近星点进动频率（periastron precession frequency）。

Conclusion: 该工作为理解 Einstein–Maxwell 引力（Einstein–Maxwell gravity）的基本性质提供了关键洞见，并给出了 RN–AdS 黑洞解轨道动力学的系统性理论描述，可服务于后续高精度天体物理观测。

Abstract: This study investigates the topological thermodynamic properties and orbital dynamics of the Reissner--Nordström anti-de Sitter (RN--AdS) black hole with logarithmic corrections solution in Einstein--Maxwell gravity. By utilizing the Duan's $Φ$-mapping theory, we investigate the topological classes within the generalized three-parameter Sharma--Mittal entropy formalism. Employing different thermodynamic ensembles, including the canonical and mixed canonical ensembles, we explore the critical, divergence points and heat capacity, which help us determine the thermodynamic stability of the BH. In addition, the Hamiltonian formalism is employed to investigate the orbital and oscillatory motion of neutral test particles. By utilizing the effective potential approach, we examine the effective force, the stability of circular orbits, and the innermost stable circular orbit (ISCO). We further analyze the trajectories of test particles and derive the radial $\left(ω_{r}\right)$, latitudinal $\left(ω_θ\right)$, and azimuthal $\left(ω_φ\right)$ oscillation frequencies, together with the periastron precession frequency. This work provides key insights into the fundamental nature of Einstein--Maxwell gravity. Moreover, these results provide a comprehensive theoretical description of the orbital dynamics of the RN--AdS black hole solution and may be useful for future high-precision astrophysical observations.

</details>


### [175] [Phase transitions in self-gravitating objects and black holes: Common properties and interpretations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25786)
*Martin Blaschke,Petr Slaný*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 论文提出一个解析型双组分模型，将自引力系统的热演化与黑洞热力学联系起来，并通过维里—热容公式定位热容变号（稳定性阈值）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在建立一个简洁的解析框架，用以刻画自引力系统热演化与黑洞热力学之间的类比，从而获得对稳定性阈值的紧凑解析洞察。

Method: 模型由单原子非相对论理想气体（monatomic nonrelativistic ideal gas）与一个由 Jüttner 气体比热（specific heat）标定的有效正压支撑项构成；由配分函数（partition function）导出比热的精确修正贝塞尔函数（modified Bessel）表达，并给出在全温区精度良好的紧凑 Padé 近似（Padé approximants）。

Result: 这些输入给出闭式的维里—热容公式（virial-capacity formula），用于确定当支撑压强与热压强相当时热容改变符号的位置；随后将该行为与 Schwarzschild 反德西特黑洞（anti-de Sitter black holes）进行对比。

Conclusion: 尽管微观物理差异显著，两类系统的热容行为均由相互竞争的压力贡献所主导并发生转换；该替代模型为自引力系统及其黑洞类比中的稳定性阈值提供了紧凑的解析洞察。

Abstract: We develop a simple analytic two-component model that links the thermal evolution of self-gravitating systems to black-hole thermodynamics. The model consists of a monatomic nonrelativistic ideal gas and an effective barotropic support calibrated by the specific heat $\hat c_V^{\rm J}(Θ)$ of a Jüttner gas. From the partition function we obtain an exact modified Bessel expression for $\hat c_V^{\rm J}(Θ)$ and supply compact Padé approximants with good accuracy across all temperatures. These inputs yield a closed virial-capacity formula, which we use to determine where the heat capacity changes sign as the support pressure becomes competitive with the thermal pressure. We then contrast this behavior with Schwarzschild anti-de Sitter black holes. Despite the disparate microphysics, both systems exhibit a switch in heat-capacity behavior governed by competing pressure contributions. The surrogate model provides compact analytic insight into stability thresholds in self-gravitating systems and their black-hole analogues.

</details>


### [176] [Revisiting Kerr-Newman-(A)dS Black Holes in \(f(R)\) Gravity: A Kerr-Schild Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25992)
*Göksel Daylan Esmer,Saliha Türkmen*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在Kerr–Schild框架下重新推导f(R)引力中的Kerr–Newman–(A)dS度规，并考察非恒定曲率解的可能性；结果表明，场方程在该拟设下强制Ricci标量为常数，从而将带电情形下近期确立的不可能性（no-go）结果由无电荷黑洞推广至电charged黑洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已知在恒定曲率（constant-curvature）情形下，f(R)引力场方程约化为带有效宇宙学常数的Einstein形式，使f(R)黑洞解与广义相对论（general relativity）解直接对应；本文旨在以Kerr–Schild方法重新审视该推导，并检验非恒定曲率（non-constant-curvature）解是否存在。

Method: 采用Kerr–Schild拟设（ansatz）处理f(R)引力的场方程，先独立于Kerr–Schild函数求解Maxwell方程以确定电磁源，再处理引力场方程；并以二次f(R)引力（quadratic f(R) gravity）作为代表性模型检验非恒定曲率解的可能性。

Result: Maxwell方程可独立于Kerr–Schild函数求解，电磁源在场方程之前即可确定；在二次f(R)模型中，Kerr–Schild拟设下的场方程强制Ricci标量（Ricci scalar）为常数，所得几何仍归约为Kerr–Newman–(A)dS族。

Conclusion: 在所考察的框架内，非恒定曲率的带电黑洞解无法实现；该分析将近期针对无电荷黑洞（uncharged black holes）的不可能性结果（no-go result）推广至电charged配置。

Abstract: It is known that, in the constant-curvature regime, the field equations of $f(R)$ gravity reduce to the Einstein form with an effective cosmological constant, establishing a direct correspondence between stationary and axisymmetric black hole solutions in $f(R)$ gravity and their counterparts in general relativity, including the Kerr--Newman--(A)dS family. Within this framework, we revisit the derivation of these metrics using the Kerr--Schild approach for illustrative purposes. The Maxwell equations are solved independently of the Kerr--Schild function, allowing the electromagnetic source to be determined before addressing the gravitational field equations. We then investigate the possibility of non-constant-curvature solutions, taking quadratic $f(R)$ gravity as a representative model. Remarkably, the field equations within the Kerr--Schild ansatz force the Ricci scalar to be constant, reducing the resulting geometries to the familiar Kerr--Newman--(A)dS family. Thus, our analysis extends the recently established no-go result for uncharged black holes to electrically charged configurations within the framework considered here.

</details>


### [177] [Log-periodic radiation cores and light rings in pure quadratic bulk gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26138)
*Sudan Hansraj,Rio Bissessur*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 在 Böhmer–Jensko 体–边界引力（bulk–boundary formulation）的纯二次模型（pure quadratic model）中，正则中心恒星不存在，故转而研究奇异核心（singular cores）；对线性状态方程的封闭系统作动力学分析，在辐射值处系统可积，周期辐射核心每周期恰好各有一个稳定与不稳定光环（light ring），其半径构成趋于中心的交错几何级数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纯二次模型中任何具有正则中心的静态度规其中心密度与压强均为零，因此没有任何仿射状态方程（affine equation of state）能支持正则非真空恒星，需研究奇异核心以刻画非真空恒星的替代图景。

Method: 采用尺度不变变量（scale-invariant variables），使各向同性条件（isotropy condition）化为两函数间的单一自治关系；在此框架下考察保持自相似解为平衡态的封闭条件，并对线性状态方程的封闭系统作动力系统分析（焦点、鞍点、跨临界分岔）；在辐射值处借助 Tolman 积分与尺度不变性求解可积性。

Result: 线性状态方程下封闭系统含一个焦点、一个鞍点与一个具非零体标量（bulk scalar）的真空点，后者与鞍点经跨临界分岔（transcritical bifurcation）交换稳定性；辐射值处系统可积，焦点变为被对数周期解（log-periodic solutions）环绕的非线性中心。该封闭下含物质平衡态所在直线恰与圆零测地线（circular null orbits）线重合，故每个单调面半径的周期辐射核心每周期恰有一个稳定与一个不稳定光环，其半径构成两级交错几何级数，小振荡时公比趋于 2.165。

Conclusion: 奇异核心可在辐射情形下获得确定且可积的动力学，并内蕴规律性的光环结构；光学几何中自相似解为柱面、每个核心为周期管状，其鼓包与颈部分别承载这些光环，接近此类轨道的 MIT 袋模型（MIT bag-model）位形继承该结构。

Abstract: In the pure quadratic model of the Böhmer--Jensko bulk--boundary formulation of gravity, every static metric with a regular centre has vanishing central density and pressure, so no affine equation of state admits a regular non-vacuum star. We therefore study singular cores, in scale-invariant variables in which the isotropy condition is a single autonomous relation between two functions. Closures that keep the self-similar solutions as equilibria fix the stability of two vacuum lines but leave that of the matter-bearing equilibria undetermined, except at one unphysical point, so only an equation of state yields definite dynamics. For a linear equation of state the closed system has a focus, a saddle, and a vacuum point with nonzero bulk scalar that exchanges stability with the saddle in a transcritical bifurcation. At the radiation value the system is integrable, through the Tolman integral and scale invariance, and the focus becomes a nonlinear centre surrounded by log-periodic solutions. Radiation is also exactly where the line containing the matter-bearing equilibria of this closure coincides with the line of circular null orbits. Consequently every periodic radiation core with monotonic areal radius has exactly one stable and one unstable light ring per period, at radii forming two interlaced geometric progressions towards the centre with a common ratio tending to 2.165 for small oscillations. In the optical geometry the self-similar solution is a cylinder and each core a periodic tube, whose bulges and necks carry these rings. MIT bag-model configurations whose cores approach these orbits inherit this structure.

</details>


### [178] [Notes on non-equilibrium thermodynamics of Robinson-Trautman spacetimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26203)
*Glenn Barnich*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: Robinson-Trautman 时空由未来类光无穷远处标量场的四阶抛物型热方程刻画，可改写为渐近类时观测者的能量平衡，并在将非线性记忆解释为热的情况下等价于非平衡热力学第一定律，从而给出局域温度与熵密度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在把 Robinson-Trautman 时空的演化方程与热力学语言建立联系，为从渐近观测者视角理解时空几何演化提供热力学解释框架。

Method: 将未来类光无穷远（future null infinity）上标量场的四阶抛物型热型演化方程（parabolic heat-type evolution equation）改写为渐近类时观测者（asymptotically time-like observer）的能量平衡（energy balance）；再通过将非线性记忆（non-linear memory）解释为热，转化为非平衡热力学（non-equilibrium thermodynamics）语境下的第一定律（first law）。

Result: 由此得到局域温度（local temperature）以及从环境视角看显式增长的熵密度（entropy density）。

Conclusion: Robinson-Trautman 时空的演化可被赋予非平衡热力学形式，非线性记忆充当热流并导致熵密度的单调增长。

Abstract: Robinson-Trautman spacetimes are determined by a parabolic heat-type evolution equation of fourth order for a scalar field on future null infinity. This equation may be re-written as an energy balance for an asymptotically time-like observer. In turn, it becomes a first law in the context of non-equilibrium thermodynamics if the non-linear memory is interpreted as heat. This leads to a local version of temperature and a manifestly increasing entropy density from the viewpoint of the environment.

</details>


### [179] [Hyperbolic Chiral Numerical Schemes for General Relativity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26325)
*Adam Shaw*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 基于Plebanski手征形式及其3+1形式（Ashtekar变量）构造数值相对论演化方案；引入线性两参数规范固定并作非线性推广，得到一个强双曲且含约束阻尼的多项式型修正系统，以及12分量独立演化的共形（幺模）重构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始Ashtekar系统在数值演化中不稳定，长期演化会崩溃，需通过规范固定与重构获得适用于数值相对论（numerical relativity）的稳定强双曲（strongly hyperbolic）演化方程。

Method: 在线性层面构造动力学固定微分同胚（diffeomorphisms）与内部SO(3,C)旋转的两参数一阶规范固定族，经线性场重定义后系统分解为独立的12分量与4分量扇区；将该规范固定提升为最小非线性延拓并作3+1分解，得到多项式型强双曲修正方程并加入约束阻尼（constraint damping）；另提出共形（幺模，unimodular）重构使非线性12分量扇区完全独立演化。

Result: 三类系统均通过规范波收敛测试（gauge-wave convergence test）、噪声Minkowski背景上的鲁棒稳定性测试及长时间演化检验：未修正Ashtekar系统在T~325崩溃，而规范固定的双曲系统与共形系统稳定演化至T=2000，约束违反量与度规虚部维持在10^{-10}量级。

Conclusion: 规范固定与共形（幺模）重构可显著提升Ashtekar形式演化系统的数值稳定性，并保持多项式结构与约束阻尼特性，为手征表述下的数值相对论演化提供了可行方案。

Abstract: We develop numerical relativity evolution schemes based on Plebanski's chiral formulation of general relativity and its 3+1 form, Ashtekar's variables. At the linear level, we construct a two-parameter family of first-order gauge fixings that dynamically fixes both diffeomorphisms and internal SO(3,C) rotations; after a linear field redefinition, the resulting system splits into independent 12- and 4-component sectors. Lifting this gauge fixing to its minimal nonlinear extension and performing the 3+1 decomposition yields a strongly hyperbolic modification of Ashtekar's evolution equations that remains polynomial in the fields and includes constraint damping. We also introduce a conformal (unimodular) reformulation in which the nonlinear 12-component sector evolves completely independently of the remaining fields. All three systems are tested with gauge-wave convergence tests, robust-stability tests on a noisy Minkowski background, and long-time evolutions. The unmodified Ashtekar system crashes at T ~ 325, whereas the gauge-fixed hyperbolic and conformal systems remain stable up to T = 2000, with constraint violations and the imaginary part of the metric remaining at the 10^{-10} level.

</details>


### [180] [Twinor Theory in the Newton--Hooke Limit: A Nonrelativistic Schrödinger Equation for Arbitrary Spin](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26379)
*Chen-Hsu Chien*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文推导大质量 twinor 场方程在固定粒子质量与固定单位位移宇宙学加速度下的 Newton–Hooke 极限，并给出各正整数秩 2s 的精确解正则极限所满足的一阶层级方程组。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在建立大质量 twinor 场在 Newton–Hooke 背景下的非相对论极限框架，厘清自旋振幅与辅助旋量在去除静止能量后的分离结构，并考察高自旋秩下的约束条件。

Method: 在静态无挠背景上移除静止能量，将主导自旋振幅与有限序列辅助旋量（auxiliary spinors）分离；对每个正整数秩 2s 取精确解的正则极限，导出一阶层级（first-order hierarchy），并由其最低两式得到含曲率项与相对论源双投影源项的必要 Schrödinger 方程；标量情形另行约化标量波动方程。

Result: 最低两式给出主导振幅必须满足的 Schrödinger 方程，曲率项来自有序空间导数，源项由相对论源的两个投影确定；更高阶方程施加附加条件。在内部联络平凡、宇宙学加速度参数非零的齐次 Newton–Hooke 背景上，所有极限缩并源为零迫使 s≥3/2 的主导振幅为零。

Conclusion: 大质量 twinor 场的 Newton–Hooke 极限呈现层级化结构：低阶给出 Schrödinger 型必要条件，高阶构成额外约束；在特定齐次背景下，s≥3/2 的主导振幅被强制消失，自旋零情形可通过标量波方程约化单独处理。

Abstract: The Newton--Hooke limit of the massive twinor field equations is derived at fixed particle mass and fixed cosmological acceleration per unit displacement. On a static torsion-free background, removal of the rest energy separates the leading spin amplitude from a finite sequence of auxiliary spinors. For every positive integer rank $2s$, a regular limit of exact solutions satisfies a first-order hierarchy. Its lowest two equations give a necessary Schrödinger equation for the leading amplitude, with a curvature term from the ordered spatial derivatives and a source determined by two projections of the relativistic source. The higher equations impose additional conditions. On the homogeneous Newton--Hooke background with trivial internal connection and nonzero cosmological acceleration parameter, vanishing of all limiting contracted sources forces the leading amplitude to vanish for $s\geq\tfrac32$. A separate reduction of the scalar wave equation gives the spin-zero case.

</details>


### [181] [Low-Latency Alerts of Massive Black Hole Binaries in LISA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26411)
*Rahul Srinivasan,Lorenzo Copparoni,Chantal Pitte,Enrico Barausse,Roberto Trotta*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: TRUFFLE是一种面向LISA数据的基于模拟的推断（simulation-based inference）方法，通过预警网络与近并合网络双网络架构，在秒级时间内为超大质量双黑洞并合（MBHBs）提供无偏、经统计校准的后验估计，并在重叠源存在下保持稳健。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LISA可在并合前数天至数月观测超大质量双黑洞双星，为电磁波（electromagnetic）后续观测提供预警；但现有推断方法需同时满足快速、持续更新、统计校准及对重叠源稳健等要求，存在方法学缺口。

Method: 方法按时至并合（time to coalescence）Δt_c切换两个网络：预警网络（Δt_c∈[4 h, 2 d]）估计啁啾质量（chirp mass）、质量比与并合时间；近并合网络（Δt_c∈[-1 h, +4 h]）推断含天区位置在内的九个参数。两网络均处理最近六天数据，采用多波段（multiband）时域与频域表示，经transformer汇总后条件化归一化流（normalizing flow），并在完整轨道驱动LISA响应上、对30天观测历元区间摊销训练。

Result: 在存在重叠源（包括急旋极低质量比旋进（plunging EMRIs）、可分辨银河双星（Galactic binaries）及在主并合前两小时内并合的额外MBHBs）情形下，获得无偏后验；近并合后验可经基于似然的重点采样（importance sampling）校正，神经推断耗时数秒，单GPU上似然重加权约五分钟。

Conclusion: TRUFFLE满足了LISA预警后续观测对速度、更新频率、统计校准与抗重叠源稳健性的综合需求，为快速电磁波跟踪调度提供了可行技术路径。

Abstract: The Laser Interferometer Space Antenna (LISA) will observe massive black hole binaries (MBHBs) for days to months before merger, offering opportunities for advance electromagnetic follow-up. To enable such observations, inference methods must provide rapid, continuously updated, and statistically calibrated posterior estimates, while remaining robust to overlapping sources. We present TRUFFLE (Time-evolving Rapid Updates For Follow-up of LISA Events), a simulation-based inference method for MBHBs in LISA data designed to meet these requirements. Our approach uses two networks, selected according to the time to coalescence relative to the end of the analysis window, $Δt_c$. An early-warning network, operating at $Δt_c \in [4~\mathrm{h}, 2~\mathrm{d}]$, estimates the chirp mass, mass ratio, and coalescence time to support electromagnetic follow-up scheduling. A near-merger network, operating at $Δt_c \in [-1~\mathrm{h}, +4~\mathrm{h}]$, infers nine parameters, including the sky position needed for telescope pointing. Both networks process the most recent six days of data using multiband time- and frequency-domain representations, which are summarized by a transformer to condition a normalizing flow. The networks are trained on the full orbit-driven LISA response and amortized over a 30-day range of observation epochs. The near-merger posterior can be corrected through likelihood-based importance sampling, with neural inference taking seconds and likelihood reweighting approximately five minutes on a single GPU. We obtain unbiased posteriors in the presence of overlapping sources, including plunging extreme-mass-ratio inspirals, resolvable Galactic binaries, and additional MBHBs merging up to two hours before the primary merger.

</details>


### [182] [Bianchi cosmologies with four-dimensional groups of motion in New General Relativity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26474)
*Manuel Hohmann,Paul Martin Kull,Margus Saal*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在单参数新广义相对论（New General Relativity）框架下，研究具有四维对称群的Bianchi II、III、IX型度规远平行（metric teleparallel）时空动力学，给出最一般几何与物质描述、场方程及部分解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 度规远平行（metric teleparallel）框架中的Bianchi型时空动力学尚缺乏系统研究，尤其在新广义相对论（New General Relativity）的单参数族内，需要明确这些对称性约束下的最一般几何与物质形式，以检验其宇宙学含义。

Method: 首先证明Bianchi II、III、IX型各类中最一般的度规远平行几何由四个时间自由函数描述，另四个时间函数描述符合同样对称性的最一般能量-动量张量（energy-momentum tensor）；随后推导该几何类的新广义相对论场方程（field equations），并给出部分解。

Result: 所给解中既存在类似广义相对论（General Relativity）标准Friedmann-Lemaître-Robertson-Walker宇宙学（FLRW cosmology）特征的情形，也存在对其偏离的情形。

Conclusion: Bianchi II、III、IX型度规远平行时空在新广义相对论中可一致地约化为有限个时间函数并求解，且其解可覆盖从类FLRW行为到偏离标准宇宙学的行为，为检验度规远平行理论的宇宙学预言提供基础。

Abstract: We study the dynamics of the most general metric teleparallel spacetimes of Bianchi types II, III and IX, which admit a four-dimensional symmetry group, in the 1-parameter family of New General Relativity. We first show that in each of these classes the most general metric teleparallel geometry is described by four free functions of time, while another four functions of time describe the most general energy-momentum tensor compatible with the same symmetries. We then derive the field equations of New General Relativity for this class of geometries. Finally, we present some solutions to these field equations. Our results show that both solutions resembling features of the standard Friedmann-Lemaître-Robertson-Walker cosmology of General Relativity and deviations thereof exist.

</details>


### [183] [Superluminal propagation in the magnetic NED reconstruction of the extremal Dymnikova black hole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26560)
*J. M. Romero*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 在 \(\mathcal{I}^{-1}>0\) 分支上重构了 Dymnikova 几何的非线性电动力学（nonlinear electrodynamics, NED）源，并分析其固定背景上的电磁扰动；该重构不存在麦克斯韦极限（Maxwell limit），其非常光线速度在 \(r>r_{\rm lum}=(7/3)^{1/3}P\) 处超光速，故该 NED 分支仅数学自洽，渐近区因果性不可接受。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在为 Dymnikova 几何确定一个具体的磁非线性电动力学源，并检验该重构源在因果性（causality）与双曲性（hyperbolicity）上是否物理可接受，同时与 Tomizawa–Suzuki 定理的适用前提进行对照。

Method: 在 \(\mathcal{I}^{-1}>0\) 分支上反演重构 NED 源，考察其是否会退化为麦克斯韦理论；随后在固定背景上做电磁扰动分析，计算非常光特征锥（extraordinary characteristic cone）给出的横向速度 \(v_\perp^2=(3u-5)/2\)（\(u=r^3/P^3\)），分析主系统的逐点双曲性与局部二次能量，并导出径向有效势 \(V_{\rm em}\) 的精确约化形式及其视界附近与无穷远处的渐近行为。

Result: 重构不含麦克斯韦极限；非常光横向速度在 \(r>r_{\rm lum}=(7/3)^{1/3}P\) 时超光速。主系统在有限外部区域 \(r\ge r_e\) 逐点双曲且局部二次能量为正，但至空间无穷远不具有一致双曲性。有效势在视界附近满足 \(V_0\propto\varepsilon^2\)，在无穷远以 \(27r^4/(4P^6)\) 增长，并在数值网格上到 \(r=50P\) 保持为正。

Conclusion: 该 Dymnikova 重构不满足 Tomizawa–Suzuki 定理的前提（缺少麦克斯韦极限），却仍产生超光速的非常光子，说明该定理的假设并非超光速现象的必要条件。重构的 NED 分支作为 Dymnikova 背景之源在数学上自洽，但相对物理度规在渐近区不具因果可接受性；有效势的全局正性与准正规模（quasinormal）谱列为待解问题。

Abstract: We reconstruct the magnetic nonlinear electrodynamics (NED) source of the
  Dymnikova geometry on the branch \(\Iinv>0\) and analyze its
  electromagnetic perturbations on the fixed background. The reconstruction
  does not admit a Maxwell limit. The extraordinary characteristic cone
  gives a transverse speed \(v_\perp^2=(3u-5)/2\), with \(u=r^3/P^3\),
  which becomes superluminal for \(r>r_{\rm lum}=(7/3)^{1/3}P\). The
  principal system is pointwise hyperbolic in the finite exterior
  \(r\ge r_e\), with positive local quadratic energy, but not uniformly
  hyperbolic up to spatial infinity. The radial effective potential admits
  an exact reduction, \(V_{\rm em}=f\ell(\ell+1)\mathcal{A}/r^2+ff_{,r}q
  +f^2(q_{,r}+\frac34q^2)\), behaves as \(V_0\proptoε^2\) near the
  horizon, and grows as \(27r^4/(4P^6)\) at infinity. It is numerically
  positive on a fine grid up to \(r=50P\). The Dymnikova reconstruction
  does not satisfy the hypotheses of the Tomizawa--Suzuki theorem, since it
  lacks a Maxwell limit, yet it still produces superluminal extraordinary
  photons. The reconstructed NED branch is therefore mathematically
  consistent as a source of the Dymnikova background, but not causally
  acceptable with respect to the physical metric in the asymptotic region.
  The global positivity of the effective potential and the quasinormal
  spectrum are left as open problems.

</details>


### [184] [Relativistic Kinetic Theory of Collisionless Particles with Variable Mass and Frame Transformations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26585)
*Adrián Casado-Turrión,Paulo B. Ferraz,Mindaugas Karčiauskas,José Jaime Terente Díaz*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文将相对论动理学理论（relativistic kinetic theory）推广到质量可变的无碰撞点粒子气体，等价于粒子受标量力作用，并在任意共形标架（conformal frame）下给出协变构造与标架间变换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有相对论动理学通常假设粒子质量恒定；对质量可变（即受标量力）粒子气体的相空间几何与分布函数描述，以及不同共形标架之间的变换关系尚需系统建立。

Method: 以倾斜 Sasaki 度规（tilted Sasaki metric）构建适配变质量粒子的相空间几何，结合单体分布函数（one-particle distribution function）导出宏观可观测量的方法，并显式推导各物理量在共形标架间的变换。

Result: 证明了单体分布函数可始终取为规范不变（gauge-invariant）且标架不变（frame-invariant），且相应的 Liouville（无碰撞 Boltzmann）方程推广形式在每个标架中均成立。

Conclusion: 该工作为变质量粒子气体的相对论动理学提供了与标架无关的协变框架，并明确了共形标架变换下的操作性定义。

Abstract: In this work, we present the relativistic kinetic theory of a gas of collisionless point particles with non-constant mass, which can also be understood as being subject to a scalar force. Our results hold in an arbitrary conformal frame, and we explicitly derive how they transform between frames. The mathematical construction relies on a tilted Sasaki metric, whose associated phase-space geometry is adapted to particles of variable mass, as well as on the one-particle distribution function, from which macroscopic observables are derived. Finally, we show that the one-particle distribution function can always be taken to be gauge- and frame-invariant, and the corresponding generalisation of the Liouville (or collisionless Boltzmann) equation is satisfied in each frame.

</details>


### [185] [Which black hole modes ring freely? Horizon regularity, the forced ringdown transient, and GW250114](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26591)
*Jahed Abedi,Niayesh Afshordi*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 论文追问准正规模（quasinormal modes, QNMs）定义中"仅保留视界入射波"这一条件究竟排除了什么样的出射波，指出视界正则性要求出射波衰减不得慢于表面引力 κ，并导出一族"视界模（horizon modes）" ω = mΩ_H − ijκ。该类模不改变 QNM 谱，但影响并合后的铃宕（ringdown）建模；在 GW250114 中，基频模加第一视界模的模型相较基频模加第一泛音（overtone）更受数据偏好（贝叶斯因子 2–30）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统 QNM 定义通过"视界处只保留入射波"来筛除出射解，但该筛除条件对出射波的物理约束缺乏明确刻画。作者由此提出：视界正则性（horizon regularity）对被视为"丢弃"的出射波提出了什么要求，以及这类被丢弃成分是否在物理上可观测、尤其是否影响并合铃宕的信号建模。

Method: 对类标量扰动，在未来视界正则的参考系中要求一阶导数有界，据此分析出射波的衰减速率限制与光滑性条件，导出离散的视界模频率阶梯 ω = mΩ_H − ijκ（j = 1,2,…，Ω_H 为视界角速度）。进一步区分每个 QNM 的"受驱动部分（driven part）"与"自由部分（free part）"，并用 Schwarzschild 与 Kerr（主导 ℓ = m = 2）情形以及 GW250114 的铃宕模型对比（基频模加第一视界模 vs. 基频模加第一泛音）进行检验，辅以基于数值相对论（numerical relativity）校准波形的零噪声注入。

Result: 有界一阶导数排除衰减慢于 κ 的出射波，光滑性则挑出 ω = mΩ_H − ijκ 的频率阶梯。对 Schwarzschild 黑洞和 Kerr 主导 ℓ = m = 2 模，只有基频 QNM 衰减慢于 κ。由于穿过视界的源会以视界频率驱动外部，泛音在源被势垒遮挡之前持续处于受驱动状态，故在信号峰值附近拟合得到的泛音振幅一般并非自由振荡振幅。GW250114 中，基频模加第一视界模（ω = 2Ω_H − iκ）相对基频模加第一泛音的贝叶斯因子（Bayes factor）为 2–30；该拟合给出的残余黑洞更重、自旋更快，符合非线性反作用（nonlinear backreaction）的预期，且零噪声注入可复现该偏好与偏移。

Conclusion: 视界模的引入不改变 QNM 谱本身，但在并合铃宕中具有可观测意义：近峰值拟合的泛音振幅不应被直接解释为自由振荡振幅。GW250114 对"基频模 + 第一视界模"的偏好及残余参数的偏移可由广义相对论（general relativity）框架内的非线性反作用解释，且经数值相对论校准波形的零噪声注入得以复现，表明这些是广义相对论本身的特征而非分析假象。

Abstract: The quasinormal modes (QNMs) of a black hole are defined by keeping only ingoing waves at the horizon. We ask what horizon regularity requires of the outgoing waves this condition discards. For a scalar-like perturbation, bounded first derivatives in a frame regular at the future horizon rule out outgoing waves that decay more slowly than the surface gravity $κ$, and smoothness singles out a ladder of frequencies, $ω=mΩ_{\rm H}-ijκ$ with $j=1,2,\dots$ and $Ω_{\rm H}$ the horizon angular velocity. We call these the horizon modes. For Schwarzschild black holes and the dominant $\ell=m=2$ Kerr modes, only the fundamental QNM decays more slowly than $κ$. These local statements leave the QNM spectrum unchanged but matter for merger ringdowns, because a source falling through the horizon drives the exterior at the horizon frequencies. Each QNM then has a driven and a free part, and the free part takes over only once the driving decays faster than the mode. The fundamental mode separates from the driving early. The overtones stay driven until the source is hidden behind the potential barrier, so overtone amplitudes fitted near the signal peak are in general not those of free oscillations. In GW250114, a ringdown model of the fundamental mode plus the first horizon mode, $ω=2Ω_{\rm H}-iκ$, is preferred over the fundamental mode plus its first overtone, with Bayes factors between 2 and 30, for start times from $2M_f$ before to $8M_f$ after coalescence. The horizon-mode fit returns a remnant that is heavier and spins faster than the inspiral predicts, as expected from nonlinear backreaction: the horizon that emits the mode has not yet settled to its final mass and spin. Zero-noise injections of numerical-relativity-calibrated waveforms reproduce both the preference and the shift, which are therefore features of general relativity.

</details>


<div id='physics.plasm-ph'></div>

# physics.plasm-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [186] [Aggregating soft-photon recoil with a positive finite-step sampler](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26402)
*Nina Elkina,Alexander Pukhov*

Main category: physics.plasm-ph

TL;DR: 该文提出一种将软光子（soft photons）聚合成单一正向反冲的随机辐射反作用（stochastic radiation reaction）模型，保留交叉场量子电动力学（crossed-field QED）核的局部无发射概率与两个损失矩，并给出可避免逐次解析软发射的实用粒子模拟路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 逐次分辨单个软光子发射（individual soft emissions）在粒子模拟中代价过高，而高斯扩散（Gaussian diffusion）类近似无法保持能量的单向损失特征，因此需要兼顾物理保真度与计算可行性的随机模型。

Method: 把软光子聚合为单一正向反冲，同时保留交叉场QED核的局部无发射概率和两个损失矩；采用保持单侧能量损失（one-sided energy loss）的采样器，并引入状态反馈（state feedback）以减小有限步长冷却偏差（finite-step cooling bias）。

Result: 解析同步辐射矩（synchrotron moments）揭示了有限步长冷却偏差，且该偏差可通过状态反馈降低；激光与离子通道（ion-channel）测试检验了过度损失风险与有向辐射谱（directed radiation spectra）。

Conclusion: 该聚合式随机采样方案在无需解析单个软发射的前提下，为随机粒子模拟提供了可行且物理上更忠实的实现途径。

Abstract: We model stochastic radiation reaction by aggregating soft photons into one positive recoil, retaining the local no-emission probability and two loss moments of the crossed-field QED kernel. Unlike Gaussian diffusion, the sampler preserves one-sided energy loss. Analytical synchrotron moments expose a finite-step cooling bias, which state feedback reduces. Laser and ion-channel tests probe excessive-loss risk and directed radiation spectra, providing a practical route to stochastic particle simulations without resolving individual soft emissions.

</details>


<div id='hep-ph'></div>

# hep-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [187] [Another relation among the neutrino mass-squared differences?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.18781)
*I. Alikhanov*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 论文指出中微子质量平方差之间可能存在一个简洁代数关系（√Δm²₃₁+√Δm²₂₁)/(√Δm²₃₁−√Δm²₂₁)=√2，与最新全局拟合高度吻合，并据此给出对太阳与大气质量平方差的紧致约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确定中微子绝对质量标度（absolute neutrino mass scale）仍是粒子物理的重要挑战；建立中微子质量之间的精确数学联系有助于简化该问题，并可为质量产生机制（mass-generation mechanism）提供线索。

Method: 提出并检验质量平方差（mass-squared differences）之间的一种简单代数关系，将其与近期中微子振荡数据全局分析（global analyses of neutrino oscillation data）的最佳拟合值对比，并分析该关系对中微子扇区独立参数数目的削减作用及其在夸克与带电轻子物理中的等价表示。

Result: 该关系与全局拟合最佳拟合值吻合良好；据此得到 Δm²₂₁=(7.445±0.062)×10⁻⁵ eV²、Δm²₃₁=(2.548±0.041)×10⁻³ eV² 的紧致约束。

Conclusion: 下一代反应堆与加速器中微子实验将显著降低系统误差，其中江门地下中微子天文台（JUNO）可在亚百分比（sub-percent）水平独立测量这些参数，从而对该关系给出严格而直接的检验。

Abstract: Determining the absolute neutrino mass scale remains a compelling challenge in particle physics. Establishing precise mathematical connections among the neutrino masses could significantly facilitate this task and shed additional light on the underlying mass-generation mechanism. We highlight the possible existence of a simple algebraic relation between the mass-squared differences, $$\frac{\sqrt{Δm^2_{31}}+\sqrt{Δm^2_{21}}}{\sqrt{Δm^2_{31}}-\sqrt{Δm^2_{21}}}=\sqrt{2},$$ which agrees remarkably well with the best-fit values of recent global analyses of neutrino oscillation data. We then investigate the implications of adopting this relation and how it decreases the number of independent parameters in the neutrino sector. We demonstrate that there are alternative representations well known in quark and charged-lepton physics. Furthermore, we place a tight constraint on the solar neutrino mass splitting, $Δm^2_{21}=(7.445 \pm 0.062) \times 10^{-5} \text{ eV}^2$, and also obtain $Δm^2_{31}=(2.548 \pm 0.041) \times 10^{-3} \text{ eV}^2$. Next-generation reactor and accelerator neutrino experiments are expected to significantly reduce systematic uncertainties. In particular, the Jiangmen Underground Neutrino Observatory (JUNO) will measure these parameters independently of each other at the sub-percent level, thereby providing a stringent and direct test of our results.

</details>


### [188] [Automate the geometric SMEFT at dimension eight](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25016)
*Benoît Assi,Adam Martin*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 首次给出几何标准模型有效场论（geoSMEFT）的完整八维（dimension-eight）实现，以 UFO 模型形式供 MadGraph 等事件产生器使用，并配套开发了识别过程相关算符与生成限制卡（restriction card）的独立工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为使高阶有效场论（EFT）效应可用于对撞机（collider）现象学研究，需要一个包含全部八维算符、并保持一致截断阶数（至 1/Λ^4）的完整事件产生器实现，同时降低事件产生的计算成本。

Method: 构建包含全部 674 个重子数守恒（baryon-number-conserving）算符的 UFO 模型，费米子算符采用最小味破坏（minimal flavour violation），并同时提供 Murphy 基（Murphy basis）；一致纳入六维贡献与电弱输入参数修正至 1/Λ^4 阶，并将八维平方项作为 EFT 截断诊断量。通过独立计算与 Ward 恒等式（Ward identities）进行验证，另开发树级算符识别与限制卡生成工具。

Result: 模型经独立计算与 Ward 恒等式验证通过；配套工具可在树级（tree level）识别给定过程涉及的全部算符（含经由输入参数偏移进入者），报告其预期能量标度行为（energy scaling）并生成限制卡以削减事件产生成本；同一几何构造可将两点与三点函数（two- and three-point functions）计算推广至八维以上、直至 1/Λ^2 的任意阶。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了目前首个完整的八维 geoSMEFT 事件产生器实现及实用配套工具，模型与工具均已公开，可用于对撞机 EFT 研究并支持更高阶的解析推广。

Abstract: We present the first complete dimension-eight implementation of the geometric Standard Model Effective Field Theory (geoSMEFT) as UFO models for event generators such as MadGraph. The models contain all 674 baryon-number-conserving operators, with minimal flavour violation for the fermionic operators, and are also provided in the Murphy basis. We include dimension-six contributions and electroweak input-parameter corrections consistently through order $1/Λ^4$, and provide dimension-eight squared contributions as a diagnostic of EFT truncation. We validate the implementation against independent calculations and Ward identities. To make these models practical for collider studies, we develop a standalone tool that identifies all operators contributing to a given process at tree level, including those entering through input-parameter shifts. It reports their expected energy scaling and generates a restriction card to reduce the computational cost of event generation. The same geometric construction allows us to calculate two- and three-point functions beyond dimension eight, to arbitrary order in $1/Λ^2$. The models and the tool are publicly available.

</details>


### [189] [Resonance-aware NLOPS matching for off-shell $t\bar t+tW$ production in association with a Higgs boson](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25044)
*Patrick E. Hurley,Tomáš Ježo,Jonas M. Lindert*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该文提出新的NLO QCD蒙特卡罗产生器bb4lH，在共振感知的POWHEG-RES框架内以四味方案对t tbar H与tWH的完整离壳末态（双轻子衰变）进行统一描述并匹配部分子簇射，无需图移除或图减除方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: t tbar H与tWH产生模式在离壳区域相互交织，传统on-shell描述与图移除/减除方案难以统一且一致地处理，需要能同时包含双共振、单共振与非共振拓扑及干涉的统一计算框架。

Method: 在四味方案下以有质量b夸克计算Born过程pp→b bbar e+νe μ-νbarμ H，矩阵元包含全部共振拓扑、干涉与自旋关联，顶夸克与W玻色子均采用复质量方案（complex-mass scheme），基于OpenLoops振幅在POWHEG-RES中实现，并使用allrad多重辐射方案与基于矩阵元的共振投影器；半轻子事例可由双轻子事例获得。另给出两个新的on-shell POWHEG产生器（含首个tWH on-shell产生器）。

Result: 所有产生器均通过与已知固定阶结果及窄宽度极限（narrow-width limit）的比较得到验证；给出LHC上双轻子与半轻子通道的NLO与NLOPS预言。与对应on-shell描述相比，包容性观测量符合良好，但在对重建顶夸克线型敏感的观测量及tWH富集区域出现显著差异。

Conclusion: 离壳统一处理对LHC上t tbar H事例分析及t tbar H与tWH产生的分离具有直接意义，on-shell近似在这些敏感区域不足以保证精度。

Abstract: We present a new NLO QCD Monte Carlo generator for the complete off-shell final state encompassing $t\bar tH$ and $tWH$ production with dileptonic decays, matched to parton showers in the resonance-aware POWHEG-RES framework. At Born level the calculation corresponds to the process $pp\to b\bar b\,e^+ν_e\,μ^-\barν_μ\,H$ in the four-flavour scheme with massive $b$ quarks. The complete matrix elements include all double-resonant, single-resonant and non-resonant topologies, all interferences and spin correlations, with both top quarks and $W$ bosons treated in the complex-mass scheme. This approach provides a unified and consistent treatment of $t\bar{t} H$ and $tWH$ production modes without the need for a diagram-removal or diagram-subtraction prescription. The generator, dubbed $\texttt{bb4lH}$, is implemented in the POWHEG-RES framework with OpenLoops amplitudes, and takes advantage of the multiple-radiation scheme ($\texttt{allrad}$) and of matrix-element-based resonance projectors. Semileptonic events are also supported and can be obtained from dileptonic ones, including QCD radiation in the hadronic $W$-boson decay. In addition, we present two new on-shell POWHEG generators for $t\bar{t} H$ and $tWH$ production, the latter being the first of its kind. We validate all generators against known fixed-order results and in the narrow-width limit, and present NLO and NLOPS predictions for the LHC in the dileptonic and semileptonic channels. Tuned comparisons with the corresponding on-shell descriptions show good agreement for inclusive observables, while sizeable differences arise in observables sensitive to the reconstructed top-quark line shape and in $tWH$-enriched regions. These findings are of direct relevance for the LHC analysis of $t\bar{t} H$ events and for the separation of $t\bar{t} H$ and $tWH$ production.

</details>


### [190] [The BSM Rollercoaster, 1980-2000](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25060)
*Savas Dimopoulos*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文是作者对1978年至2000年代初超越标准模型（beyond-the-Standard-Model, BSM）物理的个人历史回顾，以"情感过山车"为线索，梳理两波乐观浪潮及其间的信心收缩，二者最终均因缺乏决定性实验证实而落空。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者旨在不仅记录当时提出了哪些理论，更要说明这些想法为何在当时令人信服、信心为何起伏，以及后续证据如何改变人们对"何为解释"的理解。

Method: 采用个人历史回顾（personal historical retrospective）的叙述方式，按时间顺序围绕理论动机与实验线索展开，包括自然性（naturalness）、Technicolor、质子衰变传闻、最小超对称标准模型（Minimal Supersymmetric Standard Model）、LEP与SLC精确数据、大额外维度（large extra dimensions）、宇宙学常数的人择论证（anthropic argument）、宇宙加速膨胀与弦景观（string landscape）等主题。

Result: 两波乐观（1978年前后与1991年LEP/SLC数据指向超对称统一）均未获决定性实验确认，最终因未发现超伴子（superpartners）及LEP悖论（LEP paradox）加剧而再度失望；自然性的智识地位因1987年人择论证、1998年宇宙加速发现与弦景观的出现而改变，并导向分裂超对称（Split Supersymmetry）。

Conclusion: 该回顾表明BSM物理的兴衰不仅取决于理论构想，也取决于实验证据对"可解释性"标准的重塑；后期证据改变了自然性论证的地位与理论探索的方向。

Abstract: This contribution is a personal historical retrospective on beyond-the-Standard-Model (BSM) physics from 1978 to the early 2000s, organized around its emotional rollercoaster. I trace two waves of optimism separated by a multiyear contraction of confidence, both ultimately ending in disappointment when decisive experimental confirmation failed to arrive.
  The story begins in 1978, when the Standard Model emerged as the front-runner. It then moves from the first wave of excitement over naturalness and Technicolor, rumored proton-decay events, and the construction of the Minimal Supersymmetric Standard Model, to the contraction of confidence in the late 1980s. A dramatic revival came in 1991, when the LEP and SLC precision data pointed toward supersymmetric unification, but it eventually gave way to renewed disappointment over the absence of superpartners and the sharpening of the LEP paradox.
  I also describe how the realization that Newtonian gravity had been tested only down to distances of order centimeters opened the door to large extra dimensions. Finally, I discuss how Weinberg's 1987 anthropic argument for the cosmological constant, the 1998 discovery of cosmic acceleration, and the emergence of the string landscape changed the intellectual status of naturalness itself and led to Split Supersymmetry.
  I have tried to describe not only what was proposed, but why these ideas felt compelling at the time, why confidence rose and fell, and how later evidence changed our sense of what could count as an explanation.

</details>


### [191] [Resonant Cavity Searches for Millicharged Dark Matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25846)
*Miguel González Albert,Ariel Arza,Saarik Kalia*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 现有谐振腔（resonant cavity）实验可用于搜寻轴子（axion）或暗光子（dark-photon）等超轻暗物质，本文指出同一装置亦对质量约 $1-100\,\mu\mathrm{eV}$ 的超轻毫电荷暗物质（millicharged dark matter, mDM）敏感，并预测其灵敏度可达分数毫电荷 $\varepsilon_m \sim 10^{-19}$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大量谐振腔实验原为搜寻轴子与暗光子暗物质而设计，作者希望说明这些既有装置可被复用于探测超轻毫电荷暗物质，从而拓展实验的物理目标而不需新建装置。

Method: 在背景磁场存在下，mDM 类比轴子表现为有效电流（effective current），可激发谐振腔模式；关键区别在于圆柱腔中轴子主要耦合横磁模（transverse magnetic, TM），而 mDM 主要耦合横电模（transverse electric, TE）。作者据此估算实验在可调谐并读出 TE 模时对 mDM 的灵敏度。

Result: 投影灵敏度显示可探测低至 $\varepsilon_m = e_m/e \sim 10^{-19}$ 的分数毫电荷；若 mDM 仅占暗物质总密度的子份额 $f_{\mathrm{mDM}} \lesssim 0.2\%$，则竞争性天体物理限制（astrophysical bounds）不再适用。

Conclusion: 在此子份额情形下，谐振腔实验可成为该质量区间内对 mDM 最灵敏的探测手段，凸显改造现有腔体 TE 模读取能力的科学价值。

Abstract: A plethora of resonant cavity experiments have been designed to search for ultralight dark matter (DM) candidates, such as axion or dark-photon DM. In this work, we show that these same apparatuses can be sensitive to ultralight millicharged dark matter (mDM) with masses $m_φ\sim1-100\,μ\mathrm{eV}$. Similar to axion DM, in the presence of a background magnetic field, mDM acts as an effective current which can excite the modes of a resonant cavity. Importantly, for a cylindrical cavity, axion DM dominantly couples to the transverse magnetic (TM) modes, whereas mDM dominantly couples to the transverse electric (TE) modes. We project the sensitivity to mDM that resonant cavity experiments can achieve if they are able to tune and readout their TE modes. We find that fractional millicharges as small as $ε_m=e_m/e\sim10^{-19}$ can be probed. If mDM constitutes only a subfraction $f_\mDM \lesssim0.2\%$ of the total DM density, competing astrophysical bounds do not apply. In this case, resonant cavities can be the most sensitive probes of mDM in this mass range.

</details>


### [192] [Inclusive and diffractive D meson photoproduction in $pPb$ collisions at the LHC using Pythia8 framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25933)
*Victor P. Goncalves,Luana Santana,Wolfgang Schäfer*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该文在Pythia8框架下系统分析了ep与超周边pPb碰撞中D介子的包容性与衍射光生（photoproduction）过程，并指出低横动量前向区域衍射贡献可超过15%，可在未来LHC运行中被观测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 质子与Pomeron的部分子分布函数（PDFs）存在不确定性，而D介子光生过程尤其是衍射（diffractive）成分的定量描述尚需检验，因此需要系统的理论预言与现有HERA数据对比。

Method: 基于Pythia8框架，采用不同的质子与Pomeron PDFs计算ep和超周边pPb碰撞中包容性与衍射D介子光生截面，并将结果与HERA上H1的包容性与衍射D*光生测量进行比较，同时给出LHC上D0的赝快度与横动量分布预言。

Result: 模型预言与H1测量进行了对比；结果显示在低横动量（low p_T）与前向赝快度（forward rapidity）区域，衍射贡献超过15%，提示衍射D0光生在未来LHC运行中具备可观测性。

Conclusion: 超周边pPb碰撞中包容性与衍射D0光生的测量有助于约束质子与Pomeron的PDFs，为理解衍射机制提供数据支持。

Abstract: We present a systematic analysis of inclusive and diffractive $D$-meson photoproduction in $ep$ and ultraperipheral $pPb$ collisions within the Pythia8 framework. Predictions obtained with different proton and Pomeron PDFs are compared with H1 measurements of inclusive and diffractive $D^*$ photoproduction at HERA. We also present predictions for the rapidity and transverse-momentum distributions of inclusive and diffractive $D^0$ photoproduction at the LHC. Our analysis indicates that the diffractive contribution exceeds 15\% at low $p_T$ and forward rapidities, suggesting that diffractive $D^0$ photoproduction could be accessible in future LHC runs. In addition, measurements of inclusive and diffractive $D^0$ photoproduction in ultraperipheral $pPb$ collisions can help constrain the proton and Pomeron PDFs.

</details>


### [193] [Predictions for Inclusive Production Cross Sections of the Higgs Boson at the LHC and HL-LHC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26281)
*Robin Hayes,Alexander Karlberg,Bernhard Mistlberger,Dermot Moran,Hannah Arnold,Gaetano Barone,Christian Biello,Johannes Blümlein,Valeria Botta,Christian Brønnum-Hansen,Alessandro Calandri,Benjamin Campillo,Alessandra Cappati,Andrea Cardini,Suman Chatterjee,Jiayi Chen,Michal Czakon,Valerio Dao,Goutam Das,Felix Eschment,Silvia Ferrario Ravasio,Giancarlo Ferrera,Alessandro Gavardi,Yanping Huang,Alexander Huss,Stephen Jones,Judith Katzy,Sven Yannick Klein,Matthew Lim,Stefano Manzoni,Josh McFayden,Sven-Olaf Moch,Alexander Mück,Marco Niggetiedt,Mathieu Pellen,Christian T. Preuss,Tom Schellenberger,Lukas Simon,Michael Spira,Lourdes Urda Gómez,Davide Valsecchi,Marco Vitti,Marius Wiesemann,Malgorzata Worek,Sebastian Wuchterl,Silvia Zanoli,Marco Zaro,Licheng Zhang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: LHC Higgs 工作组（LHCHWG）更新了 7–14 TeV 质子-质子碰撞中标准模型希格斯玻色子（Standard Model Higgs boson）各主要产生模式的总截面（inclusive cross sections）推荐值，中心值与 YR4 在给定不确定度内一致，但多数模式的理论不确定度减小，PDF 已成为最精确模式的主导误差来源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Yellow Report 4（YR4）之后，高阶计算与部分子分布函数（PDF）均有进展，且缺少 Run-3 的 13.6 TeV 能量点及 NNLO 以上的 PDF4LHC 推荐，需要更新截面推荐值并重新评估理论不确定度。

Method: 按产生模式分别采用专门计算：胶子聚变（gluon fusion）采用重顶夸克极限下的完整 N³LO 结果并含精确 NNLO 顶夸克质量效应；矢量玻色子聚变（vector-boson fusion）采用 N³LO 结构函数计算加非因子化修正；VH 中完成圈诱导 gg→ZH 的 NLO QCD 修正；ttH 采用 NNLO QCD 匹配 NNLL 软胶子重求和（soft-gluon resummation）并结合 NLO 电弱修正；bbH 采用四、五味方案匹配预言。N³LO 矩阵元与 NNLO PDF 的不匹配按过程估计为不确定度。

Result: 中心值与 YR4 在其不确定度范围内一致，多数产生模式的理论不确定度降低；胶子聚变的不匹配不确定度相对 YR4 翻倍，矢量玻色子聚变则估计得较好。对预测最精确的模式，PDF 现为领先的不确定度来源，其余模式仍由残余微扰不确定度主导。

Conclusion: 该结果取代 YR4 的总截面及 Ref. [arXiv:2402.09955] 的临时推荐，构成 LHCHWG 第五号报告（Report 5）的一部分。

Abstract: We present updated recommendations of the LHC Higgs Working Group (LHCHWG) for the inclusive production cross sections of a Standard Model Higgs boson in proton--proton collisions at centre-of-mass energies between $7$ and $14$ TeV, including the Run-3 energy of $13.6$ TeV, and for Higgs-boson masses between $120$ and $130$ GeV. All the major production modes are covered. The central values remain consistent with those of Yellow Report 4 (YR4) [arXiv:1610.07922] within the uncertainties quoted there, while the theoretical uncertainties are reduced for most production modes. The improvements rest on specific calculations rather than on a uniform advance: the complete N$^3$LO result in the heavy-top limit together with exact NNLO top-quark mass effects for gluon fusion; the N$^3$LO structure-function calculation supplemented by non-factorisable corrections for vector-boson fusion; the completion of the NLO QCD corrections to the loop-induced $gg\to ZH$ channel for $VH$; NNLO QCD matched to NNLL soft-gluon resummation and combined with NLO electroweak corrections for $t\bar{t}H$, and updated predictions for $tH$; and the matched four- and five-flavour-scheme prediction, with its explicit resummation-scale uncertainty, for $b\bar{b}H$. Since no PDF4LHC recommendation exists beyond NNLO, the mismatch between an N$^3$LO matrix element and NNLO distributions has to be carried as an uncertainty, estimated process by process: it is well determined for vector-boson fusion, whereas for gluon fusion it is doubled relative to YR4. PDFs are now the leading source of uncertainty for the most precisely predicted modes, while residual perturbative uncertainties dominate for the remainder. These results supersede the inclusive cross sections of YR4 and the ad interim recommendations of Ref. [arXiv:2402.09955], and form part of Report 5 of the LHCHWG.

</details>


### [194] [Who Moved My $Z$ Boson? Revealing Resonance Features Inside a Neural Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26291)
*Kevin Alberto Urquía Calderón,Midori Kato,Inar Timiryasov,Oleg Ruchayskiy*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该研究将机制可解释性（mechanistic interpretability）方法应用于基于真实LHC数据训练的生成式对撞机物理模型（ShellFlow），在模型内部发现并操控了代表Z、J/ψ与Υ共振峰（resonance）的特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机制可解释性方法此前已在语言与图像模型中揭示与人类对齐的概念，但尚不清楚类似方法能否从无标签数据训练的生成式物理模型中找到物理概念，并据此对物理量进行内部操控。

Method: 以在真实LHC数据上训练的神经生成式对撞机物理模型（ShellFlow）为对象，该模型仅学习末态粒子运动学（kinematics）而未获知哪些μ子来自共振。研究基于神经网络梯度（gradients）构造特征，并用模式识别（pattern recognition）方法提升其选择性。

Result: 在ShellFlow内部发现可表示共振的特征，如Z、J/ψ与Υ；利用这些特征可将Z玻色子质量在约80 GeV与105 GeV之间改变，去除该特征则使Z峰变宽（broader Z peak），而两者情形下其余不变质量谱（invariant mass spectrum）基本保持不变。

Conclusion: 在无标签数据上训练的生成式粒子物理模型可在内部被修改以改变某一物理量，表明机制可解释性可为物理模型提供可解释且可控的内部表征。

Abstract: Methods used in mechanistic interpretability have revealed human-aligned concepts inside language and image models. In this paper, we search for physics concepts inside a neural a generative collider physics model trained on real LHC data (ShellFlow). We discover features inside ShellFlow that represent resonances, such as the $Z$, $J/ψ$, and $Υ$. The network was never told which muons come from a resonance, it was only trained to generate the kinematics of the final-state particles. We use these features to alter aspects of the $Z$ boson: we change its mass between $\sim80$ and $105$ GeV, and removing such a feature from the neural network produces a broader $Z$ peak. In both cases the rest of the invariant mass spectrum remains mostly unchanged. The feature was built from the gradients of the neural network, and its selectivity was built using pattern recognition methods. A generative particle physics model trained on unlabeled data can be modified internally to change a physical quantity.

</details>


### [195] [Future avenues for precision oscillation measurements with compact movable reactors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26418)
*Matheus Hostert,Stephan A. Meighen-Berger,Maxim Pospelov*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该文提出一种未来实验方案：将可移动反应堆反中微子源（reactor antineutrino source）置于江门地下中微子天文台（JUNO）附近的短基线（L≲3 km）处，借助JUNO的大有效质量，在数年内实现超越大亚湾（Daya Bay）精度的θ₁₃测量。结果表明地下微堆可将θ₁₃相对精度提升至约4%，地表反应堆约三年可达2.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有及计划中的中微子实验均预计无法超越大亚湾（Daya Bay）对θ₁₃的测量精度，进一步提升精度需要创新策略以压低统计与系统不确定性（statistical and systematic uncertainties）。

Method: 考虑两种方案：位于地下实验室通道硐室中的O(20 MWth)微型反应堆（microreactor），以及位于地表的O(400 MWth)反应堆机组；两者均由近源小通量监测探测器（flux monitor）观测，同时JUNO在两条基线（two baselines）上测量反中微子通量以压制相关通量与探测器系统误差。基于大亚湾逐同位素能谱（per-isotope spectra）及其协方差（covariance），分别对高丰度低浓铀（HALEU）微型堆芯与低浓铀（LEU）堆芯建模，并讨论各类系统不确定性来源及其对θ₁₃精度的影响。

Result: 地下微堆可在十年内将JUNO的θ₁₃相对精度提升三至四倍，达到约4%；地表反应堆若能将反应堆迁移期间的发射率监测到亚百分比（sub-percent）水平，约三年即可达到当前全局拟合精度2.5%。

Conclusion: 可移动反应堆源与JUNO大探测质量的组合，为超越大亚湾精度、进一步精确测量θ₁₃提供了可行路径，其中系统不确定性（尤其发射率监测）是决定精度的关键因素。

Abstract: No planned neutrino experiment is expected to surpass Daya Bay's precision on $\thone$. Doing so requires innovative strategies to reduce statistical and systematic uncertainties. In this study, we consider a futuristic program with a movable reactor antineutrino source placed within short distances ($L\lesssim 3\,\km$) from the Jiangmen Underground Neutrino Observatory (JUNO) detector. Due to the large fiducial mass of JUNO, even a relatively small reactor can produce a statistically significant number of events over a few years to probe the $\lesssim 8.7\% \,\nuebar$ disappearance set by $\thone$. We consider two possibilities: a $\mathcal{O}(20\mwth)$ microreactor in alcoves of the access tunnel to the underground laboratory, and a $\mathcal{O}(400 \mwth)$ unit on the surface. In both cases the reactor is observed by a small flux monitor detector near the source, while JUNO measures the antineutrino flux at two baselines, suppressing correlated flux and detector systematics. Using the measured Daya Bay per-isotope spectra and its covariance, we model a high-assay low-enriched uranium (HALEU) core for the microreactor and a low-enriched uranium (LEU) core for the surface unit, and discuss various potential sources of systematic uncertainties and their impact on the precision of $\thone$. The underground microreactor could improve on JUNO's reach by a factor of three to four, to $\relsth \simeq 4\%$ over a decade, while the surface reactor would reach the present global-fit precision, $\relsth=2.5\%$, in about three years, provided the emission rate is monitored across reactor relocations to the sub-percent level.

</details>


### [196] [A New Gravitational Wave Power Law from Dissipative Sound Waves in Cosmological Phase Transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25581)
*Huai-Ke Guo,Sihao Xiao,Yang Xiao,Jin Min Yang,Yang Zhang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该文指出，宇宙学一阶相变（first-order phase transition）产生的声波源在耗散等离子体（dissipative plasma）中演化时，引力波（GW）谱在完美流体结果之外额外获得 $q^{-2}$ 的高频幂律压低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 引力波谱中的幂律行为（power-law behavior）可揭示源的物理本质；已有对宇宙学一阶相变声波源的描述多基于完美流体（perfect-fluid）近似，忽略了等离子体耗散对谱形的影响。

Method: 在耗散等离子体中分析声波源的耗散演化，考察晚期强阻尼极限（strong-damping limit）下的引力波谱，并与完美流体结果对比，同时讨论源轮廓（source profile）、等离子体输运性质与源寿命的作用。

Result: 相对完美流体结果，谱获得额外的 $q^{-2}$ 压低；该指数对源轮廓基本不敏感，而转折点（turnover）与压低强度可探测等离子体输运及源寿命。弱耦合隐藏扇区（hidden sector）等离子体中的转折频率对规范耦合（gauge coupling）敏感，可落在空间干涉仪目标所在的毫赫兹（millihertz）频段。

Conclusion: 精细的引力波谱为探测微观等离子体动力学（microscopic plasma dynamics）提供了新途径。

Abstract: Power-law behavior in cosmological gravitational wave (GW) spectra reveals the physical nature of their sources. For sound waves generated by cosmological first-order phase transitions, we identify a new high-frequency power law arising from the dissipative evolution of the acoustic source. In a dissipative plasma, the GW spectrum acquires an additional $q^{-2}$ suppression relative to the perfect-fluid result in the late-time, strong-damping limit. The exponent is largely insensitive to the source profile, while the turnover and suppression strength probe plasma transport and the source lifetime. In particular, the turnover frequency in weakly coupled hidden sector plasmas is sensitive to the underlying gauge coupling and can lie in the millihertz band targeted by space-based interferometers, opening a way to probe microscopic plasma dynamics through the detailed GW spectrum.

</details>


### [197] [Geometric Formulation of Covariant Spin Operator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24946)
*Yi-Ning Wang,Jiang-Hao Yu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该文将粒子自旋空间视为速度流形上的伴丛纤维，引入广义相对论式的平行移动，把不同速度处的自旋移至共同参考系后耦合，并借助旋量-螺旋度变量构造协变的螺旋度振幅基。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相对论量子理论中洛伦兹推进（Lorentz boosts）导致动量依赖的维格纳转动（Wigner rotations），使任意参考系下粒子自旋的耦合变得复杂；不同粒子自旋位于不同纤维上，缺乏统一的比较与耦合方式。

Method: 把粒子自旋空间表述为速度流形（velocity manifold）上伴随丛（associated bundle）的纤维，类比广义相对论（general relativity）引入平行移动（parallel transport），并利用旋量-螺旋度变量（spinor-helicity variables）构造沿测地线的平行移动算符，证明其由小群协变性（little group covariance）唯一确定。

Result: 该构造给出协变自旋算符与轨道算符的显式形式，并得到具有确定自旋与轨道角动量的协变三点振幅基（3-point amplitude basis）；更高点振幅可构造性地获得。

Conclusion: 所建立框架可应用于振幅、形状因子（form factors）与关联函数（correlation functions）等的协变分波展开（partial wave expansion），为任意参考系下的自旋耦合提供统一表述。

Abstract: In relativistic quantum theory, Lorentz boosts induce momentum-dependent Wigner rotations, complicating the coupling of particle spins in arbitrary frames. We describe particle spin spaces as fibers of an associate bundle over the velocity manifold, and different particle's spins residing at distinct fibers. Analogy to general relativity, we introduce parallel transport to bring spins at different velocities into a common frame, where they can be coupled. Using spinor-helicity variables, we construct the parallel transport operator along geodesics and show how it is uniquely determined by little group covariance. This construction gives explicit form of the covariant spin and orbital operators, and yields a covariant 3-point amplitude basis with definite spin and orbital angular momentum. Higher-point amplitudes can be constructively obtained and applied to the covariant partial wave expansion for amplitudes, form factors, and correlation functions, etc.

</details>


### [198] [Weyl-induced ultrasoft renormalization of the static QCD potential](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26362)
*Michael Wilson*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 在势非相对论量子色动力学（potential nonrelativistic QCD, pNRQCD）框架下，推导了静态色单态势（static color-singlet potential）中首个曲率相关（curvature-dependent）的超软反常维度（ultrasoft anomalous dimension）。结果表明曲率扇区的紫外发散在 $\\alpha_s^2$ 阶即出现，比平直空间贡献早两阶。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文旨在刻画弯曲时空背景下重夸克偶素（quarkonium）类系统中静态势的曲率修正，特别是确定曲率相关算符的紫外行为及其反常维度，以弥补平直空间结果无法覆盖的曲率扇区重整化结构。

Method: 采用 pNRQCD 有效场论方法：在 Ricci 平坦（Ricci-flat）背景下，由电 Weyl 张量（electric Weyl tensor）在短时色电场关联函数（chromoelectric correlator）中引入 $t^{-2}$ 项，计算偶极自能（dipole self-energy）；在光学维数正规化减除方案（optical dimensional subtraction scheme）中求解补偿软红外极点（soft infrared pole）与有限单圈匹配系数（matching coefficient），并用物理度规下的费米子直接计算进行交叉验证。

Result: 对 $W=C_{\\mu\\alpha\\nu\\beta}r^\\mu u^\\alpha r^\\nu u^\\beta$ 与 $V_s^{(W)}=c_W W/r$，得到 $\\nu\\,\\partial_\\nu c_W=C_F C_A\\alpha_s^2/(3\\pi)$；同时给出曲率扇区 $\\alpha_s^2$ 阶紫外发散、有限轻味（light-flavor）项，以及受调控红外重叠（regulated infrared overlap）在消逝曲率重标度（evanescent curvature rescaling）下的抵消与软/超软常数的补偿变换。

Conclusion: 在微扰绝热区间（perturbative adiabatic regime），该反常维度确定了静态能量中曲率相关 $\\alpha_s^2\\ln\\alpha_s$ 项的系数，为弯曲背景下静态势的曲率修正提供了重整化群（renormalization group）层面的定量预言。

Abstract: We derive the leading curvature-dependent ultrasoft anomalous dimension of the static color-singlet potential in potential nonrelativistic QCD. In a Ricci-flat background the electric Weyl tensor introduces a $t^{-2}$ term in the short-time chromoelectric correlator. The resulting dipole self-energy is ultraviolet divergent at order $α_s^2$ in the curvature sector, two orders earlier than the flat-space contribution. For $W=C_{μανβ}r^μu^αr^νu^β$ and $V_s^{(W)}=c_W W/r$, we obtain $ν\,\partial_νc_W=C_F C_Aα_s^2/(3π)$. We calculate the compensating soft infrared pole and the finite one-loop matching coefficient in an optical dimensional subtraction scheme. A direct physical-metric fermion calculation reproduces the finite light-flavor term. We also exhibit the cancellation of the leading regulated infrared overlap and the compensating transformation of soft and ultrasoft constants under an evanescent curvature rescaling. In the perturbative adiabatic regime the anomalous dimension fixes the coefficient of a curvature-dependent $α_s^2\lnα_s$ term in the static energy.

</details>


### [199] [Thermal Resummation Effects on Electroweak Phase Transitions and Gravitational Waves in Conformal and Non-Conformal 2HDMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26417)
*Yu-Bo Zhao,Ji-Wei Li,Zhi-Wei Wang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该文量化了热重求和方案选择对电弱相变与引力波预测的不确定性，发现多场部分修饰（partial dressing, PD）通常给出最强、最过冷的相变及最大的引力波峰值振幅，且方案差异可高达约九个数量级，热重求和是主要理论不确定来源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 热重求和（thermal resummation）是有限温度有效势计算中的关键步骤，但其方案依赖性对电弱相变（electroweak phase transition）与引力波（gravitational wave）预言的影响缺乏系统量化，尤其在经典共形（classically conformal）与非共形双希格斯二重态模型（two-Higgs-doublet model）中尚不明确。

Method: 在相同的零温输入下，比较保留完整热函数、并由能隙方程（gap equations）确定标量质量与混合的多场部分修饰（PD）方案与Parwani、Arnold-Espinosa（AE）两种传统方案，覆盖共形与非共形双希格斯二重态模型的参数扫描，并检验PD中由于修饰蝌蚪项非梯度性质导致的积分路径依赖。

Result: 共形模型中完成的一阶相变在三种方案下均占主导；PD给出最强过冷，$T_p/T_c \simeq 0.5$，而Parwani与AE为$T_p/T_c \gtrsim 0.8$，且在三种方案均预言完成的每一点上PD的引力波峰值振幅最大。非共形模型不存在统一排序，方案选择还在大范围参数空间内改变热历史类型；代表性基准点上PD与AE的峰值振幅相差最多约九个数量级、峰值频率相差约五十倍。路径依赖使相变温度变化不超过约7%、峰值振幅变化至多约三倍，小于方案间最大差异。

Conclusion: 热重求和方案的选择是电弱相变与引力波预言中的主要不确定来源，其影响超过PD方案自身的路径歧义；因此相关预言需明确报告所采用的方案并评估其系统误差。

Abstract: We quantify how thermal resummation affects electroweak phase transitions and gravitational-wave predictions in classically conformal and non-conformal two-Higgs-doublet models. We compare multi-field partial dressing (PD), which retains the full thermal functions and determines scalar masses and mixing from gap equations, with the Parwani and Arnold-Espinosa (AE) prescriptions at identical zero-temperature inputs. Completed first-order transitions dominate the conformal scan under all three prescriptions. PD typically gives the strongest and most supercooled transitions, reaching $T_p/T_c \simeq 0.5$ versus $T_p/T_c \gtrsim 0.8$ for Parwani and AE. At every conformal point where all three predict completion, PD gives the largest gravitational-wave peak amplitude, followed by Parwani and AE. The non-conformal model has no universal ordering, and the prescription changes the type of thermal history across extended regions of parameter space. For representative benchmarks, PD and AE differ by up to about nine orders of magnitude in peak amplitude and a factor of about fifty in peak frequency. In multi-field PD, the dressed tadpole is not a gradient, so the reconstructed potential depends on the integration path. In benchmark tests, changing the path shifts the transition temperatures by at most about 7% and the peak amplitude by up to a factor of about three. This intrinsic ambiguity is smaller than the largest differences between prescriptions. Thermal resummation is a major source of uncertainty in these phase-transition and gravitational-wave predictions.

</details>


### [200] [Self-gravity depletes superradiant clouds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26541)
*Mateja Bošković,Lucas Gathercole,Thomas F. M. Spieksma,Samuel J. Witte*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 黑洞周围超轻标量场的超辐射云并非如常假设那样表现为自由场；云的自引力会在能级间重新分配粒子，显著耗竭云质量，因此超辐射信号的评估必须同时考虑自引力与环境效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型通常将黑洞周围的超辐射云（superradiant cloud）视为自由场，认为各能级独立增长并仅通过引力波（gravitational-wave）辐射缓慢衰减；本文旨在检验这一自由场近似是否成立。

Method: 通过分析云的自引力（self-gravity）效应，并将其与轴子自相互作用（axion self-interactions）进行类比，考察自引力在能级间重新分配粒子的作用及其相对强度，同时分析双星伴星（binary companion）对云耗竭的影响。

Result: 自引力使云质量耗竭数个量级，并将粒子供给更延展的能级；当衰变常数（decay constant）f_a ≳ 6×10^16 GeV (α/0.1)（α为引力精细结构常数，gravitational fine-structure constant）时，自引力超过自相互作用；双星伴星可部分抵消这种耗竭。

Conclusion: 自由场极限从未实现；任何超辐射信号（superradiant signals）的评估都必须同时计入自引力与云所处的环境。

Abstract: Superradiant clouds of ultralight scalars around black holes are often modeled as free fields, whose levels grow independently and decay slowly through gravitational-wave emission. We show that this limit is never realized. The cloud's self-gravity redistributes particles between levels, just as in the case of axion self-interactions, and dominates over self-interactions for decay constants $f_a\gtrsim6\times10^{16}\,{\rm GeV}\,(α/0.1)$, with $α$ the gravitational fine-structure constant. Self-gravity depletes the cloud by orders of magnitude and feeds more extended levels, weakening all signals that rely on the existence of a large cloud mass. A binary companion, however, can partly offset the depletion. Any assessment of superradiant signals must therefore account for both self-gravity and the cloud's environment.

</details>


### [201] [Majorana dark matter from a thick-to-thin brane junction: Modal stability, real-mediator coannihilation, and a dark-radiation imprint](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25872)
*F. A. Brito*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该文表明，七维厚-薄膜片交界（brane junction）的局域谱可容纳一个热遗迹马约拉纳暗物质（Majorana dark matter）候选者，其稳定性由模态选择定则而非人为暗宇称保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索无需人为施加暗宇称（dark parity）即可获得稳定马约拉纳暗物质粒子的几何机制，并考察其遗迹丰度与宇宙学可观测效应。

Method: 在 D=7 厚-薄膜片交界处研究局域费米子谱：壁宽各向异性（anisotropy）隔离出轻的交界费米子，四维马约拉纳质量将其劈裂为两个近邻态；横向轮廓的因子化对全部三次耦合施加模态选择定则（modal selection rule），配合软形变背景的弛豫实现较轻态稳定。

Result: 由于局域媒介粒子为实标量，相同马约拉纳粒子以 s 波湮灭，遗迹耦合几乎与马约拉纳质量无关；交界扇区为标准模型单态（Standard-Model singlet），其轻手征模构成暗辐射（dark radiation）来源。现有 CMB 数据限制其退耦温度，对 10 GeV 至 1.5 TeV 的暗物质质量给出 0.05 ≲ ΔN_eff ≲ 0.076。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了几何诱导稳定的马约拉纳暗物质方案，其暗辐射贡献落在下一代 CMB 巡天（next-generation CMB surveys）可探测范围内。

Abstract: We show that the localized spectrum of a $D=7$ thick-to-thin brane junction can host a thermal Majorana dark-matter candidate. The anisotropy of the wall widths isolates a light junction fermion, which a four-dimensional Majorana mass splits into two nearby states. The factorized transverse profiles impose a modal selection rule on all cubic couplings, and together with the relaxation of the softly deformed background this makes the lighter state stable without an imposed dark parity. Because the localized mediator is a real scalar, identical Majorana particles annihilate in the $s$ wave, and the relic coupling becomes nearly independent of the Majorana mass. The junction sector is a Standard-Model singlet whose light chiral mode is a source of dark radiation. Current CMB data restrict its decoupling temperature and imply $0.05\lesssim\dNeff\lesssim0.076$ for dark-matter masses between $10\GeV$ and $1.5\TeV$, a range accessible to next-generation CMB surveys.

</details>


<div id='math-ph'></div>

# math-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [202] [Canonical and Canonoid Transformations of Hamiltonian Systems on $q$-Cosymplectic and $q$-Contact Manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24961)
*Giuseppe Marmo,Cristina Sardón,Xuefeng Zhao*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 论文研究 q-余辛（q-cosymplectic）与 q-接触（q-contact）结构上的典范与类典范变换（canonoid transformations），给出局部正规形、无穷小自同构刻画与 Noether 型守恒/耗散关系，并用相对论质量壳约束与单极子电荷的例子加以说明，最后指出双单极子情形下均匀理论的拓扑障碍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: q-余辛与 q-接触结构为预辛（presymplectic）与预接触（precontact）几何补充了额外的一形式与归一化 Reeb 向量场族，但相对于这些数据的典范与类典范变换、尤其是其在约束力学系统（constrained mechanical systems）中的来源尚需系统研究。

Method: 对 q-余辛与均匀 q-接触（uniform q-contact）情形建立局部正规形（local normal forms），刻画无穷小自同构（infinitesimal automorphisms），推导对称性与守恒或耗散量之间的 Noether 型关系；对生成元为 X 的单参数类典范变换群，证明 [X, X_H] 为哈密顿的并确定其哈密顿函数。

Result: 在均匀 q-接触情形，定义形式的同步常数缩放（constant rescalings）对任意哈密顿量给出类典范变换；用正则相对论质量壳约束与磁单极场中电荷的全局拉格朗日描述加以例证，区分约束轨道参数与物理时间并厘清规范方向的作用，恢复了自由相对论粒子的总角动量与电荷–单极子系统的 Poincaré 角动量。对双单极子构造非均匀 2-接触结构，并证明以指定规范生成元为 Reeb 场的均匀结构存在拓扑障碍；不可通约磁荷还进一步阻碍产生单一接触流形的中间商（quotient）。

Conclusion: 该示例揭示了均匀理论的局限，并构成将其推广到非均匀结构（non-uniform structures）的动机。

Abstract: $q$-Cosymplectic and $q$-contact structures equip presymplectic and precontact geometry with additional one-forms and a normalized family of Reeb vector fields. We study canonical and canonoid transformations relative to these data, with particular attention to their origin in constrained mechanical systems. In the $q$-cosymplectic and uniform $q$-contact cases, we establish local normal forms, characterize infinitesimal automorphisms, and obtain Noether-type relations between symmetries and conserved or dissipated quantities. For a one-parameter group of canonoid transformations with generator $X$, we show that $[X,X_H]$ is Hamiltonian and determine its Hamiltonian function. In the uniform $q$-contact case, simultaneous constant rescalings of the defining forms provide canonoid transformations for every Hamiltonian. The geometric constructions are illustrated by regular relativistic mass-shell constraints and by global Lagrangian descriptions of a charge in magnetic monopole fields. These examples distinguish parameters along constraint orbits from physical time and clarify the role of gauge directions. They also recover the total angular momentum of free relativistic particles and the Poincaré angular momentum of the charge--monopole system. For two monopoles, we construct a non-uniform $2$-contact structure and prove a topological obstruction to a uniform structure with the prescribed gauge generators as Reeb fields. Irrationally related magnetic charges additionally obstruct the intermediate quotient that would produce a single contact manifold. This example identifies a limitation of the uniform theory and motivates its extension to non-uniform structures.

</details>


### [203] [On Hölder Equicontinuity of the Integrated Density of States for Random Schrödinger Operators on $\mathbb{Z}^d$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25077)
*Jan Fischer,David Hasler*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该文研究 $\mathbb{Z}^d$ 上由平移不变跳跃矩阵与独立同分布单点随机势构成的随机薛定谔算子 $H_\lambda=H_0+\lambda V$，在跳跃矩阵满足正则性假设下证明其积分态密度（integrated density of states）的 Hölder 连续性，指数可达 $2/3$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机薛定谔算子的积分态密度正则性（regularity）是理解能谱与输运性质的基础问题；已有结果多依赖无序强度或特定模型结构，本文旨在放宽对跳跃矩阵与无序强度的限制。

Method: 将算子分解为平移不变部分 $H_0$ 与随机势 $\lambda V$，其中单点势相互独立且分布绝对连续；在 $H_0$ 的正则性假设下，采用解析工具估计积分态密度的 Hölder 模。

Result: 在给定假设下，积分态密度被证明具有 Hölder 连续性，指数至 $2/3$，且该结论与无序强度 $\lambda$ 无关。

Conclusion: 结果表明积分态密度的正则性可由跳跃矩阵的正则性假设单独保证，不依赖无序强度，指数 $2/3$ 为摘要所给的上界范围。

Abstract: We study a random Schrödinger operator $H_λ= H_0 + λV$ on $\mathbb{Z}^d$ consisting of a translation invariant hopping matrix $H_0$ and a random potential $V$, given by independently distributed single site potentials with absolutely continuous distributions. Assuming a regularity assumption for the hopping matrix $H_0$, we show Hölder continuity of the integrated density of states of $H_λ$ up to exponents $2/3$, independently of the disorder strength $λ$.

</details>


### [204] [Interacting framework for the free magnetic dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25285)
*Giuseppe De Nittis*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究匀强磁场中连续费米子（continuum fermions）自由动力学的严格多体理论，证明Bogoliubov动力学保持由磁相干态框架（magnetic coherent-state frame）诱导的渐近准局域结构当且仅当它由Landau哈密顿量的函数生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该工作属于一个以磁相干态框架在CAR代数（CAR algebra）上诱导的渐近准局域结构（asymptotic quasi-local structure）为基础、旨在建立匀强磁场中连续费米子严格多体理论的研究计划，本文作为其第二篇，聚焦自由动力学这一基础情形，为后续研究微扰与相互作用磁动力学提供自洽框架。

Method: 对保持局域结构的Bogoliubov动力学进行分类，即证明其恰由Landau哈密顿量的函数生成；对该类动力学以闭式（closed form）计算跃迁系数（hopping coefficients），并分析Lieb-Robinson速度、生成元定义域及准局域代数（almost-local algebra）的归属关系。

Result: 对该类动力学导出闭式跃迁系数，证明Lieb-Robinson速度为零，并刻画准局域代数位于生成元定义域的条件；对Landau哈密顿量而言动力学呈周期性。进一步描述基态（ground states）与各温度下的KMS态，证明其在磁平移（magnetic translations）下的遍历性（ergodicity），并将正温KMS态还原为局域Gibbs态的热力学极限，最后把精确跃迁算子与Hofstadter模型相联系。

Conclusion: 结果确认框架诱导的局域结构是研究后续微扰与相互作用磁动力学的自洽设定。

Abstract: This is the second paper of a program aimed at a rigorous many--body theory of continuum fermions in a uniform magnetic field, built on the asymptotic quasi--local structure that the magnetic coherent--state frame induces on the CAR algebra. Here we study the free dynamics. We show that a Bogoliubov dynamics preserves the local structure exactly if and only if it is generated by a function of the Landau Hamiltonian. For this class we compute the hopping coefficients in closed form, prove that the Lieb--Robinson velocity vanishes, and determine when the almost--local algebra lies in the domain of the generator. For the Landau Hamiltonian the dynamics turns out to be periodic. We then describe the ground states and the KMS states at every temperature, prove their ergodicity under magnetic translations, and recover the KMS states at positive temperature as thermodynamic limits of local Gibbs states. Finally, we relate the exact hopping operator to the Hofstadter model. These results confirm that the frame--induced local structure is a consistent setting for the perturbed and interacting magnetic dynamics to be studied next.

</details>


### [205] [Painlevé transcendents in the defocusing Hirota equation with non-zero boundary conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25308)
*Shuning Lin,Engui Fan*
