Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究散焦广田方程在非零边界条件下的柯西问题，通过逆散射变换与Riemann-Hilbert问题框架，借助$\bar{\partial}$-最速下降法与双尺度极限技术，导出了两个过渡区域内的长时间渐近展开。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 散焦广田方程（defocusing Hirota equation）在非零边界条件下的长时间渐近行为尚缺乏对过渡区域（transition regions）的细致刻画，而此类区域是连接不同渐近普适类的关键，需借助Painlevé型解进行描述。

Method: 采用逆散射变换（inverse scattering transform）将问题转化为Riemann-Hilbert问题，结合$\bar{\partial}$-最速下降法（$\bar{\partial}$-steepest descent method）与双尺度极限技术（double scaling limit technique），分析$x/t$趋近于$-4$与$-8$的过渡区域。

Result: 在两个过渡区域$\mathcal{P}_a$（$a\in\{-4,-8\}$）内得到解的渐近展开：一般情形（generic case）由Painlevé II方程的Hastings--McLeod解表达，非一般情形（non-generic case）由Ablowitz--Segur解表达。

Conclusion: 结果表明，散焦广田方程在非零边界条件下的过渡区域渐近行为由Painlevé II型普适解刻画，推广了可积系统长时间渐近分析中关于过渡区结构的结果。

Abstract: We consider the Cauchy problem for the defocusing Hirota equation with non-zero boundary conditions \begin{align*} &iq_t+ q_{xx}-2\left(|q|^2-1\right)q +i\left(q_{xxx}-6|q|^2q_x\right)=0,\\ &q(x,0)=q_0(x)\to\pm 1, \quad x\to\pm\infty, \end{align*} which can be characterized using a Riemann-Hilbert problem through the inverse scattering transform. With the $\bar{\partial}$-steepest descent method and double scaling limit technique, we derive the long-time asymptotics of the solution in two transition regions \begin{align*}
  \mathcal{P}_{a}
  :=\left\{(x,t)\in\mathbb{R}\times\mathbb{R}^{+}:
  \left| {x}/{t}-a\right|t^{2/3}<C\right\},
  \quad a\in\{-4,-8\}, \end{align*} where $C>0$ is a constant. It is shown that the asymptotic expansions in two transition regions can be expressed in terms of the Hastings--McLeod solution of the Painlevé II equation in the generic case and Ablowitz--Segur solutions in the non-generic case.

</details>


### [206] [Semiclassical Averaging and Temporal Fluctuations of Quantum Dark Solitons in the Lieb-Liniger Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25343)
*Kota Kaneko,Kohei Motegi,Jun Sato*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究 Lieb–Liniger 模型中量子暗孤子态的时间演化及其与 Gross–Pitaevskii（GP）孤子的对应关系，在半经典极限下用正则动量平均的 GP 单孤子密度描述量子密度演化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Sato 等人提出的量子暗孤子态（单孔 yrast 态的等权叠加）与经典 GP 孤子之间的关系尚缺乏定量刻画，尤其缺乏在弱耦合、大系统极限下的对应关系与密度演化描述。

Method: 采用同时弱耦合与大系统的极限（c→0，N,L→∞，平均密度与标度周长 Λ 固定），以固定标度时间 τ=c·n̄·t 考察归一化量子密度，提出其为按正则动量平均的周期性单孤子 GP 密度的猜想，并用数值证据检验；在无限直线极限下解析推导初始平均剖面，并研究长期模方差与 GP 傅里叶系数二阶矩的关系。

Result: 数值证据支持上述对应猜想；不同速度解释了平均密度凹陷的展宽，而单个孤子保持形状。无限直线极限下得到初始平均剖面的解析式，密度亏损随距离呈五次方反比衰减，偏差的傅里叶变换可表示为 Bickley–Naylor 函数 Ki₃ 的级数；模方差与 GP 傅里叶系数二阶矩的关系获得数值比较支持。

Conclusion: 量子暗孤子态的密度演化可由正则动量平均的 GP 孤子系综半经典描述，yrast 跃迁频率可区分初始退相位时间尺度与涨落的有限时间平均所对应的更长尺度。

Abstract: We study the time evolution of quantum dark-soliton states of the Lieb--Liniger model, introduced by Sato--Kanamoto--Kaminishi--Deguchi as equal-weight superpositions of one-hole yrast states, and their relation to Gross--Pitaevskii (GP) solitons. We consider a simultaneous weak-coupling and large-system limit in which the repulsive coupling constant $c$ tends to zero and the particle number $N$ and ring circumference $L$ tend to infinity, with the mean density $\bar n=N/L$ and scaled circumference $Λ=L\sqrt{c\bar n}$ fixed. We conjecture that, in this limit, the normalized quantum density and its evolution at fixed scaled time $τ=c\bar n t$ are described by a canonical-momentum average of normalized periodic one-soliton GP densities, each evolving at its own velocity. We explicitly parametrize the GP family by canonical momentum and provide numerical evidence for this correspondence. The different velocities explain the spreading of the averaged density notch despite shape preservation by individual solitons. In the infinite-line limit, we derive an analytic expression for the initial averaged profile, establish an inverse-fifth-power decay of its density deficit with distance from the soliton center, and express the Fourier transform of its deviation from the background as a series of Bickley--Naylor functions $\operatorname{Ki}_3$. Following the spreading of the initial notch, we investigate temporal density fluctuations of the quantum dark solitons and propose a relation between long-time mode variances and second moments of GP Fourier coefficients, supported by numerical comparisons of individual mode variances and the total variance. Yrast transition frequencies distinguish the initial dephasing time scale from the longer scale associated with finite-time averaging of fluctuations.

</details>


### [207] [A Faà di Bruno Convolution Identity in Parafermion Operator Products](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25455)
*Sam Kault*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 给出 Faà di Bruno 卷积恒等式的组合证明（combinatorial proof），用于对位费米子（parafermion）算子乘积计算，说明潜在更奇异项抵消、领头贡献按多项式指标相加组合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该恒等式此前仅在对位费米子文献中被陈述而缺乏一般推导；本文旨在提供其系统的组合证明并加以推广。

Method: 采用组合证明（combinatorial proof）方法，基于作者博士论文中的工作，对 Faà di Bruno 卷积恒等式（Faà di Bruno convolution identity）进行一般性推导。

Result: 证明表明潜在更奇异项（more singular terms）相互抵消，且领头贡献（leading contribution）以多项式指标（polynomial indices）可加的方式组合。

Conclusion: 该工作为对位费米子算子乘积计算中使用的恒等式提供了通用推导，补足了既有文献仅陈述而未证明的空白。

Abstract: We give a detailed combinatorial proof of a Faà di Bruno convolution identity used in parafermion operator-product calculations. The identity establishes the cancellation of potentially more singular terms and shows that the leading contribution combines the polynomial indices additively. The proof, originating in the author's doctoral thesis, provides a general derivation of an identity stated in earlier parafermion literature.

</details>


### [208] [Block decimation and perfect actions for $p$-adic valued path integrals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25770)
*Su Hu,Min-Soo Kim*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究 $\mathbb{C}_p$ 值波函数的有限划分传播子，指出其迭代积分等价于两点格作用量的精确树级块退耦（block decimation），并证明细化（紫外）与粗化（红外）在 $p$ 进与实范数下角色互换，分别给出不动点极限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前期工作引入的有限划分传播子通过原子测度对中间位置积分，需澄清其与经典完美作用量（perfect actions）变分退耦的关系，并考察细化与粗化两个方向的极限行为。

Method: 将迭代积分对应为等间距链上两点格作用量的精确树级块退耦（无涨落积分）；该运动学退耦是经典完美作用量变分退耦在自由作用量处的 Fréchet 导数。随后分别分析细化（$\mathbb{Z}_p$ 支撑势的收敛幂级数、无时间步长权重）与粗化（谐振子、变分流）两种情形，并对线性化映射做本征值谱分析。

Result: 细化下，$\mathbb{Z}_p$ 上的势在有限步后于非对角稳定并在 $\mathbb{Z}_p^2$ 上一致收敛，$M$ 步后误差为 $p^{-M}$，极限为尺度协变的完美作用量，近对角由 Volkenborn 积分（Volkenborn integral）支配；带权重时无极限。谐振子在固定频率下无细化极限，但粗化下变分流可闭式求解：格点角每步乘以 $p$，耦合满足 Chebyshev 递推，无焦散，重整化质量为 $m_0\sin_p(\theta_0)/\theta_0$。线性化映射在多项式作用量上本征值为 $p^{2-b}$。

Conclusion: 在 $\mathbb{R}$ 上相关（relevant）的方向在 $\mathbb{Q}_p$ 上变为无关（irrelevant），反之亦然；实数域与 $p$ 进域的红外/紫外方向互相交换，为两类完美作用量提供了统一框架。

Abstract: In earlier work we introduced a finite-partition propagator for $\mathbb{C}_p$-valued wave functions, in which the intermediate positions are integrated against atomic measures. The iterated integral evaluates the action on the equally spaced chain between the endpoints, so each partition level is obtained by iterating an exact, tree-level block decimation of two-point lattice actions, with no integration over fluctuations. This kinematic decimation is the Fréchet derivative, at the free actions, of the variational decimation underlying classical perfect actions. Since the time step $p^n$ is small in the real norm exactly when it is large $p$-adically, refinement and coarsening (the ultraviolet and infrared directions over $\mathbb{R}$) exchange roles, and each yields one limit. Under refinement, potentials supported on $\mathbb{Z}_p$, given there by a convergent power series, and entering the slice action without the time-step weight produce an interaction that stabilizes off the diagonal after finitely many steps and converges uniformly on $\mathbb{Z}_p^2$, with sharp error $p^{-M}$ after $M$ steps, to a scale-covariant (perfect) action, governed near the diagonal by a Volkenborn integral; with the weight no limit exists. For the harmonic oscillator no refinement limit exists at fixed frequency, but under coarsening the variational flow is solved in closed form: the lattice angle is multiplied by $p$ at each step, the coupling obeys a Chebyshev recursion, no caustic occurs, and the rescaled action converges to a free action with renormalized mass $m_0\sin_p(θ_0)/θ_0$. The rescaled linearized map is diagonalizable on polynomial actions with eigenvalues $p^{2-b}$, so directions relevant over $\mathbb{R}$ are irrelevant over $\mathbb{Q}_p$ and conversely.

</details>


### [209] [Scaling limits for products of induced symplectic Ginibre matrices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25885)
*Can Atacan,Noah Aygün,Yong-Woo Lee*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究独立诱导辛 Ginibre 矩阵乘积（products of induced symplectic Ginibre matrices）在大维极限下的局部特征值关联（local eigenvalue correlations），在体区（bulk）、谱边缘（spectral edges）及谱支撑外（outside the spectral support）建立相关函数的标度极限（scaling limits），并揭示原点奇异性处的 Pfaffian 结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该工作旨在刻画矩阵乘积这一非正规（non-normal）随机矩阵模型的局部普适性（universality），特别是辛对称类（symplectic symmetry class）下乘积矩阵的精细关联结构，填补对体区、谱边缘、谱外及原点奇异点统一分析的空缺。

Method: 采用统一方法，基于斜核（skew prekernels）所满足的微分方程及其渐近分析（asymptotics），推导相关函数在大维极限下的标度极限。

Result: 在正则谱点（regular spectral points）处，极限关联与单个辛 Ginibre 系综（symplectic Ginibre ensemble）一致：靠近实轴呈 Pfaffian 结构，远离实轴呈行列式（determinantal）结构；在原点，由于极限谱密度出现奇异性，给出了参数依赖的极限 Pfaffian 预核（prekernel）的积分表示。

Conclusion: 结果表明乘积型辛 Ginibre 矩阵在正则谱点处与单矩阵系综具有相同的局部普适性，而原点奇异性需借助参数依赖的 Pfaffian 预核积分表示刻画；斜核微分方程方法为多类谱区域提供统一处理框架。

Abstract: We study the local eigenvalue correlations of products of independent induced symplectic Ginibre matrices in the large-dimensional limit. We establish the scaling limits of the correlation functions in the bulk, at the spectral edges, and outside the limiting spectral support, both near and away from the real axis. At regular spectral points, we show that the limiting correlations coincide with those of a single symplectic Ginibre ensemble, exhibiting Pfaffian structures near the real axis and determinantal structures away from it. At the origin, where the limiting spectral density develops a singularity, we derive an integral representation of the parameter-dependent limiting Pfaffian prekernel. Our proofs are based on a unified approach involving a differential equation for the skew prekernels and their asymptotics.

</details>


### [210] [Root-of-Unity Limits of Trigonometric qKZ Connections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26375)
*Alexander Varchenko*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该文在复数域上研究三角 qKZ 联络的单位根极限，证明其在每个固定次数子空间上给出三角 KZ 联络经幂映射 $z\mapsto z^m$ 的拉回（KZ 参数按 $m$ 缩放），并在一阶情形用加载超几何积分与依赖于角腔的上三角矩阵刻画归一化 qKZ 超几何围道积分的极限及其相容性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究 qKZ 联络在量子群参数趋近单位根、量子群与扭结参数趋近 1 时的退化行为，以建立 qKZ 体系与三角 KZ 体系之间的精确对应，并为超几何解的极限与基本矩阵相容性提供代数与解析描述。

Method: 考虑与 $U_q(\mathfrak{sl}_2)$ Verma 模张量积相伴的三角 qKZ 联络及其超几何解；令乘性步长趋近本原 $m$ 次单位根、参数趋近 1，取 $m$ 步 qKZ 传输在每个固定次数子空间上的极限；一阶下（最高权均为 1）计算归一化 qKZ 超几何围道积分在各角腔中的极限，并借助右弱 Bruhat 序（right weak Bruhat order）与幂映射分析基本矩阵的相容性；同时给出形式版本与 $\ell$-进（$\ell$-adic）版本。

Result: 所得极限联络是三角 KZ 联络在幂映射下的拉回，KZ 参数按 $m$ 重标度；一阶情形下极限由三角 KZ 方程的加载超几何积分（loaded hypergeometric integrals）与显式的角腔依赖上三角矩阵表示；所得到的区分基本矩阵在向内解析延拓（由右弱 Bruhat 序支配）与幂映射（含根阶复合）下均相容。

Conclusion: 三角 qKZ 联络的单位根退化可归结为三角 KZ 联络的幂拉回结构，且该结论在形式与 $\ell$-进设定下同样成立；一阶超几何解极限的显式刻画及其在解析延拓与幂映射下的相容性，为两类方程解空间的比较提供了系统框架。

Abstract: Over the field of complex numbers, we study root-of-unity limits of trigonometric qKZ connections associated with tensor products of $U_q(\mathfrak{sl}_2)$ Verma modules and of their hypergeometric solutions. As the multiplicative step approaches a primitive $m$-th root of unity and the quantum-group and twist parameters approach $1$, the $m$-step qKZ transport yields, on every fixed-degree subspace,a differential connection. We show that this connection is the pullback of the trigonometric KZ connection under the power map $z\mapsto z^m$, with the KZ parameter rescaled by $m$, and establish formal and $\ell$-adic versions.
  In degree one, with all highest weights equal to $1$, we determine the limits of normalized qKZ hypergeometric circle integrals in arbitrary angular chambers. The limits are expressed in terms of loaded hypergeometric integrals for the trigonometric KZ equations and explicit chamber-dependent upper-triangular matrices. We establish compatibility of the resulting distinguished fundamental matrices under inward analytic continuation, governed by the right weak Bruhat order,and under power maps, including composition of root orders.

</details>


### [211] [Curvature Integrability in Twinor Theory: An Ambitwistor Approach to the Twinor Googly Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26399)
*Chen-Hsu Chien*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该文通过由零自平行线（null autoparallels）定义的ambitwistor结构，处理"googly问题"的twistor类比物，在保留手征性、源与挠率的前提下给出条件性的辅助表述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对twistor理论中著名的"googly问题"（googly problem）寻求twistor（twinor）形式的类比处理，且希望避免依赖手征零曲面（chiral null surfaces），同时保留双手征性、源与挠率。

Method: 采用由零自平行线定义的ambitwistor结构；在给定的商化（quotient）、赋值（evaluation）与降（descent）假设下，用全纯仿射截面（holomorphic affine sections）重构twistor场方程DY=J，并借助对应空间上同调（correspondence-space cohomology）实现收缩的手征方程与梯方程。

Result: 对应空间上同调实现了收缩的手征与梯（ladder）方程，包括梯方程的焊接耦合（soldering coupling）与质量归一化源；对给定的非归一化共形联络（non-normal conformal connection），无穷twistor平行性（infinity-twistor parallelism）可导出能量-动量方程，并辅以独立的旋流方程（spin-current equations）。

Conclusion: 这些构造给出的是一个条件性的辅助表述（conditional auxiliary formulation）；本构源（constitutive sources）与引力规定（gravitational prescriptions）仍作为动力学输入保留，尚未被该框架决定。

Abstract: The twinor analogue of the googly problem is addressed through an ambitwistor structure defined by null autoparallels, retaining both chiralities, sources and torsion without requiring chiral null surfaces. Under the stated quotient, evaluation and descent assumptions, holomorphic affine sections reconstruct the twinor field equation $DY=J$. Correspondence-space cohomology realizes the contracted chiral and ladder equations, including the ladder's soldering coupling and mass-normalization source. For a prescribed non-normal conformal connection, infinity-twistor parallelism recovers the energy--momentum equation, supplemented by independent spin-current equations. These constructions give a conditional auxiliary formulation; constitutive sources and gravitational prescriptions remain dynamical input.

</details>


<div id='nucl-ex'></div>

# nucl-ex [[Back]](#toc)

### [212] [Measurements of inclusive $J/ψ$ production in Au+Au collisions at $\sqrt{s_{\rm NN}}$ = 14.6, 17.3, 19.6, and 27 GeV with the STAR experiment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25487)
*The STAR Collaboration*

Main category: nucl-ex

TL;DR: STAR 实验测量了 14.6–27 GeV Au+Au 碰撞中近快度区包容性 J/ψ 产额，发现中心碰撞 R_AA 约 0.5，且在低能区 R_AA 随横动量增大而上升，与 200 GeV 结果不同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用相对论重离子对撞机能区扫描（energy scan）的低能 Au+Au 数据，考察 J/ψ 产额压低（suppression）随碰撞能量与中心度的演化，以区分热核物质与冷核物质效应。

Method: 在 |y| < 1.0 近快度区测量 √s_NN = 14.6、17.3、19.6、27 GeV 的包容性 J/ψ 产额，并以插值得到的 p+p 参考计算核修正因子（nuclear modification factor, R_AA），分析其随中心度与横动量（p_T）的依赖关系。

Result: R_AA 由外围向中心碰撞呈下降迹象，在 0–20% 中心度区间约 0.5，且各碰撞能量下相近；与 200 GeV 时 R_AA 几乎不依赖 p_T 不同，14.6–27 GeV 下 R_AA 随 p_T 呈上升趋势；对 p_T 积分的中心碰撞 R_AA 在 14.6–200 GeV 范围内在不确定度内无显著变化。

Conclusion: 测量结果可由同时包含热核物质与冷核物质效应的输运模型（transport models）定性描述，表明低能区 J/ψ 压低机制与高能区存在差别。

Abstract: Inclusive $J/ψ$ production at mid-rapidity ($|y| < 1.0$) in Au+Au collisions at center-of-mass energies per nucleon-nucleon pair ($\sqrt{s_{\rm NN}}$) of 14.6, 17.3, 19.6, and 27 GeV is measured with the STAR detector at the Relativistic Heavy Ion Collider. The nuclear modification factor $R_{\rm AA}$ is used to quantify modifications of $J/ψ$ production relative to an interpolated $p$+$p$ reference. The measured $R_{\rm AA}$ shows a hint of a decreasing trend from peripheral to central collisions, reaching a value of approximately 0.5 in the 0-20% centrality class across all collision energies. Unlike similar measurements in Au+Au collisions at $\sqrt{s_{\rm NN}}$ = 200 GeV, where $J/ψ$ $R_{\rm AA}$ shows little dependence on transverse momentum ($p_{\rm {T}}$), the corresponding $R_{\rm AA}$ at the lower collision energies of 14.6-27 GeV shows a tendency to increase with $p_{\rm {T}}$. Additionally, the inclusive $J/ψ$ $R_{\rm AA}$, integrated over $J/ψ$ $p_{\rm {T}}$, in central collisions exhibits no significant variation within uncertainties over the energy range of 14.6 to 200 GeV. These results can be qualitatively described by transport models that incorporate hot and cold nuclear matter effects.

</details>

# math.OA [[Back]](#toc)

### [213] [Invariants of subfactors associated to Petrescu's Hadamard matrices of order 7](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25638)
*Dietmar Bisch,Remus Nicoara*

Main category: math.OA

TL;DR: 该文证明由 Petrescu 六次根 $7\times7$ Hadamard 矩阵经 Jones 基本构造得到的 Jones 指标为 7 的子因子其标准不变量极小，主图为 Coxeter-Dynkin 图 A$_\infty$，解决了一个长期公开问题，并将结论推广到多族 Hadamard 矩阵。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Petrescu 的 Hadamard 矩阵定义了指标为 7 的不可约超有限子因子，但其标准不变量是否极小、主图是否为 A$_\infty$ 长期未决，该文旨在解决这一公开问题。

Method: 核心新思想是关于 Hadamard 矩阵 profile 的同余式，用于限制 Jones 平面代数（planar algebra）描述中最低权空间上旋转的阶；结合一致迹界（uniform trace bound）在子序列上给出有界环形重数（annular multiplicities），从而迫使主图范数不超过 2。

Result: 所论子因子的高阶相对换位子（higher relative commutants）为 Temperley-Lieb 代数，标准不变量极小，主图为 A$_\infty$；同一结论对 Petrescu 单参数族中大多数 Hadamard 矩阵成立（每个超越相及素理想之上的剩余类为 $\pm1$ 的代数相），例外集在各固定次数下有限且为代数数。

Conclusion: 方法可推广至多类 $q>4$ 阶 Hadamard 矩阵，包括 $q\equiv4\pmod8$ 的实 Hadamard 矩阵、$\Q(i)$ 上 $q\equiv2\pmod4$ 的矩阵及 $3\mid q$、$9\nmid q$ 的立方根矩阵，均得到 A$_\infty$ 主图；六阶情形在 Diţă 族与 Karlsson 两参数族的相应相下亦成立。

Abstract: Petrescu's sixth-root $7 \times 7$ Hadamard matrix defines an irreducible, hyperfinite subfactor $N \subset M$ with Jones index 7. It is built from the spin model commuting square obtained from the Hadamard matrix by iterating Jones' basic construction. We prove that the standard invariant of this subfactor is minimal, i.e. the higher relative commutants consists of Temperley-Lieb algebras. Thus, the principal graphs of the subfactor are the Coxeter-Dynkin graph A$_\infty$. This solves a long-standing open problem. The main new idea is a congruence for the profile of the Hadamard matrix that restricts the order of rotation on the lowest-weight spaces in Jones planar algebra description of the standard invariant. Together with a uniform trace bound, this gives bounded annular multiplicities on a subsequence, which forces the principal graph to have norm at most two. We also show that the same result holds for most Hadamard matrices in Petrescu's one-parameter family. Specifically, the associated subfactors have Temperley-Lieb standard invariant at every transcendental phase and at every algebraic phase with residue $\pm1$ at a prime above three. In each fixed degree the exceptional set is finite and consists of algebraic numbers.
  Our methods apply to several other families of Hadamard matrices of order $q >4$ as well, and we show that the associated subfactors have A$_\infty$ principal graphs. These include real Hadamard matrices of order $q\equiv4\pmod8$, Hadamard matrices over $\Q(i)$ of order $q\equiv2\pmod4$ and cube-root matrices of order $q$ with $3\mid q$ and $9\nmid q$. For order six Hadamard matrices, we obtain the same result at every transcendental phase in Diţă's family through Haagerup's matrix and at every algebraically independent pair of phases in Karlsson's two-parameter family.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.mes-hall'></div>

# cond-mat.mes-hall [[Back]](#toc)

### [214] [Chern Numbers from Symmetry-Enforced Zeros and Dispersion Shifts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25028)
*Yoonseok Hwang,Henry Davenport,Frank Schindler*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该论文提出利用波函数零点及其局域涡旋（vorticity）作为表征 Chern 拓扑（Chern topology）的互补手段，并证明晶格对称性可强制零点出现、固定涡旋的模（modulo）旋转阶数，弱局域微扰引起的色散移动正比于重叠模方，从而无需测量波函数相位即可识别 Chern 拓扑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 常用 Berry 曲率（Berry curvature）表述虽能刻画 Chern 拓扑，但需要波函数相位信息。作者希望建立一种基于波函数零点与局部涡旋的互补表征，并使之可仅通过色散测量实现，避免相位测量。

Method: 通过选取任意参考态并与 Chern 能带（Chern band）计算波函数重叠（wave-function overlaps），分析晶格对称性（crystalline symmetry）对零点及其涡旋的约束；同时推导弱局域微扰下色散移动正比于重叠绝对平方的关系，并推广至多分量、多能带波函数及动量空间可消失的参考态。

Result: 晶格对称性能够强制重叠零点出现，并依据能带与参考态角动量之差把涡旋固定到旋转阶数的模。弱局域微扰产生正比于重叠模方的色散移动，使零点和局部结构可直接从色散读出；结合对称性可在不测相位的情况下识别 Chern 拓扑。零点附近色散移动还给出量子几何张量（quantum metric）的下界。

Conclusion: 该方法为 Chern 拓扑提供了一种基于零点与涡旋的互补刻画，兼容多分量、多能带波函数，允许直接使用紧局域态（compact localized states）与平带（flat-band）系统的已知实空间和动量空间性质作为参考态，并暗示零点附近色散可作为量子几何的探测手段。

Abstract: Wave-function zeros and their local vorticities provide a complementary characterization of Chern topology to more commonly used Berry-curvature formulations. Using wave-function overlaps between a Chern band and freely chosen reference states, we show that crystalline symmetry can enforce their zeros and fix their vorticities modulo the rotation order from the angular momenta of the band and reference state. We further show that weak local perturbations produce band-dispersion shifts proportional to the absolute square of the overlap, allowing its zeros and local structure to be read off directly from the dispersion. Combined with crystalline symmetry, this can identify Chern topology without measuring wave-function phases. Our construction applies to multicomponent and multiband wave functions and allows reference states whose momentum-space representation may vanish at some momenta. This permits compact localized states to be used directly as reference states and makes use of their known real- and momentum-space properties in flat-band systems. The dispersion shift near an overlap zero further provides a lower bound on the quantum metric at the zero.

</details>


### [215] [Topological (in)equivalence encoded in zeros of wavefunction overlaps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25037)
*Carolina Paiva,Bruno Mera,Roni Ilan,Raquel Queiroz*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作提出用两个占据子空间之间的重叠矩阵（overlap matrix）直接读取能带结构的相对拓扑（relative topology），将 Chern 不变量与 Z₂ 不变量统一于同一框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上拓扑等价性需通过连续形变判据或分别计算各类拓扑不变量来判断，缺乏一个同时涵盖 Chern 与 Z₂ 情形的统一、可直接从能带数据读取的判据。

Method: 以重叠矩阵行列式作为布里渊区上某线丛（line bundle）的全局截面，其在子空间映射不可逆处取零；构造曲率集中于零点的奇异联络（singular connection），由相位绕数（phase winding）为每个零点赋予整数荷。

Result: 在二维 class A 中总荷等于两系统 Chern 数之差；在 class AII 中时间反演配对反号零点，相对 Z₂ 不变量为半个布里渊区内总荷的奇偶性，Kane-Mele Pfaffian 作为特例被恢复；零点与两系统插值过程中的能隙闭合相对应。

Conclusion: 该构造在 Haldane、Kane-Mele 与 Qi-Wu-Zhang 模型中得以展示，包括仅由旋转对称性（rotation symmetry）强制零点出现的情形，表明晶格对称性可约束零点的位置与荷。

Abstract: Topological equivalence between two gapped band structures is determined by whether their occupied states can be continuously deformed into one another without closing the energy gap or breaking the protecting symmetries. We show that this relative topology can be read directly from the overlap matrix between the two occupied subspaces, in a way that treats Chern and $\mathbb{Z}_2$ invariants within a single framework. The determinant of the overlap matrix defines a global section of a line bundle over the Brillouin zone, which vanishes at the momenta where the map between the subspaces fails to be invertible. A singular connection, whose curvature is concentrated at these zeros, assigns each zero an integer charge given by its phase winding. In two-dimensional class A, the total charge equals the difference between the Chern numbers of the two systems. In class AII, time reversal pairs zeros of opposite charge, and the relative $\mathbb{Z}_2$ invariant is the parity of the total charge in half the Brillouin zone; the Kane-Mele Pfaffian is recovered as a special case. The zeros coincide with the gap closings of an interpolation between the two systems, and crystalline symmetries constrain their positions and charges. We illustrate the construction in the Haldane, Kane-Mele, and Qi-Wu-Zhang models, including cases where rotation symmetry alone enforces the zeros.

</details>


### [216] [Circuit model for gate-induced excitation of quantum Hall edges tuned by DC gate bias](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25332)
*Kodai Sugizaki,Takaaki Mano,Go Yusa*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 研究栅极诱导边缘激发中输入电压与激发波形的关系，发现偏置电压跨越夹断电压（pinch-off voltage）时波形在时域由类一阶微分响应转为与输入成正比，并可用单一等效电路模型（equivalent circuit model）以不同有效栅极-二维电子气电容（gate-2DEG capacitance）解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘激发（edge excitations）沿量子霍尔系统（quantum Hall system）边缘传播，对低维电子系统与介观输运（mesoscopic transport）研究具有重要意义，而栅极诱导激发的输入电压与激发波形之间的关系尚待厘清。

Method: 改变施加于栅极的偏置电压（offset voltage），在时域与频域测量栅极诱导激发的波形及频率依赖，并构建等效电路模型进行拟合。

Result: 时域上激发波形由类一阶微分响应转变为与输入波形成正比的响应；频域上栅极激发的频率依赖性在偏置电压跨越夹断电压时显著变化，且可由单一等效电路模型描述，模型拟合所得有效电容与器件结构计算值一致。

Conclusion: 夹断电压两侧有效栅极-二维电子气电容的差异可统一解释激发波形的时域与频域变化，模型拟合电容与结构计算电容的一致性支持该等效电路描述的合理性。

Abstract: Edge excitations, which propagate along the edge of a quantum Hall system, are of great interest in the study of low-dimensional electron systems and mesoscopic transport phenomena. In this study, we investigate the relationship between the input voltage and the excitation waveform for gate-induced excitations. By varying the offset voltage applied to the gate, we observe that the excitation waveform changes from a first-derivative-like response to a response proportional to the input waveform in the time domain. In the frequency domain, we observe that the frequency dependence of the gate excitation changes significantly as the offset voltage crosses the pinch-off voltage. We show that these changes can be described by a single equivalent circuit model with different effective gate-2DEG capacitances on either side of the pinch-off voltage. In addition, we confirm that the effective capacitance obtained from the fitting of the model with the experimental data is consistent with the capacitance calculated from the device structure.

</details>


### [217] [Topological Bound States in the Continuum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25493)
*Dan-Na Liu,J. Villavicencio,Pierre A. Pantaleon*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作在 AB 堆叠双层石墨烯（AB-stacked bilayer graphene）的质量墙结（mass-wall junction）中揭示了拓扑型连续谱束缚态（bound states in the continuum, BIC），并区分了谷拓扑对界面传播的作用与约束条件绕数（winding）对完全解耦的独立控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有对拓扑界面态的研究主要关注其沿界面的传播，而界面态与外部导电连续谱之间的解耦机制（即束缚而非共振）尚缺乏统一描述；本文旨在将界面传播与禁闭两类拓扑角色联系起来。

Method: 采用 AB 堆叠双层石墨烯中的质量墙结作为模型体系，通过分析谷拓扑（valley topology）给出的界面态与禁闭条件（confinement conditions）的绕数（winding）来刻画束缚态；并考察结长度与层间偏压（interlayer bias）调节下的能级合并与共振宽度标度行为。

Result: 谷拓扑支持沿墙传播的界面态，而禁闭条件的另一种绕数决定其与导电引线的精确解耦；调节结长度或层间偏压可使带相反电荷的束缚态合并并在不改变谷拓扑的前提下消除禁闭；在一般临界点，该态仍保持束缚，邻近共振宽度随动量呈四次方（quartically）开启。

Conclusion: 结果表明拓扑在导电连续谱中可分别承担界面传播与禁闭两种互补功能，并给出由绕数控制的连续谱束缚态判据及可调的临界行为，为双层石墨烯结中的束缚态工程提供依据。

Abstract: We uncover topological bound states in the continuum in AB-stacked bilayer graphene mass-wall junctions. Valley topology supports interface states propagating along the wall, while a distinct winding of the confinement conditions governs exact decoupling from conducting leads. Tuning the junction length or interlayer bias can merge oppositely charged bound states and remove confinement without changing the valley topology. At generic critical points, the state remains bound and nearby resonance widths open quartically with momentum. These results connect complementary roles of topology in interface propagation and confinement within a conducting continuum.

</details>


### [218] [Higher-order magnetic hopfions in uniaxial chiral magnets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25883)
*Vladyslav M. Kuchkin,Nikolai S. Kiselev*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该文提出单轴各向异性（uniaxial anisotropy）可稳定一系列零及任意正霍普夫指数（Hopf index）的结与链状霍普夫子（hopfions），并给出基于斯格明子编织（skyrmion braids）映射的系统构造方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手性磁体（chiral magnets）虽被认为可承载霍普夫子，但实验观测到的构型主要局限于霍普夫子环（hopfion rings）、螺旋结子（heliknotons）及其团簇，构型种类有限。

Method: 引入单轴各向异性，在各向异性—磁场（anisotropy–field）参数空间中确定稳定区域；并建立将二维斯格明子编织映射为三维磁织构（three-dimensional magnetic textures）的系统构造方案。

Result: 发现单轴各向异性可稳定多种零霍普夫指数与任意正霍普夫指数的结状、链状霍普夫子，并给出其稳定性相区；稳定霍普夫子的最小能量随霍普夫指数近似线性增长。

Conclusion: 单轴各向异性为霍普夫子家族提供了新的稳定机制与可构造路径，扩展了手性磁体中拓扑织构的可实现范围，并揭示能量与拓扑指数的线性标度关系。

Abstract: Chiral magnets host hopfions, but their experimentally observed configurations are largely limited to hopfion rings, heliknotons, and their clusters. Here, we show that uniaxial anisotropy stabilizes a diverse family of knotted and linked hopfions with zero and arbitrary positive Hopf indices. We identify their stability regions in the anisotropy -- field parameter space and introduce a systematic construction based on mapping skyrmion braids into three-dimensional magnetic textures. We find that the minimum energy of stable hopfions increases approximately linearly with the Hopf index.

</details>


### [219] [Field-free deterministic spin-orbit torque magnetization switching using monolayer WTe2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25755)
*Ganesh Hanchanahal,Arnav Jain,Shrushti Tapar,Saroj P Dash,Bhaskaran Muralidharan*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 基于单层 WTe2 的自旋轨道力矩（spin-orbit torque, SOT）器件在 1T_d 与 1T' 两相中均可实现无场确定性磁化翻转；1T_d 的 x-ribbon 构型因电流诱导自旋极化更强而翻转更快，被确定为无场 SOT-MRAM 的候选自旋源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自旋轨道力矩磁存储器（SOT-MRAM）走向实用化的关键障碍之一是需要外加磁场才能实现磁化翻转，即缺乏无场翻转（field-free switching）能力；本工作据此评估单层 WTe2 作为自旋源材料实现无场翻转的可行性。

Method: 采用非平衡格林函数（non-equilibrium Green's function）框架，将铁磁层建模为交换耦合宏自旋（exchange-coupled macrospin），对比单层 WTe2 的 1T_d 与 1T' 两相及不同 x-ribbon 几何，并分析翻转动力学对偏置电压、ribbon 取向与磁层宽度的依赖。

Result: 两相体系均实现翻转；1T_d 的 x-ribbon 构型产生的电流诱导自旋极化（current-induced spin polarization）显著大于 1T'，从而在相邻磁层上产生更大的类阻尼力矩（damping-like torque）并带来更快的翻转。1T' 器件的翻转时间随磁层宽度呈强烈非单调变化，而 1T_d 器件在较宽宽度范围内近乎恒定，与边缘主导（edge-dominated）的力矩行为一致。

Conclusion: x-ribbon 构型的 1T_d-WTe2 被认定为无场 SOT-MRAM 的有前景自旋源材料，其优势在于更强的类阻尼力矩、更快的翻转及对磁层宽度不敏感的可扩展性。

Abstract: An important step towards the practical implementation of spin-orbit torque magnetic memory is the field-free switching of the magnetization vector. In this work, we investigate spin-orbit torque-driven magnetization dynamics and switching devices comprising monolayer $\mathrm{WTe}_2$ as the spin-source material. We perform a comparative analysis of the two phases of $\mathrm{WTe}_2$, namely the $1\mathrm{T}_d$ and $1\mathrm{T}'$ with the necessary symmetries for field-free deterministic magnetization switching. We demonstrate switching in both systems and analyse the dependence of the switching dynamics on the applied bias voltage, ribbon orientation and width of the magnetic layer. Modelling the ferromagnet as an exchange-coupled macrospin within a non-equilibrium Green's function framework, we find that the $x$-ribbon geometry of the $1\mathrm{T}_d$ $\mathrm{WTe}_2$ phase generates a significantly larger current-induced spin polarization than the $1\mathrm{T}'$ phase, resulting in a larger damping-like torque on the adjacent magnetic layer and faster switching. The switching time of $1\mathrm{T}'$ devices varies strongly and non-monotonically with the width of the magnetic layer. In $1\mathrm{T}_d$ devices, it remains nearly constant over a broad range of widths, consistent with an edge-dominated torque. These results identify $x$-ribbon $1\mathrm{T}_d$-$\mathrm{WTe}_2$ as a promising spin source for field-free SOT-MRAM.

</details>


### [220] [Helices at Twisted Ferroelectric Interfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25828)
*Kristina Holsgrove,Ronan Lynch,Conor McCluskey,Eoin Moynihan,Andrew Rogers,Xinxin Hu,Boris Croes,Loan Hajdari-Manigault,Maria Lazarut,Eadaoin Anderson,Sam Seddon,Anna Razumnaya,Yurii Tikhonov,Ana M. Sanchez,Marios Hadjimichael,Amit Kumar,Raymond McQuaid,Jordi Arbiol,Gustau Catalan,Richard Beanland,Igor Lukyanchuk,Lukas M. Eng,Salia Cherifi-Hertel,Tchavdar Todorov,J. Marty Gregg*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该研究报告，两块铁电铌酸锂（ferroelectric lithium niobate）单晶形成的扭转界面（twisted interfaces）中存在由螺型位错（screw dislocations）分隔的相干键合、弹性变形的纳米尺度结构螺旋（structural helices）。这些螺旋具有手性（chirality），可通过4D STEM、电子叠层成像（electron ptychography）与二次谐波圆偏振二色性（second harmonic optical circularly-polarised dichroism）探测，并因压电与挠曲电效应诱发偶极螺旋，构成区别于莫尔图案与二维范德华扭转堆叠的新型“扭转电子学（twistronics）”。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有扭转体系研究集中于二维范德华材料的莫尔超晶格（moiré patterns），而强键合的氧化物扭转界面是否具有不同结构机制尚不明确。该工作旨在揭示铁电氧化物扭转界面的真实结构形态及其手性特征，从而拓展扭转电子学的材料与物理范畴。

Method: 采用四维扫描透射电子显微镜（4D STEM）及差分相位衬度（DPC）成像观测界面衬度，结合沿螺旋通道电子轨迹的建模进行解释，并用电子叠层成像直接成像结构螺旋。通过压电张量（piezoelectric tensors）与挠曲电系数（flexoelectric coefficients）推断偶极螺旋，并以样品翻转前后DPC图像一致性及二次谐波圆偏振二色性测量确认手性。

Result: 观测到由螺型位错分隔的相干键合、弹性变形纳米结构螺旋阵列，在4D STEM与DPC图像中产生显著衬度并可被叠层成像直接证实。因铌酸锂为单轴铁电体，扭转应变必然伴随偶极螺旋形成；界面二次谐波圆偏振二色性信号异常强，证实螺旋的手性。

Conclusion: 所观测的螺旋阵列不同于莫尔图案，其实现兼具塑性（plastic）与弹性（elastic）变形的晶格旋转，而非简单晶格叠加。紧密键合的扭转氧化物界面与二维范德华扭转堆叠特性显著不同，开辟了一类新的扭转电子学研究方向。

Abstract: We report that twisted interfaces, formed between two ferroelectric lithium niobate single crystals, contain arrays of coherently bonded and elastically deformed nanoscale structural helices, separated from one another by screw dislocations. The helices generate remarkable contrast in four dimensional scanning transmission electron microscopy (4D STEM) and through related differential phase contrast (DPC). Such images have been successfully rationalised by modelling the trajectory of electrons that channel along helical pathways as they traverse the interface, but the structural helices implied have also been directly imaged using electron ptychography. Importantly, as lithium niobate is a uniaxial ferroelectric, the torsional strain in these structural helices necessarily also causes dipolar helices to form, inferred through consideration of pertinent piezoelectric tensors and flexoelectric coefficients. Helices are topologically distinct from the more commonly reported vortices and, importantly, they are chiral. Helical (and hence chiral) character was confirmed by observing that DPC images were identical before and after the sample was inverted. Chirality was also established through second harmonic optical circularly-polarised dichroism measurements, in which unusually large interfacial signals were seen. It must be stressed that the arrays of helices observed are distinct from moiré patterns; they facilitate lattice rotation combining plastic and elastic deformation and are not simply a superposition of lattices, twisted with respect to one another. Thus, intimately bonded twisted oxide interfaces will have distinctly different characteristics from those of twisted stacks of two-dimensional van der Waals materials, opening up a new and distinct kind of twistronics.

</details>


### [221] [Topology-Enabled Self-Verification of Quantum Hall Resistance Standards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26016)
*DaVonne Henry,Dominick S. Scaletta,Tamar M. Padwa,Heather M. Hill,Gretchen B. Ferguson,Evelyn E. Hopkins,Mattias Kruskopf,Dean G. Jarrett,David B. Newell,Albert F. Rigosi*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 摘要提出一种“对称精密量子化可靠性判据（Symmetric Precision Quantization Reliability criterion）”，仅通过Cn对称设计的对外端子测量即可统计性地验证多单元量子化霍尔阵列电阻标准（quantized Hall array resistance standards）的内部量子化未失效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多单元量子化霍尔阵列电阻标准的规模化（scaling）需要严格验证内部量子化是否失效，但传统方法依赖对无耗散边缘态输运（dissipationless edge state transport）的验证，这一途径存在局限，故需新的验证思路。

Method: 采用基于Cn对称设计的数学框架，即对称精密量子化可靠性判据，在统计意义上仅凭外部端子测量（outer terminal measurements）完成验证，并对由量子霍尔击穿（quantum Hall breakdown）产生的隐藏局域缺陷（hidden localized defects）出现概率给出界定。

Result: 该判据能够在不依赖边缘态输运验证的前提下，从统计学上限定隐藏局域缺陷出现的可能性，从而为内部量子化的完好性提供保证。

Conclusion: 该判据在量子计算（quantum computing）与半导体测试（semiconductor testing）中具有潜在应用，但摘要中主要以其在量子电学标准（quantum electrical standards）中的应用为背景进行阐述。

Abstract: The scaling of multi-element quantized Hall array resistance standards necessitates rigorous validation that internal quantization has not broken down without relying on the verification of dissipationless edge state transport. One proposed solution is to use a Symmetric Precision Quantization Reliability criterion, a mathematical framework that statistically assures such validation strictly through outer terminal measurements of Cn-symmetric designs. This criterion essentially bounds the likelihood of hidden localized defects arising from quantum Hall breakdown and has potential uses in quantum computing and semiconductor testing but is presented in the context of its application toward quantum electrical standards.

</details>


### [222] [Investigating Thermal Boundary Conductance in Monolayer Transition Metal Dichalcogenides: A Systematic Comparison of Advanced Nanoscale Thermometry Techniques](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26129)
*Timm Swoboda,Jiaqi Yang,Arkadiusz P. Gertych,Carlos Acosta,Onurcan Kaya,Daniel Capolat Palomar,Severine Gomès,Aitor Lopeandia,Stephan Roche,Javier Rodriguez-Viejo,Marianna Sledzinska*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该文针对二维（2D）器件热管理中热边界导（thermal boundary conductance, TBC）报道值离散度过大的问题，在同一批样品上采用三种代表性热测量技术，对四种支撑于蓝宝石衬底上的过渡金属硫族化合物（TMD）单层的界面热输运进行系统交叉对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文献中单层TMD等二维材料类别的TBC报道值差异可超过一个数量级，难以判断差异源自材料本身还是测量方法，因此需要排除样品间差异的直接对比研究。

Method: 在同一组样品上同时使用拉曼热测量（Raman thermometry）、扫描热显微镜（scanning thermal microscopy, SThM）与频域热反射（frequency domain thermoreflectance, FDTR）三种方法，测量四种蓝宝石衬底上TMD单层的界面热输运，以最小化样品间差异并实现方法间的直接比较。

Result: 测量值随材料种类与测量技术不同而变化；通过直接比较可阐明差异来源，并识别各表征方法中的不确定度来源。

Conclusion: 研究结果为二维材料界面研究提供了直接的实验基准（benchmark），并为热计量学（thermal metrology）方法的选择与结果解读提供实践指导。

Abstract: Thermal boundary conductance (TBC) plays a crucial role in heat dissipation in two-dimensional (2D) devices. However, reported values for 2D material classes such as monolayer transition metal dichalcogenides (TMDs) can vary by more than an order of magnitude across the literature, making it difficult to determine whether these differences come from the materials themselves or from the measurement methods used. To address this issue, this article presents a systematic, cross-technique study of the interfacial heat transport in four TMD monolayers supported on a sapphire substrate, using three of the most representative methods: Raman thermometry, scanning thermal microscopy (SThM) and frequency domain thermoreflectance (FDTR). By conducting all measurements on the same sets of samples, the influence of sample-to-sample variation is minimized allowing for a direct comparison of the three approaches. The results reveal a variation in the measured values depending on both the material and the measurement technique. By directly comparing these results we can elucidate on the origin of the differences and underpin sources of uncertainty in each of the characterisation methods. Taken together, these results provide a direct experimental benchmark for studies of 2D material interfaces and offer practical guidance for the selection and interpretation of thermal metrology.

</details>


### [223] [Sliding-ferroelectric control of dipolar and quadrupolar excitons in R-stacked WSe$_2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26158)
*Asmund Ottesen,Shun Feng,Edoardo Lopriore,Yaroslav Zhumagulov,Kenji Watanabe,Takashi Taniguchi,Oleg V. Yazyev,Andras Kis*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 利用少层R-WSe2的栅压依赖光致发光，借助滑移铁电性（sliding ferroelectricity）调控层杂化激子（layer-hybridised excitons）的偶极构型，实现激子扩散输运的开关与偶极重构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 层间激子（interlayer excitons）的偶极特性对研究集体现象和新型光电器件至关重要，但在二维半导体同质/异质结构中，电荷空间受限与能带跃迁性质通常使偶极矩的大小与取向被锁定，缺乏调控手段。

Method: 对少层R-WSe2进行栅压依赖的光致发光（photoluminescence）测量，利用滑移铁电性控制新出现的层杂化激子的偶极构型，并以光学探针表征垂直排列的界面极化（interfacial polarisation）中间态。

Result: 滑移铁电调控可实现杂化偶极激子扩散输运的显著开/关切换，并支持渐进式与突变式的偶极重构及四极激子（quadrupolar excitons）的出现；光学探测揭示界面极化的中间态，其量级证实了多层中的累积极化（cumulative polarisation）。

Conclusion: 滑移铁电性可作为调控层间耦合与层杂化的有力工具，为铁电控制的激子互连（exciton interconnects）与新型多比特光存储器（multi-bit optical memories）奠定基础。

Abstract: A fundamental property of interlayer excitons is their dipolar character, enabling electrical tunability, enhanced exciton-exciton interactions and extended lifetime. It is important for studying collective phenomena and realizing novel optoelectronic devices. However, in homo- or heterostructures based on two-dimensional semiconductors, the magnitude and orientation of the dipole moment are commonly locked due to spatially confined charges and nature of the band transition. Here, we use gate-dependent photoluminescence of few-layer R-WSe2 to harness sliding ferroelectricity for controlling the exciton dipole configuration of emerging layer-hybridised excitons. The sliding-ferroelectric control enables a pronounced on/off switching of the diffusive transport of hybrid dipolar excitons, while further facilitating gradual and abrupt dipole reconfigurations with emerging quadrupolar excitons. Our optical probe reveals intermediate states of vertically aligned interfacial polarisation with magnitudes that confirm cumulative polarisation in multilayers. Overall, our work presents sliding ferroelectricity as a powerful tool to tune interlayer coupling and layer hybridisation. These results represent a step toward ferroelectric-controlled exciton interconnects and novel multi-bit optical memories.

</details>


### [224] [Spectral localizers in phase space: $\mathbb{Z}$ and $\mathbb{Z}_2$ invariants for higher-order, semimetal, and valley modes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26160)
*Lucien Jezequel,Christophe Mora*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作将谱局域化方法（spectral localizer）推广为统一框架，用零维拓扑不变量刻画多种拓扑无能隙模式（topological gapless modes），涵盖 Z 与 Z₂ 分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓扑无能隙模式出现在能带节点、拓扑绝缘体边界态等多种平台，各自受不同的拓扑分类与不变量支配，缺乏统一描述框架。

Method: 递归应用量子限制算符（quantum confining operators）将原哈密顿量约化为零维算符，且不要求其在位置或动量空间对角；在半经典图像下，限制算符的零流形构成包围目标无能隙模式的壳层，类似 Teo-Kane 拓扑缺陷框架。

Result: 局域化算符给出零维不变量（如 signature、行列式符号或 Pfaffian 符号），同时捕获 Z 和 Z₂ 分类，涵盖高阶拓扑绝缘体的铰链模（hinge modes）、半金属中的单个 Dirac 节点及谷选择性拓扑边界模。

Conclusion: 该单一构造统一了多种拓扑无能隙模式场景；在对称类 A、AIII、AII、D 的格点模型上验证了拓扑相变与谱局域化算符能隙闭合同时发生。

Abstract: Topologically protected gapless modes emerge across multiple platforms, ranging from nodes in the band structure to boundary states in topologically non-trivial insulators, with each case governed by distinct topological classifications and invariants. Here, we generalize the spectral localizer method to establish a unified framework for topological gapless modes. The spectral localizer reduces the original Hamiltonian to a zero-dimensional operator by recursively applying quantum confining operators, which need not be diagonal in either position or momentum space, as previously assumed. In phase space, the zero-manifold of the semiclassical confining operators forms a shell enclosing the gapless mode of interest, analogous to the Teo-Kane framework for topological defects. The localizer yields a zero-dimensional invariant, such as a signature, a determinant sign, or a Pfaffian sign, capturing both $\mathbb{Z}$ and $\mathbb{Z}_2$ classifications. This single construction thus encompasses diverse scenarios, including hinge modes in higher-order topological insulators, individual Dirac nodes in semimetals, and valley-selective topological boundary modes. We illustrate our approach using lattice models in symmetry classes A, AIII, AII, and D, verifying that topological transitions coincide with a vanishing gap in the spectral localizer operator.

</details>


### [225] [Emergent Orbital Hall Currents in Disordered and Amorphous Materials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26502)
*Jorge Martinez Romeral,Thomas Galvani,Onurcan Kaya,Stephan Roche,Aron W. Cummings,Luis M. Canonico*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作利用实空间轨道角动量（orbital angular momentum）表达式，研究单轨道方晶格与石墨烯类体系中无序驱动的实时轨道霍尔输运（orbital Hall transport），发现结构无序与静电无序均可产生本征体系所不具备的可观轨道霍尔电流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无序（disorder）在轨道输运中的作用是轨道电子学（orbitronics）中的核心未解问题，需明确其能否以及如何产生轨道霍尔电流。

Method: 采用巡游轨道角动量的实空间形式（real-space formulation），在单轨道方晶格（square-lattice）与石墨烯基体系中数值研究实时轨道霍尔输运，并引入多晶、非晶等结构无序及静电无序。

Result: 结构无序（多晶与非晶）和静电无序均能产生原洁净体系所缺失的显著轨道霍尔电流；在非晶石墨烯（amorphous graphene）中，即使电子态强局域化，该电流仍在较长时域及电荷中性点附近较宽能量窗口内持续存在。

Conclusion: 非晶量子材料（amorphous quantum materials）可作为无序工程化轨道电子学功能的有前景平台，结论与近期实验证据一致。

Abstract: The role of disorder in orbital transport remains a central open question in orbitronics. Using a real-space formulation of itinerant orbital angular momentum, we investigate the real-time or- bital Hall transport in single-orbital square-lattice and graphene-based systems. We demonstrate that both structural (polycrystalline and amorphous) and electrostatic disorder can generate siz- able orbital Hall currents that are absent in their pristine counterparts. Remarkably, in amorphous graphene, these currents persist over extended timescales and across a broad energy window around charge neutrality, despite the strong localization of electronic states. Consistent with recent exper- imental evidence, our findings establish amorphous quantum materials as promising platforms for disorder-engineered orbitronic functionality.

</details>


### [226] [Local orbital magnetization and Chern markers from equilibrium local currents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26599)
*Henrique P. Veiga,Aires Ferreira,Caio H. Lewenkopf,João M. Viana Parente Lopes*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作提出基于滤波随机向量一次性获取格点上半正定算符全部矩阵元的方法，大幅加速局域电流空间分布计算，并通过求解离散泊松方程将键电流（bond currents）重构为局域轨道磁化（local orbital magnetization）与新的局域陈标记（local Chern marker）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 局域电流承载宏观样品的轨道磁化，对非均匀磁性材料建模及磁电、拓扑响应理解至关重要，但非均匀体系中局域电流的真实感模拟成像仍具挑战，且非对角元此前需独立模拟、计算代价高。

Method: 在紧束缚模型（tight-binding models）随机估计方法基础上，用单组滤波随机向量同时获得半正定算符（positive semidefinite operators）的全部矩阵元；并将键电流表示为满足离散泊松方程（discrete Poisson equation）的环电流（loop currents），以电流涡量（current vorticity）为源项求解得到局域轨道磁化。

Result: 该磁化对化学势的导数定义出一个新的局域陈标记，完全由键电流构造，无需位置算符，且不受开放样品中投影类标记的零和规则限制，因而无边界补偿伪影，其样本平均以可控方式收敛到体陈数（bulk Chern number）。

Conclusion: 所提方法消除了非对角元独立模拟的需求，显著加速局域电流空间映射计算，并提供了一个无边界伪影、可收敛至体拓扑不变量的局域陈标记，与全局Středa关系形成类比。

Abstract: Local currents carry the orbital magnetization of a macroscopic sample and are key for modeling inhomogeneous magnetic materials and for understanding magnetoelectric and topological responses. However, producing realistic simulated images of local currents in inhomogeneous systems remains challenging. Building upon recent work on stochastic estimation methods for tight-binding models, we show that all matrix elements of positive semidefinite operators defined on a lattice can be obtained at once from a single set of filtered random vectors. This eliminates the need for independent simulations previously required for off-diagonal elements, which significantly speeds up the calculation of spatial maps of local currents. Further, we express bond currents in terms of loop currents that obey a discrete Poisson equation, with the current vorticity acting as the source term. Its solution yields a local orbital magnetization. In analogy with the global Středa relation, the derivative of this magnetization with respect to the chemical potential defines a new local Chern marker. Built entirely from bond currents, it requires no position operator and is not bound by the zero-sum rule of projector markers in open samples. Hence, it is free of boundary-compensation artifacts, and its sample average converges to the bulk Chern number in a controlled fashion.

</details>


### [227] [Fractional Charge at Dislocations from Band-Inversion Cancellation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26616)
*Vladimir Juričić,Rodrigo Soto-Garrido*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该文指出，具有相反陈数的能带反转在霍尔响应中相互抵消，并产生可打开能隙的反向传播边界模式，但缺乏对这种被抵消后仍存拓扑的体探测手段。作者证明，在位反演对称、能带反转相互抵消的两带晶体中，位错会束缚半整数电荷，从而揭示其量子化体极化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相反陈数的能带反转相互抵消后，边界模式可发生能隙化，现有手段缺少对该残余拓扑的体探测（bulk probe）。研究动机在于寻找一种不依赖边界模式、能直接反映该拓扑的体测量量。

Method: 将一大类两带晶格模型连续形变为解耦链（decoupled chains），过程中不关闭体能隙、不破坏反演对称性；在链极限下解析地、在偏离链极限时数值地计算位错（dislocation）所束缚的电荷，并借助占据键的计数确定该电荷。

Result: 位错束缚半整数电荷，其分数部分对位错芯（dislocation core）的局域微扰不敏感，因而在去除隙内芯态（in-gap core state）后仍然存在；在Weyl半金属（Weyl semimetal）对应体系中，磁通响应将该晶格电荷与各动量切片陈数所决定的电荷分离开来。

Conclusion: 半整数芯电荷可作为该被抵消拓扑的体探针，并且当隙内峰从局域谱中消失后仍持续存在，因此可在人工电子与光学晶格（optical lattices）中探测，超材料（metamaterials）可提供对芯模的互补观测途径。

Abstract: Band inversions with opposite Chern numbers cancel in the Hall response and yield counterpropagating boundary modes that can gap out. However, a bulk probe of the topology that survives such a cancellation is lacking. We here show that dislocations in inversion-symmetric two-band crystals with cancelling band inversions bind a half-integer charge, which reveals the quantized bulk polarization of the bond-centered obstructed atomic limit realized by such crystals. To demonstrate this, we continuously deform a broad class of two-band lattice models into decoupled chains, without closing the bulk gap or breaking the inversion symmetry, where the dislocation charge follows from a count of occupied bonds. Notably, the fractional part of this charge is insensitive to local perturbations of the dislocation core, and it therefore persists after the in-gap core state is removed, as we show analytically in the chain limit and numerically away from it. In the Weyl-semimetal counterpart, the response to a magnetic flux separates this crystalline charge from the charge set by the Chern number of each momentum slice. We propose that a half-integer core charge that persists after the in-gap peak disappears from the local spectrum can be detected in artificial electronic and optical lattices, with metamaterials providing complementary access to the core modes.

</details>


<div id='physics.app-ph'></div>

# physics.app-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [228] [Geometric control of diffusive relaxation: spectral design, transport bounds and transverse mode isolation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25173)
*Pengfei Zhu*

Main category: physics.app-ph

TL;DR: 该摘要研究被动、均匀傅里叶导体（Fourier conductor）在固定材料参数、体积与长度下，几何约束（geometric confinement）对热弛豫（thermal relaxation）与热输运（heat throughput）的差异化调控。核心结论是二者可由不同传导路径支配：在带弱横向交换的连续并联路径中，轴向热导保持 κV/L² 而恰有一个正绝缘弛豫率趋于零，从而实现"亚稳态温度对比保持"而非第二平衡态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 几何约束同时改变热弛豫与热通过量，但摘要指出这些效应未必由同一传导路径控制。研究动机在于厘清何时对内部平衡的几何控制可以与宏观热输运相分离，并区分串联与并联几何在固定体积与长度下的不同行为。

Method: 在被动、均匀傅里叶导体框架下区分串联与并联几何（serial and parallel geometries）。对变截面导体使用两个对数形状谐波（logarithmic shape harmonics）独立调节 Bloch 速率对，并结合精确局部输运水平集与加权的非微扰 Poincaré 界（weighted Poincaré bound）分析；进而构造容量加权网络（capacity-weighted network）与三维乘积域（product domain）实现，并用谱投影估计（spectral projection estimates）刻画观测窗口。

Result: 变截面导体可在固定有效扩散率下通过谱重分布调节选定 Bloch 速率对，但 Poincaré 界表明：若首个正绝缘速率在固定长度下趋于零，则有效轴向扩散率必须消失。该阻碍可由带有弱横向交换的连续并联路径规避：三维乘积域的轴向电导仍为 κV/L²，恰有一个正绝缘速率消失而下一个趋于由轴向、腔室与颈部扩散决定的正极限，慢速率渐近等于横向颈部电导乘以腔室逆容量之和。

Conclusion: 谱投影估计将该谱分离转化为依赖制备与读出（preparation- and readout-dependent）的观测窗口，其结果是绝缘条件下温度对比的亚稳态保持（metastable retention），而非第二平衡态。该工作识别了几何控制内部平衡可与宏观热输运分离的条件。

Abstract: Geometric confinement changes both thermal relaxation and heat throughput, but these effects need not be governed by the same conduction path. We establish a distinction between serial and parallel geometries for passive, homogeneous Fourier conductors at fixed material properties, volume and length. For a reduced variable-area conductor, two logarithmic shape harmonics independently tune two selected Bloch-rate pairs, and exact local transport level sets permit spectral redistribution at fixed effective diffusivity. A nonperturbative weighted Poincaré bound, however, requires the effective axial diffusivity to vanish if the first positive insulated rate tends to zero at fixed length. This obstruction is avoided by continuous parallel paths with weak transverse exchange. Capacity-weighted networks identify the exchange mechanism, and a three-dimensional product domain provides an exact bulk realization. Its axial conductance remains $κV/L^2$, while exactly one positive insulated rate vanishes and the next approaches a positive limit set by axial, chamber and neck diffusion. The slow rate is asymptotically the transverse neck conductance times the sum of inverse chamber capacities. Spectral projection estimates translate this separation into a preparation- and readout-dependent observation window. The result is metastable retention of a temperature contrast under insulated conditions, not a second equilibrium state. It identifies when geometrical control of internal equilibration can be separated from macroscopic heat transport.

</details>

# math.PR [[Back]](#toc)

### [229] [The Markov Property on Decorated Metric Planar Maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25081)
*Pablo Araya,Luis Fredes,Avelio Sepúlveda*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 该文引入“装饰度量平面映射（decorated metric planar maps）”：将平面映射的每条边替换为线段 $[0,w_e]$，并在所得度量空间上赋予连续函数作为“装饰（decoration）”。在顶点值通过二次哈密顿量（quadratic Hamiltonian）相互作用、边内装饰为布朗桥（Brownian bridge）的条件下，刻画了满足允许探索在边中途停止的马尔可夫性质的定律族恰为玻尔兹曼装饰度量映射。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典装饰平面映射（decorated planar maps）与统计物理模型（如 Ising 模型、高斯自由场（Gaussian free field））的耦合已有研究，但缺乏将边视为连续线段的度量结构与马尔可夫性质统一的框架。作者希望在此度量设定下，借助马尔可夫性质公理化地刻画自然的概率定律族。

Method: 以带边界的 4-正则映射（即带边界四角化（quadrangulation）的对偶）为对象，定义一种允许探索在边中间停止的马尔可夫性质；在该性质及温和正则性假设下，对重根不变（rerooting-invariant）的定律族进行分类，并与玻尔兹曼装饰度量映射对应；进而证明强马尔可夫性质，并识别诱导马尔可夫分解的随机子映射为适当滤流下的停映（stopping maps）。

Result: 在温和正则性假设下，满足该马尔可夫性质的重根不变定律族恰为玻尔兹曼装饰度量映射；同时建立了强马尔可夫性质，并证明诱导马尔可夫分解的随机子映射恰为相应滤流的停映；对经典装饰平面映射也得到类似结论。

Conclusion: 该工作以马尔可夫性质为公理，统一刻画了装饰度量平面映射上的玻尔兹曼定律，并给出强马尔可夫性质与停映的精确对应；结果同时推广至经典装饰平面映射，为相关统计物理模型的概率研究提供了框架。

Abstract: We introduce decorated metric planar maps: planar maps whose edges $e$ are replaced by segments $[0,w_e]$, together with a continuous function on the resulting metric space, the decoration. We consider laws under which, conditionally on the metric map, the decoration follows a statistical physics model such as the Ising model or the Gaussian free field: the values at the vertices interact through a quadratic Hamiltonian and, given these values, the decoration along each edge is a Brownian bridge. Working with 4-regular maps with a boundary (the duals of quadrangulations with a boundary), we define a Markov property that allows explorations to stop in the middle of an edge. We show that, under mild regularity assumptions, the rerooting-invariant families of laws satisfying this Markov property are exactly the Boltzmann decorated metric maps. We also prove a strong Markov property and show that the random submaps inducing a Markovian decomposition are exactly the stopping maps for a suitable filtration. Finally, we obtain analogous results for classical decorated planar maps.

</details>


### [230] [The 1d Stochastic Dirac Operator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25233)
*Laure Dumaz,Cyril Labbé*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 论文构造了定义在 $L^2(\mathbb{R},\mathbb{R}^2)$ 上、由两个参数 $\\kappa_0,\\kappa$ 索引的“一维随机 Dirac 算子（1d Stochastic Dirac Operator）”，并研究其谱性质：$\\kappa=0$ 时谱为绝对连续，$\\kappa>0$ 时为纯点谱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者指出该随机 Dirac 算子在一维随机 Schrödinger 算子（random Schrödinger operators）的小无序极限（small disorder limit）中具有一般性（generically）出现，从而揭示某种普适行为（universal behavior），本文旨在对该奇异算子作详细构造与分析。

Method: 对定义于 $L^2(\\mathbb{R},\\mathbb{R}^2)$ 上的奇异算子给出详细构造，并分析其谱性质；对 $\\kappa>0$ 的情形刻画特征函数形状，另建立有限体积算子到无穷体积算子的特征值/特征函数序列的收敛结果。

Result: 谱在 $\\kappa=0$ 时为绝对连续（absolutely continuous），在 $\\kappa>0$ 时为纯点谱（pure point）；$\\kappa>0$ 时特征函数形状由带漂移的双边布朗运动（two sided Brownian motion with drift）的指数给出，并与 Rifkind 与 Virág 的结果相关联；同时得到有限体积到无穷体积的精细收敛结果。

Conclusion: 该随机 Dirac 算子可视为一维随机 Schrödinger 算子普适小无序行为的有效描述，其谱类型由参数 $\\kappa$ 决定，且有限体积谱数据以精细方式收敛至无穷体积极限。

Abstract: We introduce a random Dirac operator on $L^2(\mathbb{R},\mathbb{R}^2)$ that we call $1d$ Stochastic Dirac Operator and which is indexed by two parameters $κ_0,κ$. In two companion articles, we show that this operator arises generically as the small disorder limit of one-dimensional random Schrödinger operators, thus revealing a universal behavior. In the present paper, we provide a detailed construction of this singular operator, and investigate its spectral properties. In particular, we show that its spectrum is either absolutely continuous when $κ= 0$, or pure point when $κ>0$. In the latter case, we show that the shape of the eigenfunctions is given by the exponential of a two sided Brownian motion with drift, in connection with a result of Rifkind and Virág. Finally, we establish a delicate convergence result of the sequence of eigenvalues / eigenfunctions from the finite-volume operator to the infinite-volume one.

</details>


### [231] [Dynamical edge universality and the Airy$_β$ line ensemble](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25364)
*Jiaoyang Huang,Shengjing Xu,Yulei Xu*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 该论文证明三类相互作用粒子系统（含解析势的 β-Dyson 布朗运动、加权 β-corners 过程及一类离散时间马尔可夫链）对任意 β>0 的动力学边缘普适性，即在非平衡初始数据下，当极限密度具有正则平方根边缘时，Airy_β 线系综在正时刻涌现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有工作多针对平衡或特定初始条件，本文旨在将边缘普适性推广至一般非平衡初值（general non-equilibrium initial data）与任意 β>0 的三类系统，统一刻画 Airy_β 线系综的动态涌现。

Method: 方法结合动力学环路方程（dynamical loop equations）与特征流估计（characteristic-flow estimates），建立移动边缘附近的局部律（local laws）及极值粒子的上界；随后识别重标度 Stieltjes 变换任一子列极限的随机演化，并借助 Airy_β 线系综的极点演化刻画（pole-evolution characterization）完成收敛证明。

Result: 以移动确定性边缘为中心，分别在 n^{-2/3} 与 n^{-1/3} 尺度上重标度空间与时间后，对每个固定 k，顶部 k 个粒子的轨迹在紧时间区间上依一致收敛拓扑联合收敛于 Airy_β 线系综的顶部 k 条曲线。

Conclusion: 结果表明动力学边缘普适性对任意 β>0 与一般非平衡初值成立，Airy_β 线系综作为普适极限在正则平方根边缘条件下于正时刻涌现，并将三类相互作用粒子系统的边缘行为纳入统一框架。

Abstract: We prove dynamical edge universality for three families of interacting particle systems for every $β>0$: $β$-Dyson Brownian motion with an analytic potential, weighted $β$-corners processes, and a class of discrete-time Markov chains. Our results apply to general non-equilibrium initial data and show that the Airy$_β$ line ensemble emerges at positive times whenever the limiting density has a regular square-root edge.
  More precisely, after centering at the moving deterministic edge and rescaling space and time on the $n^{-2/3}$ and $n^{-1/3}$ scales, respectively, the trajectories of the top $k$ particles converge jointly in distribution to the top $k$ curves of the Airy$_β$ line ensemble, for every fixed $k$, in the topology of uniform convergence on compact time intervals.
  The proof combines dynamical loop equations with characteristic-flow estimates to establish local laws near the moving edge and upper bounds on the extremal particle. We then identify the stochastic evolution of every subsequential limit of the rescaled Stieltjes transform and establish convergence through the pole-evolution characterization of the Airy$_β$ line ensemble.

</details>


### [232] [Analyticity of $\operatorname{Sine}_β$ pair correlations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26344)
*Yun Li,Yahui Qu,Benedek Valkó,Bálint Virág*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 该文证明：在任一固定非零间距处，$\operatorname{Sine}_\beta$ 的点对相关函数（pair correlation function）可关于 $\beta$ 全纯延拓至 $\operatorname{Re}\beta>0$。证明综合随机游走表示、耦合所得的拉伸指数傅里叶衰减与复 $\beta$ 加权能量估计三类工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 摘要未显式陈述研究动机，仅从目标可推断：作者关注 $\operatorname{Sine}_\beta$ 点对相关函数对参数 $\beta$ 的解析依赖性（analytic dependence），试图将 $\beta$ 的定义域从既有范围延拓到半平面 $\operatorname{Re}\beta>0$。

Method: 证明由三部分构成：随机相位矩的随机游走表示（random walk representation of stochastic sine phase moments）、通过耦合（coupling）得到的拉伸指数傅里叶衰减（stretched-exponential Fourier decay），以及针对复 $\beta$ 的加权能量估计（weighted energy estimates）。

Result: 在固定非零间距下，$\operatorname{Sine}_\beta$ 的点对相关函数关于 $\beta$ 全纯（holomorphic）并可延拓到 $\operatorname{Re}\beta>0$；该结论对每个非零间距均成立。

Conclusion: 论文建立了 $\operatorname{Sine}_\beta$ 点对相关函数在 $\operatorname{Re}\beta>0$ 上的全纯延拓性，表明复参数 $\beta$ 框架下该方法组合（随机游走表示、耦合型衰减估计、加权能量估计）足以控制解析延拓。

Abstract: We prove that the pair correlation function of $\operatorname{Sine}_β$ extends holomorphically in $β$ to $\Reβ>0$ at every fixed nonzero separation. Our proof combines a random walk representation of the stochastic sine phase moments, stretched-exponential Fourier decay obtained by coupling, and weighted energy estimates for complex $β$.

</details>


### [233] [Generic arm separation for planar percolation models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26458)
*Loïc Gassmann,Ioan Manolescu,Maran Mohanarangan*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 本文提出一个适用于任意平面渗流模型（planar percolation models）的通用臂分离（arm separation）陈述，其探索算法与具体模型无关，并覆盖所有多色臂事件（polychromatic arm events）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 臂分离是研究临界与近临界平面渗流系统的关键工具，与 RSW 估计结合可证明臂事件概率的准乘性（quasi-multiplicativity）、联系不同事件的导数并识别普适臂指数；但已有陈述多针对特定模型，缺乏一般性。

Method: 论证基于对以臂存在为条件的构型进行探索，使“已暴露”（exposed）的臂以正概率被揭示；该探索算法独立于渗流模型，适用于所有多色臂事件。

Result: 给出臂分离的一个非常通用的表述，适用于任意平面渗流模型；作为应用，证明了临界平面 FK 渗流（FK-percolation）的一个强臂分离结果。

Conclusion: 臂分离可被推广至任意平面渗流模型的通用框架，并在临界平面 FK 渗流上得到强形式的验证。

Abstract: Arm separation is an important technical tool in the study of critical and near-critical planar percolation systems. When combined with RSW estimates, it proves quasi-multiplicativity of arm-event probabilities, links the derivatives of different events and allows the identification of universal arm exponents. We provide a very generic statement of arm separation that applies to arbitrary planar percolation models. Our argument is based on exploring configurations conditioned on the existence of arms in such a way that "exposed" arms are revealed with positive probability. The exploration algorithm is independent of the percolation model and applies to all polychromatic arm events. As an application, we prove a strong arm-separation result for critical planar FK-percolation.

</details>


### [234] [On Romik's rationality conjectures for critical percolation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26570)
*Melda Akyazi-Zavadski,Nathanaël Berestycki,Sunil Chhita*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 该文利用Pfaffian公式证明了完全堆积O(1)环模型（即方形格点临界面键渗流的界面表示）在半无限圆柱上嵌套拱形事件的概率为N的有理函数，并在半平面极限下仍保持有理，从而验证了Romik有理猜想的关键部分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Romik的有理猜想（rationality conjectures）预测半无限圆柱上子匹配事件（submatching events）的概率由特定有理函数给出，且半平面极限下变为二进有理数；本文旨在证明这一猜想的核心情形。

Method: 借助经qKZ形式推导的Fonseca–Zinn-Justin恒等式，将概率表示为全对称自互补平面分拆（TSSCPPs）枚举数之比；再通过其二聚体表示（dimer representation）应用Kasteleyn理论得到Pfaffian，并用Zeilberger与Gosper算法求值所得超几何和，最后取N→∞极限。

Result: 得到周长为2N的半无限圆柱上k个嵌套拱形（k<N）概率的显式Pfaffian公式，证明其为N的有理函数，并证明对应半平面概率仍为有理数；同时给出Ayyer与Chhita的逆Kasteleyn矩阵元的一个简化表达式。

Conclusion: 研究确立了Romik猜想（rationality conjectures）的关键方面，展示了可积性方法（Kasteleyn理论、qKZ形式）与计算机代数求和算法相结合在界面概率精确计算中的有效性。

Abstract: The completely packed $O(1)$ loop model can be viewed as an interface representation of critical bond percolation on the square lattice. We consider submatching events, which prescribe how these interfaces pair finitely many consecutive boundary points. Romik's rationality conjectures predict that the probabilities of these events on the semi-infinite cylinder are given by specific rational functions, which even become dyadic rationals in the half-plane limit.
  We derive explicit Pfaffian formulae for the probability of $k$ nested arches on the semi-infinite cylinder of circumference $2N$, valid for $k<N$. We deduce that these probabilities are rational functions of $N$ and, by taking the limit $N\to\infty$, that the corresponding half-plane probabilities remain rational. This establishes key aspects of Romik's conjectures.
  Our proof uses identities of Fonseca and Zinn-Justin, derived through the qKZ formalism, which express these probabilities as ratios of enumeration numbers for totally symmetric self-complementary plane partitions (TSSCPPs). These plane partitions admit a dimer representation, allowing us to apply Kasteleyn theory. We evaluate the resulting hypergeometric sum using Zeilberger's and Gosper's algorithms and then take the half-plane limit $N\to\infty$. Along the way, we derive a somewhat simplified expression for the inverse Kasteleyn matrix entries (encoding correlations in TSSCPPs) of Ayyer and Chhita, which might be of independent interest.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.supr-con'></div>

# cond-mat.supr-con [[Back]](#toc)

### [235] [Umklapp-enhanced high-$T_c$ topological superconductivity mediated by magnons in $p$-wave magnets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25015)
*Kristoffer Leraand,Asle Sudbø*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该文表明在 $p$ 波磁体的微观模型中，磁振子交换（magnon exchange）可诱导节点型拓扑超导（nodal topological superconductivity）。弱耦合多带能隙方程给出节点 $p$ 波能隙，其临界温度随费米面靠近磁布里渊区边界而急剧上升；动量分辨绕数预测对称保持边缘处存在零能马约拉纳平带（Majorana flat bands）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于探究磁振子作为配对媒介能否在 $p$ 波磁体这类微观模型中产生具有节点与拓扑特征的超导态，并厘清其与费米面几何的关系。

Method: 对巡游电子与局域自旋交换耦合的多带体系求解弱耦合能隙方程（weak-coupling multiband gap equation），并计算动量分辨绕数（momentum-resolved winding numbers）以判定体节点投影下的边缘态。

Result: 得到节点 $p$ 波能隙，临界温度在费米面接近磁布里渊区边界时急剧升高；该区域中小约化动量磁振子通过 Umklapp 过程在区两侧的费米面段间散射电子，因磁单胞不同格点的电子—磁振子顶点抵消较弱而增强配对。绕数分析预测对称保持边缘处存在零能马约拉纳平带。

Conclusion: 磁振子交换是节点拓扑超导的有效机制，Umklapp 散射与磁单胞内顶点结构是配对增强的关键，并预言可由边缘马约拉纳平带检验的拓扑节点相。

Abstract: We show that magnon exchange can induce nodal topological superconductivity in a microscopic model of a $p$-wave magnet. Solving the weak-coupling multiband gap equation for itinerant electrons exchange-coupled to localized spins, we find a nodal $p$-wave gap and a critical temperature that rises sharply as the Fermi surface approaches the magnetic Brillouin-zone boundary. In this regime, small-reduced-momentum magnons can scatter electrons between Fermi-surface segments on opposite sides of the zone. These Umklapp processes enhance pairing because the contributions from different sites in the magnetic unit cell cancel less in their electron-magnon vertices than in those for ordinary scattering at comparable reduced momenta. Momentum-resolved winding numbers predict zero-energy Majorana flat bands at symmetry-preserving edges where the bulk nodes project to distinct momenta.

</details>


### [236] [First order vortex-unbinding transition in 2D topological superconductors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25018)
*Vera Mikheeva,Elio J. König*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 研究含孤立马约拉纳零模（Majorana zero mode）的二维拓扑超导体中BKT转变（Berezinskii–Kosterlitz–Thouless transition）的命运，指出马约拉纳杂化可将其驱动为一级相变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当马约拉纳局域长度（Majorana localization length）ξ_F 超过涡旋核心尺度 ξ 时，涡旋间的马约拉纳杂化会引入额外短程吸引通道，从而可能改变传统BKT相变性质，这一情形此前缺乏系统刻画。

Method: 构建描述稀薄涡旋气体（dilute vortex gas）的有效场论（effective field theory），将普通BKT相场与编码马约拉纳介导相互作用的附加场耦合；采用重整化群（renormalization-group）与瞬子（instanton）方法分析相变性质。

Result: 马约拉纳杂化在名义 T_BKT 以上的双涡旋自由能（two-vortex free energy）中产生亚稳极小值（metastable minimum），并可将相变驱动为一级相变，亚稳相以指数小的速率衰变。

Conclusion: 提出约瑟夫森耦合的 p+ip 超导岛阵列（Josephson-coupled p+ip superconducting islands）作为实现所需尺度层级的微观平台，并讨论一级相变的实验特征，尤其是宽度由共存窗口决定的迟滞回线（hysteresis loop）。

Abstract: We study the fate of the Berezinskii--Kosterlitz--Thouless (BKT) transition in a two-dimensional topological superconductor whose vortex cores each host an isolated Majorana zero mode, energetically well separated from the bulk gap and other bound states of the vortex. When the Majorana localization length $ξ_F$ exceeds the vortex core size $ξ$, nearby vortices acquire an additional short-range attractive channel from Majorana hybridization, modifying the vortex-antivortex interaction at short distances and generating a metastable minimum in the two-vortex free energy above the nominal $T_{BKT}$. We construct an effective field theory for the resulting dilute vortex gas, coupling the ordinary BKT phase field to an additional field encoding the Majorana-mediated interaction, and show, using renormalization-group and instanton methods, that this hybridization can drive the transition first order, with the metastable phase decaying at an exponentially small rate. We propose an array of Josephson-coupled $p+ip$ superconducting islands as a microscopic platform realizing the required hierarchy of scales. Finally, we discuss experimental signatures of this first-order transition, in particular a hysteresis loop whose width is set by the coexistence window.

</details>


### [237] [Magnetic order in Tb and Dy substituted La3Ni2O7 and effects of high-pressure high-temperature O2 annealing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25349)
*Yuhang Deng,Farhad Taraporevala,Jui-Chia Lee,Zhekai Yang,Gabriel Mee,Swadhin Rout,Richard Erber,Fábio Abud,Renato F. Jardim,Maxim Avdeev,Jeffrey W. Lynn,M. Brian Maple*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 在La3Ni2O7中用磁性镧系元素（Ln = Pr、Eu、Gd、Tb、Dy、Ho、Er、Tm、Yb）部分取代La，系统研究其对结构、输运与磁性的影响；Tb、Dy取代样品在退火前于约13 K和11 K以下出现长程磁序（long-range magnetic order），氧退火后磁信号被强烈抑制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在探究磁性镧系取代（magnetic lanthanide substitution）对La3Ni2O7的影响，以寻找可研究磁序（magnetic order）与压力诱导超导（pressure-induced superconductivity）相互作用的材料平台。

Method: 合成La3-xLnxNi2O7多晶样品（Ln = Pr、Eu、Gd、Tb、Dy、Ho、Er、Tm、Yb），并结合电输运（electrical transport）、磁化（magnetization）测量以及X射线与中子粉末衍射（x-ray and neutron powder diffraction），在高压高温氧退火（high-pressure high-temperature oxygen annealing）前后进行表征。

Result: 氧退火促使La3Ni2O7多型相（polymorphs）形成，使电阻呈现更类金属（metallic-like）的温度依赖，而密度波转变温度（density-wave transition temperature）仅有小幅变化。未退火的La2.7Tb0.3Ni2O7与La2.7Dy0.3Ni2O7在磁化率和中子衍射中显示额外磁峰，分别在约13 K和11 K以下建立长程磁序；氧退火后磁信号显著被抑制，可能与伴随的结构改变相关。

Conclusion: Tb、Dy取代与Pr、Nd、Sm取代形成对比，后者不引入磁序；据此La3-xTbxNi2O7与La3-xDyxNi2O7可作为探索磁序与压力诱导超导相互作用的候选平台。

Abstract: To study the influence of magnetic lanthanide (Ln) substituents on La3Ni2O7, polycrystalline samples of La3-xLnxNi2O7 (Ln = Pr, Eu, Gd, Tb, Dy, Ho, Er, Tm, and Yb) were synthesized and characterized by electrical transport, magnetization, and x-ray and neutron powder diffraction before and after high-pressure high-temperature oxygen annealing. The annealing process led to the formation of La3Ni2O7 polymorphs and more metallic-like temperature dependence of the electrical resistance while changing the density-wave transition temperature only slightly. For unannealed La2.7Tb0.3Ni2O7 and La2.7Dy0.3Ni2O7, distinct features in the magnetic susceptibility and additional magnetic peaks in neutron diffraction patterns indicate that both samples develop long-range magnetic order below ~13 and 11 K, respectively. The magnetic signatures were strongly suppressed following oxygen annealing, suggesting a possible association with the accompanying structural modifications. These behaviors distinguish Tb and Dy from Pr, Nd, and Sm substituents which do not introduce magnetic ordering in the system, establishing La3-xTbxNi2O7 and La3-xDyxNi2O7 as promising platforms for exploring the interplay between magnetic order and pressure-induced superconductivity in La3Ni2O7.

</details>


### [238] [Generalized Critical-State Analysis of Local Field Screening in High-Pressure Yttrium Polyhydride](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25456)
*A. G. Gavriliuk,I. A. Troyan,A. A. Mironovich,A. G. Ivanova,H. -K. Mao,V. V. Struzhkin*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该研究用 $^{57}$Fe 穆斯堡尔（Mössbauer）传感器测量 171 GPa、4.1 K 下钇多氢化物（yttrium polyhydride）附近的局域磁场，证明高压超氢化物中的磁通排斥（flux expulsion）源于钉扎临界态（pinned critical state），而非平衡迈斯纳效应（Meissner effect）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高压超氢化物中磁通排斥的解释长期存在争议，需要区分其究竟是平衡迈斯纳效应还是钉扎主导的非平衡临界态（critical state）。

Method: 以 $^{57}$Fe 穆斯堡尔传感器通过核前向散射（nuclear forward scattering）追踪局域磁感应强度 $B_{\rm sensor}(B_a)$；不预设恒流 Bean 态，而用 Shantsev–Clem–Sanchez 方法求解实际薄盘几何的临界态问题，并比较多种场依赖的广义临界态模型。

Result: 局域磁感应曲线在约 1.5 T 附近几乎与正常态线重合；Kim 模型与指数模型均能复现测量的初始磁化分支（virgin branch），而恒定 $J_c$ 的 Bean 模型失效。两者给出的零场临界电流密度分别为 $(4.38\pm1.48)\times10^{11}$ 与 $(2.45\pm0.22)\times10^{11}$ A/m$^2$，量级上与颗粒状 YH$_6$ 的输运估计一致。

Conclusion: 观测到的磁通排斥是钉扎临界态而非平衡迈斯纳效应；该工作同时提供了一种在极端条件下探测局域超导性（local superconductivity）的通用方法。

Abstract: We resolve the long-standing controversy over the interpretation of magnetic-flux expulsion in high-pressure superhydrides by measuring the local magnetic field adjacent to yttrium polyhydride at 171 GPa and 4.1 K with a $^{57}$Fe Mössbauer sensor. Nuclear forward scattering tracks the local induction $B_{\rm sensor}(B_a)$, which nearly merges with the normal-state line near 1.5 T. Rather than assuming a constant-current Bean state, we solve the critical-state problem for the actual thin-disk geometry using the Shantsev--Clem--Sanchez method and compare generalized field-dependent critical-state models. Both the Kim and exponential laws reproduce the measured virgin branch, whereas the constant-$J_c$ Bean model fails. The corresponding zero-field critical-current densities, $(4.38\pm1.48)\times10^{11}$ and $(2.45\pm0.22)\times10^{11}$ A/m$^2$, respectively, are consistent in order of magnitude with transport estimates for granular YH$_6$. These results establish that the observed flux expulsion is a pinned critical state, not the equilibrium Meissner effect, and demonstrate a general methodology for probing local superconductivity under extreme conditions.

</details>


### [239] [Comparative First-Principles Study of the Physical Properties, Phonon-Mediated Superconductivity and Functional Potential of Technetium-Based Binary Borides TcB2, Tc2B and Tc3B](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25554)
*M. Rabi Us Sani Shapon,Jubair Hossan Abir,Md. Asif Afzal,Raihana Shams Islam,Saleh Hasan Naqib*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该文以第一性原理（first-principles）比较TcB2（20 GPa）、Tc2B与Tc3B（0 GPa）三种过渡金属硼化物，发现其力学、电子与光学响应各异但均为金属性；超导转变温度由电子-声子匹配而非费米面态密度大小主导，TcB2最高（24.92 K）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二元过渡金属硼化物（binary transition-metal borides）的声子介导超导（phonon-mediated superconductivity）取决于金属d态、金属-硼成键与晶格振动之间的平衡，需厘清化学计量与结构差异如何影响力学、电子结构及超导性能。

Method: 在统一的第一性原理框架下，将20 GPa下的P6/mmm相TcB2与0 GPa下的I4/mcm相Tc2B、P4/mmm相Tc3B进行对比，考察力学响应、费米能级态特征、电荷再分布、自旋-轨道耦合（spin-orbit coupling）影响、光学响应及电子-声子耦合（electron-phonon coupling, EPC），并以μ* = 0.10估算Tc。

Result: TcB2在压缩条件下最刚性，Tc2B延展性（ductility）倾向最强，Tc3B居中；三者均保持金属性，费米能级态主要由Tc-4d贡献，Tc-d/B-p混合程度各异。电荷再分布在TcB2中更具方向性且以B为中心，在Tc2B与Tc3B中则更离域于Tc网络。自旋-轨道耦合不改变金属性；光学响应呈金属性且依赖电场极化，具显著可见光反射与强紫外吸收。低频富Tc振动主导EPC；Tc值分别为24.92、11.07与15.12 K。尽管Tc3B的N(EF)更高，TcB2与EPC活性低频模耦合最强。

Conclusion: 超导性能的层级（superconducting hierarchy）取决于电子与声子的匹配（electronic-phonon matching），而非仅由费米能级态密度（density of states at the Fermi level）大小决定；该结论为过渡金属硼化物超导体的成分与结构设计提供依据。

Abstract: Phonon-mediated superconductivity in binary transition-metal borides reflects a balance between the metal d-states, metal-boron bonding, and the lattice vibrations that couple to them. Here, we compare P6/mmm TcB2 at 20 GPa with I4/mcm Tc2B and P4/mmm Tc3B at 0 GPa within a first-principles framework. Their mechanical responses differ appreciably: TcB2 is the most rigid under its compressed condition, Tc2B shows the strongest tendency toward ductility, and Tc3B falls between the two. Despite these differences, all three phases remain metallic. The states at the Fermi level are dominated by Tc-4d character, although the extent of Tc-d/B-p mixing varies from one compound to another. Charge redistribution is more directional and B-centered in TcB2, but more delocalized around the Tc network in Tc2B and Tc3B. Inclusion of spin-orbit coupling leaves all three compounds metallic. The optical response remains metallic and electric field polarization dependent, with substantial visible reflectivity and strong ultraviolet absorption. Low-frequency Tc rich vibrations dominate the electron-phonon coupling (EPC). With μ* = 0.10, the Tc values of TcB2, Tc2B, and Tc3B are 24.92, 11.07, and 15.12 K, respectively. Although Tc3B exhibits a higher electronic density of states at the Fermi level, N(EF), TcB2 couples most strongly to EPC-active low-frequency modes, showing that the superconducting hierarchy depends on electronic-phonon matching rather than DOS magnitude alone.

</details>


<div id='math.SP'></div>

### [240] [Optimal long-time derivative estimates for underdamped Langevin dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25961)
*Lukang Sun*

Main category: math.SP

TL;DR: 研究弱阻尼朗之万（underdamped Langevin）算子 $\mathcal{A}$ 在 $L^2(\mu)$ 上生成半群 $P_t=e^{t\mathcal{A}}$ 的导数长时间衰减行为；在半群各阶导数上给出 $t^{-2m}$ 与 $t^{-(2m+1)}$ 的最优多项式衰减率，且不要求 Gibbs 测度（Gibbs measure）有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有结果多针对有限 Gibbs 测度或零阶半群本身的衰减，缺乏对半群任意阶算子导数与梯度导数的长时间定量刻画；本文旨在取消 $\mu$ 有限性假设并给出与自由动力学相符的最优衰减指数。

Method: 在势函数 Hessian 的加权相对有界（weighted relative bound）假设下，建立靠近虚轴（imaginary axis）的预解式估计（resolvent estimate），该估计对任意光滑势均成立，其推导依赖生成元与速度反转（velocity reversal）的伴随关系，以及速度变量上的高斯 Poincaré 不等式（Gaussian Poincaré inequality）。

Result: 对任意整数 $m\ge 0$，长时间下成立 $\|\mathcal{A}^m P_t h_0\|_{L^2(\mu)}^2 \le C_m t^{-2m}\|h_0\|^2$ 与 $\|\nabla_{x,v}\mathcal{A}^m P_t h_0\|_{L^2(\mu)}^2 \le D_m t^{-(2m+1)}\|h_0\|^2$；自由动力学（free dynamics）表明这些指数是最优的。

Conclusion: 结果给出了不依赖 Gibbs 测度有限性的高阶导数最优衰减率，并显示其证明核心是普适的虚轴附近预解式估计，可用于刻画弱阻尼朗之万半群在 $L^2(\mu)$ 中的长时间正则化行为。

Abstract: We study the long-time behaviour of derivatives of the semigroup $P_t=e^{t\mathcal{A}}$ generated by the underdamped Langevin (kinetic Fokker-Planck) operator $\mathcal{A}$ on $L^2(μ)$, where the Gibbs measure $μ$ is not required to be finite. Under a weighted relative bound on the Hessian of the potential, we prove that for every integer $m\ge 0$ the squared $L^2(μ)$ norms of $\mathcal{A}^m P_t h_0$ and of $\nabla_{x,v}\mathcal{A}^m P_t h_0$ are bounded by $C_m t^{-2m}\|h_0\|^2$ and $D_m t^{-(2m+1)}\|h_0\|^2$ respectively for large times, and the free dynamics shows that these exponents are optimal. The proof rests on a resolvent estimate near the imaginary axis, valid for every smooth potential, which follows from the adjoint relation between the generator and the velocity reversal and from the Gaussian Poincaré inequality in the velocity variable.

</details>

### [241] [Testing the Universality of RAR Model with Constraints on Fermionic Dark Matter for M31](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24991)
*Aqsa Yasmin,Ibrar Hussain,Mubasher Jamil*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 将 Ruffini–Argüelles–Rueda（RAR）暗物质模型应用于仙女座星系 M31，发现在费米子质量不超过 60 keV 时，简并费米核可替代中心黑洞提供约 1.4×10^8 M_⊙ 的中心质量，并与银河系结果在 48–60 keV 区间兼容。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAR 模型此前主要应用于银河系，且费米子质量取值范围因 S 星轨道拟合与热力学稳定性分析而不一致；本文通过检验 M31 这一质量更大的星系系统，考察该模型能否用单一费米子质量同时描述两个星系。

Method: 在广义相对论下求解自引力费米子气的相对论平衡方程（relativistic equilibrium equations），费米子质量取 mc^2 = 6–60 keV，并叠加核球与盘的重子模型计算总旋转曲线；同时与 NFW 和 Burkert 剖面拟合作对比，并进行核心引力坍缩稳定性分析。

Result: 对所有测试质量，费米核均位于 0.19 pc 以内并提供所需中心质量；总旋转曲线统计上可接受（χ^2/N_dof ≈ 1.18），但在 3–11 kpc 低于盘数据，归因于所采用的重子模型。NFW 与 Burkert 拟合 χ^2 更低但需额外黑洞。稳定性约束给出 mc^2 ≲ 60 keV。

Conclusion: 结合银河系早先的 48–345 keV 范围，单一费米子质量仅在 48–60 keV（含 56 keV）内可同时描述 M31 与银河系；若采用热力学稳定性给出的 194–387 keV 范围，则不存在能提供 M31 中心质量的费米核，M31 必须包含黑洞。

Abstract: The Ruffini--Argüelles--Rueda (RAR) model describes dark matter as a self-gravitating system of massive fermions in thermodynamic equilibrium within general relativity. It suggests a dual morphology in galaxies: a highly degenerate quantum core mimicking a central supermassive black hole, and a classical halo that explains galactic rotation curves. Earlier applications of the model to the Milky Way restricted the fermion mass to $mc^{2}=48\text{--}345~\text{keV}$, and the orbits of the S-stars around Sagittarius~A$^{\ast}$ (Sgr~A$^{\ast}$) favored $mc^{2}=56~\text{keV}$, whereas a recent thermodynamic stability analysis allows only $mc^{2}\approx194\text{--}387~\text{keV}$. In this work, we apply the RAR model to the Andromeda galaxy (M31), which hosts a dark compact object of approximately $1.4\times10^{8}~M_{\odot}$ within $0.19~\text{pc}$, roughly 33 times the mass of Sgr~A$^{\ast}$. We solve the relativistic equilibrium equations for $mc^{2}=6\text{--}60~\text{keV}$. For all these masses, the fermionic core lies within $0.19~\text{pc}$ and provides the central mass without a black hole. With a bulge and disk added, the total rotation curve is statistically acceptable ($χ^{2}/N_{\text{dof}}\approx1.18$) but lies below the disk data at $3\text{--}11~\text{kpc}$, an offset attributed to the baryonic models we adopted. The Navarro--Frenk--White (NFW) and Burkert profiles fitted to the same data give a lower $χ^{2}$ but require an additional black hole. The stability of the M31 core against gravitational collapse limits the fermion mass to $mc^{2}\lesssim60~\text{keV}$. With the earlier Milky Way range, a single fermion can therefore describe both galaxies only for $mc^{2}=48\text{--}60~\text{keV}$, which includes $56~\text{keV}$. With the thermodynamic range, no fermionic core can supply the central mass of M31, so M31 would have to host a black hole.

</details>


<div id='math.NA'></div>
