### [242] [Variational integrators for time-dependent Lagrangian systems on Lie groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25313)
*Leonardo Colombo,María Emma Eyrea Irazú,David Martín de Diego,María Daniela Sánchez,Marcela Zuccalli*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文发展了一类可变时间步长（fixed-step）变分积分器（variational integrators），用于李群（Lie groups）上的时变拉格朗日系统，给出约化动力学并通过后向误差分析（backward error analysis）刻画其长期行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对时变拉格朗日系统在李群上的几何数值积分，需要保持辛结构、李-泊松结构（Lie-Poisson structure）与守恒律，并给出长期稳定性的严格刻画。

Method: 推导连续欧拉-拉格朗日方程、能量平衡与诺特定理（Noether conservation laws）；利用收缩映射（retractions）构造时间指标离散拉格朗日量，显式处理中点-Cayley（midpoint-Cayley）格式；通过变分误差分析证明二阶精度，并构造 T*(R×G) 上精确辛、可投影的扩展及形式修正哈密顿量。

Result: 相空间映射为辛映射；左不变情形下约化为保持余伴随轨道（coadjoint orbits）与 Casimir 函数（在 G 连通时）的李-泊松映射；中点-Cayley 方法具二阶精度；在均匀解析性等假设下，最优截断实现指数精度插值与修正扩展哈密顿量在指数长时间区间上的近守恒。SO(3)^N 姿态网络的连续动力学静态共识集局部渐近稳定，离散格式精确保总空间动量。

Conclusion: 所提出的不动步长变分积分器在几何结构保持与长期稳定性方面具有理论保证，数值实验验证了二阶收敛性与扩展能量平衡。

Abstract: We develop fixed-step variational integrators for time-dependent Lagrangian systems on Lie groups, derive their reduced dynamics, and analyze their long-time behavior by backward error analysis. We derive the continuous Euler-Lagrange equations, energy balance, and Noether conservation laws, and construct time-indexed discrete Lagrangians using retractions, with the midpoint-Cayley scheme treated explicitly. On their regularity domains, the resulting phase-space maps are symplectic. For left-invariant discrete Lagrangians, they reduce to Lie-Poisson maps that preserve coadjoint orbits and, when $G$ is connected, every Casimir function. A variational error analysis establishes second-order accuracy of the midpoint-Cayley method. We also construct an exactly symplectic, projectable extension on $T^*(\mathbb R\times G)$ whose formal modified Hamiltonian is affine in the momentum conjugate to time and admits symmetry reduction. Under uniform analyticity, coefficient bounds, and domain-containment assumptions, optimal truncation yields exponentially accurate interpolation and near-conservation of the modified extended Hamiltonian over exponentially long intervals. For a connected attitude network on $SO(3)^N$ with damping and uniformly positive, non-increasing coupling weights, we prove local asymptotic stability of the static consensus set for the continuous dynamics and exact preservation of total spatial momentum by the discrete scheme. Numerical experiments on the network and on a time-dependent rigid body on $SO(3)$ illustrate second-order convergence and the extended energy balance.

</details>


### [243] [Perturbation Algorithm and Iterative Realization of a Four-Layer Semi-Discrete Scheme for an Evolutionary Problem with a Variable Operator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26136)
*David Gulua,Jemal Rogava,Zurab Vashakidze*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 针对算子为 $A+B(t)$（$A$ 自伴正定、$B(t)$ 有界且依赖时间）的 Hilbert 空间演化方程 Cauchy 问题，借助扰动算法将三阶精度纯隐式四层半离散格式化归为三个两层格式，并用迭代法求解，仅需反演不依赖时间的算子 $I+\tau A$，证明了 $O(\tau^3)$ 的误差估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对含时间依赖有界扰动 $B(t)$ 的演化方程，全隐式高阶格式每步都需反演随 $t$ 变化的算子，计算代价高；本文旨在设计一种使反演算子不随时间变化的高阶隐式求解方案。

Method: 采用扰动算法（perturbation algorithm）将三阶精度的纯隐式四层半离散格式（即三步向后微分公式，three-step backward differentiation formula）分解为三个两层格式；在各两层格式中把含 $B(t)$ 的项移至右端，用迭代法求解，使每步只需反演定常算子 $I+\tau A$，并用 Gronwall 引理（Gronwall's lemma）的离散类比给出先验估计（a priori estimates）。

Result: 证明了迭代收敛速率为公比 $O(\tau)$ 的几何级数（geometric progression）；在关于初始若干层网格函数差商的假设下，证明了近似解误差为 $O(\tau^3)$ 阶，并通过数值实验（numerical experiments）加以验证。

Conclusion: 该扰动分解与迭代求解相结合的方案在保持三阶时间精度的同时，将每步计算归结为对时间无关算子的反演，兼具高阶精度与实现效率。

Abstract: In a Hilbert space, we consider the Cauchy problem for an evolutionary equation with the operator $A+B(t)$, where $A$ is a self-adjoint positive definite operator and $B(t)$ is a bounded operator depending on time. By means of the perturbation algorithm, the purely implicit four-layer semi-discrete scheme of the third order of approximation (the three-step backward differentiation formula) is reduced to three two-layer schemes, whose solutions are used to construct an approximate solution of the original problem. In each two-layer scheme, the term containing $B(t)$ is moved to the right-hand side, and the resulting equation is solved by an iterative method, so that only the time-independent operator $I+τA$ has to be inverted; the convergence of the iterations at the rate of a geometric progression with a ratio of order $τ$ is proved. A priori estimates for the two-layer and four-layer schemes are obtained by means of a discrete analogue of Gronwall's lemma. An estimate of order $τ^3$ for the error of the approximate solution is proved under an assumption on the difference quotients of the computed grid functions on the first layers. The results are illustrated by numerical experiments.

</details>


<div id='physics.flu-dyn'></div>

# physics.flu-dyn [[Back]](#toc)

### [244] [Flux-induced condensation in a radial geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26236)
*Yoav Tsori*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 在二元混合物圆环（annulus）内，内边界半透且与固定化学势的渗透侧交换物质，由此产生组分稳态通量；该驱动系统存在稳态流与熵产生，但其稳态在 Model B 动力学下等价于对数型有效外场中的平衡剖面。当驱流通量超过阈值时，内边界周围形成凝聚环并被静止前沿（front）钉扎、不发生粗化，且驱通量只移动相界线而不改变临界温度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通常认为搅拌与剪切会抑制相分离，但通过边界的输运可能反而诱导相分离。作者旨在揭示这种边界驱动下相分离的产生机制与标度行为。

Method: 研究充满二维圆环、在静止状态下处处稳定的二元混合物；内边界为半透膜（semipermeable），与固定化学势的渗透液交换物质，使某一组分以稳态通量穿过圆环。在 Model B 动力学框架下将稳态映射为对数型有效外场中的平衡剖面，并结合解析推导与数值求解验证。

Result: 驱动强度由渗透液与边界渗透率（permeability）决定而非外部强加。超过阈值通量后形成凝聚环，其静止前沿半径随体驱动力与通量之比呈指数依赖；前沿被有效场钉扎、不粗化，阈值通量仅按系统尺寸的逆对数（inverse logarithm）降低。界面张力（interfacial tension）导致临界核（critical nucleus）与折叠（fold）现象，后者对应凝聚态消失的阈值并给出闭式解。数值解确认上述预言并显示由两个鞍结分岔（saddle-node bifurcation）界定的滞回环（hysteresis loop）。

Conclusion: 边界输运驱动的二元混合物可表现出以对数有效场为本质的平衡式稳态，从而诱导并钉扎相分离。该机制给出通量阈值、前沿半径、临界核与折叠阈值的可解析刻画，并揭示滞回行为；由对称性，该理论亦可描述汇（sink）周围的空化（cavitation）现象。

Abstract: Stirring and shear usually suppress phase separation, but transport through a boundary can induce it. We study a binary mixture that is stable everywhere at rest and fills a two-dimensional annulus. The inner boundary is semipermeable and exchanges material with a permeate held at a fixed chemical potential, so that a steady flux of one component crosses the annulus. Although this driven system carries a steady current and produces entropy, within Model B dynamics its steady state is an equilibrium profile in an effective external field that is logarithmic in the distance from the center. Its strength is not imposed from outside but set by the permeate and the permeability of the boundary. Above a threshold driving flux, a condensed annulus forms around the inner boundary. It is bounded by a stationary front whose radius depends exponentially on the ratio of the bulk driving force to the flux. The front is pinned by the effective field and does not coarsen, and the threshold flux decreases only as the inverse logarithm of the system size. The driving flux shifts the phase lines but leaves the critical temperature unchanged. Interfacial tension gives rise to a critical nucleus and to a fold, at which the condensed state ceases to exist, with a threshold that we obtain in closed form. Numerical solutions confirm these predictions and reveal a hysteresis loop bounded by two saddle-node bifurcations. By symmetry, the theory also describes cavitation around a sink.

</details>


### [245] [Theory of magneto-electrokinetics in unidirectional electrolyte flow: Hydrodynamic potential and Hall effect](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26208)
*Yoav Tsori*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文建立稀薄电解质在单向流动与流动电流条件下磁动电动力学输运的理论框架，推导含磁致流体动力学势的类玻尔兹曼离子分布，并给出霍尔势的解析表达及二维入口瞬态的长度尺度分离判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 液态金属中的标准磁流体力学（magnetohydrodynamics）以电子为载流子且电导率近似均匀，而电解质具有空间变化的离子密度及渗透压等附加力，现有理论难以直接套用，故需针对电解质建立专门框架。

Method: 在稳态条件下将耦合的纳维–斯托克斯（Navier–Stokes）、能斯特–普朗克（Nernst–Planck）与泊松（Poisson）方程化为可解析处理的形式，推导同时包含静电势与磁场诱导的新颖流体动力学势（hydrodynamic potential）的类玻尔兹曼（Boltzmann-like）离子分布，并将理论应用于泊肃叶流（Poiseuille flow）及仅含反离子、无外加盐的流动情形，同时分析通道入口附近的二维空间瞬态。

Result: 导出感应霍尔势（Hall potential）表达式，其随流速与磁场强度近似线性增长，且基本与盐浓度无关；磁致流体动力学势显式依赖流动剖面且在近壁处不被屏蔽。二维入口分析显示明显的长度尺度分离：霍尔电场与空间电荷几乎瞬时响应磁场，而宏观盐浓度尾迹则在由佩克莱数（Péclet number）决定的更长格雷茨长度（Graetz length）上发展。

Conclusion: 该工作为微流控（microfluidic）与电化学系统中的离子输运提供了新的理论工具，并证实一维框架在入口瞬态问题中的适用性。

Abstract: We develop a theoretical framework for magneto-electrokinetic transport in dilute electrolytes under unidirectional flow and streaming current conditions. Unlike standard magnetohydrodynamics in liquid metals, where charge carriers are electrons and conductivity is typically uniform, electrolytes exhibit spatially varying ion densities and additional forces such as osmotic pressure. In steady state, the coupled Navier--Stokes, Nernst--Planck, and Poisson equations become analytically tractable. We derive a Boltzmann-like expression for ion distributions that incorporates both the electrostatic potential and a novel hydrodynamic potential arising from the magnetic field. This potential depends explicitly on the flow profile and is not screened close to the bounding walls. Applying the theory to Poiseuille flow, we derive expressions for the induced Hall potential and demonstrate its near-linear dependence on flow velocity and magnetic field strength, while remaining largely independent of salt concentration. To validate this one-dimensional framework, we analyze the two-dimensional spatial transients near the channel entrance. We demonstrate a separation of length scales: the Hall electric field and space charge adjust to the magnetic field almost instantaneously, whereas the macroscopic salt concentration wake develops over a much longer Graetz length dictated by the Péclet number. We also examine a similar flow with counterions only and no added salt, determining the resulting potential and ion distributions. This work offers new theoretical tools for understanding ion transport in microfluidic and electrochemical systems.

</details>
